### [1] [Learning 3D Affordances for Blade Insertion in Cluttered Stowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02549)
*Tianyu Li,Harpreet Sawhney,Minju Jung,Aditya Mehrotra,Kunal Mehrotra,Mudit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VulcanVoxel提出了一种空间推理方法，使用3D占用场上的掩码自编码器来预测机器人刀片在杂乱织物箱中的可行走自由空间，优于基于位姿的基线方法，并支持从RGB到体素的快速推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多操作任务需要推理自由空间的可通行性，但现有方法将可通行性建模为SE(3)位姿分布，这是在一个错误域中回答几何问题。标准生成方法从单峰数据中无法恢复多模态的几何可行解。

Method: VulcanVoxel使用3D占用场上的掩码自编码器（Masked Autoencoder），以场景几何为条件重构刀片占用场，在每个体素局部计算可行性，从而从单峰数据中恢复多模态预测。采用蒸馏学生网络实现从RGB到体素的快速推理。

Result: 在10,000个真实仓库收纳场景中训练，无需人工标注，top-5覆盖率达0.89，优于最佳位姿基线的0.71；蒸馏学生模型实现30ms的RGB到体素推理，而体素到体素基线需1.4秒。

Conclusion: 刀片可通行性是空间对象，是3D空间的几何可行子集。位姿参数缺乏推理未观测放置可行性的结构，标准生成目标只能忠实学习执行策略产生的单峰分布，无法恢复几何替代方案。VulcanVoxel通过空间推理有效解决了这一问题。

Original Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require reasoning about free-space affordances: discovering volumes where an extended rigid tool can safely navigate, complementary to surface contact affordances for grasping. Robotic stowing is a canonical instance, where a blade must sweep items aside inside cluttered fabric bins to create insertion space. Production stow systems generate millions of such episodes, but standard approaches with unimodal data infer affordances as SE(3) pose distributions, a geometric question asked in the wrong domain. VulcanVoxel keeps inference spatial: a masked autoencoder over 3D occupancy fields reconstructs blade occupancy conditioned on scene geometry, computing feasibility locally at each voxel and recovering multi-modal predictions from unimodal data. Blade affordances are spatial objects, subsets of 3D space defined by geometric feasibility. Pose parameters carry no structure for reasoning whether unobserved placements are feasible, and standard generative objectives including flow matching faithfully learn the unimodal distribution produced by execution policies and cannot recover geometric alternatives. Trained on 10,000 real warehouse stow episodes without human annotation, VulcanVoxel achieves top-5 coverage of 0.89 versus 0.71 for the best pose-based baseline, with a distilled student providing RGB-to-voxel inference in 30 ms. vs. 1.4 s. for voxel-to-voxel. We have released a dataset of real blade insertion cycles with RGB-D observations and pose trajectories at https://www.armbench.com/blade_insertion. html.

中文摘要翻译: 许多操作任务需要推理自由空间的可通行性：发现扩展刚性工具可以安全导航的体积区域，作为抓取表面接触可通行性的补充。机器人收纳是一个典型实例，其中刀片必须在杂乱织物箱内将物品扫到一旁以创建插入空间。生产级收纳系统会产生数百万个这样的操作片段，但使用单峰数据的标准方法将可通行性推理为SE(3)位姿分布，这是在错误域中提出的几何问题。VulcanVoxel保持了推理的空间性：基于3D占用场的掩码自编码器以场景几何为条件重构刀片占用场，在每个体素局部计算可行性，并从单峰数据中恢复多模态预测。刀片可通行性是空间对象，是由几何可行性定义的3D空间子集。位姿参数缺乏推理未观测放置是否可行的结构，而包括流匹配在内的标准生成目标只能忠实学习执行策略产生的单峰分布，无法恢复几何替代方案。在10,000个真实仓库收纳片段上训练且无需人工标注，VulcanVoxel实现了0.89的top-5覆盖率，而最佳位姿基线仅为0.71；其蒸馏学生网络提供30毫秒的RGB到体素推理，而体素到体素推理需1.4秒。我们已在https://www.armbench.com/blade_insertion.html发布了包含RGB-D观测和位姿轨迹的真实刀片插入循环数据集。

</details>


### [2] [DELTAVID: Enhancing Fine-Grained Spatiotemporal Perception with Cross-Video Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02551)
*Yankai Yang,Yancheng Long,Bin Wen,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DELTAVID 提出了一种可验证的代理任务框架，通过跨视频找差异（spot-the-difference）来增强视频多模态大模型的细粒度时空感知能力，并构建了 DELTAVID-10K 和 DELTAVID-Bench 数据集，实验表明该方法显著提升了跨视频差异理解能力，并将细粒度证据推理能力迁移至多项通用视频理解基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频多模态大模型在开放域视频理解上取得了显著进展，但在精细的局部时空感知方面仍存在不足——当两个视频全局语义几乎相同、仅在短时间跨度或小区域上有差异时，当前模型往往无法发现变化并提供可靠证据。为此，本文提出利用跨视频差异作为可扩展的代理监督信号，来增强模型的细粒度时空感知能力。

Method: 提出 DELTAVID 可验证代理任务框架，核心是将跨视频找差异转化为可训练的感知信号：模型通过比较相似视频，识别局部变化、判断时间边界并组织空间证据。为使其可扩展训练且可可靠评估，进一步构建 DELTAVID-10K 训练集和 DELTAVID-Bench 评估基准，将真实视频中的可控局部差异转化为带证据标签的训练和测试样本。

Result: DELTAVID 在跨视频差异理解任务上显著提升了性能，并且将学到的局部证据推理能力迁移到了通用视频理解基准上，包括 MMVU、MLVU、Video-MME、VideoHolmes、VideoMMMU、LVBench、TempCompass 和 LongVideoBench。

Conclusion: 跨视频差异不仅是诊断细粒度感知失败的有效方式，也是一种可扩展的代理监督信号，能够推动视频多模态大模型从粗粒度语义理解迈向细粒度时空证据推理。

Original Abstract: Video multimodal large language models have made strong progress on open-ended video understanding, but they still lack precise local spatiotemporal perception. When two videos share almost the same global semantics and differ only in a short time span or a small region, current models often fail to find the change and provide reliable evidence. We propose DELTAVID, a verifiable proxy-task framework that enhances fine-grained spatiotemporal perception with cross-video differences. The key idea is to turn cross-video spot-the-difference into a trainable perception signal, where a model identifies local changes, judges temporal boundaries, and organizes spatial evidence by comparing similar videos. To make this signal scalable to train and reliable to evaluate, we further introduce DELTAVID-10K and DELTAVID-Bench, which convert controllable local differences in real videos into evidence-labeled training and test samples. Experiments show that DELTAVID substantially improves performance on cross-video difference understanding and transfers the learned local evidence ability to general video understanding benchmarks, including MMVU, MLVU, Video-MME, VideoHolmes, VideoMMMU, LVBench, TempCompass, and LongVideoBench. These results show that cross-video differences are not only an effective way to diagnose fine-grained perception failures, but also a scalable proxy supervision that moves Video MLLMs from coarse semantic understanding toward fine-grained spatiotemporal evidence reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 视频多模态大模型在开放域视频理解上取得了显著进展，但在精确的局部时空感知方面仍存在不足。当两个视频几乎共享相同的全局语义、仅在一个短时间跨度或小区域上存在差异时，当前模型往往无法发现变化并提供可靠证据。我们提出 DELTAVID，一个可验证的代理任务框架，通过跨视频差异增强细粒度时空感知。其核心思想是将跨视频找差异转化为可训练的感知信号：模型通过比较相似视频，识别局部变化、判断时间边界并组织空间证据。为使该信号可扩展训练且可可靠评估，我们进一步引入 DELTAVID-10K 和 DELTAVID-Bench，将真实视频中的可控局部差异转化为带证据标签的训练和测试样本。实验表明，DELTAVID 显著提升了跨视频差异理解任务的性能，并将学到的局部证据能力迁移到通用视频理解基准上，包括 MMVU、MLVU、Video-MME、VideoHolmes、VideoMMMU、LVBench、TempCompass 和 LongVideoBench。这些结果表明，跨视频差异不仅是诊断细粒度感知失败的有效方式，也是一种可扩展的代理监督信号，推动视频多模态大模型从粗粒度语义理解迈向细粒度时空证据推理。

</details>


### [3] [Interpretable machine learning predicts Parkinson's disease severity using motion-corrected QSM MRI and multiband multiecho fMRI features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02553)
*Aixa X. Andrade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究使用可解释机器学习，基于QSM和多频多回波静息态fMRI提取的ReHo特征预测帕金森病运动严重程度。影像模型具有预测价值，组合模型最优（解释45.4%方差），75%的参与者预测误差在±5分内。SHAP分析揭示了小脑、丘脑、纹状体、岛叶和运动皮层等关键脑区特征。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 客观的神经影像生物标志物能够捕捉临床检查无法直接观察到的脑部变异，有望改进帕金森病运动功能评估。现有研究缺乏对结构影像（QSM）与功能影像（ReHo）在预测运动严重程度中各自贡献的系统比较，本文旨在探究二者在不同预测目标下的差异化作用。

Method: 从28名受试者（24名帕金森病患者、4名对照）中提取区域QSM和ReHo特征。设计13组特征集实验，涵盖纯影像、纯临床、影像加临床、完整特征、简化特征和多模态输入。采用支持向量回归、弹性网络、随机森林和XGBoost模型，通过嵌套交叉验证训练。评估指标包括合并留出集R²、RMSE、MAE、Pearson相关性、置换检验以及预测误差在±5分内的参与者比例。

Result: 纯影像模型携带显著的预测信号，而纯临床模型表现较弱。完整fMRI、完整QSM和临床变量的组合提供了最佳全局拟合，解释了运动严重度45.4%的方差。精选QSM加临床变量产生了最接近临床的预测结果，75.0%的参与者预测误差在±5分内，且在前列模型中MAE最低。SHAP突出了小脑、丘脑、纹状体、岛叶和运动皮层等特征。

Conclusion: QSM和多频多回波fMRI衍生的ReHo特征捕捉了帕金森病运动严重程度的独特且可解释的维度。该结果表明，结构和功能影像根据临床预测目标的不同而发挥不同作用，二者互补有助于实现更精准的运动功能评估。

Original Abstract: Introduction: Objective neuroimaging biomarkers may improve Parkinson's disease motor assessment by capturing brain variation not directly observable from clinical examination. We used interpretable machine learning to predict current motor severity, measured by MDS-UPDRS Part III, from QSM and multiband multi-echo resting-state fMRI-derived ReHo features.
  Methods: Regional QSM and ReHo features were extracted from 28 participants, including 24 individuals with Parkinson's disease and 4 controls. Thirteen feature-set experiments evaluated imaging-only, clinical-only, imaging-plus-clinical, full, reduced, and multimodal inputs. Support vector regression, Elastic Net, Random Forest, and XGBoost models were trained using nested cross-validation. Performance was assessed using pooled held-out R^2, RMSE, MAE, Pearson correlation, permutation testing, and the proportion of participants predicted within +/-5 MDS-UPDRS Part III points.
  Results: Imaging-only models carried meaningful predictive signal, whereas the clinical-only model performed weakly. Full fMRI, full QSM, and clinical variables provided the strongest global fit, explaining 45.4% of variance in motor severity. Selected QSM plus clinical variables produced the most clinically close predictions, with 75.0% of participants predicted within +/-5 points and the lowest MAE among top-performing models. SHAP highlighted cerebellar, thalamic, striatal, insular, and motor cortical features.
  Conclusion: QSM and multiband multi-echo fMRI-derived ReHo capture distinct, interpretable dimensions of Parkinson's disease motor severity. These findings show that structural and functional imaging contribute differently depending on the clinical prediction goal.

中文摘要翻译: 引言：客观的神经影像生物标志物可通过捕捉临床检查无法直接观察到的脑部变异，改善帕金森病的运动功能评估。本研究使用可解释机器学习，基于QSM和多频多回波静息态fMRI衍生的ReHo特征预测当前运动严重程度（以MDS-UPDRS Part III衡量）。方法：从28名受试者（包括24名帕金森病患者和4名对照）中提取区域QSM和ReHo特征。设计了13组特征集实验，评估纯影像、纯临床、影像加临床、完整特征、简化特征和多模态输入。采用支持向量回归、弹性网络、随机森林和XGBoost模型，通过嵌套交叉验证进行训练。使用合并留出集R²、RMSE、MAE、Pearson相关性、置换检验以及预测误差在±5 MDS-UPDRS Part III分内的参与者比例评估性能。结果：纯影像模型携带显著的预测信号，而纯临床模型表现较弱。完整fMRI、完整QSM和临床变量的组合提供了最佳全局拟合，解释了运动严重度45.4%的方差。精选QSM加临床变量产生了最接近临床的预测结果，75.0%的参与者预测误差在±5分内，且在前列模型中取得了最低MAE。SHAP突出了小脑、丘脑、纹状体、岛叶和运动皮层等特征。结论：QSM和多频多回波fMRI衍生的ReHo捕捉了帕金森病运动严重程度的独特且可解释的维度。这些发现表明，结构性影像和功能性影像根据临床预测目标的不同而发挥不同作用。

</details>


### [4] [Reliability-Aware Monocular Depth Supervision for Sparse-View Neural Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02554)
*Wei-Teng Chu,Yashasvini Gopalan,Changju Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在稀疏视角户外驾驶场景中，如何使用单目深度先验（Depth Anything V2）进行神经重建，发现该策略对Splatfacto的渲染质量和几何精度有明显提升，但对Mip-NeRF-360效果有限，且需通过光度掩码选择性应用深度监督。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 户外驾驶场景中相机通常沿狭窄前向轨迹运动，多视角重叠有限，导致稀疏视角神经重建极具挑战。虽然单目深度估计器可提供密集几何先验，但其预测存在噪声且在不同图像区域可靠性不一致，因此需要研究如何有效且选择性地利用单目深度监督。

Method: 使用Depth Anything V2作为密集单目深度先验，通过尺度-平移拟合将其预测对齐到度量深度，并利用基于RGB基线的光度掩码选择性地应用深度监督。在Mip-NeRF-360和Splatfacto两种场景表示上评估该策略。

Result: 在KITTISeq02 every2稀疏视图下，掩码单目深度监督对Mip-NeRF-360的渲染提升微乎其微且未改善度量几何；而Splatfacto受益明显，PSNR从14.903提升至15.932，RMSE从0.542降至0.100。进一步实验表明Splatfacto的提升来自于选择可靠的低误差区域，而非单纯减少深度监督像素数。在Bicycle场景中，深度监督在提升几何质量的同时会损害RGB渲染质量。

Conclusion: 单目深度先验对欠约束的稀疏视图重建有用，但应选择性地应用并设置适中的权重。

Original Abstract: Sparse-view neural reconstruction is challenging in outdoor driving scenes, where cameras usually move along a narrow forward-facing trajectory and provide limited multi-view overlap. Although monocular depth estimators can provide dense geometric priors, their predictions are noisy, and not uniformly reliable across image regions. In this work, we study monocular depth supervision for sparse-view neural reconstruction. We use Depth Anything V2 as a dense monocular depth prior, align its predictions to metric depth using scale-shift fitting, and apply depth supervision selectively through photometric masks generated from an RGB-only baseline model. We evaluate this strategy on two representative scene representations: Mip-NeRF-360 and Splatfacto. On KITTISeq02 under an every2 sparse-view setting, masked monocular depth supervision gives only marginal rendering gains for Mip-NeRF-360 and does not improve metric geometry. In contrast, Splatfacto benefits more clearly, improving PSNR from 14.903 to 15.932 and reducing RMSE from 0.542 to 0.100. Additional KITTISeq05 experiments and matched-ratio mask ablations further show that the gains for Splatfacto come from selecting reliable low-error regions rather than simply reducing the number of depth-supervised pixels. Additional experiments on the Bicycle scene show that depth supervision can improve geometry while hurting RGB rendering quality when multi-view coverage is already strong. Overall, our results suggest that monocular depth priors are useful for under-constrained sparse-view reconstruction, but should be applied selectively and with moderate weighting.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏视角的神经重建在户外驾驶场景中极具挑战性，因为相机通常沿狭窄的前向轨迹运动，提供的多视角重叠有限。虽然单目深度估计器可以提供密集的几何先验，但其预测存在噪声，且在不同图像区域间的可靠性不一致。在这项工作中，我们研究了稀疏视角神经重建中的单目深度监督问题。我们使用Depth Anything V2作为密集单目深度先验，通过尺度-平移拟合将其预测对齐到度量深度，并利用基于RGB基线模型生成的光度掩码，选择性地应用深度监督。我们在两种有代表性的场景表示上评估了该策略：Mip-NeRF-360和Splatfacto。在KITTISeq02数据集的every2稀疏视图设置下，掩码单目深度监督对Mip-NeRF-360的渲染提升微乎其微，且未改善度量几何。相比之下，Splatfacto受益更为明显，PSNR从14.903提升至15.932，RMSE从0.542降至0.100。额外的KITTISeq05实验和匹配比例掩码消融实验进一步表明，Splatfacto的提升来源于选择可靠的低误差区域，而非单纯减少深度监督的像素数量。在Bicycle场景上的额外实验表明，当多视角覆盖已经较强时，深度监督虽然可以改善几何质量，但会损害RGB渲染质量。总体而言，我们的结果表明，单目深度先验对于欠约束的稀疏视图重建是有用的，但应选择性地应用并采用适中的权重。

</details>


### [5] [Do Diabetic Foot Ulcer Segmentation Models Generalize? A Cross-Dataset Benchmark of CNN and Transformer Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02555)
*Abderrahmane Benfatah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了三种代表性分割架构（U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer-B2）在糖尿病足溃疡分割任务上的跨数据集泛化能力，发现所有模型在跨域场景下性能显著下降，且架构族（而非模型复杂度）是决定泛化性能的关键因素——基于Transformer的SegFormer-B2表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有糖尿病足溃疡分割模型虽在数据集内报告高精度，但其在来自不同临床来源的数据上的行为几乎未被衡量，缺乏对模型跨医院泛化能力的系统性基准测试。

Method: 在统一的无泄漏协议下，使用FUSeg/AZH合并数据集训练三种代表性分割架构（卷积型U-Net和DeepLabV3+、Transformer型SegFormer-B2），然后在两个独立外部数据集（DFUC2022和Medetec）上进行零微调评估，并对2160张外部测试图像进行逐图像失败分析。

Result: 所有模型在域内表现良好（Dice 0.80-0.83），但在跨数据集时性能显著下降。SegFormer-B2在两个外部集上泛化最佳（DFUC2022 Dice 0.557，Medetec Dice 0.786），优于两个卷积模型；DeepLabV3+泛化甚至弱于更简单的U-Net。SegFormer-B2在DFUC2022上的灾难性失败率最低（31.1%），Wilcoxon符号秩检验确认排名一致性（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 架构族（而非模型复杂度）驱动跨医院泛化能力，Transformer架构在跨数据集场景下具有显著优势。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models for diabetic foot ulcer (DFU) segmentation routinely report high accuracy, but they are almost always trained and tested on the same dataset, leaving their behaviour on data from a different clinical source largely unmeasured. We benchmark three representative segmentation architectures -- U-Net and DeepLabV3+ (convolutional) and SegFormer-B2 (Transformer) -- under an identical, leakage-screened protocol: training on the combined FUSeg/AZH wound data and evaluating, without fine-tuning, on two independent external datasets (DFUC2022 and Medetec). All models achieve strong in-domain performance (Dice 0.80--0.83) but degrade substantially across datasets. The degradation is, however, architecture-dependent: SegFormer-B2 generalizes best on both external sets (DFUC2022 Dice 0.557, Medetec Dice 0.786), outperforming both convolutional models, while the more complex DeepLabV3+ generalizes worse than the simpler U-Net. Per-image failure analysis on 2,160 images across both external test sets confirms that SegFormer-B2 produces the fewest catastrophic failures on DFUC2022 (31.1%), compared with U-Net (38.5%) and DeepLabV3+ (43.0%). The consistent ranking across two independent external sources, confirmed by Wilcoxon signed-rank tests (p < 0.001 on both datasets), indicates that architecture family, not model complexity, drives cross-hospital generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 用于糖尿病足溃疡（DFU）分割的深度学习模型通常报告较高的准确率，但这些模型几乎总是在同一数据集上训练和测试，因此其在不同临床来源数据上的行为在很大程度上未被衡量。我们在一个统一、无泄漏的协议下对三种代表性分割架构——U-Net和DeepLabV3+（卷积型）以及SegFormer-B2（Transformer型）——进行了基准测试：在合并的FUSeg/AZH伤口数据上训练，并在两个独立的外部数据集（DFUC2022和Medetec）上进行评估，不进行微调。所有模型在域内均表现出色（Dice系数0.80-0.83），但在跨数据集时性能显著下降。然而，这种下降是架构依赖的：SegFormer-B2在两个外部集上泛化最佳（DFUC2022 Dice 0.557，Medetec Dice 0.786），优于两个卷积模型，而更复杂的DeepLabV3+的泛化能力弱于更简单的U-Net。对两个外部测试集共2160张图像的逐图像失败分析证实，SegFormer-B2在DFUC2022上产生的灾难性失败最少（31.1%），相比之下U-Net为38.5%，DeepLabV3+为43.0%。两个独立外部源上的排名一致性（Wilcoxon符号秩检验确认，两个数据集均p<0.001）表明，驱动跨医院泛化能力的是架构族，而非模型复杂度。

</details>


### [6] [How many labels do you need? A decision framework for cross-habitat marine species recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02559)
*Alzayat Saleh,Mostafa Rahimi Azghadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个决策框架，量化了将视觉识别系统跨海洋栖息地迁移时的标注成本与识别精度之间的权衡。实验表明，使用冻结的自监督基础模型（DINOv2）搭配线性分类器，每类仅需10-20张标注图像即可在新站点实现可靠识别，将标注工作量减少约一个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生态学家在将自动化图像识别系统部署到新站点时，缺乏基于证据的标注工作量指导，无法平衡标注成本与识别精度。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖5个数据集、3个海洋、3个分类群的基准测试，评估了4种识别模型（DINOv2、CLIP、ResNet-50、EfficientNet-B4）在4种迁移策略（线性探测、LoRA、视觉提示微调、全参数微调）下的表现，涉及三种迁移协议：同栖息地跨站点迁移、跨数据集地理迁移、少样本适应曲线。

Result: 1) 冻结的自监督基础模型（DINOv2+线性分类器，仅1538个可训练参数）泛化能力优于大了四个数量级的全微调卷积基线；2) 该模型学习的是物种诊断性、栖息地不变的表征，而基线模型编码了在新站点失效的栖息地特异性捷径；3) 每类仅需10-20张标注图像即可实现新站点的可靠识别，标注成本降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 部署到新站点的生态监测项目可以采用冻结的开放基础模型（DINOv2）搭配简单的线性分类器，每类仅需标注10-20张图像（约每站点1-4小时），即可实现可靠识别。该框架允许项目根据预期精度跨站点、生态系统和平台预算标注工作量。

Original Abstract: Automated image recognition is increasingly used to scale ecological monitoring beyond manual annotation, yet ecologists lack evidence-based guidance on how much labelling effort reliable deployment at new sites requires. We present a decision framework quantifying the trade-off between labelling effort and recognition accuracy when transferring vision systems across marine habitats. The benchmark spans five datasets, three oceans, and three taxonomic groups (fish, corals, invertebrates), from tropical reefs in the Great Barrier Reef and French Polynesia to a temperate Danish fjord. We evaluated four recognition models (DINOv2, CLIP, ResNet-50, EfficientNet-B4) under four adaptation strategies (linear probing, LoRA, Visual Prompt Tuning, full fine-tuning) across three protocols: within-habitat transfer across 20 reef sites (240 runs), cross-dataset geographic transfer along a difficulty gradient (40 runs), and few-shot adaptation curves with 0-100 labelled samples per class (648 runs). Frozen self-supervised foundation features (DINOv2 + linear classifier, 1,538 trainable parameters) generalised to unseen reef sites at least as well as fully fine-tuned convolutional baselines four orders of magnitude larger; they learned species-diagnostic, habitat-invariant representations, whereas baselines encoded habitat-specific shortcuts that fail at new sites. As few as 10-20 labelled images per species sufficed to deploy reliable recognition at a new site, cutting annotation effort by roughly an order of magnitude.
  Solution. Programmes expanding to new sites can deploy reliable recognition by pairing a frozen, open foundation model (DINOv2) with a simple linear classifier and annotating only 10-20 images per species - roughly 1-4 hours per site. The framework lets programmes budget labelling effort against expected accuracy across sites, ecosystems, and platforms.

中文摘要翻译: 自动化图像识别正越来越多地被用于将生态监测扩展到人工标注之外，然而生态学家缺乏基于证据的指导，无法确定在新站点进行可靠部署需要多少标注工作量。我们提出了一个决策框架，用于量化将视觉系统跨海洋栖息地迁移时的标注成本与识别精度之间的权衡。该基准测试涵盖五个数据集、三个海洋和三个分类群（鱼类、珊瑚、无脊椎动物），从大堡礁和法属波利尼西亚的热带珊瑚礁到温带丹麦峡湾。我们评估了四种识别模型（DINOv2、CLIP、ResNet-50、EfficientNet-B4）在四种迁移策略（线性探测、LoRA、视觉提示微调、全参数微调）下的表现，涉及三种协议：跨20个珊瑚礁站点的同栖息地迁移（240次运行）、沿难度梯度的跨数据集地理迁移（40次运行），以及每类0-100个标注样本的少样本适应曲线（648次运行）。冻结的自监督基础模型特征（DINOv2 + 线性分类器，1538个可训练参数）泛化到未见过的珊瑚礁站点的能力，至少与大了四个数量级的全微调卷积基线模型相当；它们学习了物种诊断性、栖息地不变的表征，而基线模型编码了在新站点失效的栖息地特异性捷径。每类仅需10-20张标注图像即可在新站点部署可靠的识别，将标注工作量减少了大约一个数量级。解决方案：扩展到新站点的项目可以通过结合冻结的开放基础模型（DINOv2）与简单的线性分类器，并仅标注每类10-20张图像（约每站点1-4小时），来部署可靠的识别。该框架允许项目根据跨站点、生态系统和平台的预期精度来预算标注工作量。

</details>


### [7] [Inpainting U-Net for seamless pedestrian-level wind prediction across urban morphologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02560)
*Jingzi Huang,Claire E. Heaney,Tao Li,Xinzhe Li,Graham O. Hughes,Maarten van Reeuwijk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段U-Net框架，用于高效预测真实城市形态下的行人级时均风速，第一阶段进行逐块预测，第二阶段通过基于修复的细化模型消除块边界伪影，在UrbanTALES数据集上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高保真数值模拟（如LES）在行人级风速预测中计算成本过高，难以用于城市设计与风舒适度的快速评估，因此需要一种高效且灵活的代理模型。

Method: 提出两阶段U-Net框架：第一阶段（M1）利用归一化建筑高度和来流信息逐块预测风速场，支持任意大小城市域；第二阶段（M2）作为基于修复的细化模型，利用更大上下文窗口结合M1预测与局部形态信息，通过高斯-赛德尔迭代方案消除块边界伪影。

Result: M1能捕捉风速场的主要空间分布，在低速和中速区域表现良好，但高速峰值精度不足；M2显著减少了块边界伪影并改善了空间连续性。在未见过的城市案例中，框架能合理再现平均风速和空间变异性，但最大风速仍被低估。

Conclusion: 该两阶段U-Net框架为真实城市形态下高分辨率行人级风速预测提供了一种高效且灵活的代理模型。

Original Abstract: Pedestrian-level wind prediction is essential for urban design and wind-comfort assessment, but high-fidelity simulations such as LES remain computationally expensive for rapid evaluation. This study develops a two-stage U-Net framework for efficient prediction of time-averaged pedestrian-level wind speed over realistic urban morphologies. The model is trained and evaluated using the UrbanTALES dataset, which contains realistic city configurations under different approaching wind directions. In the first stage, a baseline U-Net model (M1) predicts wind fields patch-by-patch from normalised building height and fetch information. This formulation allows application to urban domains of arbitrary size, but independent patch inference can introduce discontinuities at patch boundaries. To address this, a second U-Net model (M2) is introduced as an inpainting-based refinement model. M2 uses a larger contextual window containing the initial M1 prediction and local morphology to reduce discontinuities using neighbouring flow information. During full-field inference, M2 is applied iteratively using a Gauss-Seidel scheme until convergence. Results show that M1 captures the main spatial distribution of pedestrian-level wind speed and performs well in low- and moderate-velocity regions, although high-velocity peaks are less accurate. M2 substantially reduces patch-boundary artefacts and improves spatial coherence. Across unseen urban cases, the framework reproduces mean velocity and spatial variability reasonably well, while maximum velocities remain underestimated. Overall, the proposed framework provides an efficient and flexible surrogate model for high-resolution pedestrian-level wind prediction across realistic urban morphologies.

中文摘要翻译: 行人级风速预测对于城市设计和风舒适度评估至关重要，但高保真模拟（如大涡模拟）因计算成本过高而难以用于快速评估。本研究开发了一个两阶段U-Net框架，用于高效预测真实城市形态下的时均行人级风速。模型使用UrbanTALES数据集进行训练和评估，该数据集包含不同来流方向下的真实城市配置。在第一阶段，基线U-Net模型（M1）根据归一化的建筑高度和来流信息逐块预测风场。这种设计允许模型应用于任意大小的城市区域，但逐块独立推理可能会在块边界处引入不连续性。为解决这一问题，引入了第二个U-Net模型（M2）作为基于修复的细化模型。M2使用包含初始M1预测和局部形态信息的更大上下文窗口，利用邻近流场信息减少不连续性。在全场推理过程中，M2通过高斯-赛德尔迭代方案迭代应用直至收敛。结果表明，M1能够捕捉行人级风速的主要空间分布，在低速和中速区域表现良好，但高速峰值的精度较低。M2显著减少了块边界伪影并改善了空间连续性。在未见过的城市案例中，该框架能够合理再现平均风速和空间变异性，但最大风速仍被低估。总体而言，所提出的框架为真实城市形态下的高分辨率行人级风速预测提供了一种高效且灵活的代理模型。

</details>


### [8] [Double-Helix Active Geometry: LiDAR-Anchored Multi-View Depth with Selective Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02561)
*Jinwen Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DH-Active，一种轻量级、免训练的几何后端，将消费级深度传感器（如iPhone LiDAR）视为度量标尺而非唯一深度来源：利用近场有效深度通过PnP锚定双视角相对位姿，再对无深度返回的视觉可追踪点进行三角化测量；通过视差/重投影门控在几何条件不适定处主动放弃估计，形成显式空洞和选择性评分。CPU上中位延迟1.11ms，比GPU上的DINOv2-L视觉分支快约38倍，在多个基准上恢复深度中位相对误差1.4%-6.7%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 消费级深度传感器（如iPhone LiDAR）有效范围短、返回稀疏，现有方法要么依赖纯深度传感器（范围受限），要么依赖纯视觉（需大量训练数据或计算资源），缺乏一种轻量、高效且能在近场稀疏深度与远场视觉线索之间建立几何约束的解决方案。

Method: 1) 将传感器视为度量标尺：近场有效深度点通过PnP锚定双视图的相对位姿；2) 对无深度返回的视觉可追踪点在该位姿下进行三角化测量；3) 视差/重投影门控机制在几何条件不适定时主动放弃估计（留下显式空洞和选择性评分），避免强制估计引入误差；4) 核心前端包括螺旋采样、稀疏反投影和空洞分类，无需预处理和多视图恢复。

Result: 1) 核心前端CPU中位延迟1.11ms（OpenCV 14线程），比GPU上DINOv2-L视觉分支快约38倍；2) 在iPhone双摄、TUM RGB-D和ARKitScenes基准上，恢复深度中位相对误差1.4%-6.7%；3) 在仅用2m内深度设置尺度、激光扫描为真值的ARKitScenes协议中，远场覆盖率64.2%，中位相对误差13.4%；4) 对比了多种失败替代方案：单帧散焦、经典聚焦堆叠深度、散焦-LiDAR融合、点对点ICP、注意力空洞重采样；5) 1.26B参数学习模型在oracle尺度对齐后仍更准确，但DH-Active零学习参数。

Conclusion: DH-Active以零学习参数、近毫秒级CPU延迟实现了有竞争力的度量稀疏深度恢复，核心贡献在于显式放弃机制（在几何不适定处留下空洞而非强行估计）以及将传感器视为度量标尺的几何后端思路，展示了纯几何方法在消费级深度传感器场景中的有效性和效率优势。

Original Abstract: Consumer depth sensors such as the LiDAR scanner on recent iPhones provide metric range, but their useful range is short and their returns are sparse. We present DH-Active, a lightweight, training-free geometry back-end that treats the sensor as a metric ruler rather than the sole source of depth. Near-field returns anchor the metric relative pose of two views through PnP; visually trackable samples without a valid depth return are then triangulated under that pose. A parallax/reprojection gate abstains wherever the geometry is ill-conditioned, leaving an explicit hole and a selective score instead of forcing an estimate. The measured core front end, including spiral sampling, sparse back-projection, and hole taxonomy but excluding preprocessing and multi-view recovery, runs at 1.11 ms median latency on CPU (OpenCV using 14 threads), about 38 times faster than a DINOv2-L visual branch on GPU in our timing setup. Across two iPhone captures and the public TUM RGB-D and ARKitScenes benchmarks, held-out depth is recovered at 1.4 to 6.7 percent median relative error. In a controlled ARKitScenes protocol that uses only returns within 2 m to set scale and an independent laser scan as ground truth, DH-Active achieves 64.2 percent scene-median coverage of evaluable far-field candidates at 13.4 percent scene-median relative error; direct triangulation from the device trajectory is not usable. We also report the alternatives that failed in our tests: single-frame defocus, classical focus-stack depth, defocus-LiDAR fusion, point-to-point ICP over a good visual-inertial track, and attention-to-holes resampling. A 1.26 B learned model remains more accurate after oracle scale alignment. The contribution here is narrower: metric sparse depth, explicit abstention, zero learned parameters, and near-millisecond CPU cost.

中文摘要翻译: 消费级深度传感器（如最新iPhone上的LiDAR扫描仪）能够提供度量范围信息，但其有效范围短且返回点稀疏。本文提出DH-Active，一种轻量级、免训练的几何后端，将传感器视为度量标尺而非唯一的深度来源。近场有效深度返回通过PnP锚定双视图间的度量相对位姿；无有效深度返回的视觉可追踪样本在该位姿下进行三角化测量。视差/重投影门控机制在几何条件不适定时主动放弃估计，留下显式空洞和选择性评分，而非强制给出估计值。核心前端（包括螺旋采样、稀疏反投影和空洞分类，排除预处理和多视图恢复）在CPU上（OpenCV，14线程）的中位延迟为1.11毫秒，在我们的计时设置中比GPU上的DINOv2-L视觉分支快约38倍。在两个iPhone拍摄数据以及公开的TUM RGB-D和ARKitScenes基准上，恢复的遮挡深度中位相对误差为1.4%至6.7%。在一个受控的ARKitScenes协议中——仅使用2米内的返回点设置尺度，并以独立激光扫描为真值——DH-Active对可评估的远场候选点实现了64.2%的场景中位覆盖率，中位相对误差为13.4%；而直接从设备轨迹进行三角化测量则不可用。我们还报告了在测试中失败的替代方案：单帧散焦、经典聚焦堆叠深度、散焦-LiDAR融合、基于优质视觉惯性轨迹的点对点ICP以及注意力空洞重采样。一个1.26B参数的学习模型在oracle尺度对齐后仍然更准确。本文的贡献更为专注：度量稀疏深度、显式放弃机制、零学习参数以及近毫秒级CPU计算成本。

</details>


### [9] [Entropy-Coded MS-VQ-VAE with Learned Priors for Ultra-Low Bitrate Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02562)
*Manikanta Kotthapalli,Banafsheh Rekabdar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明在基于向量量化的视频编码器中，离散潜表示可以完全避开连续潜表示在极低比特率（<0.1 bpp）下因不可微速率信号导致的率失真优化困境。通过调节码本大小K∈{128,256,512,1024}，模型在UCF101上达到0.043-0.064 bpp，比H.265 CRF 36的LPIPS质量更好且比特数减少5-7.6倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于连续潜表示的学习型视频编码器在极低比特率（<0.1 bpp）下无法有效运作，因为缺乏可微的速率信号，拉格朗日优化无法在极端压缩比下有效平衡重建质量与比特率。离散潜表示可以完全规避这一限制。

Method: 基于MS-VQ-VAE架构，通过调节码本大小K∈{128,256,512,1024}在统一训练协议下绘制率失真曲线上的四个工作点；采用EMA稳定的码本更新与死码重启（dead-code restart）解决梯度-based VQ在K≤512时的灾难性坍塌问题；利用学习型自回归先验利用码本使用的非均匀分布（呈幂律分布）将实际比特率推至远低于码本信息上限。

Result: 在500个UCF101测试片段上，模型在0.043-0.064 bpp下运行，比H.264和H.265在该分辨率下的实际地板分别低3.3-5倍和5-7.6倍；所有MS-VQ-VAE配置在感知质量（LPIPS）上均优于H.265 CRF 36，K=1024时LPIPS绝对差距0.072且比特数减少5.1倍；码本分析证实了幂律指数分布和70-85%的熵效率。

Conclusion: 离散潜表示结合向量量化与自回归先验可以有效解决极低比特率下的编码难题，建立了一个原理性的学习型熵编码流水线。

Original Abstract: Learned video codecs based on continuous latent representations struggle to operate reliably below 0.1 bits per pixel~(bpp): without a differentiable rate signal, Lagrangian optimisation cannot effectively trade reconstruction quality for bitrate at extreme compression ratios. We demonstrate that discrete latent representations sidestep this limitation entirely. In a vector-quantized~(VQ) codec, the codebook size~$K$ imposes a hard information ceiling of $\log_2 K$ bits per symbol; a learned autoregressive prior then exploits the non-uniform distribution of code usage -- which we show follows a power law -- to push actual bitrates well below this ceiling, without any rate-penalty tuning.
  Building on the MS-VQ-VAE architecture introduced in~\cite{kotthapalli2026msvqvae}, we sweep $K \in \{128, 256, 512, 1024\}$ under a uniform training protocol to trace four operating points on the rate-distortion~(RD) curve. We identify and resolve a critical training instability: gradient-based VQ collapses catastrophically at $K \leq 512$, whereas EMA-stabilised codebook updates with dead-code restart maintain full utilisation across all configurations. On 500 UCF101 test clips ($64\!\times\!64$, 32~frames), our models operate at 0.043-0.064~bpp -- 3.3-5$\times$ below H.264's practical floor and $5$-$7.6\times$ below H.265's floor at this resolution. Every MS-VQ-VAE configuration outperforms H.265 CRF\,36 on perceptual quality (LPIPS) despite using $5$-$7.6\times$ fewer bits. At $K{=}1024$, the model surpasses H.265 CRF\,36 on LPIPS by a margin of 0.072 absolute while using $5.1\times$ fewer bits. Codebook analysis confirms power-law index distributions and 70-85\% entropy efficiency, establishing the pipeline as a principled learned entropy coder.

中文摘要翻译: 基于连续潜表示的学习型视频编码器在低于0.1比特每像素（bpp）的极低码率下难以可靠运行：由于缺乏可微的速率信号，拉格朗日优化无法在极端压缩比下有效权衡重建质量与比特率。我们证明离散潜表示可以完全规避这一限制。在向量量化（VQ）编解码器中，码本大小K为每个符号施加了log₂ K比特的硬信息上限；学习型自回归先验利用码本使用的非均匀分布——我们证明其遵循幂律分布——将实际比特率推至远低于该上限，无需任何率惩罚调参。基于Kotthapalli等人提出的MS-VQ-VAE架构，我们在统一训练协议下遍历K∈{128,256,512,1024}，绘制率失真曲线上的四个工作点。我们识别并解决了一个关键的训练不稳定性问题：当K≤512时，基于梯度的VQ会发生灾难性坍塌，而采用EMA稳定的码本更新与死码重启策略可在所有配置下保持满利用率。在500个UCF101测试片段（64×64分辨率，32帧）上，我们的模型在0.043-0.064 bpp下运行——比H.264在该分辨率下的实际地板低3.3-5倍，比H.265低5-7.6倍。尽管使用的比特数减少5-7.6倍，所有MS-VQ-VAE配置在感知质量（LPIPS）上均优于H.265 CRF 36。在K=1024时，模型在使用5.1倍更少比特的情况下，LPIPS比H.265 CRF 36高出0.072的绝对差距。码本分析证实了幂律指数分布和70-85%的熵效率，确立了该流水线作为原理性学习型熵编码器的地位。

</details>


### [10] [Attention Dynamics in Diffusion Models: A Visual Analytics Framework for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02563)
*Yiran Xiao,George Legrady*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个可视化分析框架，用于探索扩散模型中跨注意力图的逐步骤演化、时间集中性和空间关系，支持对人类与AI协作生成过程的结构化分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型工作流依赖聚合注意力或标量摘要，将时间变化与图像空间证据分离，难以解释语义结构的涌现和演化过程。

Method: 将定量度量与数据驱动的阶段识别相结合，通过交互式工作流实现跨注意力行为的结构化分析，包含时序和空间视图的联动。

Result: 在包含60个提示词的Stable-Diffusion类基准测试上，展示了可重复、可解释的模式，验证了时序与空间视图联动有助于观察和讨论生成过程。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于更有效地支持人类与AI在生成过程中的协作，弥合了时间动态与图像空间证据之间的分析鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Diffusion-based text-to-image models can synthesize complex and highly structured visual content, yet the emergence and evolution of semantic structure remain difficult to interpret. Many existing workflows rely on aggregated attention or scalar summaries that separate temporal change from image-space evidence. To address this gap, we present a visual analytics framework for exploring attention dynamics in diffusion models: the step-indexed evolution of token-level cross-attention maps, their temporal concentration, and their spatial relationships. Our approach enables structured analysis of attention behavior across generation steps by integrating quantitative measures with data-driven stage identification in an interactive workflow. Case studies on a structured 60-prompt Stable-Diffusion-class benchmark illustrate recurring, interpretable patterns within this setting and show how linked temporal and spatial views facilitate the observation and discussion of generative processes, supporting more effective human-AI collaboration.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散的文生图模型能够合成复杂且高度结构化的视觉内容，然而语义结构的涌现和演化仍然难以解释。许多现有工作流依赖于聚合注意力或标量摘要，将时间变化与图像空间证据分离。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了一个用于探索扩散模型中注意力动态的可视分析框架：即token级交叉注意力图的逐步骤索引演化、其时间集中性及其空间关系。我们的方法通过将定量度量与数据驱动的阶段识别整合到交互式工作流中，实现了对跨生成步骤的注意力行为的结构化分析。在包含60个提示词的结构化Stable-Diffusion类基准测试上的案例研究表明，该设置中存在可重复、可解释的模式，并展示了时序与空间视图的联动如何促进对生成过程的观察与讨论，从而支持更有效的人机协作。

</details>


### [11] [From Raw Segmentations to Simulation-Ready Cardiac Meshes: An Automated Framework for Anatomical Reconstruction and Virtual Cohort Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02564)
*Francesco Fabbri,Martino Andrea Scarpolini,Paolo Ciancarella,Francesco Tudisco,Roberto Verzicco,Alessandro Ricci,Francesco Viola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种半自动流水线，能在几分钟内将基于CT的心脏分割结果转换为适用于多物理仿真的水密、同拓扑网格，并支持统计形状建模和虚拟人群生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有心脏计算模型多局限于单一个体或患者特异性解剖结构，难以纳入人群水平的变异性以进行不确定性量化；同时，医学图像分割结果常存在伪影、网格缺陷或域不连续问题，难以转化为拓扑一致且适合多物理仿真的几何模型。

Method: 基于现代深度学习分割方法，结合基于模板的配准阶段来规整伪影并强制网格质量约束；采用Chamfer距离变形策略将高质量模板向每个分割心脏变形，在保持拓扑的同时匹配各腔室。

Result: 在58例健康心脏CT扫描上验证，生成的网格水密、同拓扑且具有一致的点对应关系；主成分分析表明低维潜在空间能高效捕获人群变异性；高斯混合模型可生成合成解剖结构。

Conclusion: 该开源框架提供了从原始分割到可仿真心脏几何的完整路径，能够生成解剖上一致的虚拟人群，支持大规模基于计算机的临床研究。

Original Abstract: Computational models of the human heart are widely used to study electromechanical and fluid-dynamical cardiac function and to support applications such as in silico clinical trials. However, most studies remain limited to single or patient-specific anatomies, restricting the inclusion of population-level variability required for uncertainty quantification. A key challenge is translating medical-image segmentations, which may contain artifacts, mesh defects or disjoint domains, into topologically coherent geometries suitable for multiphysics simulations.
  In this work, we present a semi-automatic pipeline that converts CT-based segmentations into simulation-ready cardiac meshes within a few minutes while preserving anatomical and topological consistency. Building on modern deep learning segmentation methods, the framework incorporates a template-based registration stage to regularize artifacts and enforce mesh-quality constraints. A Chamfer-distance morphing strategy deforms a high-quality template toward each segmented heart, matching individual chambers while preserving topology.
  The resulting meshes are watertight, isotopological, and endowed with consistent point-to-point correspondence. The pipeline is validated on 58 healthy cardiac CT scans, including all cardiac chambers and proximal vessel segments. The resulting meshes can be represented in a unified shape space, enabling the construction of a statistical shape model of the heart and major vessels. Principal Component Analysis shows that a low-dimensional latent space efficiently captures population variability, while Gaussian Mixture Modeling enables synthetic anatomy generation. Overall, the proposed framework (released open-source) provides a pathway from raw segmentations to simulation-ready cardiac geometries, enabling anatomically consistent virtual cohorts for large-scale in silico studies.

中文摘要翻译: 人体心脏的计算模型被广泛用于研究电机械和流体动力学心脏功能，并支持如计算机模拟临床试验等应用。然而，大多数研究仍局限于单一或患者特定的解剖结构，限制了纳入用于不确定性量化所需的人群水平变异性。一个关键挑战是如何将医学图像分割结果（可能包含伪影、网格缺陷或不连通域）转化为拓扑一致且适用于多物理仿真的几何模型。
  本文提出了一种半自动流水线，能够在几分钟内将基于CT的分割结果转换为可仿真的心脏网格，同时保持解剖和拓扑一致性。该框架基于现代深度学习分割方法，引入了一个基于模板的配准阶段来规整伪影并强制网格质量约束。一种Chamfer距离变形策略将高质量模板向每个分割心脏变形，在保持拓扑的同时匹配各个腔室。
  生成的网格是水密的、同拓扑的，并且具有一致的点对应关系。该流水线在58例健康心脏CT扫描上进行了验证，涵盖了所有心脏腔室和近端血管节段。生成的网格可以在统一形状空间中表示，从而能够构建心脏及主要血管的统计形状模型。主成分分析表明，低维潜在空间能够高效捕获人群变异性，而高斯混合模型则能够生成合成解剖结构。总体而言，所提出的框架（已开源）提供了从原始分割到可仿真心脏几何的路径，能够为大规模计算机模拟研究生成解剖一致的虚拟人群。

</details>


### [12] [Coordinate Singularities Break Conformal Coverage for Gaze and Head Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02565)
*Mohammadreza Jamalifard,Yaxiong Lei,Parastoo Azizinezhad,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在弯曲输出空间（如球面、3D旋转）中使用平坦坐标图表（如偏航-俯仰角、欧拉角）进行共形预测评分会导致几何失真，在坐标奇点附近覆盖崩溃（标称90%目标下实际降至38.9%-57.5%），并证明这是结构性问题。作者提出无坐标的测地线评分方法可消除该失真，无需重新训练且计算成本可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多视觉任务的输出空间是弯曲的（如球面上的注视方向、3D头部旋转），但现有方法常在平坦坐标图表（如偏航-俯仰角、欧拉角）中定义预测头、残差和共形分数，这种评分选择会在坐标奇点附近引入系统性几何失真，导致覆盖保证失效。

Method: 论文从理论上证明标量阈值化会改变图表坐标预测集的大小但保持其扭曲的轴比不变；提出使用黎曼体积密度作为诊断指标来检测覆盖崩溃位置；并引入无坐标的测地线评分（geodesic scoring）来消除坐标选择带来的几何失真。

Result: 在ETH-XGaze、Gaze360、BIWI、AFLW2000-3D四个数据集上，标称90%目标覆盖率的切片条件覆盖在坐标奇点区域下降30-50个百分点：注视俯仰>70°时降至38.9%（ETH-XGaze）和42.0%（Gaze360）；头部姿态俯仰>60°（接近万向锁）时降至57.5%（BIWI）和55.2%（AFLW2000-3D），而边缘覆盖率仍保持在90%附近。

Conclusion: 在弯曲输出空间中，基于平坦坐标图表的共形分数会导致坐标奇点附近覆盖崩溃，这是一个结构性问题。无坐标的测地线评分方法可有效消除这种失真，无需重新训练，计算成本极低。

Original Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free reliability guarantees for vision systems, but these guarantees depend on how prediction errors are measured in the output space. Many vision tasks produce outputs on curved spaces (e.g. gaze directions on the sphere or 3D head rotations), yet intermediate prediction heads, residuals, uncertainty estimates, or conformal scores are often defined in flat coordinate charts such as yaw-pitch or Euler angles. We show that this scoring choice introduces systematic geometric distortion near coordinate singularities (large pitch angles on the sphere and poses approaching gimbal lock in 3D rotations). Across four datasets (ETH-XGaze, Gaze360, BIWI, AFLW2000-3D), slice-conditional coverage at a nominal 90% target drops by 30-50 percentage points in these regions, falling to 38.9% on ETH-XGaze and 42.0% on Gaze360 at gaze pitch above 70 degrees, and to 57.5% on BIWI and 55.2% on AFLW2000-3D at head pose pitch above 60 degrees near gimbal lock, despite marginal coverage remaining near 90%. We prove that this is structural. Scalar thresholding changes the size of chart-coordinate prediction sets but leaves their distorted axis ratios unchanged. To diagnose this hidden failure mode, we show that a simple geometric quantity, the Riemannian volume density, strongly correlates with where coverage collapse occurs. Finally, we show that coordinate-free geodesic scoring removes this distortion. It requires no retraining and adds negligible computational cost.

中文摘要翻译: 共形预测为视觉系统提供了无分布假设的可靠性保证，但这些保证取决于输出空间中预测误差的度量方式。许多视觉任务在弯曲空间上产生输出（例如球面上的注视方向或3D头部旋转），但中间预测头、残差、不确定性估计或共形分数通常定义在平坦坐标图表中，如偏航-俯仰角或欧拉角。我们证明这种评分选择会在坐标奇点附近（球面上的大俯仰角和3D旋转中接近万向锁的姿态）引入系统性的几何失真。在四个数据集（ETH-XGaze、Gaze360、BIWI、AFLW2000-3D）上，标称90%目标覆盖率的切片条件覆盖在这些区域下降了30-50个百分点：ETH-XGaze和Gaze360上注视俯仰角超过70°时分别降至38.9%和42.0%；BIWI和AFLW2000-3D上头部姿态俯仰角超过60°（接近万向锁）时分别降至57.5%和55.2%，尽管边缘覆盖率仍保持在90%附近。我们证明这是结构性的。标量阈值化会改变图表坐标预测集的大小，但保持其扭曲的轴比不变。为了诊断这种隐藏的失效模式，我们证明一个简单的几何量——黎曼体积密度——与覆盖崩溃发生的位置高度相关。最后，我们证明无坐标的测地线评分可消除这种失真。该方法无需重新训练，且增加的计算成本可忽略不计。

</details>


### [13] [MAGE: View-guided Point Cloud Completion with Efficient Modality Alignment and Adaptive Geometry Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02568)
*Weize Quan,Zhengwei Wu,Kai Wang,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的几何感知框架MAGE，通过高效模态对齐和自适应几何增强来解决视图引导的点云补全中的跨模态几何不一致问题，在合成和真实数据集上均取得了优越性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视图的点云补全方法存在模态对齐能力弱和自几何增强能力有限的问题，导致性能受限，亟需解决跨模态几何不一致这一核心挑战。

Method: 1) 几何感知模态对齐：融合共享自注意力Transformer和跨模态重建监督，将图像和点云特征拉近到共享的3D隐空间；2) 自适应几何感知自注意力模块：同时考虑局部几何感知注意力计算和空间可变特征融合；3) 几何感知锚点精炼模块：在适当监督下重组织锚点以进一步提升补全性能。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一几何感知框架有效解决了跨模态几何不一致问题，显著提升了视图引导的点云补全性能。

Original Abstract: View-based point cloud completion aims to recover a complete 3D shape from a partial point cloud, guided by a single-view image. However, existing approaches often suffer from limited performance due to weak modality alignment and limited self-geometry enhancement. To overcome these challenges, we propose a unified geometry-aware framework that integrates efficient modality alignment and adaptive geometry enhancement, mainly to address cross-modal geometric inconsistency of view-guided point cloud completion. Specifically, we propose a geometry-aware modality alignment by integrating a shared self-attention Transformer and cross-modality reconstruction supervision, which aims to bring features of the image and point cloud close to each other in a shared latent space describing the 3D object. To enhance the perception of global shape and local geometric details, we propose an adaptive geometry-aware self-attention module, which simultaneously considers local geometry-aware attention computation and the spatially-variant feature fusion. In addition, we apply a geometry-perceptive anchor refinement module to reorganize the anchor points (representing a local region of the shape) under appropriate supervision, further boosting the completion performance of our method. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over existing approaches. Our code will be available at https://github.com/weizequan/MAGE.

中文摘要翻译: 基于视图的点云补全旨在从单视图图像引导下的部分点云中恢复完整的3D形状。然而，现有方法由于模态对齐能力弱和自几何增强能力有限，往往性能受限。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种统一的几何感知框架，该框架集成了高效模态对齐与自适应几何增强，主要针对视图引导点云补全中的跨模态几何不一致性问题。具体而言，我们通过整合共享自注意力Transformer和跨模态重建监督提出了几何感知模态对齐方法，旨在将图像和点云的特征在描述3D对象的共享隐空间中彼此拉近。为了增强对全局形状和局部几何细节的感知，我们提出了自适应几何感知自注意力模块，该模块同时考虑了局部几何感知注意力计算和空间可变特征融合。此外，我们应用了几何感知锚点精炼模块，在适当的监督下重组织锚点（表示形状局部区域），进一步提升了我们方法的补全性能。在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在性能上优于现有方法。我们的代码将在https://github.com/weizequan/MAGE上开源。

</details>


### [14] [Uncertainty-Aware Last-Layer Adaptation of RETFound for Referable Diabetic Retinopathy Screening Under Dataset Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02569)
*Karim Mardhani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基于RETFound的糖尿病视网膜病变筛查中，多种不确定性感知末层适配方法进行了安全性导向的实证评估，发现贝叶斯方法可改善内部安全操作点，但跨数据集泛化有限，强调了安全-覆盖率评估与外部验证的必要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视网膜病变筛查模型多关注聚合准确率，缺乏对安全性（如假阴性控制）和选择性转诊行为的系统评估，尤其是在分布偏移下的鲁棒性尚不明确，亟需以安全性为中心的方法学比较。

Method: 以RETFound作为冻结特征编码器，在APTOS 2019和DDR两个公开数据集上比较了五种末层方法：缓存特征softmax头、事后温度缩放、变分贝叶斯末层、对角拉普拉斯末层近似、SNGP风格缓存特征头。通过敏感性、选择性转诊行为、安全-覆盖率曲线等指标进行安全性导向评估。

Result: 在APTOS上，不确定性感知操作点改善了敏感性和选择性转诊行为，最优结果拒绝了约20%的案例并将接受案例的假阳性降至零，但假阴性减少伴随高假阳性代价。在DDR上，贝叶斯方法趋势一致但效果更弱，APTOS训练的SNGP检查点迁移不佳。假阴性减少并非贝叶斯方法独有。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知末层头能改善内部安全性导向操作点，但可信的视网膜筛查需要明确的安全-覆盖率评估和第二数据集验证；仅依赖聚合准确率不足以评估模型在安全关键场景中的实际表现。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a safety-centered empirical evaluation of uncertainty-aware last-layer adaptation for referable diabetic retinopathy screening using RETFound, a self-supervised vision-transformer retinal foundation model used here as a frozen feature encoder, and the public APTOS 2019 and DDR diabetic retinopathy fundus image datasets. We compare a cached-feature softmax head, post-hoc temperature scaling, variational Bayesian last-layer heads, a diagonal Laplace last-layer approximation, and an SNGP-style cached-feature head. On APTOS, uncertainty-aware operating points improved sensitivity and selective-referral behavior. The strongest APTOS selective-referral result deferred approximately 20 percent of cases and reduced accepted-case false negatives to zero while preserving high accepted-case specificity. However, threshold tuning also reduced false negatives at high false-positive cost, so false-negative reduction alone was not unique to Bayesian modeling. On DDR, native Bayesian heads qualitatively reproduced the APTOS direction but with weaker tradeoffs, while the APTOS-trained SNGP checkpoint transferred poorly and failed to provide useful external selective-referral behavior. These results highlight the value of safety-centered evaluation beyond aggregate accuracy: uncertainty-aware last-layer heads can improve internal safety-oriented operating points, but trustworthy retinal screening claims require explicit safety-coverage evaluation and second-dataset validation under shift.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种以安全为中心的经验性评估，针对基于RETFound的可转诊糖尿病视网膜病变筛查中的不确定性感知末层适配方法。RETFound是一种自监督的视觉变换器视网膜基础模型，在此用作冻结特征编码器，研究使用了公开的APTOS 2019和DDR糖尿病视网膜病变眼底图像数据集。我们比较了缓存特征softmax头、事后温度缩放、变分贝叶斯末层头、对角拉普拉斯末层近似以及SNGP风格的缓存特征头。在APTOS上，不确定性感知操作点改善了敏感性和选择性转诊行为。最强的APTOS选择性转诊结果拒绝了约20%的案例，并将接受案例的假阴性降至零，同时保持了高接受案例特异性。然而，阈值调整也在高假阳性代价下减少了假阴性，因此假阴性减少并非贝叶斯建模所独有。在DDR上，原生贝叶斯头定性地复现了APTOS的趋势但权衡效果较弱，而APTOS训练的SNGP检查点迁移不佳，未能提供有用的外部选择性转诊行为。这些结果凸显了超越聚合准确率的安全性导向评估的价值：不确定性感知末层头可以改善内部安全性导向操作点，但可信的视网膜筛查声明需要明确的安全-覆盖率评估以及在分布偏移下的第二数据集验证。

</details>


### [15] [Dual-Adaptive SAM3: Hierarchical Routing over Low-Rank Expert Layers for Parameter-Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02571)
*Ying Chen,Jinyue Li,Kun Wang,Qiankun Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DA-SAM3通过双自适应特化机制（任务感知的动态专家路由+参数感知的分解参数化专家），在保持高分割精度的同时大幅降低参数开销，解决SAM3适配医学影像时的参数效率和计算成本问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAM3虽为开放词汇分割带来新范式，但将其适配到医学影像领域面临两大挑战：朴素微调参数效率低，标准MoE方法计算开销过大，限制了临床可用性。

Method: 提出双自适应特化机制：1) 任务自适应——动态专家路由器(DER)根据视觉输入和文本概念提示联合推理，稀疏激活最相关专家；2) 参数自适应——分解参数化专家(DPE)将每个专家表示为共享冻结基座+轻量可训练低秩增量，降低MoE参数开销超80%。

Result: 在多个公开医学分割基准上，DA-SAM3匹配或超越全微调SAM3和标准MoE基线，并比当前最优方法提升5%，同时具有可解释性。

Conclusion: DA-SAM3通过双自适应机制有效平衡了医学影像分割的精度与参数效率，验证了稀疏专家激活和低秩分解在视觉-语言模型医学适配中的有效性。

Original Abstract: The Segment Anything Model with Concepts (SAM3) heralds a new paradigm for open-vocabulary segmentation through natural language interaction, offering significant potential for medical image analysis. However, effectively adapting such a powerful vision-language model to the diverse and nuanced domain of medical imaging remains a key challenge. Naive fine-tuning is parameter-inefficient, while standard Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) methods introduce prohibitive computational overhead, limiting their clinical applicability. To address this, we propose Dual-Adaptive SAM3 (DA-SAM3), a novel framework that achieves both high segmentation accuracy and extreme parameter efficiency via a dual-adaptive specialization mechanism. Our first adaptation is task-aware: a Dynamic Expert Router (DER) that sparsely activates the most relevant experts by jointly reasoning about the visual input and the textual concept prompt, mimicking a clinical consultation process. Our second adaptation is parameter-aware: a Decomposed Parameterized Experts (DPE) design that represents each expert as a shared frozen base (inherited from the pretrained SAM3) and a lightweight trainable low-rank delta, reducing MoE parameter overhead by over 80\%. Extensive experiments on multiple public medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that Dual-Adaptive SAM3 not only matches or exceeds the accuracy of fully fine-tuned SAM3 and standard MoE baselines, but also achieves a notable 5\% gain over current state-of-the-art methods, with interpretable results validating its effectiveness. The code is available at: https://github.com/Reconsider80/DA-SAM3.

中文摘要翻译: 具备概念理解的 Segment Anything Model (SAM3) 通过自然语言交互开创了开放词汇分割的新范式，在医学图像分析领域展现出巨大潜力。然而，如何有效地将这种强大的视觉-语言模型适配到多样且细微的医学影像领域仍是一项关键挑战。朴素微调方法参数效率低下，而标准的混合专家（MoE）方法则带来难以承受的计算开销，限制了其临床适用性。为解决这一问题，我们提出 Dual-Adaptive SAM3 (DA-SAM3)，一种通过双自适应特化机制同时实现高分割精度和极致参数效率的新型框架。我们的第一项自适应是任务感知的：一个动态专家路由器（DER），通过联合推理视觉输入和文本概念提示来稀疏激活最相关的专家，模拟临床会诊过程。第二项自适应是参数感知的：一种分解参数化专家（DPE）设计，将每个专家表示为一个共享的冻结基座（继承自预训练 SAM3）和一个轻量级可训练的低秩增量，从而将 MoE 参数开销降低 80% 以上。在多个公开医学分割基准上的大量实验表明，Dual-Adaptive SAM3 不仅匹配或超越了全微调 SAM3 和标准 MoE 基线的精度，还比当前最先进的方法实现了 5% 的显著提升，可解释的结果验证了其有效性。代码已开源在：https://github.com/Reconsider80/DA-SAM3。

</details>


### [16] [Additive Causal Construction for Transferable and Reconfigurable Cross-System Learning in Multi-Source Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02572)
*Zhizhong Fu,Wei Zhou,Zhaoyang Jiang,Yulong Lin,Yifu Hou,Xiaorong Ding,Qiang Yan,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出加性因果构建（ACC）框架，通过干预一致性和不确定性量化解决多源图像融合中的跨系统差异与纠缠问题，并在ColorMNIST和医学影像任务上验证了其提升分布外泛化能力的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多源图像融合场景中，异构输入由不同生成机制驱动，融合过程中存在跨系统差异（CSD）和跨系统纠缠（CSE），导致分布外预测时性能显著下降。现有方法缺乏对这两种问题的系统性处理。

Method: 提出ACC框架，从两个层面刻画信息融合：1）通过干预一致性建立多系统共享的因果“锚点”，实现因果图可迁移性（CGT）；2）将融合过程形式化为因果构建，通过不确定性量化建模构建路径的可靠性，实现因果图可重构性（CGR）。基于此，提出ACC-CRL可学习实例化方法，通过内容-机制解耦学习跨系统的联合因果内容表征，在共享锚点下进行响应对齐以缓解CSD，并融入结构不确定性自适应调控融合过程以抑制CSE。

Result: 在合成数据ColorMNIST和真实多中心医学影像任务（微血管侵犯预测）上的系统实验表明，所提方法在保持分布内性能的同时显著提升了分布外泛化能力，验证了基于机制对齐与不确定性建模的ACC-CRL策略的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ACC-CRL通过机制对齐和不确定性建模有效解决了开放环境中的跨系统差异与纠缠问题，为多源图像融合中的分布外泛化提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Original Abstract: In multi-source image fusion scenarios, heterogeneous inputs are typically driven by distinct generative mechanisms and can be viewed as a composition of multiple causal systems. However, cross-system discrepancy (CSD) and cross-system entanglement (CSE) commonly arise during the fusion process, often leading to significant performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) predictions. To address the CSD and CSE issues, we propose the additive causal construction (ACC) framework, which characterizes information fusion at two levels: firstly, it establishes causal "anchors" shared among multiple systems through intervention consistency to enable causal graph transferability (CGT); and secondly, it formalizes the fusion process as causal construction and models the reliability of constructed paths through uncertainty quantification to ensure causal graph reconfigurability (CGR). Building upon this, we revisit the traditional causal representation learning (CRL) with ACC and propose ACC-CRL as a learnable instantiation of the framework. The method explores joint causal content representations across systems via content-mechanism decoupling, and performs response alignment under shared anchors to mitigate CSD. Furthermore, it incorporates structural uncertainty to adaptively regulate the fusion process, thereby suppressing unstable CSE. We conduct systematic experiments on synthetic data (ColorMNIST) and real-world multi-center medical imaging tasks (microvascular invasion (MVI) prediction). The results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly improves OOD generalization while maintaining in-distribution (ID) performance, validating the effectiveness and robustness of the ACC-CRL strategy based on mechanism alignment and uncertainty modeling in open environments.

中文摘要翻译: 在多源图像融合场景中，异构输入通常由不同的生成机制驱动，可视为多个因果系统的组合。然而，融合过程中常出现跨系统差异（CSD）和跨系统纠缠（CSE），导致在分布外（OOD）预测下性能显著下降。为解决CSD和CSE问题，我们提出加性因果构建（ACC）框架，从两个层面刻画信息融合：首先，通过干预一致性建立多系统间共享的因果"锚点"，以实现因果图可迁移性（CGT）；其次，将融合过程形式化为因果构建，并通过不确定性量化建模所构建路径的可靠性，以确保因果图可重构性（CGR）。在此基础上，我们用ACC重新审视传统因果表征学习（CRL），并提出ACC-CRL作为该框架的可学习实例化方法。该方法通过内容-机制解耦探索跨系统的联合因果内容表征，并在共享锚点下进行响应对齐以缓解CSD；同时融入结构不确定性以自适应调控融合过程，从而抑制不稳定的CSE。我们在合成数据（ColorMNIST）和真实多中心医学影像任务（微血管侵犯（MVI）预测）上进行了系统实验。结果表明，所提方法在保持分布内（ID）性能的同时显著提升了分布外泛化能力，验证了基于机制对齐与不确定性建模的ACC-CRL策略在开放环境中的有效性与鲁棒性。

</details>


### [17] [Symmetry-Structured Neural Completion of Islamic Geometric Patterns from Sparse Control Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02573)
*Hassan Ugail,Irfan Mehmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种嵌入对称性几何知识的神经补全框架，通过轨道绑定预测在候选格点上生成具有精确旋转对称性的伊斯兰几何图案矢量图，在保持高保真度的同时提供严格的对称性保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 伊斯兰几何图案由严格的旋转对称性和构造规则支配，现有神经方法仅从数据统计学习这些规则，无法保证输出图案的精确对称性和几何有效性。本文旨在将形式化几何知识嵌入神经补全框架，而非依赖纯数据驱动学习。

Method: 提出一种对称性约束的神经补全框架：给定稀疏控制几何和目标对称阶数，系统在候选格点上通过预测边和有界曲线细化来完成矢量图案。格点边按循环群组织成旋转轨道，通过轨道内约束预测或推理时投影来强制对称性。轨道绑定变体提供构造性保证：对于任意输入和轨道级选择规则，输出精确N重对称。

Result: 在程序生成的伊斯兰几何设计图上，1) 干净输入下强制精确对称性不损失保真度；2) 缺失控制几何时，无结构解码器保真度下降且破坏对称性，重训练后恢复大部分保真度但无法保证精确有效性；3) 对称性结构化推理始终保持零违规。数据增强提升保真度，对称性结构则保证有效性。

Conclusion: 数据增强和对称性结构应对不同的失效模式：增强改善数据损坏下的保真度，对称性结构保证几何有效性。该框架为依赖精确几何结构的可缩放矢量装饰图案提供了知识约束的、有保证的神经补全方法。

Original Abstract: Islamic geometric patterns are governed by exact rotational symmetry and strict construction rules. This paper treats these rules as formal geometric knowledge and embeds them in a neural completion framework, rather than leaving them to be learned statistically from data. Given sparse control geometry and a target symmetry order, the system completes the pattern as a vector graph by predicting edges and refinements of bounded curves over a candidate lattice whose edges are organised into rotational orbits under the cyclic group. Symmetry is enforced either by constraining predictions within these orbits or by projecting them onto them during inference. The orbit-tied variant provides a constructive guarantee: for any input and any orbit-level selection rule, it produces exact N-fold symmetry, preserves anchor points, and keeps all refinements within prescribed bounds. These properties are verified numerically. The study focuses on rotational symmetry, and all quantitative results are obtained from procedurally generated graphs inspired by Islamic geometric design rather than from a historical corpus. On clean inputs, enforcing exact validity produces no measurable loss in fidelity. When control geometry is missing, an unstructured decoder loses fidelity and breaks symmetry; retraining on corrupted inputs recovers much of the fidelity but not exact validity. Symmetry-structured inference, by contrast, keeps violations at zero throughout. The results show that augmentation and symmetry structure address distinct failure modes: augmentation improves fidelity under corruption, while symmetry structure guarantees validity. The framework therefore provides a knowledge-constrained, guarantee-backed approach to neural completion for scalable vector ornaments whose validity depends on exact geometric structure.

中文摘要翻译: 伊斯兰几何图案受精确的旋转对称性和严格的构造规则支配。本文将这些规则视为形式化几何知识，并将其嵌入神经补全框架中，而非让网络从数据中统计学习。给定稀疏控制几何和目标对称阶数，系统通过预测边和有界曲线的细化，在候选格点上完成矢量图案的补全；该格点的边按循环群组织成旋转轨道。对称性通过约束轨道内的预测或在推理时向轨道投影来强制执行。轨道绑定变体提供了一种构造性保证：对于任意输入和任意轨道级选择规则，它都能产生精确的N重对称性，保留锚点，并使所有细化保持在规定范围内。这些性质通过了数值验证。本研究聚焦于旋转对称性，所有定量结果均来自受伊斯兰几何设计启发的程序生成图，而非历史语料库。在干净输入上，强制精确有效性不会产生可测的保真度损失。当控制几何缺失时，无结构解码器损失保真度并破坏对称性；在受损输入上重新训练可恢复大部分保真度，但无法保证精确有效性。相比之下，对称性结构化推理始终保持违规为零。结果表明，数据增强和对称性结构应对不同的失效模式：增强改善数据损坏下的保真度，而对称性结构保证有效性。因此，该框架为依赖精确几何结构的可缩放矢量装饰图案提供了一种知识约束的、有保证的神经补全方法。

</details>


### [18] [Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning: Evaluating Criterion-Shift Adaptation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02575)
*Kaiyun Yang,Ruilin Yang,Zhimin Yao,J. Wang,Wei Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的上下文学习设置——准则条件上下文学习（CC-ICL），要求模型在固定任务语义下根据潜在准则调整预测。为此构建了CC-Bench基准，并发现现有模型存在刚性边界偏差，而简单的多准则训练策略能有效缓解该偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有上下文学习（ICL）中任务一旦被归纳，其决策标准便固定不变。然而现实应用中许多任务具有稳定的高层意图，但其决策准则会因具体需求而变化。现有模型缺乏在这种准则偏移下灵活调整决策的能力。

Method: 1）提出CC-ICL新设置，要求模型从上下文中推断潜在准则并相应调整预测；2）设计两个互补指标：Criterion Invariance（准则不变性）和Criterion Sensitivity（准则敏感性）；3）构建CC-Bench多领域基准，采用双层数据层级结构，在任务固定情况下支持基于活跃准则的真实标签变化；4）提出简单的多准则训练策略。

Result: 现有多数模型表现出刚性边界偏差，难以将决策与潜在准则对齐；即使简单的多准则训练策略也能显著减少该偏差，提升准则敏感性，使7B规模模型超越专有模型，且不降低多模态通用性能。

Conclusion: 模型在准则条件上下文学习中存在刚性边界偏差，多准则训练策略是缓解该偏差的有效方法，能提升小规模模型的竞争力。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models can perform new tasks without parameter updates through in-context learning (ICL), whose core mechanism is utilizing the support set for task induction. In the standard ICL setting, once the task is induced, its decision criterion remains fixed. However, in real-world applications, many tasks exhibit a stable high-level intent, while their decision criteria shift according to specific requirements. Thus, we introduce a new setting, denoted as Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning (CC-ICL), where models must infer the latent criterion from context and adjust predictions accordingly under fixed task semantics. To evaluate this capability, we propose two complementary metrics, Criterion Invariance and Criterion Sensitivity, capturing the model's robustness and adaptability under criterion shifts. We further construct CC-Bench, a multi-domain benchmark that supports evaluation under the CC-ICL setting. By employing a dual-level data hierarchy, CC-Bench enables legitimate ground-truth variation conditioned on the active criterion even when the task remains fixed. Experiments on CC-Bench reveal that most models exhibit a rigid boundary bias, struggling to align their decisions with the latent criterion. We also find that even a simple multi-criterion training strategy can significantly reduce this bias, improving Criterion Sensitivity and enabling 7B-scale models to surpass proprietary models without degrading general multimodal performance.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型可以通过上下文学习（ICL）在不更新参数的情况下执行新任务，其核心机制是利用支持集进行任务归纳。在标准ICL设置中，一旦任务被归纳，其决策标准便固定不变。然而，在现实应用中，许多任务具有稳定的高层意图，但其决策准则会因具体需求而变化。因此，我们引入了一种新的设置，称为准则条件上下文学习（CC-ICL），要求模型在固定任务语义下，从上下文中推断潜在准则并相应调整预测。为了评估该能力，我们提出了两个互补指标——准则不变性和准则敏感性，分别捕捉模型在准则偏移下的鲁棒性和适应性。我们进一步构建了CC-Bench，一个支持CC-ICL设置下评估的多领域基准。通过采用双层数据层级结构，CC-Bench能够在任务固定的情况下实现基于活跃准则的合法真实标签变化。在CC-Bench上的实验表明，大多数模型存在刚性边界偏差，难以使其决策与潜在准则对齐。我们还发现，即使是一个简单的多准则训练策略也能显著减少该偏差，提升准则敏感性，并使7B规模模型在不降低通用多模态性能的情况下超越专有模型。

</details>


### [19] [PixCon: Clean-Positive Contrastive Learning for Foundation-Model Semi-Supervised Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03068)
*Ebenezer Tarubinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PixCon，一个基于干净阳性像素对比学习的半监督语义分割框架。在DINOv2等基础模型教师下，伪标签污染已极少（98%干净），因此关键不再是过滤伪标签，而是如何通过对比学习构建更优的嵌入空间。PixCon通过只存储学生已正确分类的标签像素来保证阳性集零污染（ρ_F=0），避免了传统置信度过滤方法的缺陷。实验表明PixCon在多个基准上匹配或改进强基线，且零污染保证在教师较弱时提供鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 半监督语义分割长期关注"哪些伪标签可信"并通过置信度过滤来解决。但DINOv2等基础模型教师已能产生98%干净的伪标签，使得过滤不再是瓶颈。真正的精度提升空间在于如何通过类别结构组织嵌入空间。现有对比方法（如ReCo、U$^2$PL）使用置信度过滤的伪标签构建记忆库，仍会引入污染，污染在InfoNCE梯度中会带来有害的假正项，其比例ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)不可忽视。

Method: 提出PixCon框架：1）每个类别维护一个记忆库，只存储学生已正确分类的有标签像素，通过构造保证阳性集零污染（ρ_F=0）。2）采用单分支一致性骨干网络，不增加推理参数，无需特定阈值。3）对监督InfoNCE梯度进行一阶分析，揭示污染危害机制：假正项与ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)成比例，并在Pascal（0.018）和ADE20K（0.106）上实际测量而非假设。

Result: 在Pascal VOC、Cityscapes和ADE20K上，PixCon在计算匹配的单切换协议下匹配或改进了基于DINOv2的强基线UniMatch V2：在Pascal 1/8标注下每个种子均有提升（每种子约+0.2 mIoU），三种子平均达87.90，与已发表的UniMatch V2-B持平。分析表明零污染保证主要在教师较弱时提供鲁棒性，精度提升来源于更干净的阳性监督。

Conclusion: 在基础模型教师下污染已很少见，PixCon的ρ_F=0保证主要作为教师变弱时的鲁棒性机制，而精度提升来自更干净的阳性监督。干净阳性对比学习成为基础模型半监督语义分割中鲁棒、低成本的默认选择。

Original Abstract: Semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SSSS) has long turned on one question, which pseudo-labels to trust, and answered it with ever more careful confidence filtering. Foundation backbones change the regime: with a DINOv2 teacher a strict threshold already retains a measured 98%-clean pseudo-label set, so the accuracy that remains lives not in the filter but in how the embedding space is structured by class. We propose PixCon, a clean-positive pixel-contrastive framework. PixCon maintains a per-class memory bank that admits only labeled pixels the student already classifies correctly, guaranteeing a contamination-free positive set ($ρ_F=0$) by construction, unlike prior contrastive SSSS banks (ReCo, U$^2$PL) built from confidence-filtered pseudo-labels. It is a single branch over a consistency backbone, adds no inference-time parameters, and needs no bank-specific threshold. A first-order analysis of the supervised-InfoNCE gradient explains why contamination hurts: its false-positive term scales as $ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)$, which we measure (0.018 on Pascal, 0.106 on ADE20K) rather than assume. Across Pascal VOC, Cityscapes, and ADE20K, PixCon matches or improves a strong DINOv2-based UniMatch V2 baseline in a compute-matched one-switch protocol: it improves every Pascal-1/8 seed (a per-seed gain of about +0.2 mIoU) and its three-seed mean reaches 87.90, the published UniMatch V2-B figure. Because contamination is already rare under foundation-model teachers, our analysis indicates the $ρ_F=0$ guarantee acts chiefly as robustness as teachers weaken, while the accuracy gain comes from cleaner positive supervision, making clean-positive contrast a robust, low-cost default for foundation-model SSSS.

中文摘要翻译: 半监督语义分割（SSSS）长期以来都围绕着一个核心问题——该信任哪些伪标签，并通过日益精细的置信度过滤来回答这一问题。基础骨干网络改变了这一格局：使用DINOv2教师时，严格的阈值已经可以保留经测量达到98%干净的伪标签集，因此剩余的精度提升空间不在于过滤机制，而在于嵌入空间如何按类别进行结构化组织。我们提出PixCon，一个干净阳性像素对比学习框架。PixCon为每个类别维护一个记忆库，该记忆库仅接纳学生已经正确分类的有标签像素，从而通过构造保证阳性集零污染（ρ_F=0），这与先前基于置信度过滤伪标签构建的对比SSSS记忆库（如ReCo、U$^2$PL）不同。PixCon是在一致性骨干网络上构建的单分支架构，不增加推理时参数，也不需要特定于记忆库的阈值。对监督InfoNCE梯度的一阶分析解释了污染为何有害：其假正项与ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)成比例，我们在Pascal上测量为0.018，在ADE20K上测量为0.106，而非仅作假设。在Pascal VOC、Cityscapes和ADE20K上，PixCon在计算匹配的单切换协议下匹配或改进了基于DINOv2的强基线UniMatch V2：它改进了Pascal 1/8标注下的每个种子（每种子约+0.2 mIoU的增益），其三种子均值达到87.90，与已发表的UniMatch V2-B指标持平。由于在基础模型教师下污染已很少见，我们的分析表明ρ_F=0保证主要作为教师变弱时的鲁棒性机制，而精度提升来自更干净的阳性监督，使得干净阳性对比学习成为基础模型半监督语义分割中鲁棒、低成本的默认选择。

</details>


### [20] [Classroom Behavior Monitoring with YOLO An Empirical Study in Higher Education Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02580)
*Sinh Vu Trong,Dung Nguyen Manh,Hieu Hoang Minh,Hieu Pham Trung,Thu Pham Ha,Nhu Le Hoang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BAV-Classroom课堂行为数据集，评估了多种计算机视觉模型，发现YOLOv11表现最佳，并揭示了学生注意力在课堂后期显著下降的现象，证明了自动课堂监控的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统课堂行为观察方法主观性强且缺乏可扩展性，需要一种自动化的、客观的课堂监控方法以评估学生参与度和提升教学质量。

Method: 构建了BAV-Classroom课堂视频数据集，标注9种行为类别；评估并对比了多种前沿计算机视觉模型，其中YOLOv11表现最优。

Result: YOLOv11在课堂行为识别任务中取得最佳性能；实验表明学生在课程后期注意力集中度显著下降，揭示了持续保持学生参与度的挑战。

Conclusion: 应用计算机视觉进行自动课堂监控是可行的，能够为学术质量管理提供有价值的洞察。

Original Abstract: Classroom behavior monitoring plays a vital role in evaluating student engagement and improving teaching effectiveness. Traditional observation methods remain subjective and lack scalability. This study introduces a real-world dataset of classroom videos collected at the Banking Academy of Vietnam (BAV-Classroom dataset), annotated with nine distinctive behavioral categories. State-of-the-art Computer Vision models were evaluated and compared, with YOLOv11 achieving the best performance. Experimental results indicate that students' concentration often decreases notably during the final part of lectures, highlighting challenges in sustaining engagement. Our findings demonstrate the feasibility of applying computer vision for automated classroom monitoring, providing valuable insights for academic quality management.

中文摘要翻译: 课堂行为监测在评估学生参与度和提升教学效果方面起着至关重要的作用。传统的观察方法仍然具有主观性且缺乏可扩展性。本研究引入了在越南银行学院收集的课堂视频真实世界数据集（BAV-Classroom数据集），标注了九种不同的行为类别。研究评估并比较了多种前沿计算机视觉模型，其中YOLOv11取得了最佳性能。实验结果表明，学生的注意力在课程后期往往显著下降，凸显了维持学生参与度的挑战。我们的研究结果证明了将计算机视觉应用于自动化课堂监控的可行性，为学术质量管理提供了宝贵的见解。

</details>


### [21] [Towards Standardized Light Field Quality Assessment: Hybrid Subjective Benchmarking and Objective Metric Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03494)
*Saeed Mahmoudpour,Mylene C. Q. Farias,Gi-Mun Um,Myllena A. Prado,Ismael Seidel,Leonardo de Sousa Marques,Leonardo Andrade,Shengyang Zhao,Carla L Pagliari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个标准化的光场质量评估（QA）工作流，用于JPEG Pleno标准化活动，该工作流集成了基准生成、混合主观评估和客观指标分析。研究发现，现有客观指标在编码伪影上表现良好，但在视图合成失真下性能显著下降，并强调了视图池化策略对未来光场质量指标设计的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在标准化背景下，沉浸式媒体编码解决方案需要可靠且可重复的主观质量评估流程，以及在不同失真类型下保持准确的客观质量指标。现有方法缺乏一个统一的、标准化的光场质量评估工作流，且对包含视图合成和重建技术的处理管道中的失真关注不足。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段标准化工作流：（1）基准生成，涵盖传统编码伪影和视图合成/重建失真；（2）混合主观评估方法，结合参考锚定质量评分与感知模糊区域的针对性成对细化；（3）大规模客观指标系统评估，从全局预测精度、局部一致性和失真族鲁棒性三个维度进行分析。

Result: 多种客观指标在纯编码刺激下表现出良好的一致性，但当引入视图合成失真后性能显著下降。分析揭示了视图池化策略对未来光场质量指标设计的关键影响。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复现且标准化就绪的细粒度光场质量评估框架，同时识别了当前客观指标在新兴编码管道下的关键局限性。注释数据集已公开。

Original Abstract: Benchmarking immersive media coding solutions, especially in the standardization context, requires reliable and reproducible subjective quality assessment (QA) procedures, along with objective quality metrics that remain accurate across different distortion types. This paper presents a standardized workflow for light field QA, developed and deployed in the context of JPEG Pleno standardization activities, which integrates benchmark generation, a hybrid subjective evaluation, and objective metric analysis into a common workflow. The benchmark is designed to encompass not only traditional coding-only artifacts but also distortions that arise in processing pipelines in which light field encoding is accompanied with view synthesis and reconstruction techniques. A hybrid subjective method is proposed enabling fine-grained assessment by combining reference-anchored quality rating with targeted pairwise refinement in perceptually ambiguous regions. The reliability of subjective scores is verified using statistical consistency analyses between observers of two cohorts. Finally, a large set of objective metrics is systematically evaluated in terms of global prediction accuracy, local agreement in ambiguous quality regions, and robustness across distortion families. The results show that several metrics achieve strong agreement for coding-only stimuli, but their performance consistently drops when view synthesis distortions are included. The analysis further highlights the importance of view-pooling strategy in the design of future light field quality metrics. The work provides a reproducible and standardization-ready framework for fine-grained light field QA, while identifying key limitations of current objective metrics under emerging coding pipelines. The subjectively annotated dataset is publicly available at https://plenodb.jpeg.org/lfqa/objectivecfp.

中文摘要翻译: 在标准化背景下，沉浸式媒体编码解决方案的基准测试需要可靠且可重复的主观质量评估流程，以及在不同失真类型下保持准确的客观质量指标。本文提出了一个标准化的光场质量评估工作流，该工作流在JPEG Pleno标准化活动的背景下开发和部署，将基准生成、混合主观评估和客观指标分析整合到一个通用工作流中。该基准设计不仅涵盖传统的纯编码伪影，还涵盖了光场编码伴随视图合成和重建技术的处理管道中产生的失真。提出了一种混合主观方法，通过将参考锚定质量评分与感知模糊区域的针对性成对细化相结合，实现细粒度评估。通过两个观察者队列之间的统计一致性分析验证了主观评分的可靠性。最后，从全局预测精度、模糊质量区域的局部一致性以及跨失真族的鲁棒性三个维度系统评估了大量客观指标。结果表明，多种指标在纯编码刺激下表现出一致性，但当引入视图合成失真后性能显著下降。分析进一步强调了视图池化策略在未来光场质量指标设计中的重要性。该工作为细粒度光场质量评估提供了一个可复现且标准化就绪的框架，同时识别了当前客观指标在新兴编码管道下的关键局限性。主观注释数据集已在https://plenodb.jpeg.org/lfqa/objectivecfp公开。

</details>


### [22] [Evaluating Intellectual Property Guardrails of Generative Image Models: A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02582)
*Austin T. Hoag,Apostolos Modas,Yunhao Ba,Julienne M. LaChance,Jinru Xue,Wiebke Hutiri,Jan Simson,Tiffany Georgievski,Alex Towli,Joseph Smith,Yuki Mitsufuji,Alice Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个自动化评估基准，用于测试生成式图像模型在IP防护机制下的表现。实验评估了14种主流文生图模型（含3种开源模型和11种私有模型），发现所有模型均能生成可识别IP的图像，但拒绝率因模型和IP类别（虚构角色、名人肖像、商业标志）而异，其中商业标志被拒绝最少。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成式图像模型能够生成与可识别知识产权高度相似甚至复制的图像，现有模型虽声称有IP防护机制，但缺乏系统性评估基准和自动化测试方法，因此需要构建标准化的评估流程来量化各模型的IP防护效果与侵权倾向。

Method: 构建了一个包含虚构角色、名人肖像和商业标志三类IP的自动化评估基准，设计了一套自动化流水线来检测模型输出中是否存在可识别IP；选取14种广泛使用的文生图模型（3种自托管开源模型+11种私有模型）进行系统性测试。

Result: 所有私有模型都因IP防护机制在某种程度上拒绝生成，但拒绝率在不同模型间差异显著；不同IP类别的拒绝率也有较大差异：商业标志被拒绝的频率最低，平均生成成功率最高；截至2026年3月，所有模型仍能轻易生成含有可识别IP的图像。

Conclusion: 当前主流文生图模型虽然部署了IP防护机制，但防护效果参差不齐，尤其在商业标志方面防护严重不足，所有模型均未完全解决可识别IP的生成问题，亟需更有效的防护措施。

Original Abstract: Generative image models are capable of producing images that bear a strong resemblance to, or replicate, recognizable intellectual property (IP). In this technical report, we present a benchmark and automated evaluation pipeline to test for evidence of IP guardrails in generative image models along with the propensity for these models to generate images with recognizable IP. The IP categories we tested include fictional characters, celebrity likeness, and commercial logos and do not encompass the full range of IP which may be implicated by image generation models. We evaluated fourteen widely used text-to-image models, including three self-hosted open weights models and eleven private models. While all of the private models were observed to refuse generations at some level due to IP guardrails, the frequency of generation refusals varied substantially among models. The refusal rates also varied considerably across the different IP categories tested. Commercial logos were refused least frequently and were successfully generated at the highest rate, on average. Though the rate varies, all models tested readily generated images containing recognizable IP as of March 2026.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式图像模型能够生成与可识别知识产权高度相似甚至直接复制的图像。在本技术报告中，我们提出了一个基准测试和自动化评估流程，用于检测生成式图像模型中的IP防护机制，以及这些模型生成含有可识别IP图像的倾向性。我们测试的IP类别包括虚构角色、名人肖像和商业标志，并未涵盖图像生成模型可能涉及的全部IP范围。我们评估了十四种广泛使用的文生图模型，包括三种自托管开源权重模型和十一种私有模型。虽然观察到所有私有模型均因IP防护机制而在某种程度上拒绝了生成，但不同模型的生成拒绝率差异显著。不同IP类别的拒绝率也存在较大差异。商业标志被拒绝的频率最低，平均生成成功率最高。尽管拒绝率各异，但截至2026年3月，所有被测试的模型均能轻易生成含有可识别IP的图像。

</details>


### [23] [Probabilistic Robustness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03797)
*Yi Zhang,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用概率鲁棒性（PR）替代对抗鲁棒性（AR）作为医学图像分类模型可信度的更实用度量，并在MedMNIST v2数据集上系统评估了常用深度学习模型在自然 corruption 下的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学图像分类的可信度研究主要聚焦于对抗鲁棒性（AR），但AR基于最坏情况假设，在真实医疗应用中代表性不足。因此需要一种更贴近实际部署场景的鲁棒性评估方法。

Method: 构建了一套面向医学图像分类的自然 corruption 设置，并在MedMNIST v2数据集上对常用深度学习模型进行系统性概率鲁棒性评估。

Result: 实验从统计视角提供了评估深度学习模型可信度的依据，表明概率鲁棒性比对抗鲁棒性更能反映模型在真实医疗场景中的表现。

Conclusion: 概率鲁棒性（PR）是一种更实用的模型可信度衡量指标，有助于推动深度学习模型在医学成像应用中的可信部署。

Original Abstract: Deep learning (DL) has shown strong performance in medical image classification, but its trustworthy deployment remains challenging in safety-critical clinical settings, where prediction errors under perturbations may lead to severe consequences. Existing studies mainly focus on adversarial robustness (AR) from a worst-case perspective; however, such settings may be less representative of real medical applications. In this work, we investigate probabilistic robustness (PR) as a more practical measure of model trustworthiness. To this end, we construct a set of natural corruption settings for medical image classification and systematically evaluate commonly used DL models on MedMNIST v2 dataset. Our study provides a statistically grounded perspective on assessing the trustworthiness of DL models, thereby supporting their more trustworthy deployment in medical imaging applications.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习（DL）在医学图像分类中表现出强大性能，但其在安全关键的临床环境中的可信部署仍具有挑战性，因为在扰动下出现的预测错误可能导致严重后果。现有研究主要从最坏情况角度关注对抗鲁棒性（AR）；然而，此类设置对真实医疗应用的代表性可能不足。在本工作中，我们研究概率鲁棒性（PR），将其作为模型可信度的一种更实用的度量。为此，我们构建了一套面向医学图像分类的自然 corruption 设置，并在MedMNIST v2数据集上系统评估了常用的深度学习模型。我们的研究从统计视角提供了评估深度学习模型可信度的依据，从而支持其在医学成像应用中更可信的部署。

</details>


### [24] [RotateAttention: RoPE-Aware Rotation and Range Rectification for INT4 Quantized Attention in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02584)
*Yaofu Liu,Wanli Lan,Jinxi Li,Binhang Yuan,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对基于DiT并采用3D RoPE的视频生成模型，提出RotateAttention混合精度INT4 FlashAttention框架，通过RoPE感知的旋转矩阵和范围优化的P量化解决低比特量化中的数值异常和对称量化浪费问题，实现1.68倍加速且几乎无损生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有低比特量化方法在DiT视频生成模型上存在两个关键挑战：1) 用于缓解Q/K异常的在线旋转矩阵难以与RoPE兼容；2) 非负注意力矩阵P的对称量化浪费了INT4一半的动态范围。现有方法未解决这些问题。

Method: 1) RoPE感知旋转（RoPE-aware Rotation）：采用可合并到RoPE中的旋转矩阵或低开销矩阵来缓解RoPE诱导的Q/K异常；2) 范围优化的P量化（Range-optimized P Quantization）：使用固定缩放因子和零点充分挖掘INT4数值范围；3) 选择性地对精度敏感的注意力块和去噪步使用FP16回退，形成混合精度INT4 FlashAttention框架。

Result: 保持与全精度基线几乎一致的视频生成质量，实现最高1.68倍端到端加速和2.2倍kernel级加速。

Conclusion: RotateAttention通过解决RoPE场景下Q/K异常和P矩阵对称量化浪费问题，有效加速了基于DiT和3D RoPE的视频生成模型，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速。

Original Abstract: In \textbf{DiT-based video generation models equipped with 3D Rotary Position Embeddings (3D RoPE)}, the attention mechanism remains a primary computational bottleneck due to its quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length. While quantized \textbf{FlashAttention} offers a promising path toward hardware acceleration, existing low-bit quantization methods overlook two critical challenges in this setting: \textbf{1)} applying online rotation matrices -- a widely used technique for mitigating outliers in Queries ($Q$) and Keys ($K$) -- is difficult to reconcile with \textbf{RoPE}; and \textbf{2)} the non-negative attention matrix $P = \exp(QK - \max(QK))$ makes symmetric quantization waste half of the 4-bit dynamic range. In this work, we observe that the outlier distributions of $Q$ and $K$ are strongly affected by the dimensional partitioning of \textbf{3D RoPE}. Based on this finding, we propose \textbf{RotateAttention}, an efficient \textbf{mixed-precision INT4 FlashAttention} framework tailored for \textbf{DiT-based video generation models with 3D RoPE}, using selective \textbf{FP16 fallback} for accuracy-sensitive attention blocks and denoising steps. RotateAttention introduces two core techniques: \textbf{1) RoPE-aware Rotation}, which employs either mergeable rotation matrices that can be fused into RoPE or negligible-overhead matrices to mitigate RoPE-induced outliers in $Q$ and $K$; and \textbf{2) Range-optimized $P$ Quantization}, which uses fixed scales and zero-points to fully exploit the \textbf{INT4 numerical range} with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that \textbf{RotateAttention} preserves video generation quality nearly identical to full-precision baselines while achieving up to 1.68$\times$ end-to-end speedup and 2.2$\times$ kernel-level acceleration.

中文摘要翻译: 在基于DiT并配备3D旋转位置编码（3D RoPE）的视频生成模型中，注意力机制由于序列长度的二次复杂度仍然是主要的计算瓶颈。虽然量化的FlashAttention为硬件加速提供了有前景的路径，但现有的低比特量化方法忽略了该场景下的两个关键挑战：1）应用在线旋转矩阵（一种广泛用于缓解查询Q和键K中异常值的技术）难以与RoPE协调；2）非负注意力矩阵P = exp(QK - max(QK))使得对称量化浪费了INT4一半的动态范围。在本工作中，我们观察到Q和K的异常值分布受到3D RoPE维度划分的强烈影响。基于这一发现，我们提出了RotateAttention，一个针对基于DiT并采用3D RoPE的视频生成模型量身定制的高效混合精度INT4 FlashAttention框架，对精度敏感的注意力块和去噪步采用选择性FP16回退。RotateAttention引入了两项核心技术：1）RoPE感知旋转，采用可合并到RoPE中的可融合旋转矩阵或低开销矩阵来缓解RoPE诱导的Q和K异常；2）范围优化的P量化，使用固定缩放因子和零点充分挖掘INT4数值范围，同时最小化计算开销。实验表明，RotateAttention保持了与全精度基线几乎一致的视频生成质量，同时实现了最高1.68倍的端到端加速和2.2倍的kernel级加速。

</details>


### [25] [ContiStain: Cross-Domain Relation-Preserving Distillation for Continual Multi-Domain Virtual IHC Staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03851)
*Fuqiang Chen,Yifeng Wang,Hongpeng Wang,Yongbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContiStain 提出了一种面向免疫组化连续虚拟染色的多域关系蒸馏框架，通过MoE特征提取器和关系保持蒸馏策略，解决连续学习中先前生物标志物性能退化的问题，在MIST数据集上显著优于顺序微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在临床实践中，新的生物标志物数据通常是按时间顺序依次获取的，对顺序到达的数据进行微调会破坏潜在空间中生物标志物表示之间的结构关系，导致先前学习的生物标志物性能严重下降。

Method: (i) 使用混合专家（MoE）特征提取器构建域感知的结构化特征空间，减少跨生物标志物域的表征干扰；(ii) 提出关系保持蒸馏策略，在连续适应过程中显式强制保持已学习生物标志物域之间的跨域标记级余弦相似度矩阵的一致性。

Result: 在MIST数据集上的四域顺序虚拟IHC染色设置下，相比顺序微调，FID降低11.1，ConchFID降低60.9，实现了可扩展且稳健的多域虚拟染色。

Conclusion: ContiStain通过维持跨域结构一致性，在适应新域的同时缓解了遗忘问题，实现了可扩展且稳健的多域虚拟染色。

Original Abstract: A unified multiplex virtual staining model enables scalable and non-destructive multiplex analysis from H&E slides while promoting parameter efficiency, shared pathological knowledge, and consistent cross-biomarker representations. However, in clinical practice, data for new biomarkers are typically acquired sequentially over time. Fine-tuning on such temporally arriving data leads to severe performance degradation on previously learned biomarkers, as sequential optimization disrupts the structured relationships among biomarker representations in the latent space. To address this issue, we propose ContiStain, an IHC multi-domain relational distillation framework for continual virtual staining. We first (i) construct a domain-aware structured feature space using a mixture-of-experts (MoE) feature extractor to reduce representation interference across biomarker domains. Based on this stabilized feature space, we then (ii) propose a relation-preserving distillation strategy that explicitly enforces the consistency of cross-domain token-level cosine similarity matrices between learned biomarker domains during continual adaptation. By maintaining cross-domain structural coherence, ContiStain mitigates forgetting while retaining adaptability to new domains. Experiments on the MIST dataset under a four-domain sequential virtual IHC staining setting show improved stability, reducing FID and ConchFID by 11.1 and 60.9 compared to sequential fine-tuning, enabling scalable and robust multi-domain virtual staining. Code is released at https://github.com/ccitachi/ContiStain.

中文摘要翻译: 统一的多重虚拟染色模型能够从H&E切片实现可扩展且非破坏性的多重分析，同时提升参数效率、共享病理知识并保持跨生物标志物表示的一致性。然而，在临床实践中，新生物标志物的数据通常是按时间顺序依次获取的。对这类时序到达的数据进行微调会导致先前学习到的生物标志物性能严重下降，因为顺序优化破坏了潜在空间中生物标志物表示之间的结构关系。为解决这一问题，我们提出了ContiStain——一种用于连续虚拟染色的免疫组化多域关系蒸馏框架。我们首先(i) 使用混合专家（MoE）特征提取器构建域感知的结构化特征空间，以减少跨生物标志物域的表征干扰。在此基础上，我们进一步(ii) 提出一种关系保持蒸馏策略，在连续适应过程中显式强制保持已学习生物标志物域之间的跨域标记级余弦相似度矩阵的一致性。通过维持跨域结构一致性，ContiStain在保留对新域适应能力的同时缓解了遗忘问题。在MIST数据集上的四域顺序虚拟免疫组化染色设置下进行的实验表明，相比顺序微调，ContiStain在稳定性上有所提升，FID和ConchFID分别降低了11.1和60.9，从而实现了可扩展且稳健的多域虚拟染色。代码已发布在https://github.com/ccitachi/ContiStain。

</details>


### [26] [Reliability-Aware CT-MRI Registration: A Quality Engineering Framework with Stability Analysis and Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02585)
*Nisreen Albzour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于数据驱动阈值的多模态CT-MRI配准可靠性评估框架，将配准质量分为绿/黄/红三级风险类别，并验证了其在多解剖部位（脑、腹、颈）的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CT-MRI配准方法仅报告聚合质量指标，缺乏针对每个病例的可靠性信号，无法满足临床对配准结果可信度的需求。

Method: 使用刚性变换和仿射变换对90对CT-MRI切片进行配准；计算Delta NMI、Delta SSIM、Dice重叠、配准稳定性和逆一致性误差，组合为单一评分R；基于训练患者数据学习风险阈值，并直接应用于测试患者。

Result: 仿射配准在NMI和SSIM上优于刚性配准，产生44%的绿色分类（刚性为33%）；可靠性过滤后的配准改善了平均对齐轮廓；腹部配准的可靠性优于脑部配准；权重敏感性分析显示Dice重叠是主导可靠性成分。

Conclusion: 该框架为多模态配准提供了可解释的质量控制层，但风险阈值反映的是统计验证而非临床验证。

Original Abstract: Multimodal CT-MRI registration is central to image-guided radiotherapy, surgical navigation, and diagnostic workflows, but most pipelines report only aggregate quality metrics without per-case reliability signals. We propose a reliability-aware framework that converts registration quality into Green/Yellow/Red risk categories using data-learned thresholds. CT images were registered to T1-weighted MRI using rigid and affine transformations on 90 paired slices from 18 patients across brain, abdominal, and neck anatomies. Reliability was assessed using Delta NMI, Delta SSIM, Dice overlap, registration stability, and inverse consistency error, combined into a single score R. Thresholds learned from training patients were applied unchanged to held-out test patients. Affine registration outperformed rigid registration on NMI and SSIM, yielding 44% Green classifications versus 33% for rigid. Reliability-filtered registrations improved the average alignment profile compared with unfiltered methods. Per-anatomy analysis showed substantial variation, with stronger reliability for abdominal registrations than brain registrations. Weight sensitivity analysis identified Dice overlap as the dominant reliability component. The proposed framework provides an interpretable quality-control layer for multimodal registration, while risk thresholds reflect statistical rather than clinical validation.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态CT-MRI配准是图像引导放疗、手术导航和诊断工作流程的核心，但大多数管道仅报告聚合质量指标，缺乏针对每个病例的可靠性信号。我们提出一个可靠性感知框架，使用数据学习的阈值将配准质量转换为绿/黄/红风险类别。对来自18例患者的脑部、腹部和颈部解剖结构的90对配对切片，采用刚性和仿射变换将CT图像配准到T1加权MRI。通过Delta NMI、Delta SSIM、Dice重叠、配准稳定性和逆一致性误差评估可靠性，并将其组合为单一评分R。从训练患者学习的阈值未经修改地应用于留出测试患者。仿射配准在NMI和SSIM上优于刚性配准，产生44%的绿色分类，而刚性配准为33%。可靠性过滤后的配准相比未过滤方法改善了平均对齐轮廓。按解剖部位的分析显示出显著差异，腹部配准的可靠性强于脑部配准。权重敏感性分析确定Dice重叠是主导可靠性成分。所提出的框架为多模态配准提供了可解释的质量控制层，而风险阈值反映的是统计验证而非临床验证。

</details>


### [27] [Homer: Understanding Long-form Videos with Hierarchical Memory and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02588)
*Yixin Ji,Fanghua Ye,Juntao Li,Bo Zhao,Zexuan Qiu,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER，一种层次化在线记忆探索与推理框架，通过构建多尺度记忆结构（原始感知层、实体层、事件层及显式时序因果连接），并配备智能体推理器进行多轮记忆检索与验证，解决了多模态大模型在小时级长视频在线推理中因紧凑视觉表示缺乏语义结构、记忆组织仅依赖时间邻近性而无法进行多跳叙事推理的问题。在M3-Bench和Video-MME-Long上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线长视频理解方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是紧凑视觉表示缺乏语义结构，二是基于时间邻近性的记忆组织缺乏显式因果链接，导致LLM在每次查询时都需要从零重建多跳叙事推理路径。受人类层次化视频理解方式启发，作者希望弥合这一差距。

Method: HOMER框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化记忆模块，从原始感知层到重复出现的实体层，再到由显式时序和因果关系连接的事件层，镜像长视频的多尺度结构；(2) 智能体推理器，模拟人类推理方式，通过多轮记忆检索定位相关场景、查找细节并组合答案，同时配备验证和纠错机制确保每一步的正确性。

Result: HOMER在M3-Bench-robot上超越此前最佳智能体方法+5.5分，在M3-Bench-web上+10.8分，在Video-MME-Long上+4.4分。在三种不同LLM骨干网络上均取得一致提升，表明其具有模型无关的结构化检索能力。

Conclusion: HOMER通过层次化记忆结构和主动推理机制，显著提升了多模态大模型在在线长视频理解上的多跳叙事推理能力，且其能力提升具有模型无关性，可作为通用结构化检索框架。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models excel on short clips but struggle on hour-long videos in an online setting, where frames are processed incrementally under limited memory. Existing online methods either retain compact visual representations that lack semantic structure, or build higher-level memory stores organized around temporal proximity rather than explicit causal links, leaving multi-hop narrative reasoning to be reconstructed by the LLM at every query. We bridge this gap with \textsc{Homer}, a Hierarchical Online Memory Exploration and Reasoning framework. \textsc{Homer}'s memory mirrors the multi-scale structure of long videos, ranging from raw perception, to recurring entities, to events connected by explicit temporal and causal relations. Its agentic reasoner then explores this memory the way humans do, locating the relevant scene, looking up details, and composing the answer through multi-round memory retrieval, with a harness that verifies and corrects each step. \textsc{Homer} outperforms the previous best agent method by $+5.5$, $+10.8$, and $+4.4$ points on M3-Bench-robot, M3-Bench-web, and Video-MME-Long, and consistently lifts three various LLM backbones, indicating a model-agnostic structural capability for grounded retrieval over long videos.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型在短视频片段上表现出色，但在在线设置下的时长视频理解中面临挑战——此时帧序列需在有限内存下增量处理。现有的在线方法要么保留紧凑但缺乏语义结构的视觉表示，要么构建围绕时间邻近性（而非显式因果链接）组织的高级记忆存储，导致每次查询时多跳叙事推理都需要由LLM从头重建。我们提出HOMER（层次化在线记忆探索与推理框架）来弥合这一差距。HOMER的记忆结构镜像了长视频的多尺度结构，从原始感知层，到重复出现的实体层，再到由显式时序和因果关系连接的事件层。其智能体推理器模拟人类的探索方式，通过多轮记忆检索定位相关场景、查找细节并组合答案，同时配备验证和纠错机制确保每一步的正确性。HOMER在M3-Bench-robot、M3-Bench-web和Video-MME-Long上分别超越此前最佳智能体方法+5.5、+10.8和+4.4分，并在三种不同LLM骨干网络上均取得一致提升，表明其具有模型无关的结构化检索能力，能够实现在长视频上的有根基的检索。

</details>


### [28] [CPR: Chained Perceptual Refinement for Coarse-to-Fine Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02591)
*Si-Yuan Lu,Hanruo Zhu,Ziquan Zhu,Gaojie Jin,Zeyu Fu,Lu Yin,Ke Li,Lu Liu,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CPR（链式感知精化）是一种从粗到细的框架，通过动态预测精化区域的位置和范围，在固定GPU内存下实现高分辨率医学图像的高效精确分析，在五个数据集上优于现有方法，最高提升2.27个百分点，并实现高达19.6倍的GFLOPs降低。

<details>
