  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高分辨率医学图像包含细粒度、空间稀疏的诊断关键线索，但现有深度模型要么均匀处理全分辨率图像导致巨大计算和内存开销，要么通过下采样丢失诊断细节。亟需一种在保持诊断保真度的同时实现高效计算的方法。

Method: 提出链式感知精化（CPR）框架，采用从粗到细的策略：先通过低分辨率全局视图进行全局感知，再动态预测需要精化的区域位置和空间范围，从原始图像中提取高分辨率证据，并逐步将其与全局上下文进行增量式集成。通过固定骨干网络输入大小同时收缩感知场，实现恒定峰值GPU内存。

Result: 在五个医学影像数据集和多种骨干架构上，CPR一致优于固定分辨率及多尺度的最先进基线方法，比第二名方法提升高达2.27个百分点；在匹配精度下实现高达19.6倍的GFLOPs降低，建立了高分辨率医学图像分析中精度与效率的优越权衡。

Conclusion: CPR框架有效解决了高分辨率医学图像分析中精度与效率的矛盾，通过动态局部精化策略在保持诊断保真度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为医学图像分析提供了一种新的高效范式。

Original Abstract: High resolution medical images contain fine grained, spatially sparse cues that are critical for diagnosis, yet preserving full resolution incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Most deep models process images uniformly, leading to redundant computation or loss of diagnostic detail under downsampling. We propose Chained Perceptual Refinement, CPR, a coarse to fine framework that formulates medical image analysis as a sequential global to local decision process. Starting from a low resolution global view, CPR dynamically predicts the location and spatial extent of refinement regions, extracts high resolution evidence from the original image, and incrementally integrates it with global context. By keeping the backbone input size fixed while contracting the perceptual field, CPR preserves diagnostic fidelity with constant peak GPU memory. Extensive experiments on five medical imaging datasets and multiple backbone architectures demonstrate that CPR consistently outperforms both fixed resolution and multi scale state of the art baselines, achieving improvements of up to 2.27 percentage points over the second best method. It also achieves up to a 19.6 fold reduction in GFLOPs at matched accuracy, establishing a superior accuracy and efficiency trade off for high resolution medical image analysis. The code is available on GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率医学图像包含细粒度、空间稀疏的线索，这些线索对诊断至关重要，但保持全分辨率会带来巨大的计算和内存成本。大多数深度学习模型对图像进行均匀处理，导致计算冗余或在下采样时丢失诊断细节。本文提出链式感知精化（Chained Perceptual Refinement, CPR），一种从粗到细的框架，将医学图像分析形式化为一个从全局到局部的序贯决策过程。从低分辨率全局视图出发，CPR动态预测精化区域的位置和空间范围，从原始图像中提取高分辨率证据，并将其与全局上下文进行增量式集成。通过保持骨干网络输入尺寸固定同时收缩感知场，CPR以恒定的峰值GPU内存保持诊断保真度。在五个医学影像数据集和多种骨干架构上的大量实验表明，CPR一致优于固定分辨率和多尺度的最先进基线方法，相比第二名方法提升高达2.27个百分点。在匹配精度下，CPR实现了高达19.6倍的GFLOPs降低，为高分辨率医学图像分析建立了优越的精度与效率权衡。代码已在GitHub上开源。

</details>


### [29] [H-OPD: Confidence Aware Heterogeneous Multi-Teacher Multimodal On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02592)
*Qixiang Yin,Huanjin Yao,Yuchen Cai,Jianghao Chen,Ziyi Wang,Min Yang,Fei Su,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出H-OPD，一种置信度感知的异构多教师同策略蒸馏框架，通过token级教师仲裁替代传统的样本级路由，在多模态推理中实现动态教师选择，显著提升了推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OPD方法在多模态推理中采用静态教师路由，基于模态或任务类型为每个样本分配单一教师，忽略了视觉定位与抽象推理可能主导不同解码步骤的问题，导致单一教师无法为完整轨迹提供充分监督。

Method: 1) 提出H-OPD框架，采用token级教师仲裁替代任务/样本级路由；2) 通过视觉到语言的描述转换使纯文本教师能够访问关键视觉语义；3) 设计置信度感知的仲裁机制，在每个token上动态融合视觉-语言教师和纯文本教师的监督。

Result: 在11个广泛使用的推理基准上进行了全面评估，结果表明该方法性能优越。

Conclusion: H-OPD通过置信度感知的异构多教师蒸馏框架，有效解决了多模态推理中静态教师路由的局限性，实验证明该方法在多个推理基准上取得优异表现。

Original Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm by providing supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, existing OPD methods for multimodal reasoning usually rely on a static teacher routing, assigning each sample to a single teacher based on modality or task type. This ignores that visual grounding and abstract reasoning may dominate different decoding steps, making a single teacher insufficient for the full trajectory. To this end, H-OPD is proposed as a confidence-aware heterogeneous multi-teacher OPD framework for multimodal reasoning. By verifying the complementarity of heterogeneous teachers in the same reasoning process, H-OPD replaces task or sample level teacher routing with token-level teacher arbitration along the shared student trajectory. H-OPD employs vision-to-language description transfer to enable text-only teachers to access key visual semantics, and uses a confidence-aware arbitration mechanism to dynamically combine vision-language teacher and text-only teachers at each token. Extensive evaluations over 11 widely-used reasoning benchmarks showcase the superior performance of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）近期作为一种有效的训练后范式出现，通过在学生生成的轨迹上提供监督来实现。然而，现有的用于多模态推理的OPD方法通常依赖于静态教师路由，即基于模态或任务类型将每个样本分配给单一教师。这忽略了视觉定位和抽象推理可能主导不同的解码步骤，使得单一教师无法为完整轨迹提供充分监督。为此，本文提出了H-OPD，一种用于多模态推理的置信度感知的异构多教师同策略蒸馏框架。通过验证异构教师在相同推理过程中的互补性，H-OPD将任务级或样本级的教师路由替换为沿共享学生轨迹的token级教师仲裁。H-OPD采用视觉到语言的描述转换，使纯文本教师能够访问关键的视觉语义，并利用置信度感知的仲裁机制在每个token上动态组合视觉-语言教师和纯文本教师。在11个广泛使用的推理基准上的全面评估展示了我们方法的优越性能。

</details>


### [30] [Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance for Multimodal LLMs Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02593)
*Jaehyun Jang,Eunseop Yoon,Hee Suk Yoon,SooHwan Eom,Mark A. Hasegawa-Johnson,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRAG方法，通过关注响应-视觉注意力信号并为每个token自适应调整蒸馏权重，显著提升了多模态大语言模型的知识蒸馏效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于输出token分布的KD方法不足以压缩多模态大语言模型，而直接蒸馏注意力信号虽有效但具体哪些注意力信号最有用尚未充分探索。传统方法依赖提示-视觉注意力，但作者发现下游性能与响应-视觉注意力相似性强相关，与提示条件的注意力无关。

Method: 提出Token级响应-视觉注意力引导（TRAG），包含两点：1）将蒸馏重点从提示-视觉注意力转向响应-视觉注意力信号；2）基于注意力熵自适应加权KL散度，为每个token设置特定的蒸馏目标，从而引导学生模型精确模仿教师模型的视觉关注焦点。

Result: 实验发现：下游性能与响应-视觉注意力相似性强相关，与提示条件注意力相关性可忽略；注意力分布在各个token间存在显著方差，统一蒸馏目标次优。在多个基准上TRAG显著优于先前蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: TRAG通过聚焦响应-视觉注意力信号并采用token级自适应蒸馏目标，有效提升了多模态大语言模型的知识蒸馏性能。

Original Abstract: While knowledge distillation (KD) is widely adopted for training lightweight models by leveraging supervision from larger teacher models, relying solely on output token distributions has proven insufficient for compressing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Since output tokens are a byproduct of the model attending to visual inputs, prior works have explored explicitly distilling attention to provide a direct supervisory signal. While promising, the precise utility of which attention signals to distill remains under-explored. In this work, we challenge the conventional reliance on prompt-to-vision attention by revealing that downstream performance correlates strongly with response-to-vision attention similarity to the teacher, but negligibly with that of prompt-conditioned attention. Furthermore, we observe that attention distributions exhibit significant variance across individual tokens, indicating that a uniform distillation objective is suboptimal. To this end, we introduce Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance (TRAG), a distillation objective that 1) shifts the focus to response-to-vision signals and 2) employs token-specific objectives by adaptively weighting the Kullback-Leibler divergence based on attention entropy, effectively guiding the student to mirror the teacher's precise visual focus. Extensive experimental results on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that TRAG significantly outperforms prior distillation baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管知识蒸馏（KD）通过利用来自较大教师模型的监督信号来训练轻量级模型已被广泛采用，但仅依赖输出token分布已被证明不足以压缩多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）。由于输出token是模型关注视觉输入的副产品，先前的工作探索了显式蒸馏注意力以提供直接的监督信号。虽然这一思路有前景，但具体哪些注意力信号最有蒸馏价值仍未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们挑战了传统上对提示-视觉注意力的依赖，揭示了下游性能与教师模型的响应-视觉注意力相似性密切相关，而与提示条件注意力的相似性相关性可忽略。此外，我们观察到注意力分布在各个token之间表现出显著方差，这表明统一的蒸馏目标是次优的。为此，我们引入了Token级响应-视觉注意力引导（TRAG），这是一种蒸馏目标，它1）将重点转向响应-视觉信号，2）通过基于注意力熵自适应加权KL散度来采用token特定的目标，有效引导学生模型模仿教师模型的精确视觉关注焦点。在多个基准上的广泛实验结果表明，TRAG显著优于先前的蒸馏基线方法。

</details>


### [31] [An automated method of identifying incorrectly labelled images based on the sequences of loss functions of deep learning networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02594)
*Zhipeng Zhang,Wenhui Shou,Wengting Ma,Dongjia Xing,Qingqing Xu,Li-Qun Xu,Qingxia Fan,Ling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种通过分析深度学习分类网络多个训练周期的损失函数序列来自动识别医学图像中错误标签的方法，经专家复核修正后可提升模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像人工标注中高达10%的标签可能存在错误，这会降低深度学习模型的性能，因此需要一种自动化的方法来识别和纠正错误标签。

Method: 通过分析深度学习分类网络在多个训练周期（epochs）上的损失函数序列，自动识别可能被错误标注的医学图像，识别出的图像由专家审查并重新标注。

Result: 在眼底图像数据集上，该方法识别出75.31%的人为翻转标签样本，假阳性率仅4.85%；修正后模型准确率从95.93%（6%标签噪声）提升至96.50%（1.5%噪声），接近无噪声时的96.57%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过自动化标签质量控制，能有效识别错误标注并提升模型性能，接近理想的无噪声水平。

Original Abstract: Deep learning is widely applied in medical image analysis, but up to 10% of manually labelled images may be incorrect, degrading model performance. This paper proposes an automated method to identify incorrectly labelled medical images by analyzing sequences of loss functions from deep learning classification networks over multiple training epochs. Identified images can be reviewed and relabelled by experts, improving dataset quality and model performance. Two experiments validate the method on a fundus image dataset for referable diabetic retinopathy screening. In the first, 6% (648) of 10,788 gold-standard labels were intentionally flipped. The method identified 75.31% (488) of the flipped samples, with only 4.85% (492) false positives among correctly labelled samples. In the second, reviewing and correcting the 980 identified samples (9.1% of the dataset) and retraining the model improved best accuracy on an independent test set from 95.93% (with 6% label noise) to 96.50% (with 1.5% noise), approaching the ideal 96.57% (with 0% noise). The results demonstrate the method's effectiveness in improving model performance through automated label quality control.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习在医学图像分析中被广泛应用，但人工标注的图像中可能有多达10%存在错误，从而降低模型性能。本文提出一种自动化方法，通过分析深度学习分类网络在多个训练周期上的损失函数序列，识别医学图像中被错误标注的样本。识别出的图像可由专家审查并重新标注，从而提高数据集质量和模型性能。两个实验在用于可转诊糖尿病视网膜病变筛查的眼底图像数据集上验证了该方法。在第一个实验中，10,788个金标准标签中有6%（648个）被人为翻转。该方法识别出了75.31%（488个）的翻转样本，且在正确标注样本中的假阳性率仅为4.85%（492个）。在第二个实验中，对识别出的980个样本（占数据集的9.1%）进行审查和纠正后重新训练模型，在独立测试集上的最佳准确率从95.93%（6%标签噪声）提升至96.50%（1.5%噪声），接近理想的96.57%（0%噪声）。结果表明，该方法通过自动化标签质量控制能有效提升模型性能。

</details>


### [32] [CIPHER: Causal Intervention Pathways for Healthcare Equity and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02596)
*Xinyu Jia,Weidong Guo,Wangyuan Zhao,Yi Guo,Zeju Li,Yuanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CIPHER框架，通过结构因果模型揭示敏感属性通过四条因果路径影响医学图像生成，并利用扩散模型对四条路径进行系统性干预，在胸部X光和皮肤镜数据集上将最差组别性能差异平均降低35.8%，同时提升整体诊断准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学诊断深度学习模型在敏感子组（如种族、性别）上存在显著的性能差异，而生成式数据增强策略仅处理敏感属性与图像特征之间的部分依赖路径，忽视了敏感属性通过四条不同因果路径影响图像内容的复杂结构。

Method: 基于结构因果模型形式化医学图像生成过程，揭示四条因果路径；提出CIPHER框架，利用配备无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）和空文本反转（null-text inversion）的扩散骨干网络，实现患者特定解剖结构的忠实重建以及反事实图像的精确可编辑合成，从而对所有四条因果路径进行系统性干预。

Result: 在胸部X光和皮肤镜基准测试中，在标准分布和偏移分布数据上，CIPHER相比疾病条件合成基线将最差组别性能差异平均降低35.8%，同时提升了总体诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 多路径干预策略能有效减少医学诊断模型中的群体公平性差异，同时提升模型鲁棒性和整体性能，验证了结构化因果建模在医学图像公平性增强中的价值。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models for medical diagnosis frequently exhibit substantial performance disparities across sensitive subgroups (e.g., race, sex), even when average accuracy is high. While generative data augmentation offers a route to mitigate this, existing strategies are suboptimal; they typically address only one or two dependency channels between sensitive attributes and image features. We formalize the medical image formation process via a structural causal model, revealing that sensitive attributes actually influence image content through four distinct pathways-a structural complexity neglected by prior works. Based on this insight, we introduce CIPHER (Causal Intervention Pathways for Healthcare Equity and Robustness), a framework designed to systematically intervene on all four causal paths. To achieve this, CIPHER utilizes a diffusion backbone equipped with classifier-free guidance and null-text inversion. This technical design enables the faithful reconstruction of patient-specific anatomy while allowing for the precise, editable synthesis of counterfactuals required to break sensitive dependency chains. We tested CIPHER using chest X-ray and dermoscopy benchmarks across both standard and shifted data distributions. By employing a multi-pathway intervention strategy, our model reduced worst-group disparities by an average of 35.8% compared to disease-conditioned synthesis baselines, while also improving total diagnostic accuracy

中文摘要翻译: 用于医学诊断的深度学习模型即使在平均准确率较高的情况下，也经常在敏感子组（例如种族、性别）之间表现出显著的性能差异。虽然生成式数据增强提供了一条缓解这一问题的途径，但现有策略并不理想；它们通常只处理敏感属性与图像特征之间的一两条依赖通道。我们通过结构因果模型形式化了医学图像生成过程，揭示了敏感属性实际上通过四条不同的路径影响图像内容——这是先前工作忽视的结构复杂性。基于这一洞见，我们提出了CIPHER（面向医疗公平性与鲁棒性的因果干预路径框架），该框架旨在系统性地干预所有四条因果路径。为此，CIPHER利用了一个配备无分类器引导和空文本反转的扩散骨干网络。这种技术设计能够在重建患者特定解剖结构的同时，实现对反事实图像的精确可编辑合成，从而打破敏感的依赖链条。我们在标准分布和偏移数据分布下的胸部X光和皮肤镜基准测试上评估了CIPHER。通过采用多路径干预策略，我们的模型相比疾病条件合成基线将最差组别性能差异平均降低了35.8%，同时提升了总体诊断准确率。

</details>


### [33] [Evaluating Agentic Harness Systems for Autonomous Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02598)
*Jie Lin,Zongyi Chen,Qiaoling Zheng,Liuyi Wang,Hengyi Jiang,Jiabao Chen,Xiang Liu,Yinghong Yang,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACP-Bench是一个评估AI智能体在计算病理学全流程中自主执行能力的基准框架，通过对41个病理学任务在9个模型和3个智能体框架上的369条完整轨迹进行评估，发现工作流启动、任务解释和诊断报告较成熟，但工具执行、结果绑定和迭代修订能力薄弱，端到端自主完成仍罕见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将通用AI智能体系统适配到病理学特定任务中面临工具、证据标准和临床边界等多重挑战，现有评估缺乏对自主计算病理学全流程工作流能力的标准化审计标准。

Method: 构建ACP-Bench基准框架，覆盖41个病理学任务（24个生物标志物、7个形态学、10个预后任务），跨越6个体系统分组和9个终点家族，对9个模型和3个智能体框架（Claude Code、Codex、Open Code）的369条完整轨迹从工作流执行、诊断性能和临床边界对齐三个维度进行评估，结合专家裁定过程审计、诊断评估和病理学家验证的安全性审查。

Result: 工作流启动、任务解释和诊断报告较为成熟，但工具绑定执行、结果绑定和反思性工作流修订能力不足，正式的端到端完成仍然罕见。

Conclusion: ACP-Bench提供了一个可复用的标准，用于在声称可靠的临床自主性之前审计智能体系统是否能有效执行病理学工作流。

Original Abstract: Autonomous computational pathology (ACP) converts high-level pathology analysis goals into executable, traceable and clinically bounded workflows. Realizing this capability requires adapting general agentic harness systems to pathology-specific tasks, tools, evidence standards and clinical claim boundaries. We contribute ACP-Bench, a framework that adapts existing harness systems from computational pathology support toward ACP workflow capability. ACP-Bench evaluates 41 pathology workflow tasks, including 24 biomarker, 7 morphology and 10 prognosis tasks spanning 6 body-system groups and 9 endpoint families. The benchmark evaluates 9 models and 3 harness groups (Claude Code, Codex and Open Code), yielding 369 complete trajectories. ACP-Bench evaluates each trajectory across workflow execution, diagnostic performance and clinical-boundary alignment, combining expert-adjudicated process audits, diagnostic assessment and pathologist-validated safety review. Across evaluated systems, workflow initiation, task interpretation and diagnostic reporting were more mature than tool-bound execution, result binding and reflective workflow revision, and formal end-to-end completion remained rare. ACP-Bench provides a reusable standard for auditing whether agentic systems can operationalize pathology workflows before claims of reliable clinical autonomy.

中文摘要翻译: 自主计算病理学（ACP）将高层次的病理学分析目标转化为可执行、可追溯且受临床约束的工作流。实现这一能力需要将通用智能体框架适配到病理学特定任务、工具、证据标准和临床声明边界。我们提出了ACP-Bench，这是一个将现有计算病理学支持的智能体框架适配到ACP工作流能力的评估框架。ACP-Bench评估了41个病理学工作流任务，包括24个生物标志物任务、7个形态学任务和10个预后任务，涵盖6个体系统分组和9个终点家族。该基准对9个模型和3个智能体框架组（Claude Code、Codex和Open Code）进行了评估，获得了369条完整轨迹。ACP-Bench从工作流执行、诊断性能和临床边界对齐三个维度评估每条轨迹，结合了专家裁定的过程审计、诊断评估和病理学家验证的安全性审查。在评估的系统中，工作流启动、任务解释和诊断报告相对成熟，而工具绑定执行、结果绑定和反思性工作流修订能力较弱，正式的端到端完成仍然罕见。ACP-Bench提供了一个可复用的标准，用于在声称可靠的临床自主性之前审计智能体系统是否能有效执行病理学工作流。

</details>


### [34] [CV-DCLR: Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement for Robust Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02601)
*Can Wang,Jiangnan Li,Mingyu Li,Yining Song,Kangrui Ren,Min Gan,Jinfu Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CV-DCLR框架，通过双流互纠机制（视觉似然流+因果重要性流）和自适应门控机制，解决零样本学习中的语义纠缠问题，在CUB、SUN、AWA2基准上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本学习方法采用全局对齐方式，导致模型过度拟合视觉相似类别间的虚假相关性，无法捕捉真正的类别身份特征。语义纠缠（如狼与哈士奇的混淆）是零样本学习的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement (CV-DCLR)框架，包含双流互纠机制：视觉似然流建模观测模式，因果重要性流通过反事实干预验证候选原型的结构必要性。自适应门控机制动态调节特征响应，放大因果特征并抑制视觉上合理但结构无关的干扰。

Result: 在CUB、SUN、AWA2基准上，采用严格语义纠缠注入协议，CV-DCLR在高模糊场景下显著优于现有方法。现有模型在纠缠下性能灾难性退化，而CV-DCLR保持鲁棒性能，有效解缠真实类别身份与语义混淆因素。

Conclusion: CV-DCLR框架通过因果干预和动态标签精炼，有效解决了零样本学习中的语义纠缠问题，为高模糊场景下的视觉-语义关联建模提供了新的因果视角。

Original Abstract: Zero-Shot Learning (ZSL) facilitates knowledge transfer via shared semantic spaces. However, a critical bottleneck in this paradigm is Semantic Entanglement, where visual representations are inevitably conflated with visually similar semantic concepts, such as distinguishing the intrinsic traits of a Wolf from the shared features of a Husky. Existing global alignment methods often indiscriminately maximize correlations between visual and semantic modalities, leading models to overfit spurious similarities rather than capturing distinctive class identities. To address this fundamental limitation, we propose the Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement (CV-DCLR) framework. Unlike traditional approaches that rely on superficial visual statistics, CV-DCLR recalibrates visual-semantic associations via a Dual-Stream Mutual Correction Mechanism. This includes a Visual Likelihood Stream to model observational patterns and a Causal Importance Stream that verifies the structural necessity of candidate prototypes through Counterfactual Intervention. Acting as a logical filter, our adaptive gating mechanism dynamically modulates feature responses to amplify genuine causal traits while suppressing visually plausible but structurally irrelevant distractors. Extensive experiments on the CUB, SUN, and AWA2 benchmarks under a rigorous Semantic Entanglement Injection protocol demonstrate that CV-DCLR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in high-ambiguity scenarios. Specifically, while existing models suffer catastrophic degradation under entanglement, our framework maintains robust performance, effectively disentangling true class identities from semantic confounders.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本学习（ZSL）通过共享语义空间促进知识迁移。然而，该范式的关键瓶颈在于语义纠缠，即视觉表示不可避免地与视觉上相似的语义概念混淆，例如区分狼的内在特征与哈士奇的共享特征。现有的全局对齐方法通常不加区分地最大化视觉与语义模态之间的相关性，导致模型过度拟合虚假相似性，而非捕捉独特的类别身份。为解决这一根本性局限，我们提出了因果视觉动态标签精炼（CV-DCLR）框架。不同于依赖浅层视觉统计的传统方法，CV-DCLR通过双流互纠机制重新校准视觉-语义关联。该机制包括一个用于建模观测模式的视觉似然流，以及一个通过反事实干预验证候选原型结构必要性的因果重要性流。作为逻辑滤波器，我们的自适应门控机制动态调节特征响应，以放大真正的因果特征，同时抑制视觉上合理但结构无关的干扰。在严格语义纠缠注入协议下，针对CUB、SUN和AWA2基准的大量实验表明，CV-DCLR在高模糊场景中显著优于现有方法。具体而言，当现有模型在纠缠下遭受灾难性退化时，我们的框架保持鲁棒性能，有效解缠真实类别身份与语义混淆因素。

</details>


### [35] [DynaWM: A Base-VLA-Guided World Foundation Model for Moving-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02604)
*Chongkei Chang,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaWM提出了一种基于基础VLA引导的世界基础模型，通过Mamba-3动作编码器、V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器和本体状态编码器联合条件化流匹配DiT，生成运动感知动作轨迹，用于动态移动物体操控。在DynaGrasp-32基准和DynaGrasp-1600数据集上，该方法在多种基座VLA模型上取得了显著性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将世界模型融入高性能基座VLA架构中进行端到端微调，但不当的微调会降低预训练基座VLA模型的性能，尤其是在动态移动物体操控任务中面临挑战。

Method: DynaWM包含三个编码器：Mamba-3动作编码器对基座VLA生成的动作块进行编码得到动作条件表征；V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器提取多视角观测历史特征；本体状态编码器编码机械臂本体状态。这些特征联合条件化一个流匹配DiT（Flow-Matching Diffusion Transformer），重新生成运动感知的动作轨迹。

Result: 在微调基座VLA上，DynaWM在SmolVLA、X-VLA、π0和π0.5上分别提升7.19、45.31、1.88和10.94个百分点；在粗调基座VLA上分别提升35.13、44.06、35.69和26.13个百分点。消融实验表明，视觉编码提升成功率27.50%，去除动作条件导致成功率下降45.44%。

Conclusion: DynaWM作为一种基础VLA引导的世界基础模型，能适配多种微调和粗调基座VLA检查点，显著提升动态移动物体操控性能，验证了动作条件和视觉编码对任务成功的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) models have received widespread attention, many challenges remain in manipulating dynamic moving objects. In most existing approaches, end-to-end forward or inverse dynamics models, i.e., world models, are incorporated into high-performance base VLA architectures, which may degrade the performance of well-pretrained base VLA models due to inappropriate fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose DynaWM, a base-VLA-guided world foundation model that adapts to a wide variety of fine-tuned and coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints for moving-object manipulation. DynaWM uses a Mamba-3-based action encoder to encode the base action chunk produced by the base VLA into an action-conditioning representation, a V-JEPA 2.1 vision encoder to extract features from multi-view observation history, and a proprioceptive state encoder to encode robotic-arm proprioceptive states. These feature representations jointly condition a flow-matching DiT to regenerate motion-aware action trajectories for moving-object manipulation. For systematic evaluation, we construct the DynaGrasp-32 benchmark, covering six categories of moving-object manipulation tasks, including velocity variation, trajectory variation, and multi-object manipulation, as well as the DynaGrasp-1600 dataset, which consists of 32 scenarios, 1,600 demonstration trajectories, and approximately 1.53M images. For fine-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, DynaWM achieves percentage improvements of 7.19, 45.31, 1.88, and 10.94 over SmolVLA, X-VLA, π0, and π0.5, respectively. For coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, performance increases by 35.13, 44.06, 35.69, and 26.13 percentage, respectively. Ablation experiments show that visual encoding enhances success by 27.50%, while reducing success by 45.44% if action conditioning is removed.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已获得广泛关注，但在操控动态移动物体方面仍面临诸多挑战。在现有大多数方法中，端到端的前向或逆向动力学模型（即世界模型）被融入高性能基座VLA架构中，但这可能因不当的微调而降低预训练良好的基座VLA模型的性能。本文提出DynaWM，一种由基座VLA引导的世界基础模型，能够适配多种微调和粗调的基座VLA检查点，用于移动物体操控。DynaWM使用基于Mamba-3的动作编码器将基座VLA生成的基座动作块编码为动作条件表征，使用V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器从多视角观测历史中提取特征，并使用本体状态编码器编码机械臂本体状态。这些特征表征联合条件化一个流匹配扩散变换器（Flow-Matching DiT），以重新生成面向移动物体操控的运动感知动作轨迹。为进行系统评估，我们构建了DynaGrasp-32基准，涵盖六类移动物体操控任务，包括速度变化、轨迹变化和多物体操控，以及DynaGrasp-1600数据集，包含32个场景、1600条演示轨迹和约153万张图像。在微调的基座VLA检查点上，DynaWM相比SmolVLA、X-VLA、π0和π0.5分别提升了7.19、45.31、1.88和10.94个百分点。在粗调的基座VLA检查点上，性能分别提升了35.13、44.06、35.69和26.13个百分点。消融实验表明，视觉编码可将成功率提升27.50%，而去除动作条件则使成功率下降45.44%。

</details>


### [36] [Latent Visual Cache for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02607)
*Yongheng Zhang,Zhipeng Xu,Hao Wu,Yinghui Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent Video Cache (Latent-VC)，一种在解码器中插入的循环潜在视觉缓存，通过保留紧凑的视觉记忆来缓解视频推理中的视觉锚定衰减问题，在多个视频基准上优于强基线且生成更短响应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大规模多模态模型在视频推理中采用“一次读取、多次生成”范式，导致生成时视觉锚定逐渐衰减（Visual Anchoring Decay），缺乏在推理过程中持续保持视觉记忆的有效机制。

Method: 在解码器中插入循环潜在视觉缓存（Latent-VC），通过监督对比缓存对齐和基于视觉锚定的GRPO（含潜在锚定奖励）进行训练，利用原生解码器隐藏状态保持训练-推理严格对齐，基于Qwen3.5-9B构建。

Result: 在六个视频基准上持续优于强CoT和SFT+GRPO基线，在锚定密集型及长视频任务上提升尤为显著；同时以更短响应获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 潜在视觉缓存通过保留视觉证据而非依赖更长的文本推理链来提升视频推理能力。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning requires Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to remain grounded in dense evidence, yet existing systems largely adopt "read-once, generate-many" paradigm, in which visual grounding weakens during generation. This phenomenon has been widely observed and is known as Visual Anchoring Decay. To fill this gap, we introduce Latent Video Cache (Latent-VC), a recurrent latent visual cache inserted into the decoder to preserve compact visual memories throughout reasoning. The cache is trained with supervised contrastive cache alignment and vision-grounded GRPO with a latent grounding reward, while maintaining strict train-inference alignment through native decoder hidden states. Built on Qwen3.5-9B, Latent-VC consistently outperforms strong CoT and SFT+GRPO baselines across six video benchmarks, with especially clear gains on grounding-intensive and long-video tasks. In addition, it also achieves higher accuracy with substantially shorter responses, suggesting that latent visual caching improves video reasoning by preserving visual evidence rather than relying on longer textual chains.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理要求大型多模态模型（LMMs）在密集证据中保持锚定，然而现有系统普遍采用"一次读取、多次生成"范式，导致生成过程中视觉锚定逐渐减弱。这一现象已被广泛观测到，称为视觉锚定衰减（Visual Anchoring Decay）。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了潜在视频缓存（Latent Video Cache, Latent-VC），一种插入解码器的循环潜在视觉缓存，用于在整个推理过程中保留紧凑的视觉记忆。该缓存通过有监督对比缓存对齐和基于视觉锚定的GRPO（含潜在锚定奖励）进行训练，并利用原生解码器隐藏状态保持训练-推理严格对齐。基于Qwen3.5-9B构建的Latent-VC在六个视频基准上持续优于强CoT和SFT+GRPO基线，在锚定密集型及长视频任务上增益尤为明显。此外，它以显著更短的响应获得了更高的准确率，表明潜在视觉缓存通过保留视觉证据而非依赖更长的文本推理链来提升视频推理能力。

</details>


### [37] [Privacy-Preserving Industrial Ergonomics: mmWave-Based Automated REBA Scoring and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02611)
*Xuhan Zhang,Zhuangzhuang Dai,Luis J. Mans,Victor Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于毫米波雷达的端到端多任务学习框架，用于隐私保护的人体工效学评估。该框架通过时空骨干网络重建3D人体骨架，并回归REBA风险评分，在MMFi数据集上达到77.78%的分类准确率，推理延迟仅5.70毫秒，在高风险场景下显著优于传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统工效学评估依赖REBA观测工具，人工监测劳动强度大，基于视觉的自动化方法存在隐私问题。因此需要一种非侵入式、隐私保护的自动化评估方法。

Method: 提出端到端多任务学习框架：1) 时空骨干网络从毫米波雷达点云重建3D人体骨架；2) 回归头基于骨架生成REBA风险评分；3) 多目标损失函数融合生物力学约束和时序平滑约束以克服雷达点云稀疏性；4) 过采样策略解决高风险姿态数据不平衡问题。

Result: 在MMFi数据集上，分类准确率77.78%，推理延迟5.70ms（实时性能）；高风险场景REBA MAE为0.93，显著优于直接回归和两阶段流水线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架为非侵入式工业工效学评估提供了一种鲁棒的隐私保护解决方案，在保持实时性能的同时在高风险姿态识别上表现优越。

Original Abstract: Work-related Musculoskeletal Disorders (WMSDs) require continuous ergonomic assessments. While Rapid Entire Body Assessment (REBA) is a gold-standard observation tool, manual monitoring is labor-intensive, and vision-based automation leads to privacy concerns. This paper proposes a novel end-to-end multi-task learning framework for privacy-preserving ergonomic assessment using millimetre-wave (mmWave) radar. A spatio-temporal backbone reconstructs 3D human skeletons, which serves as the biomechanical foundation for a subsequent regression head to generate REBA risk scores. To overcome the sparsity of radar point clouds, we utilise a multi-objective loss function incorporating biomechanical limits and temporal smoothness constraints. Furthermore, we implement an oversampling strategy to address the imbalance of high-risk postures in existing datasets. Experimental results on MMFi dataset demonstrate that our framework achieves a Categorical Accuracy of 77.78% and real-time performance with an inference latency of 5.70 ms. Our method reaches a High-risk REBA MAE of 0.93, which significantly outperforms both direct regression and two-stage pipelines in high-risk scenarios, providing a robust solution for non-invasive industrial ergonomic assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 工作相关肌肉骨骼疾病（WMSDs）需要持续的人体工效学评估。尽管快速全身评估（REBA）是黄金标准观测工具，但人工监控劳动强度大，基于视觉的自动化方法存在隐私问题。本文提出一种新颖的端到端多任务学习框架，利用毫米波（mmWave）雷达实现隐私保护的人体工效学评估。该框架通过时空骨干网络重建3D人体骨架，作为后续回归头生成REBA风险评分的生物力学基础。为克服雷达点云的稀疏性，我们设计了一个结合生物力学约束和时序平滑约束的多目标损失函数。此外，我们采用过采样策略来应对现有数据集中高风险姿态的不平衡问题。在MMFi数据集上的实验结果表明，我们的框架实现了77.78%的分类准确率，推理延迟仅5.70毫秒，达到实时性能。我们的方法在高风险场景下取得了0.93的REBA平均绝对误差（MAE），显著优于直接回归和两阶段流水线方法，为非侵入式工业人体工效学评估提供了稳健的解决方案。

</details>


### [38] [Fusion: A Framework for Unified Sequential Token AdaptatIon in VisiOn TraNsformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02612)
*Aravind Pradeep,Samira Nazari,Mahdi Taheri,Christian Herglotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fusion 提出了一种统一的自适应推理框架，通过协调 token 合并、提前退出和 token 剪枝三种机制，以分阶段设计实现协作而非竞争，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算开销、校准误差和推理能耗，并在多种 ViT 骨干和数据集上展示出良好的可迁移性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Vision Transformer 在处理图像时对所有区域分配几乎相同的计算量，导致大量冗余计算。现有自适应推理方法虽尝试通过 token 压缩或提前终止来降低开销，但同时组合这些机制时会导致中间表示不稳定和精度下降。作者旨在解决这些机制之间的竞争问题，实现三者协同工作。

Method: 提出 Fusion 框架，采用分阶段设计：(1) 首先进行 token 合并；(2) 然后评估置信度，对置信度足够的样本提前退出；(3) 仅对需继续推理的样本执行 token 剪枝。通过这种顺序设计使三种机制协作而非竞争。此外引入轻量级路由模块，根据每个输入自适应调整压缩强度，并支持推理时无需重新训练即可调节精度-延迟权衡。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1k 上使用 DeiT-S，Fusion 在同等计算预算下匹配或超越 SOTA 自适应 ViT 方法，并将校准误差降低高达 4 倍，推理能耗降低 48%。在 ImageNet-100、CIFAR-100 和 ImageNette 上使用多种 ViT 骨干网络，无需特定数据集调参即可展示一致的可迁移性。

Conclusion: Fusion 通过分阶段的协调设计，成功实现了 token 合并、提前退出和 token 剪枝三种自适应推理机制的协同工作，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了计算成本、校准误差和能耗，并且具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformers achieve strong image classification accuracy but process all image regions with nearly the same computation, even when many regions are redundant or uninformative. Recent adaptive inference methods reduce this cost by selectively compressing tokens or terminating inference early, but combining these mechanisms often causes unstable intermediate representations and accuracy degradation. We introduce Fusion, a unified adaptive inference framework that coordinates token merging, early exiting, and token pruning through a simple staged design: tokens are merged first, confidence is evaluated next, and pruning is applied only to samples that continue inference. This ordering allows the three mechanisms to operate cooperatively rather than competitively. Fusion further includes lightweight routing modules that adapt compression strength to each input and support inference-time adjustment of the accuracy--latency trade-off without retraining. On ImageNet-1k with DeiT-S, Fusion matches or surpasses state-of-the-art adaptive ViT methods at comparable compute budgets while reducing calibration error by up to $4\times$ and inference energy by $48\%$. Experiments across ImageNet-100, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette with multiple ViT backbones demonstrate consistent transferability without dataset-specific tuning.

中文摘要翻译: Vision Transformer 在图像分类上取得了很强的精度，但对所有图像区域分配几乎相同的计算量，即使许多区域是冗余或无信息的。最近的自适应推理方法通过选择性地压缩 token 或提前终止推理来降低这一成本，但将这两种机制结合往往会导致中间表示不稳定和精度下降。我们提出 Fusion，一个统一的自适应推理框架，通过简单的分阶段设计协调 token 合并、提前退出和 token 剪枝：首先合并 token，接着评估置信度，然后仅对继续推理的样本进行剪枝。这种顺序使三种机制能够协作而非竞争地运行。Fusion 还包含轻量级路由模块，能够根据每个输入自适应调整压缩强度，并支持推理时无需重新训练即可调节精度-延迟权衡。在 ImageNet-1k 上使用 DeiT-S，Fusion 在同等计算预算下匹配或超越了最先进的自适应 ViT 方法，同时将校准误差降低高达 4 倍，推理能耗降低 48%。在 ImageNet-100、CIFAR-100 和 ImageNette 上使用多种 ViT 骨干网络的实验表明，无需特定数据集调参即可实现一致的可迁移性。

</details>


### [39] [SE-UNet: Singular Equivariant Imaging for Real-World Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02628)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SE-UNet，一种无需大规模预训练、通过几何等变性（D4群）和奇异值门控约束来求解不适定逆成像问题的框架，在CIFAR-10上实现了80%像素缺失的零样本修复，PSNR比Deep Image Prior提升超过4 dB。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在图像合成中表现出色，但在实际逆问题中面临两个主要障碍：需要大规模数据集，以及难以施加严格的物理约束。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一种数据高效的约束生成途径。

Method: 将生成过程视为受几何等变性（D4群对称性）和奇异值门控约束的优化问题，构建SE-UNet框架，通过强归纳偏置标准化解空间，实现无需预训练的零样本推理。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上实现了80%像素缺失的零样本修复，PSNR比Deep Image Prior基线提升超过4 dB，并表现出"奇异快照"收敛特性——快速锁定到信号流形。

Conclusion: SE-UNet提供了一种数据高效的约束生成途径，在理论先验与实际部署之间架起了桥梁，验证了强归纳偏置可替代大规模数据预训练。

Original Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis, their application to real-world inverse problems is often hampered by the need for massive datasets and the difficulty of imposing strict physical constraints. In this work, we introduce \textbf{SE-UNet} (Singular Equivariant UNet), a framework designed to solve ill-posed imaging tasks without extensive pre-training. By treating generation as an optimization problem constrained by geometric equivariance ($D_4$ group) and singular value gating, SE-UNet effectively standardizes the solution space. We demonstrate that these strong inductive biases allow for state-of-the-art zero-shot inpainting results (80\% missing pixels) on CIFAR-10. Our method surpasses Deep Image Prior (DIP) baselines by over 4 dB in PSNR and exhibits a characteristic "singular snap" convergence -- rapidly locking into the signal manifold. SE-UNet thus offers a data-efficient pathway for constrained generation, aligning with the ReALM-GEN goal of bridging theoretical priors with practical deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管扩散模型已经革新了图像合成领域，但其在现实世界逆问题中的应用常常受到大规模数据集需求以及难以施加严格物理约束的阻碍。本文提出SE-UNet（奇异等变UNet），一个无需大量预训练即可求解不适定成像任务的框架。通过将生成过程视为受几何等变性（D4群）和奇异值门控约束的优化问题，SE-UNet有效地标准化了解空间。我们证明，这些强归纳偏置使得在CIFAR-10上实现了80%像素缺失的零样本修复，结果达到了最先进水平。该方法在PSNR上比Deep Image Prior（DIP）基线提升超过4 dB，并表现出一种独特的"奇异快照"收敛特性——快速锁定到信号流形。因此，SE-UNet为约束生成提供了一条数据高效的途径，与ReALM-GEN在理论先验与实际部署之间架起桥梁的目标相契合。

</details>


### [40] [CLABTOOLKIT: An Open-Source Toolkit for Routine Processing, Manipulation, and Visualization of Neuroimaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02638)
*Yasser Alemán-Gómez,Nino Hervé,Patric Hagmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为CLABTOOLKIT的开源Python工具包，将神经影像研究中涉及的多模态数据（体积、表面、流线、连接矩阵）操作整合到统一的面向对象框架中，提供五种核心数据结构及19个模块，支持BIDS管理、FreeSurfer集成、弥散MRI处理、形态分析、图论网络分析和GPU加速可视化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经影像研究需要操作异构数据结构（如原始MRI体积、体素分割、皮层表面网格、纤维束图和连接矩阵），涉及多个接口和文件格式不兼容的工具，迫使研究人员反复实现常规但技术要求较高的操作，缺乏统一的操作框架。

Method: CLABTOOLKIT通过将体积、表面和流线数据表示为可互操作的Python对象，定义了五种核心数据结构（Parcellation、Surface、AnnotParcellation、Tractogram、Connectome），封装了常见的神经解剖学实体，并提供一致的方法来加载、处理和导出标准神经影像格式的数据。工具包包含按六层组织的19个模块，暴露13个面向对象类（含234个方法）和207个独立函数，并采用JSON配置系统实现无需修改代码的工作流定制。

Result: 工具包兼容Python 3.9-3.12，采用Apache 2.0许可开源，源代码、文档和示例工作流均已在GitHub上发布。

Conclusion: CLABTOOLKIT与现有神经影像库不同，它将颜色和查找表管理、分割操作、多表面可视化和纤维束成像工具整合在单一框架中，填补了现有工具通常分别处理这些任务的空白。

Original Abstract: Neuroimaging research requires manipulating heterogeneous data structures, including raw MRI volumes, volumetric parcellations, cortical surface meshes, tractograms, and connectivity matrices, across tools with incompatible interfaces and file formats, forcing researchers to repeatedly re-implement routine but technically demanding operations. We present CLABTOOLKIT, an open-source Python package that consolidates these operations into a single, coherent framework by representing volumetric, surface, and streamline data as interoperable Python objects. Five core data structures (Parcellation, Surface, AnnotParcellation, Tractogram, and Connectome) encapsulate common neuroanatomical entities and provide consistent methods for loading, processing, and exporting data across standard neuroimaging formats (e.g., NIfTI, GIFTI, FreeSurfer annotations, TCK/TRK), including connectome generation from a parcellation and scalar-map projection onto tractogram streamlines. Complementary modules support BIDS dataset management, FreeSurfer integration, diffusion MRI processing, morphometric analysis, graph-theoretical network analysis, and GPU-accelerated multi-panel visualization via PyVista. The toolkit comprises 19 modules organised into six layers, exposing 13 object-oriented classes with 234 methods and 207 standalone functions, and a JSON-based configuration system enables workflow customization without code changes. Unlike existing neuroimaging libraries, which typically address these tasks separately, CLABTOOLKIT combines color and lookup-table management, parcellation manipulation, multi-surface visualization, and tractography utilities within a single framework. CLABTOOLKIT is compatible with Python 3.9-3.12 and released under the Apache 2.0 license. Source code, documentation, and example workflows are available at https://github.com/connectomicslab/clabtoolkit.

中文摘要翻译: 神经影像研究需要操纵异构数据结构，包括原始MRI体积、体素分割、皮层表面网格、纤维束图和连接矩阵，这些操作涉及接口和文件格式不兼容的多种工具，迫使研究人员反复实现常规但技术要求较高的操作。我们提出了CLABTOOLKIT，一个开源Python包，通过将体积、表面和流线数据表示为可互操作的Python对象，将这些操作整合到一个统一、连贯的框架中。五种核心数据结构——Parcellation（分割）、Surface（表面）、AnnotParcellation（注释分割）、Tractogram（纤维束图）和Connectome（连接组）——封装了常见的神经解剖学实体，并提供一致的方法用于加载、处理和导出标准神经影像格式（如NIfTI、GIFTI、FreeSurfer注释、TCK/TRK）的数据，包括从分割生成连接组以及将标量图投影到纤维束图流线上。补充模块支持BIDS数据集管理、FreeSurfer集成、弥散MRI处理、形态测量分析、图论网络分析以及基于PyVista的GPU加速多面板可视化。该工具包包含按六层组织的19个模块，暴露了13个面向对象类（含234个方法）和207个独立函数，基于JSON的配置系统支持无需修改代码的工作流定制。与现有神经影像库（通常分别处理这些任务）不同，CLABTOOLKIT将颜色和查找表管理、分割操作、多表面可视化和纤维束成像工具结合在单个框架中。CLABTOOLKIT兼容Python 3.9-3.12，并在Apache 2.0许可下发布。源代码、文档和示例工作流可在https://github.com/connectomicslab/clabtoolkit获取。

</details>


### [41] [BiSLW: Bi-Spectral Latent Watermarking for Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02643)
*Aryan Pandit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiSLW是一种可训练的双谱潜空间水印框架，通过在互补频带中联合嵌入对齐的身份信号，在保持高保真度的同时实现了对扩散模型生成内容的鲁棒归属和认证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的扩散模型水印技术要么将潜张量视为静态空间特征图，要么依赖像素域修改，且大多未显式利用潜空间的内部频率结构进行双频带冗余嵌入，因而易受扩散的随机性和再生攻击的影响。

Method: 提出BiSLW框架，利用扩散潜变量的固有频率结构，设计双频带水印框架：低频分量编码全局语义，高频分量捕获精细纹理。通过可学习的编码器将水印独立注入两个频带，再重组后解码；双谱解码器从每个频带恢复水印，并通过跨频带一致性约束强制语义嵌入与纹理嵌入对齐。

Result: 相比先前的潜扩散水印方法，PSNR提升超过3 dB；在强再生攻击和常见失真下保持近乎完美的比特精度；计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: BiSLW在感知保真度和鲁棒性之间实现了强平衡，是一种高效且可靠的双频带潜空间水印方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have transformed visual content synthesis, yet they remain vulnerable to unauthorized usage and lack reliable attribution methods. Existing watermarking techniques often treat latent tensors as static spatial feature maps or depend on pixel-domain modification, and most do not explicitly leverage the internal frequency structure of the latent space for dual-band redundant embedding, leaving them susceptible to the stochastic nature of diffusion and regeneration attacks. We introduce BiSLW, a trainable bi-spectral latent watermarking framework that jointly embeds aligned identity signals across complementary spectral bands of the decoded diffusion latent using learned encoders and decoders, going beyond fixed-pattern frequency approaches. We leverage the inherent frequency structure of diffusion latents to design a dual-band watermarking framework. Low-frequency components encode global semantics, while high-frequency components capture fine texture. We exploit this structure to embed watermarks across complementary spectral bands. The watermark is independently injected into both bands via learned encoders and recombined before decoding, ensuring it becomes intrinsic to the generative trajectory. Dual spectral decoders recover the watermark from each band, while a cross-band consistency constraint enforces alignment between semantic and textural embeddings. Experiments show that BiSLW achieves a strong balance between perceptual fidelity and robustness, improving PSNR by over 3 dB compared to prior latent diffusion watermarking methods while preserving near-perfect bit accuracy under aggressive regeneration and common distortions, all with negligible computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散的生成模型已经改变了视觉内容合成的格局，但它们仍然容易受到未经授权的使用，并且缺乏可靠的归属方法。现有的水印技术通常将潜张量视为静态空间特征图或依赖于像素域修改，且大多数方法并未显式利用潜空间的内部频率结构进行双频带冗余嵌入，因而容易受到扩散的随机性和再生攻击的影响。我们提出BiSLW，一种可训练的双谱潜空间水印框架，通过可学习的编码器和解码器，在解码后的扩散潜变量的互补频带上联合嵌入对齐的身份信号，超越了固定模式的频率方法。我们利用扩散潜变量的固有频率结构来设计双频带水印框架。低频分量编码全局语义，而高频分量捕获精细纹理。我们利用这一结构在互补频带上嵌入水印。水印通过可学习的编码器独立注入两个频带，在解码前进行重组，从而确保其成为生成轨迹的内在组成部分。双谱解码器从每个频带恢复水印，同时跨频带一致性约束强制语义嵌入与纹理嵌入对齐。实验表明，BiSLW在感知保真度和鲁棒性之间实现了强平衡，相比先前的潜扩散水印方法，PSNR提升超过3 dB，同时在强再生攻击和常见失真下保持近乎完美的比特精度，且计算开销可忽略不计。

</details>


### [42] [EmoteGPT: 3D Human Facial Expressions from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02674)
*Haoran Wang,Mohit Mendiratta,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Txt2Emote基准数据集和EmoteGPT框架，将文本驱动的3D面部表情合成建模为3DMM参数空间中的回归问题，利用多模态大语言模型和<Expr>标记实现细粒度表情控制，在情感识别和表现力上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到3D方法联合生成身份、表情和纹理，难以实现细粒度的表情控制。缺乏将详细语言描述与精确表情参数配对的标注数据，亟需解决这一数据缺口和方法局限。

Method: (1) 构建Txt2Emote基准：利用GPT-4o和高保真人脸追踪器获取精细文本标注与3DMM参数；(2) 提出EmoteGPT框架：基于多模态大语言模型，引入专用<Expr>标记语义化表情表示，再解码为3DMM参数；(3) 利用大规模图像到3DMM数据增强训练。

Result: 在情感识别指标和感知表现力上超越现有最先进的文本到3D人脸合成方法；集成到虚拟人管线后可实现逼真和风格化3D虚拟人，以及表现力丰富的3D一致性2D人脸合成。

Conclusion: 将文本驱动的表情合成转化为解耦参数空间中的回归问题，配合高质量数据集和MLLM框架，实现了从文本到3D面部表情的精确控制，在多种应用场景中表现出色。

Original Abstract: Precise control of 3D facial expressions from text is crucial for virtual avatars, animation, and human-computer interaction, yet existing text-to-3D methods jointly generate identity, expression, and texture, making fine-grained expression control difficult. We instead formulate text-driven expression synthesis as a regression problem in the disentangled parameter space of a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM). This setting, however, requires paired data linking detailed language to precise expression parameters, which are missing from existing resources. To fill this gap, we introduce Txt2Emote, a benchmark of diverse 3D facial expressions with fine-grained textual annotations obtained from GPT-4o and a high-fidelity face tracker, providing both explicit descriptions detailing facial features and implicit descriptions referencing the situational context behind the expression. Leveraging this dataset, we present EmoteGPT, a text-to-3D expression framework based on a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) with a dedicated <Expr> token to semantically ground expression representations, which are then decoded into 3DMM parameters. We further improve EmoteGPT by augmenting training with large-scale image-to-3DMM data, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art text-to-3D face synthesis methods on emotion recognition metrics and in perceived expressiveness. Integrated into avatar pipelines, our method enables photorealistic and stylized 3D avatars, as well as expressive 3D-consistent 2D face synthesis from textual input.

中文摘要翻译: 从文本精确控制3D面部表情对虚拟人、动画和人机交互至关重要，但现有文本到3D方法联合生成身份、表情和纹理，难以实现细粒度的表情控制。本文转而将文本驱动的表情合成建模为3D形变模型（3DMM）解耦参数空间中的回归问题。然而，该设定需要将详细语言描述与精确表情参数配对的数据，而现有资源中缺乏此类数据。为填补这一空白，我们提出Txt2Emote基准，包含来自GPT-4o和高保真人脸追踪器获得的多样化3D面部表情及其细粒度文本标注，既提供描述面部特征的显式描述，也提供引用表情背后情境语境的隐式描述。利用该数据集，我们提出EmoteGPT——一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的文本到3D表情框架，该框架引入专用<Expr>标记以语义化表情表示，并将其解码为3DMM参数。我们进一步通过大规模图像到3DMM数据增强训练来改进EmoteGPT，使其在情感识别指标和感知表现力上超越现有最先进的文本到3D人脸合成方法。集成到虚拟人管线后，我们的方法能够从文本输入实现逼真和风格化的3D虚拟人，以及表现力丰富的3D一致性2D人脸合成。

</details>


### [43] [K9-Bench: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Canine-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02680)
*Khush Attarde,Yusuf Ali,Megha Thukral,Divye Bhutani,Thomas Ploetz,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: K9-Bench是一个面向真实家庭犬视频的多模态基准数据集，包含约5000个QA对和907个视频，覆盖5类任务，用于评估MLLMs在犬类动作与交互理解上的零样本能力。实验表明现有MLLMs在此任务上表现有限，闭源模型优于开源但仍难以进行长程组合推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动物是数百万家庭的重要组成部分，但现有MLLMs在动物中心场景（尤其是犬类）的理解与建模方面研究不足。亟需构建面向宠物任务的基准测试，以评估下一代AI模型在识别 distress 信号、构建响应式机器人伴侣等实际应用中的能力。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的、基于VLM/LLM的数据生成流水线，自动从网络挖掘犬类中心视频，并利用多跳推理机制生成细粒度QA对。同时引入偏差缓解策略消除VLM在数据构建中引入的偏差。数据集包含907个视频、约5000个QA对，覆盖5个任务类别，测试长时序、犬类中心的多模态推理能力。

Result: 前沿MLLMs在犬类中心任务上零样本表现有限。闭源模型优于开源模型，但在涉及细微姿态和交互线索的长程组合推理上表现不佳。通用思维链提示对长程推理的提升仅很有限。

Conclusion: K9-Bench不仅提供了一个犬类活动分析的新数据集，还提供了一套通用数据集构建流水线，可适配其他数据稀缺领域进行定量分析。

Original Abstract: MLLMs have shown strong zero-shot capabilities across diverse inputs such as across images, video, audio, and text. A crucial, yet underexplored, application of these models lies in understanding and modeling animal-centric scenarios. As animals are integral to millions of households, benchmarking next-generation AI models on pet-focused tasks, ranging from recognizing distress signals to enabling responsive robotic companions, is essential for building AI systems that can work alongside us. We introduce K9-Bench, a novel benchmark focused on real-world domestic dog videos, specifically targeting canine action and interaction understanding via approximately 5000 question-answer pairs across 907 videos spanning 5 distinct task categories that test long-form, canine-centric multimodal reasoning in MLLMs. To create this dataset, we propose a scalable, VLM/LLM-powered data generation pipeline that automatically mines canine-centric videos from the web and curates QA pairs requiring fine-grained, multi-hop reasoning over canine actions and temporally extended interaction sequences. We implement bias mitigation strategies designed to eliminate biases introduced by VLMs during dataset curation. Through extensive experimentation, we find that frontier MLLMs exhibit limited zero-shot performance on canine-centric tasks: although state-of-the-art closed-source models outperform open-source counterparts, they still struggle with compositional reasoning over subtle posture and interaction cues spread over long horizons. We observe that generic chain-of-thought prompting provides only modest performance for such long-horizon reasoning. Beyond a novel dataset for canine activity analysis, K9-Bench provides a general-purpose dataset construction pipeline that can be adapted to other low-data domains for quantitative analysis. Our project website is available at: https://ogmenrobotics.github.io/K9Bench.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图像、视频、音频和文本等多种输入上展现了强大的零样本能力。这类模型一个关键但尚未充分探索的应用在于理解和建模以动物为中心的场景。由于动物是数百万家庭的重要组成部分，构建面向宠物任务的下一代AI模型基准测试——从识别 distress 信号到构建响应式机器人伴侣——对于开发能与我们协同工作的AI系统至关重要。本文提出K9-Bench，一个专注于真实家庭犬视频的新型基准数据集，包含约5000个问答对，覆盖907个视频，跨越5个不同任务类别，旨在测试MLLMs在长时序、犬类中心的多模态推理能力。为构建该数据集，我们提出了一种可扩展的、基于VLM/LLM的数据生成流水线，能够自动从网络挖掘犬类中心视频，并生成需要细粒度、多跳推理的QA对，涉及犬类动作及时间上延伸的交互序列。我们实施了偏差缓解策略，以消除VLM在数据集构建过程中引入的偏差。通过大量实验，我们发现前沿MLLMs在犬类中心任务上零样本表现有限：尽管最先进的闭源模型优于开源模型，但它们仍然难以对散布在长时序中的细微姿态和交互线索进行组合推理。我们观察到，通用思维链提示对此类长程推理仅带来微小的性能提升。除了提供用于犬类活动分析的新数据集外，K9-Bench还提供了一套通用数据集构建流水线，可适用于其他数据稀缺领域进行定量分析。我们的项目网站位于：https://ogmenrobotics.github.io/K9Bench。

</details>


### [44] [S-EMBER: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Streaming Egocentric Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02689)
*Xiaodong Wang,Xuanyi Zhao,Pedro Rodriguez,Devendra Singh Sachan,Barlas Oguz,Seungwhan Moon,Shang-Wen Li,Gargi Ghosh,Xin Dong,Wen-Tau Yih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-EMBER是一个大规模流式第一人称视角情景记忆基准数据集（3,141个视频，388小时），提出了从全局离线搜索到因果流式主动回忆的范式转变，并揭示了定位悖论：语义推理随参数规模提升，但时序定位精度是架构性瓶颈，不受模型大小、分辨率或帧密度增长的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准依赖离线评估，能访问完整视频文件，无法模拟可穿戴设备流式处理的实时性，缺乏对因果、主动回忆能力的评测。

Method: 构建S-EMBER基准，包含3,141个通过Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜录制的388小时有机活动视频，提供9,448个QA对，要求通过精确时间定位提供手动视觉证明，支持灵活响应长度，实现从全局离线搜索到流式情景检索的范式转变。

Result: 发现定位悖论：语义推理能力随模型参数规模提升而改善，但时序定位精度是架构性瓶颈，即使增加模型大小、分辨率或帧密度也无法突破。

Conclusion: S-EMBER为下一代可穿戴AI代理建立了硬件真实的基准基础，用于开发可靠的情景记忆能力。

Original Abstract: As wearable devices enable continuous first-person recording, AI assistants must reason across long time horizons to recall past experiences-a capability known as episodic memory. Current benchmarks often rely on offline evaluation with access to entire video files, failing to simulate the streaming reality of wearable intelligence. We introduce S-EMBER (Streaming Egocentric Memory Benchmark for Episodic Retrieval), a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,141 videos totaling 388 hours of organic activity captured via Ray-Ban Meta smart glasses. S-EMBER formalizes grounded streaming episodic retrieval, a paradigm shift from global offline search to causal, active recall triggered by visual events in a continuous stream. We provide 9,448 QA pairs requiring manual visual proof through precise temporal localization and supporting flexible response lengths to simulate natural human-AI interaction. Our extensive benchmarking of frontier models uncovers a localization paradox: while semantic reasoning improves with parameter scale, temporal grounding precision remains a stagnant architectural bottleneck that does not benefit from brute-force increases in model size, resolution, or frame density. S-EMBER establishes a hardware-authentic foundation for developing grounded, reliable episodic memory in the next generation of wearable AI agents.

中文摘要翻译: 随着可穿戴设备实现连续第一人称视角录制，AI助手必须在长时间跨度上进行推理以回忆过去的经历——这种能力被称为情景记忆。现有基准通常依赖离线评估，能够访问完整视频文件，无法模拟可穿戴智能设备的流式实时处理现实。我们提出了S-EMBER（流式情景记忆基准），这是一个大规模基准数据集，包含通过Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜录制的3,141个视频，总计388小时的有机活动。S-EMBER形式化了基于流式情景检索的范式转变，从全局离线搜索转向由连续流中视觉事件触发的因果、主动回忆。我们提供了9,448个问答对，要求通过精确的时间定位提供人工视觉证据，并支持灵活的响应长度以模拟自然的人机交互。我们对前沿模型进行的广泛基准测试揭示了一个定位悖论：虽然语义推理能力随参数规模提升而改善，但时序定位精度仍然是一个停滞的架构性瓶颈，不受模型大小、分辨率或帧密度粗暴增长的影响。S-EMBER为开发下一代可穿戴AI代理中可靠的情景记忆奠定了硬件真实的基准基础。

</details>


### [45] [An Automated Multimodal Glaucoma Detection Framework Using ViT and a Stacking-Based Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02692)
*Ishrat Jahan,Muhammad E. H Chowdhury,Murugappan Murugappan,Kanchon Kanti Podder,Tawsifur Rahman,Shrestha Datta,Md Sakib Abrar Hossain,Md Mosarrof Hossen,Yosra Magdi Salih Mekki,Sanjiban Sekhar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态青光眼检测框架，整合眼底图像和临床数据，在样本级和患者级两种评估设置下均取得优异性能（样本级准确率97.47%，患者级准确率98.97%），并部署为端到端Web平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统青光眼诊断流程耗时且依赖专家经验，难以大规模推广。现有自动化方法多局限于单一模态或独立样本分析，未能充分利用多源数据及患者级聚合信息，因此需要一种可扩展、高精度的多模态自动化筛查方案。

Method: 提出多模态框架，包含样本级和患者级两种评估设置。使用Vision Transformer (ViT)提取眼底图像深度视觉特征，结合临床特征，采用经典机器学习分类器（堆叠集成三个最优分类器）进行分类。患者级设置中，对每个患者的多张眼底图像表示与临床信息进行聚合后预测。

Result: 在PAPILA数据集上：样本级多模态分类准确率97.47%、F1分数97.50%；患者级检测准确率和F1分数均达98.97%。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态框架在青光眼检测任务上表现出色，并已部署为端到端Web平台，可有效支持自动化青光眼筛查和临床决策。

Original Abstract: Glaucoma is a progressive eye disease that can lead to irreversible vision loss if not detected at an early stage. Conventional diagnostic procedures are often time-consuming and rely heavily on expert interpretation, limiting their scalability for large-scale screening. In this study, glaucoma detection is investigated under two evaluation settings: sample-wise, where individual samples are analyzed independently, and patient-wise, where data from each patient are aggregated for final prediction. An automated multimodal framework is proposed that integrates fundus images with clinical data. Under the sample-wise setting, detection is performed using fundus images and clinical features individually, as well as through their multimodal combination. Under the patient-wise setting, predictions are obtained by aggregating multiple fundus image representations with corresponding clinical information for each patient. Deep visual features are extracted using a Vision Transformer (ViT) architecture and classified using classical machine-learning models, with a stacking-based ensemble of the three best-performing classifiers employed to optimize performance. Experiments conducted on the publicly available PAPILA dataset demonstrate strong diagnostic performance, achieving 97.47% accuracy and a 97.50% F1-score for sample-wise multimodal classification, and 98.97% accuracy and F1-score for subject-wise detection. The proposed framework is further deployed as an end-to-end web-based platform to support automated glaucoma screening and clinical decision support.

中文摘要翻译: 青光眼是一种进行性眼部疾病，若未在早期阶段检测，可能导致不可逆的视力丧失。传统的诊断流程通常耗时且高度依赖专家解读，限制了其在大规模筛查中的可扩展性。本研究在两种评估设置下对青光眼检测进行探究：样本级（独立分析单个样本）和患者级（对每个患者的数据进行聚合以得到最终预测）。提出了一种自动化的多模态框架，将眼底图像与临床数据相融合。在样本级设置下，分别利用眼底图像和临床特征进行检测，并通过多模态组合进行检测。在患者级设置下，通过对每个患者的多张眼底图像表示及其对应的临床信息进行聚合来获得预测结果。利用Vision Transformer (ViT)架构提取深度视觉特征，并使用经典机器学习模型进行分类，同时采用基于堆叠集成的方法组合三个性能最优的分类器以优化性能。在公开的PAPILA数据集上进行的实验证明了强大的诊断性能：样本级多模态分类准确率达到97.47%，F1分数为97.50%；患者级检测的准确率和F1分数均达到98.97%。所提出的框架进一步部署为端到端Web平台，以支持自动化青光眼筛查和临床决策支持。

</details>


### [46] [Property-Constrained 3D Porous Media Reconstruction from 2D Images via Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02693)
*Ali Sadeghkhani,Brandon Bennett,Arash Rabbani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）框架，仅用2D薄片图像训练即可生成具有可控孔隙度的3D多孔介质体积，无需昂贵的3D训练数据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统3D多孔介质生成方法依赖昂贵且稀缺的3D训练数据，且难以同时实现属性可控生成与2D到3D的跨维度重建。

Method: 采用3D生成器与2D判别器的混合架构，通过多轴切片提取从2D数据学习3D一致结构；使用增强U-Net分割模型提取孔隙度标签作为条件。

Result: 在白云石-硬石膏和纯白云石两种碳酸盐岩样本上验证：成功生成捕获岩性特征（如硬石膏包裹体、细晶纹理）的逼真3D体积；孔隙度控制R²达0.93，异质和均质样本的平均绝对误差分别为0.019和0.010。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥合了2D训练数据与3D生成之间的鸿沟，实现了对岩石油物属性的精确控制，为3D多孔介质建模提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: This study presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) framework for generating 3D porous media volumes with controlled porosity, trained exclusively on 2D thin section images. The key innovation lies in combining property-conditioned generation with 2D-to-3D reconstruction, eliminating the need for expensive 3D training data while maintaining control over petrophysical properties. The framework employs a hybrid architecture with a 3D generator and 2D discriminator, where multi-axis slice extraction enables learning 3D-consistent structures from 2D training data. Porosity labels are extracted using an Enhanced U-Net segmentation model. The methodology was demonstrated on two carbonate samples with different lithologies: dolomite-anhydrite and pure dolomite. Results show that the framework successfully generates realistic 3D volumes capturing lithological features such as anhydrite inclusions and fine crystalline textures. Porosity control achieved an $R^2$ of 0.93, with mean absolute errors of 0.019 and 0.010 for the heterogeneous and homogeneous samples, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究提出了一种条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）框架，用于生成具有可控孔隙度的三维多孔介质体积，该框架仅使用二维薄片图像进行训练。其关键创新在于将属性条件生成与二维到三维重建相结合，在消除对昂贵三维训练数据需求的同时，保持了对岩石物理属性的控制。该框架采用混合架构，包含三维生成器和二维判别器，通过多轴切片提取从二维训练数据中学习三维一致的结构。孔隙度标签通过增强U-Net分割模型提取。该方法在两种不同岩性的碳酸盐岩样本上进行了验证：白云石-硬石膏和纯白云石。结果表明，该框架成功生成了逼真的三维体积，捕捉到了硬石膏包裹体和细晶纹理等岩性特征。孔隙度控制实现了0.93的R²值，异质和均质样本的平均绝对误差分别为0.019和0.010。

</details>


### [47] [Aircraft Detection in Satellite Imagery using Deep Learning Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02699)
*Mujahir Hussain Abbasi,S. Beghin,A. T. R. Krishna Priya,P. Jamuna,G. Anandhakumari,S. Jaanaa Rubavathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合图像增强和YOLOv11的卫星图像目标检测框架，在航空器检测任务上取得了95% mAP的优异性能，适用于实时监控等场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星图像目标检测面临噪声、图像质量波动和复杂背景等挑战，现有方法检测精度不足，需要一种结合图像预处理与深度学习的框架来提升检测准确性。

Method: 首先使用Gabor滤波器处理输入图像以突出重要特征并降噪，然后进行归一化确保数据均匀分布，最后基于YOLOv11模型快速学习并识别目标特征。

Result: 该方法在航空器检测任务上实现了95%的mAP、97%的精确率、85%的召回率和91%的F1分数，展现出优越的检测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架能够准确识别卫星图像中的航空器，适用于监控、空中交通监测和遥感分析等实时应用场景。

Original Abstract: The object detection in satellite imagery has garnered considerable attention due to its extensive real-world applications and the inherent challenges it presents, including noise, fluctuating image quality, and intricate backgrounds. This paper proposed a framework for object detection that combines image enhancement and Deep Learning (DL) to make detection more accurate. First, a Gabor filter is used to process the input image to bring out important features and reduce noise. Then, normalization is applied to make sure that the data is evenly distributed so that the model works properly. After that, a model based on YOLOv11 is used to quickly learn and find object features. The proposed method achieves a mAP of 95%, precision of 97%, recall of 85%, and F1-score of 91%, which demonstrates the superior aircraft detection performance. These results show the framework accurately identify aircraft in satellite imagery and is suitable for real-time applications such as surveillance, air traffic monitoring and remote sensing analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 卫星图像中的目标检测因其广泛的现实应用以及所面临的固有挑战（包括噪声、图像质量波动和复杂背景）而备受关注。本文提出了一种结合图像增强和深度学习的目标检测框架，以提高检测精度。首先，使用Gabor滤波器处理输入图像，以突出重要特征并减少噪声。然后，应用归一化确保数据均匀分布，从而使模型正常工作。之后，基于YOLOv11的模型被用于快速学习和发现目标特征。所提出的方法实现了95%的平均精度（mAP）、97%的精确率、85%的召回率和91%的F1分数，展现了优越的航空器检测性能。这些结果表明，该框架能够准确识别卫星图像中的航空器，适用于监控、空中交通监测和遥感分析等实时应用场景。

</details>


### [48] [VLRC: Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency as a scalable signal for better feed-forward 3D pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02707)
*Marwane Hariat,David Filliat,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLRC，一种利用冻结的视觉-语言表征作为语义多视图监督信号的辅助目标，无需额外3D标注，通过重投影特征一致性提升前馈3D模型的重建精度和零样本开放词汇语义分割能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的前馈3D模型训练要么依赖昂贵的几何监督，要么依赖自监督光度目标，两者都只能提供不完整的学习信号，缺乏可扩展的语义级监督。

Method: VLRC将预测的3D重建结果中的密集视觉-语言特征重投影到多个视图，强制对应图像位置间的特征一致性，作为辅助目标与现有自监督或监督方法无缝集成。

Result: 在室内和室外基准测试中，VLRC在3D重建精度和零样本开放词汇3D语义分割任务上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过将几何与语言锚定特征对齐，VLRC不仅改进了深度和相机估计，还实现了更连贯的多视图语义融合，推动了开放词汇3D场景理解。

Original Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models are commonly trained using either expensive geometric supervision or self-supervised photometric objectives, both of which provide incomplete learning signals. We introduce Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency (VLRC), a scalable auxiliary objective that exploits frozen vision-language representations as semantic multi-view supervision. Given a predicted 3D reconstruction, VLRC reprojects dense vision-language features across views and enforces feature consistency between corresponding image locations, requiring no additional 3D annotations. The objective integrates seamlessly with both self-supervised monocular reconstruction and supervised-pretrained feed-forward 3D models during unlabeled adaptation. By aligning geometry with language-grounded features, VLRC not only improves depth and camera estimation but also enables more coherent multi-view semantic fusion for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding. Experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in 3D reconstruction accuracy and zero-shot open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 前馈3D模型通常使用昂贵的几何监督或自监督光度目标进行训练，但两者都只能提供不完整的学习信号。我们提出了视觉-语言重投影一致性（VLRC），这是一种可扩展的辅助目标，利用冻结的视觉-语言表征作为语义多视图监督。给定预测的3D重建结果，VLRC将密集的视觉-语言特征跨视图重投影，并强制对应图像位置间的特征一致性，无需额外的3D标注。该目标在无标签自适应过程中，既能与自监督单目重建无缝集成，也能与经过监督预训练的前馈3D模型无缝集成。通过将几何与语言锚定特征对齐，VLRC不仅提升了深度和相机估计，还能为开放词汇的3D场景理解实现更连贯的多视图语义融合。在室内和室外基准上的实验表明，VLRC在3D重建精度和零样本开放词汇3D语义分割方面均取得一致提升。

</details>


### [49] [When Does Resolution Help a Frozen Backbone? Global Attention at Resolution Predicts Scalable Adaptation for Camouflaged and Marine Animal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02708)
*Tyler Rust,Chandra Kambhamettu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了冻结视觉基础模型在细粒度分割中的骨干网络选择问题。发现各向同性ViT（全局注意力+高分辨率token）随分辨率提升持续改进性能，而分层骨干（局部窗口注意力+池化降采样）在较低分辨率即饱和。通过六骨干对照实验和编辑实验确认：池化不影响性能提升，但移除全局注意力会消除收益。提出SALT流水线（侧干、注意力门控U-Net、低秩微调），使用各向同性骨干在伪装、海洋和显著目标数据集上取得SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究冻结视觉基础模型在细粒度分割任务中，骨干网络选择如何影响低秩适配器的性能，以及分辨率与精度之间的转换关系。

Method: 通过六种骨干网络的对照实验建立模式，编辑骨干网络（移除全局注意力/保留池化）进行因果分析，提出SALT流水线：包含侧干（Side-stem）、注意力门控U-Net（Attention-gated U-Net）和低秩微调（Low-rank Tuning），仅使用RGB输入和强各向同性骨干。

Result: 各向同性ViT在全局注意力+高分辨率下持续随分辨率提升；分层骨干在较低分辨率即饱和。池化保留不影响性能，移除全局注意力则消除收益。SALT在各向同性骨干下，在四个数据匹配的伪装集上取得最佳S-measure，在海洋和显著目标数据集上领先，在MAS3K上达到mIoU 0.878的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 细粒度分割中低秩适配的效果取决于骨干网络是否对高分辨率token集应用全局注意力：各向同性骨干因全局注意力而能从分辨率提升中持续获益，分层骨干因早期局部注意力+池化而受限。该效应特异性针对低秩适配。

Original Abstract: Adapting frozen vision foundation models to fine-grained segmentation now largely depends on backbone selection. Whether the backbone applies global attention to a high-resolution token set predicts whether a low-rank adapter turns resolution into accuracy. Isotropic ViTs attend globally over the full grid and keep improving with resolution; hierarchical backbones confine early attention to local windows and pool the grid before their global stages, plateauing at lower resolutions. A controlled six-backbone study establishes the pattern, and editing the backbone points to the cause: pooling keeps the benefit, removing global attention does not. The effect is specific to low-rank adaptation. Under one fixed pipeline, SALT (Side-stem, Attention-gated U-Net, Low-rank Tuning), one RGB-only pass on a strong isotropic backbone wins the best S-measure on the four data-matched camouflaged sets, and leads every marine and salient set. It reaches a new state of the art on both marine-animal benchmarks (MAS3K mIoU 0.878).

中文摘要翻译: 将冻结的视觉基础模型适配到细粒度分割，如今在很大程度上取决于骨干网络的选择。骨干网络是否对高分辨率token集应用全局注意力，决定了低秩适配器能否将分辨率转化为精度。各向同性ViT在整个网格上进行全局注意力，并随分辨率提升持续改进；分层骨干将早期注意力限制在局部窗口内，并在进入全局阶段前对网格进行池化降采样，因此在较低分辨率处即饱和。一项包含六种骨干网络的对照实验确立了这一模式，而对骨干网络的编辑指向了原因：保留池化仍能保持收益，移除全局注意力则无法保持。该效应是低秩适配所特有的。在单一固定流水线SALT（侧干、注意力门控U-Net、低秩微调）下，仅需一次RGB输入，使用强各向同性骨干便在四个数据匹配的伪装集上取得了最佳S-measure，并在所有海洋和显著目标数据集上领先。该方法在两个海洋动物基准（MAS3K mIoU 0.878）上达到了新的最先进水平。

</details>


### [50] [RayTun3R: Online Camera Adaptation in 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02711)
*Daniil Sinitsyn,Nikita Araslanov,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RayTun3R，一种轻量级相机适配方法，通过仅微调位置编码相关的少量参数（10,752个可训练参数），解决3D基础模型在鱼眼相机几何下的性能退化问题，在110°-200°视场角的鱼眼数据集上将旋转误差降低2-12倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D基础模型（如DUSt3R、MASt3R等）在针孔图像上表现优异，但在鱼眼相机几何下性能急剧下降。作者发现这种失败部分源于预训练3D基础模型的位置编码中存在针孔相机偏差，因此需要一种轻量级的适配方法来消除该偏差。

Method: RayTun3R保持预训练网络固定，仅适配与token位置和相机几何相关的轻量组件：(1) 学习参数高效的残差校正，应用于绝对位置编码和旋转位置编码；(2) 使用无参数的token化方法；(3) 对预测网格坐标进行校正以消除残余的针孔假设。总共仅10,752个可训练参数。

Result: 在视场角110°-200°的多种鱼眼数据集上：(1) 旋转误差相比未适配模型降低2-12倍；(2) 优于LoRA且可训练参数少约14倍；(3) 优于无适配的基线方法，同时避免了它们的多视图推理成本；(4) 深度精度上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RayTun3R通过极少量可训练参数即可有效消除3D基础模型在鱼眼相机下的针孔偏差，适配后零额外推理开销，是一种高效、实用的跨相机几何迁移方案。

Original Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models, such as DUSt3R, MASt3R, VGGT, $π^3$, and Depth Anything 3, provide strong feed-forward depth and pose estimates on pinhole imagery, but degrade sharply under fisheye camera geometry. We show that this failure is partly caused by a pinhole camera bias in the positional encodings of pretrained 3D foundation models, and propose RayTun3R, a lightweight camera adaptation approach. It keeps the pretrained network fixed and adapts only lightweight components tied to token position and camera geometry. RayTun3R learns parameter-efficient residual corrections to absolute and rotary positional encodings, together with parameter-free tokenization and corrections to prediction-grid coordinates that remove residual pinhole assumptions. The resulting adapter contains only 10,752 trainable parameters and can be learned from a short temporal segment using geometric losses. Once adapted, RayTun3R transfers effectively to the remaining frames of the sequence without incurring additional runtime costs. Across diverse fisheye datasets with fields of view from $110^\circ$ to $200^\circ$, our adapter reduces rotation error by $2$-$12\times$ relative to the unadapted model, outperforms LoRA while using $\sim\!14\times$ fewer trainable parameters, improves pose over adaptation-free baselines while avoiding their multi-view inference cost, and remains competitive on depth accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的3D基础模型（如DUSt3R、MASt3R、VGGT、$π^3$和Depth Anything 3）在针孔图像上提供了强大的前馈深度和位姿估计，但在鱼眼相机几何下性能急剧下降。我们发现这种失败部分源于预训练3D基础模型的位置编码中存在针孔相机偏差，并提出了RayTun3R，一种轻量级的相机适配方法。该方法保持预训练网络固定，仅适配与token位置和相机几何相关的轻量组件。RayTun3R学习参数高效的残差校正，应用于绝对位置编码和旋转位置编码，同时结合无参数的token化方法以及对预测网格坐标的校正，以消除残余的针孔假设。所得到的适配器仅包含10,752个可训练参数，可以通过短时间段的序列使用几何损失进行学习。一旦适配完成，RayTun3R能有效迁移到序列的其余帧，且不增加额外推理成本。在视场角从110°到200°的多种鱼眼数据集上，我们的适配器相比未适配模型将旋转误差降低了2-12倍，优于LoRA同时使用了约14倍更少的可训练参数，在无适配基线上改善了位姿估计并避免了其多视图推理成本，同时在深度精度上保持竞争力。

</details>


### [51] [Global Pose Control for Generative View Synthesis in Normalized Object Coordinate Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02712)
*Zhibing Li,Amogh Gupta,Behnoosh Parsa,Dan Casas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可定制归一化对象坐标空间（NOCS）的新颖视角合成方法，通过绝对相机位姿控制和多模态条件注入，实现从单张或少量无位姿图像生成高质量、姿态一致的新视图。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有新颖视角合成方法缺乏全局且直观的视角控制能力，要么依赖输入图像的相对相机位姿，要么只能生成稀疏的全局视图，严重限制了NVS在下游任务中的应用。

Method: 1) 将NVS重新定义为图像编辑问题，基于先进编辑模型构建以利用其泛化能力；2) 在可定制的NOCS中仅使用目标视图的绝对相机位姿，无需输入图像的位姿或世界坐标系；3) 通过上下文多模态条件注入策略，以专用相机token形式注入相机信息；4) 引入文本描述定义物体的规范坐标框架以消除NOCS歧义；5) 构建具有一致对齐方向和NOCS文本定义的高质量数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能够从任意无位姿图像中鲁棒地生成具有准确且一致方向的新视图，在各类物体类别上均达到最先进的图像质量和保真度。

Conclusion: 通过将NVS转化为图像编辑问题并利用NOCS的绝对位姿控制，结合多模态条件注入和文本定义，有效解决了现有方法在视角控制上的局限性，实现了高精度、高泛化性的新颖视角合成。

Original Abstract: Novel View Synthesis (NVS) enables the generation of unseen views of a scene from a single or multiple images, allowing users to freely explore an object from any viewpoint. Despite the recent impressive qualitative improvements of generative models for this task, existing methods struggle to provide global and intuitive control of target viewpoints because they either use input-relative camera poses or are limited to generating sparse global views. This lack of global pose control severely limits the number of downstream tasks potentially enabled by NVS. To address this limitation, we propose a novel approach for precise camera control in a customizable Normalized Object Coordinate Space (NOCS), requiring single or few unposed images. Our method operates solely on the absolute camera pose of the target view in NOCS, eliminating the need for a relative world frame or camera poses of the input images. Unlike previous methods that treat NVS as a standalone generation task, we formulate it as an image editing problem and build upon state-of-the-art editing models to leverage their superior generalization capability. Camera information is injected as dedicated camera tokens via an in-context multi-modal conditioning strategy. To alleviate the inherent ambiguity of NOCS, we incorporate text descriptions that explicitly define the object's canonical coordinate frame, which also enhances generalization to unseen object categories. Furthermore, we curate a high-quality dataset with consistently aligned orientations and corresponding NOCS text definitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method robustly generates novel views with accurate and consistent orientations from arbitrary unposed images across diverse categories, achieving state-of-the-art image quality and fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 新颖视角合成（NVS）能够从单张或多张图像生成场景的未见视角，使用户能够从任意视角自由探索物体。尽管近年来生成模型在该任务上取得了令人印象深刻的定性提升，现有方法缺乏全局且直观的目标视角控制能力，因为它们要么使用输入相关的相机位姿，要么局限于生成稀疏的全局视图。这种全局位姿控制的缺失严重限制了NVS可能启发的下游任务数量。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种新颖方法，在可定制的归一化对象坐标空间（NOCS）中实现精确的相机控制，仅需单张或少量无位姿图像。我们的方法仅依赖目标视图在NOCS中的绝对相机位姿，无需相对世界坐标系或输入图像的相机位姿。不同于以往将NVS视为独立生成任务的方法，我们将其表述为图像编辑问题，并基于最先进的编辑模型构建，以利用其卓越的泛化能力。相机信息通过上下文多模态条件注入策略以专用相机token的形式注入。为缓解NOCS固有的歧义性，我们引入文本描述来显式定义物体的规范坐标框架，这也增强了对未见物体类别的泛化能力。此外，我们构建了一个具有一致对齐方向和对应NOCS文本定义的高质量数据集。大量实验表明，我们的方法能够从不同类别的任意无位姿图像中鲁棒地生成具有准确且一致方向的新视图，实现了最先进的图像质量和保真度。

</details>


### [52] [Diagnosing Aerial-View Object Detectors with Foundational Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02718)
*Stanislav Panev,Minhyek Jeon,Vaishnavi Khindkar,Ahish Deshpande,Celso M de Melo,Shuowen Hu,Shayok Chakraborty,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向航拍车辆检测的合成诊断框架，利用文本引导生成和属性控制编辑构建可控测试环境，发现预训练检测器在特定场景下的性能弱点，并据此指导高效数据补充，在三个检测架构和三个真实数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模图像生成模型在航拍和遥感领域作为诊断工具的潜力尚未被探索，现有真实数据集难以隔离评估不同场景和环境条件对检测器性能的影响。

Method: 构建了一个包含文本引导生成、属性控制编辑和自动属性验证的合成诊断框架，生成可控测试集，对预训练检测器进行细粒度评估，识别性能弱点后用少量真实数据定向补充。

Result: 合成场景下的性能趋势与真实世界弱点高度一致；基于诊断结果定向补充少量真实数据，在弱类别上AP50提升最高达13%，且所需样本量远少于非定向数据增强。

Conclusion: 受控的合成探测能够预测真实域的性能差距并指导高效数据收集；所提框架是模块化的，可随生成模型或视觉-语言模型能力的演进而升级。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale image generative models enable photorealistic scene synthesis with controllable attributes. Beyond data augmentation, their potential as diagnostic tools for trained vision systems remains unexplored in the aerial and remote sensing domains. We introduce a synthetic diagnostic framework for aerial-view vehicle detection that combines text-guided generation, attribute-controlled editing, and automated attribute verification to construct a controllable synthetic testbed. This enables fine-grained evaluation of pretrained detectors under diverse scene types and environmental conditions that are difficult to isolate in real datasets. Across three detection architectures and three real aerial datasets, synthetic scene-wise performance trends closely match real-world weaknesses. Guided by these diagnostics, targeted supplementation with small real datasets from the identified weak categories yields improvements of up to 13% AP50 while requiring substantially fewer additional samples than non-targeted augmentation. Our results show that controlled synthetic probing can predict real-domain performance gaps and guide efficient data collection. The proposed diagnostic framework is modular and can incorporate alternative generative or vision-language models as capabilities evolve. Our code and datasets are available here: https://humansensinglab.github.io/AVODDiag/

中文摘要翻译: 大规模图像生成模型的最新进展使得具有可控属性的逼真场景合成成为可能。除了数据增强之外，这些模型作为训练好的视觉系统诊断工具的潜力在航拍和遥感领域尚未被探索。我们提出了一种面向航拍视角车辆检测的合成诊断框架，该框架结合了文本引导生成、属性控制编辑和自动属性验证，构建了一个可控的合成测试平台。这使得能够在真实数据集中难以隔离的多种场景类型和环境条件下，对预训练检测器进行细粒度评估。在三个检测架构和三个真实航拍数据集上，合成场景下的性能趋势与真实世界的弱点高度一致。基于这些诊断结果，从识别出的弱类别中定向补充少量真实数据，在AP50指标上最高可提升13%，同时所需的额外样本量远少于非定向数据增强。我们的结果表明，受控的合成探测能够预测真实域的性能差距，并指导高效的数据收集。所提出的诊断框架是模块化的，可以随着生成模型或视觉-语言模型能力的演进而集成替代方案。我们的代码和数据集可在以下网址获取：https://humansensinglab.github.io/AVODDiag/

</details>


### [53] [GRCD: Grounded Region Change Detection for Multi-Finding Chest X-Ray Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02719)
*OFM Riaz Rahman Aranya,Peyman Najafirad,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRCD框架，用于从胸部X光图像对中生成具有空间定位的多发现随访报告，通过区域引导的变化令牌模块和双路径策略融合时间变化信息，在多项指标上超越现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么仅从单张图像生成报告而不建模时序上下文，要么虽有时序信息但缺乏空间定位，少数结合时序推理与空间定位的方法又局限于单发现描述，无法处理混合变化方向的多发现报告。

Method: 首先通过识别并修正源标注中的两类系统性标注错误，构建了严格清洗的时序胸部X光图像对数据集；然后提出区域引导变化令牌模块，编码各解剖区域跨时间的变化信息，并通过双路径策略（前置空间令牌+门控交叉注意力）注入语言模型。

Result: 在多发现测试集上，GRCD在文本生成和临床准确率指标上均优于现有基线，在变化检测方面取得了显著提升。消融实验证实双路径设计优于任一单独整合策略，且区域级变化编码对多发现生成至关重要。

Conclusion: GRCD通过区域引导的变化编码和双路径注入策略，有效实现了胸部X光对中带空间定位的多发现随访报告生成，填补了该研究方向的空白。

Original Abstract: Radiologists routinely compare current and prior chest X-rays to track disease progression, producing follow-up reports that describe multiple findings, each localised to an anatomical region and annotated with a temporal change status. Existing automated methods either generate reports from a single image without modelling temporal context, or incorporate temporal information but do not ground their outputs spatially. The few approaches that combine temporal reasoning with spatial grounding are restricted to single-finding descriptions, leaving multi-finding reports with mixed change directions unaddressed. We present GRCD, a framework for grounded report generation from chest X-ray pairs in the multi-finding setting. We first construct a rigorously cleaned dataset of temporal chest X-ray pairs by identifying and correcting two systematic labelling errors in the source annotations. We then introduce a Region-Guided Change Token module that encodes per-region temporal change across anatomical structures and injects this signal into a language model through a dual-pathway strategy combining prepended spatial tokens with gated cross-attention. On a multi-finding test set, GRCD outperforms existing baselines on text generation and clinical accuracy metrics, with gains in change detection. Ablation studies confirm that the dual-pathway design outperforms either integration strategy in isolation on text and clinical metrics, and that region-level change encoding is necessary for multi-finding generation. Code is available at https://github.com/UTSA-VIRLab/GRCD

中文摘要翻译: 放射科医生通常会比较当前和先前的胸部X光片来追踪疾病进展，生成描述多个发现的随访报告，每个发现都定位到解剖区域并标注了时间变化状态。现有的自动化方法要么从单张图像生成报告而不建模时序上下文，要么整合了时序信息但未在空间上对输出进行定位。少数将时序推理与空间定位结合的方法局限于单发现描述，无法处理混合变化方向的多发现报告。本文提出了GRCD，一个用于在多发观设定下从胸部X光片对生成空间定位报告的框架。我们首先通过识别并修正源标注中的两类系统性标注错误，构建了严格清洗的时序胸部X光片对数据集。然后我们引入了一个区域引导变化令牌模块，该模块编码跨解剖结构的每区域时间变化，并通过一种结合前置空间令牌与门控交叉注意力的双路径策略将此信号注入语言模型。在多发观测试集上，GRCD在文本生成和临床准确率指标上均优于现有基线，并在变化检测方面取得了显著提升。消融研究证实，双路径设计在文本和临床指标上均优于任一单独整合策略，并且区域级变化编码对于多发现生成是必要的。代码可在 https://github.com/UTSA-VIRLab/GRCD 获取。

</details>


### [54] [Provable Pruning for Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting via Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02721)
*Waseem Mousa,Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定渲染分辨率下3D高斯泼溅场景可以被可证明地压缩为加权子集（coreset），并提出基于敏感度采样的重要性估计方法，在极低微调或无微调场景下达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法大多是启发式的，缺乏对渲染目标的多乘性近似保证，严重依赖昂贵的后剪枝微调来恢复质量。本文旨在回答一个基本问题：3DGS场景能否被可证明地替换为更小的加权子集，同时保留目标函数的质量。

Method: (1) 理论上证明无限制条件下不存在非平凡乘性3DGS coreset，但渲染分辨率依赖条件下存在；(2) 提出首个针对3DGS的加权coreset构造定理，通过敏感度（sensitivity）采样高斯，即计算每个高斯在全场景目标中的可证明重要性分数；(3) 在显式有效性和对数透射率稳定性假设下，将目标保证转化为渲染保证。

Result: 在仅剪枝和极短微调场景下达到SOTA性能，表明原则性的重要性估计既有理论意义又有实用价值。

Conclusion: 基于敏感度采样的可证明压缩方法在激进压缩且无/极少恢复计算时表现最强，将理论保证与实际部署需求相结合。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality real-time novel-view synthesis, but practical scenes often contain millions of Gaussians, making compression essential for deployment on limited hardware. Existing reduction methods are effective but mostly heuristic: they provide no multiplicative approximation guarantee for the rendered objective, and thus rely heavily on costly post-pruning finetuning to recover quality. We ask a basic question: can a 3DGS scene be provably replaced by a much smaller weighted subset (coreset) while preserving the objective of interest? We first show that, in the unrestricted setting, no non-trivial multiplicative 3DGS coreset exists. We then show that multiplicative guarantees are not impossible, but resolution-dependent. For a prescribed rendering resolution, such as representative views or grids of views/rays, we provide the first weighted coreset construction theorem for 3DGS. The construction samples Gaussians by sensitivity: provable importance scores measuring each Gaussian's role in the full-scene objective. Finally, under explicit validity and log-transmittance stability assumptions, we turn this objective guarantee into a rendering guarantee. Empirically, our method is strongest where deployment needs it most: aggressive compression with no or minimal recovery compute. In prune-only and very short finetuning regimes, it achieves state-of-the-art performance, showing that principled importance estimation can be both theoretically meaningful and practically useful. Open-source code is available at https://github.com/waseem-m/3dgs_provable_coresets.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）能够实现高质量实时新视角合成，但实际场景通常包含数百万个高斯体，因此压缩对于在有限硬件上部署至关重要。现有的缩减方法虽然有效，但大多是启发式的：它们对渲染目标不提供多乘性近似保证，因此严重依赖昂贵的后剪枝微调来恢复质量。我们提出一个基本问题：3DGS场景能否被可证明地替换为更小的加权子集（coreset），同时保留感兴趣的目标函数？我们首先证明，在无限制条件下，不存在非平凡的乘性3DGS coreset。然后我们证明，乘性保证并非不可能，而是依赖于分辨率。对于预设的渲染分辨率（如代表性视图或视图/光线网格），我们提供了首个针对3DGS的加权coreset构造定理。该构造通过敏感度（sensitivity）来采样高斯体：可证明的重要性分数，用于衡量每个高斯体在全场景目标函数中的作用。最后，在显式有效性和对数透射率稳定性假设下，我们将这种目标保证转化为渲染保证。实验上，我们的方法在部署最需要的地方表现最强：激进压缩且无或极少恢复计算。在仅剪枝和极短微调场景下，该方法达到了SOTA性能，表明原则性的重要性估计既有理论意义又有实用价值。

</details>


### [55] [Signal from Space: Detecting Schools and Towers to Bridge the Digital Divide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02724)
*Zakarya Elmimouni,Sandor Farkas,Fares Fourati,Vladimir Daigele,Walid Mathlouthi,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖高分辨率卫星影像的视觉框架，通过迁移学习同时检测学校和信号塔，并分析两者空间关系以评估网络连接可用性，从而为学校联网计划（Giga Initiative）提供可扩展的解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球仍有大量学龄儿童因学校未联网而无法接入互联网，尤其缺乏可靠的三方数据集来评估学校及周边基础设施。现有方法依赖稀疏或噪声大的数据，难以大规模高效完成学校位置映射和连接性评估。

Method: 提出一套纯视觉、可扩展的框架，利用高分辨率卫星影像和迁移学习，在标注数据极少的情况下同时检测学校和信号塔。通过分析检测到的学校与附近信号塔的空间距离，作为连接可用性的代理指标。

Result: 在莱索托地区的真实卫星影像上验证，该方法在区域范围内展现出强性能，证明纯影像驱动的管线可用于大规模基础设施映射。

Conclusion: 该纯影像驱动的框架可减少对辅助数据的依赖，支持数据驱动的连接投资优先级排序，助力在服务不足区域优先部署网络连接。

Original Abstract: Reliable internet access is essential for modern education, yet millions of school-aged children especially in developing regions remain offline due to unconnected schools. The Giga Initiative aims to connect every school to the internet, but doing so at scale requires efficient methods to map schools and assess surrounding connectivity infrastructure without relying on sparse or noisy third-party datasets. In this work, we propose a scalable, vision-only framework that uses high-resolution satellite imagery and transfer learning to address both tasks simultaneously. By adapting pre-trained object detection models to new geographical regions with minimal labeled data, we detect schools and cell towers directly from space. We then analyze the spatial relationship between detected schools and nearby towers as a proxy for connectivity availability. This purely imagery-driven pipeline enables large-scale infrastructure mapping, reduces dependency on auxiliary data, and supports data-driven prioritization of connectivity investments in underserved areas. Our approach is demonstrated on real satellite imagery from Lesotho, showing strong performance across this region.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的互联网接入对现代教育至关重要，然而全球仍有数百万学龄儿童——尤其是在发展中地区——因学校未联网而无法上网。Giga Initiative 旨在连接每一所学校，但要大规模实现这一目标，就需要在不依赖稀疏或含噪音的第三方数据集的前提下，高效地绘制学校地图并评估周边连接基础设施。本文提出一个可扩展的纯视觉框架，利用高分辨率卫星影像和迁移学习同时解决上述两个任务。通过将预训练的目标检测模型迁移到新的地理区域，仅需极少量标注数据即可直接从卫星影像中检测出学校和信号塔。随后，我们分析检测到的学校与附近信号塔之间的空间关系，作为连接可用性的代理指标。这一纯影像驱动的管线能够实现大规模基础设施映射，减少对辅助数据的依赖，并支持在服务不足区域进行数据驱动的连接投资优先级排序。该方法在莱索托的真实卫星影像上进行了验证，展现了该区域内的强劲性能。

</details>


### [56] [Biomechanics-aware Multi-view Markerless Motion Capture of Dexterous Hand Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02796)
*Pouyan Firouzabadi,J. D. Peiffer,Kunal Shah,Anton Sobinov,Lee E. Miller,R. James Cotton,Wendy M. Murray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于梯度优化的生物力学重建方法，用于手部无标记运动捕捉（MMC）。与传统两步法相比，该方法在121个视频记录中全部收敛，而两步法在15%的记录中失败，且新方法在手部运动学追踪上更符合生物力学原理，对遮挡更鲁棒。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管无标记运动捕捉技术在人体运动生物力学分析中广泛应用，但在复杂手部运动中的应用仍落后于其他肌肉骨骼系统。现有两步法（先三角测量3D关键点，再求解约束逆运动学）存在收敛失败和对遮挡敏感的问题。因此，本研究旨在评估一种将基于梯度的优化方法与生物力学模型相结合的端到端重建方法，以实现对手部灵巧、无约束运动的准确追踪。

Method: 采用定制8相机系统，采集6名参与者执行11种任务（涵盖6种手部姿态、5种物体操作任务）的121个视频记录。提出一种将梯度优化方法与生物力学模型耦合的MMC管道（端到端方法），并与传统两步法（先三角测量2D关键点到3D，再通过约束逆运动学施加生物力学约束）进行直接对比。通过定性视觉检查和计算机视觉指标进行定量评估。

Result: 1）提出的方法在所有121个视频记录中均成功生成解，而两步法在15%的记录中未能收敛。2）在剩余视频中，提出的方法比两步法生成更符合生物力学的手部运动学结果。3）在涉及物体的任务中，提出的方法对遮挡效应更鲁棒。端到端方法能更有效地利用可用的2D数字关键点信息。

Conclusion: 基于梯度的优化结合生物力学模型的端到端MMC方法在自动追踪灵巧手部运动方面优于传统两步法。该方法具有支持临床评估、康复监测和人类运动控制研究的潜力。

Original Abstract: Markerless motion capture (MMC) techniques have been widely beneficial in biomechanical analysis of human movement; however, application to complex motions of the hand lags other musculoskeletal systems. The primary goal of this study was to evaluate the performance of a biomechanical reconstruction method that implements a gradient-based optimization approach with a biomechanical model in the loop for tracking dexterous, unconstrained hand movements using MMC. Using a custom, 8-camera setup, we acquired 121 video recordings from 6 participants performing 11 different tasks that spanned 6 hand postures, 5 object manipulation tasks, and involved motion of the proximal upper limb joints. Performance of the proposed MMC pipeline was directly compared to a more commonly adopted two-stage reconstruction method that first triangulates 2D keypoints from computer vision pose estimation algorithms to 3D and then enforces biomechanical constraints by solving a constrained inverse kinematics problem. Relative performance was assessed qualitatively by visual inspection and quantitatively using a computer vision metric. Our method generated solutions for all 121 video recordings; the two-stage method did not converge for 15% of the recordings. Across the remaining videos, our method produced more biomechanically plausible hand kinematics than the two-stage method and was more robust to occlusion effects during tasks that involved objects. The relative robustness of the end-to-end method suggests that it is more effective in utilizing the available 2D digital keypoint information. Automatic and biomechanically meaningful tracking of hand kinematics during dexterous movements has the potential to support clinical evaluation, rehabilitation monitoring, and studies of human motor control.

中文摘要翻译: 无标记运动捕捉（MMC）技术在人体运动的生物力学分析中已广泛应用；然而，其在复杂手部运动中的应用仍落后于其他肌肉骨骼系统。本研究的主要目标是评估一种生物力学重建方法的性能，该方法采用基于梯度的优化方式，并将生物力学模型置于循环中以实现使用MMC对灵巧、无约束手部运动的追踪。通过定制的8相机装置，我们采集了6名参与者执行11种不同任务的121个视频记录，这些任务涵盖6种手部姿态、5种物体操作任务，并涉及近端上肢关节的运动。我们将所提出的MMC管道的性能与一种更常用的两步重建方法进行了直接比较，后者首先通过计算机视觉姿态估计算法将2D关键点三角测量到3D，然后通过求解约束逆运动学问题来施加生物力学约束。通过定性视觉检查和计算机视觉指标对相对性能进行定量评估。我们的方法为所有121个视频记录生成了解；两步法在15%的记录中未能收敛。在剩余视频中，我们的方法比两步法生成了更符合生物力学的手部运动学结果，并且在涉及物体的任务中对遮挡效应更鲁棒。端到端方法的相对鲁棒性表明它能更有效地利用可用的2D数字关键点信息。在灵巧运动过程中自动且生物力学上有意义地追踪手部运动学，具有支持临床评估、康复监测和人类运动控制研究的潜力。

</details>


### [57] [Unsupervised Detection of Underground Tunnels in Ground-Penetrating Radar Using Depth-Restricted Reconstruction Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04882)
*Muhammad Junaid,Shoab A. Khan,Nisar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种完全无监督的管道下方秘密隧道检测方法，利用去噪卷积自编码器仅训练正常探地雷达数据，通过深度受限的top-k异常分数实现高精度检测，无需任何隧道标签。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统管道监控只能在破坏发生后检测到隧道，人工雷达图解释无法规模化，且监督检测器因实际隧道样本稀缺而难以应用，亟需一种无需隧道标签的无监督检测方案。

Method: 使用去噪卷积自编码器在无异常的探地雷达数据上训练，推理时通过重建误差检测异常；核心创新是提出深度受限的top-k异常分数，仅考虑隧道可能出现的深度范围内最高的重建误差进行异常判定。

Result: 在包含3条地下隧道（1.5-3米深）的实地测试集上，AUC从0.986提升至0.994，漏检从74降至17个（共634个隧道窗口）；最终系统在1600个测试窗口上达到AUC 0.994、F1 0.975、召回率0.973、精确率0.976，虚警率仅1.6%。

Conclusion: 基于物理先验的深度受限top-k异常分数能显著提升无监督隧道检测性能，且无需重新训练或标签；空间投票在弱检测器上有帮助，但强评分规则下无额外收益。

Original Abstract: Clandestine tunneling beneath oil and gas pipelines enables fuel theft, smuggling, and sabotage, yet conventional monitoring detects damage only after a pipeline has been compromised. Ground-penetrating radar (GPR) can image such tunnels non-invasively, but manual radargram interpretation does not scale to continuous corridor surveillance, and supervised detectors require tunnel examples that are scarce in practice. We present a fully unsupervised detection pipeline trained exclusively on normal subsurface radargrams collected at a purpose-built field site containing three buried tunnels at 1.5-3 m depth. A denoising convolutional autoencoder learns the structure of anomaly-free ground; at inference, tunnels are flagged by reconstruction error. Our central contribution is a depth-restricted top-k anomaly score, which pools the highest reconstruction errors only within the depth band where tunnels can physically occur. This physically motivated rule raises AUC from 0.986 to 0.994 and cuts missed detections from 74 to 17 of 634 tunnel windows, relative to whole-image scoring, without any retraining or labels.
