We further show that the optimal top-k fraction interacts with the depth restriction - 1% pooling is best on full images, 5% once scoring is depth-restricted - and that spatial voting across overlapping survey windows helps weak per-image detectors but offers no benefit once the scoring rule is strong. The final system attains AUC 0.994, F1 0.975, recall 0.973, and precision 0.976 on 1,600 field test windows spanning 55 survey lines, at a 1.6% false-alarm rate, using no tunnel labels for training, scoring, or threshold calibration.

中文摘要翻译: 输油气管线下方秘密隧道的挖掘可导致燃料盗窃、走私和破坏活动，然而传统监测手段只能在管道被破坏后才能检测到损害。探地雷达能够非侵入性地对这种隧道成像，但人工雷达图解释无法扩展到连续走廊监控，有监督检测器则需要实际中稀缺的隧道样本。我们提出了一种完全无监督的检测流程，专门在目标建造的试验场地收集的正常地下雷达图上训练，该场地包含三条埋深1.5-3米的人工隧道。去噪卷积自编码器学习无异常地层的结构；推理时通过重建误差标记隧道。我们的核心贡献是深度受限的top-k异常分数，该分数仅在隧道可能出现的深度范围内聚合最高的重建误差。相比全图评分，这一基于物理先验的规则将AUC从0.986提升至0.994，并在634个隧道窗口中漏检数从74降至17，无需任何重新训练或标签。我们进一步揭示：最优top-k比例与深度限制存在交互——全图评分时1%池化最佳，深度受限后5%最佳；跨重叠扫描窗口的空间投票有助于弱单图检测器，但一旦评分规则足够强则无额外收益。最终系统在涵盖55条测线的1,600个现场测试窗口上，以1.6%的虚警率实现了AUC 0.994、F1 0.975、召回率0.973、精确率0.976，且训练、评分和阈值校准均未使用任何隧道标签。

</details>


### [58] [Track the Noise, Move the World:3D-Grounded Motion-Consistent Noise for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02798)
*Long Vu,Tan Ngo,Animesh Karnewar,Amir Habibian,Binh-Son Hua,Hung Bui,Minh Hoai Nguyen,Phong Nguyen-Ha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCaMo 是一个统一框架，通过在扩散模型的输入噪声空间中结合局部点跟踪噪声扭曲（控制物体运动）与全局球面噪声采样（控制相机运动），实现对视频中物体轨迹和相机视角的联合精确控制，无需修改模型架构或添加适配器，在 Wan 2.1（14B）等大模型上以轻量 LoRA 微调达到 SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像-文本到视频扩散模型在合成高质量视频方面表现出色，但缺乏对物体运动和相机运动在同一生成过程中的精确且统一的控制能力。

Method: UniCaMo 通过直接在扩散模型的输入噪声中构建统一的 3D 运动一致噪声空间：利用稀疏 3D 点轨迹沿目标物体运动轨迹扭曲参考帧的高斯噪声（局部跟踪引导噪声扭曲），同时使用虚拟球面噪声表示为相机运动下新出现的场景区域提供全局一致的噪声值（全局球面噪声采样），二者结合以保持几何和时间一致性。该方法仅修改输入噪声，无需辅助适配器、控制分支或架构修改，仅需轻量 LoRA 微调。

Result: 在 Wan 2.1（14B）等大规模预训练视频扩散模型上，以轻量 LoRA 微调即可在标准可控视频生成基准上实现视频质量和运动可控性方面的 SOTA 结果。

Conclusion: UniCaMo 通过在噪声空间层面实现物体轨迹与相机视角的联合控制，为视频扩散模型提供了统一、精确且无需修改架构的可控生成方案。

Original Abstract: Modern image-and-text-to-video diffusion models can synthesize highly realistic videos by iteratively denoising an initial Gaussian noise tensor conditioned on reference image and text inputs. However, existing approaches still lack precise and unified controllability over both object motion and camera motion within a single generation process. We present UniCaMo, a unified framework that enables simultaneous control of object trajectories and camera viewpoints by directly constructing the input noise of the diffusion model. Specifically, UniCaMo builds a shared 3D-grounded motion-consistent noise space across latent video frames. Sparse 3D point tracks are used to warp the Gaussian noise of the reference frame along desired object trajectories, while a virtual spherical noise representation provides globally consistent noise values for newly revealed scene regions under camera motion. By combining local track-guided noise warping with global sphere-based noise sampling, UniCaMo maintains geometric and temporal consistency under both object movement and viewpoint changes. Because UniCaMo modifies only the input noise, it requires no auxiliary adapters, control branches, or architectural changes to the underlying video diffusion model. With lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on large pretrained video diffusion models, including Wan 2.1 (14B), UniCaMo achieves state-of-the-art results in both video quality and motion controllability on standard controllable video generation benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像-文本到视频扩散模型能够通过在给定参考图像和文本输入的条件下，对初始高斯噪声张量进行迭代去噪，从而合成高度逼真的视频。然而，现有方法在单一生成过程中仍缺乏对物体运动和相机运动的精确统一控制。我们提出 UniCaMo，一个统一的框架，通过直接构造扩散模型的输入噪声，实现对物体轨迹和相机视角的同时控制。具体而言，UniCaMo 在视频帧的潜在空间之间构建了一个共享的、基于 3D 场景的运动一致噪声空间。利用稀疏 3D 点轨迹沿期望的物体运动路径扭曲参考帧的高斯噪声，同时采用虚拟球面噪声表示为相机运动下新出现的场景区域提供全局一致的噪声值。通过将局部轨迹引导的噪声扭曲与全局球面噪声采样相结合，UniCaMo 在物体运动和视角变化下保持了几何一致性和时间一致性。由于 UniCaMo 仅修改输入噪声，因此不需要额外的适配器、控制分支或对底层视频扩散模型进行架构修改。通过在包括 Wan 2.1（14B）在内的大规模预训练视频扩散模型上进行轻量级 LoRA 微调，UniCaMo 在标准可控视频生成基准上实现了视频质量和运动可控性方面的最先进结果。

</details>


### [59] [Conversational Human Audio-visual Talking Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02799)
*Junhao Song,Lluis Guasch,Xilin He,Zhongyu Yang,Yingfang Yuan,Weicheng Xie,Linlin Shen,Haijun Lin,Shizhe Liu,Wei Pang,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHAT是一个从单文本提示生成配对、互响应的音视频对话片段的框架，可替代昂贵且敏感的真人对话数据采集，实验表明其优于现有方法，且生成的数据集能有效提升下游交互头部生成模型的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模双人交互视听对话数据集的采集耗时、昂贵且涉及伦理敏感问题，亟需一种可扩展的合成数据生成方法替代真人数据采集。

Method: CHAT框架统一了大语言模型和说话人脸模型，并引入交互式音频与人脸行为精炼模块，实现从单一文本提示生成内容多样、身份可变且对齐的双人对话视频片段。

Result: 客观和主观评估下CHAT均优于现有方法；合成的CHAT-AVD-50k数据集作为下游交互头部生成任务的预训练数据，在REACT 2024上持续提升PerFRDiff和ReactDiff的性能。

Conclusion: CHAT为真人双人交互数据采集提供了一种可扩展的低成本替代方案，有效解决了数据获取中的伦理和成本难题。

Original Abstract: Large-scale dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue (DIAD) datasets provide fundamental data resources for developing humanoid interactive virtual agents and digital humans. However, collecting such data is time-consuming, expensive, and ethically sensitive. To address this, we propose CHAT, a new dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue generation (DIADG) framework that generates diverse, paired, and mutually responsive speech-face dialogue clips from a single textual prompt. CHAT unifies large language models and talking face models with interactive audio and facial behaviour refinement modules, enabling the generation of aligned dyadic dialogue clips with diverse contents and facial identities. Experiments show that CHAT outperforms existing related methods designed for similar tasks under both objective and subjective evaluations. Moreover, our synthesised CHAT-AVD-50k dataset serves as effective pre-training data for downstream interactive head generation, consistently improving PerFRDiff and ReactDiff on REACT 2024. CHAT offers a scalable alternative to the costly and ethically sensitive collection of real dyadic interaction data.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模双人交互视听对话数据集为开发类人交互虚拟智能体和数字人提供了基础数据资源。然而，采集此类数据耗时、昂贵且涉及伦理敏感问题。为此，我们提出CHAT，一种新型双人交互视听对话生成框架，能够从单一文本提示生成多样化、成对且相互响应的语音-人脸对话片段。CHAT统一了大语言模型和说话人脸模型，并配有交互式音频与人脸行为精炼模块，从而能够生成内容多样、人脸身份可变的对齐双人对话片段。实验表明，在客观和主观评估下，CHAT均优于为类似任务设计的现有相关方法。此外，我们合成的CHAT-AVD-50k数据集可作为下游交互头部生成任务的有效预训练数据，在REACT 2024上持续提升PerFRDiff和ReactDiff的性能。CHAT为成本高昂且涉及伦理敏感的真人双人交互数据采集提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。

</details>


### [60] [Prior Bias in Vision Language Models on UML Diagram Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02853)
*Zaiyu Cheng,Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了先验冲突的UML类图基准，发现视觉语言模型（VLMs）在关系箭头反转时方向准确率平均下降33.48%，揭示了模型依赖预训练先验而非真正视觉理解的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究将VLMs应用于UML类图理解，但无法判断模型是真正读取图示中的关系箭头，还是仅依赖关于类之间典型关系的预训练先验知识作答。

Method: 设计一个受控的UML基准测试，将每个符合先验的类图与对应的先验冲突版本配对——保持类名和布局不变，仅反转关系箭头方向。评估了8个开源VLM（InternVL3.5、Qwen3系列）和2个闭源前沿模型（GPT-5.4、GPT-5.4 Mini）。

Result: 开源模型在箭头反转后关系方向准确率平均下降33.48%，GPT-5.4 Mini仍有10%的差距；在三类条件更难的场景下，开源模型精度骤降45.28%，GPT-5.4系列平均下降18.62%；扩展模型规模仅带来有限的、依赖于模型家族的改善。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在理解UML类图时存在先验驱动的失败模式，模型并未真正进行图示接地（diagram-grounded）的理解，而更依赖预训练知识。

Original Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to software engineering artifacts, especially UML class diagrams whose meaning depends on visual notation. Yet, it is unclear whether VLMs actually read such diagrams or instead answer from pretrained priors about how classes typically relate. We introduce a controlled UML benchmark in which each prior-conforming diagram is paired with its prior-conflicting counterpart that (1) preserves the same class names and layout while (2) reverses only the relation arrow. We evaluate eight open-source VLMs from two model families, InternVL3.5 and Qwen3, alongside two closed-source frontier models GPT-5.4 and GPT-5.4 Mini. Across the eight open-source models, reversing the arrow reduces relation-direction accuracy by 33.48% on average, while GPT-5.4 Mini retains a 10% gap. In the harder three-class condition, accuracy drops sharply by 45.28% for open-source models, and even 18.62% for the GPT-5.4 family on average. Scaling provides only limited improvements and is family-dependent. Our benchmark presents a diagnostic prior-driven failure in diagram-grounded software understanding. Our artifact is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UMLKnowledgeConflict-8461.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地被应用于软件工程制品，尤其是其含义依赖于视觉符号的UML类图。然而，目前尚不清楚VLMs是真正读取了这些图示，还是仅仅根据关于类之间通常如何关联的预训练先验知识来作答。我们引入了一个受控的UML基准测试，其中每个符合先验的类图都与对应的先验冲突版本配对——该版本在（1）保持相同类名和布局的同时，（2）仅反转关系箭头的方向。我们评估了来自两个模型家族（InternVL3.5和Qwen3）的八个开源VLM，以及两个闭源前沿模型GPT-5.4和GPT-5.4 Mini。在八个开源模型中，反转箭头导致关系方向准确率平均下降33.48%，而GPT-5.4 Mini仍有10%的差距。在更困难的三类条件下，开源模型精度骤降45.28%，即使是GPT-5.4家族平均也下降了18.62%。扩展模型规模仅带来有限的改善，且改善程度依赖于模型家族。我们的基准测试揭示了在基于图示的软件理解中存在一种先验驱动的失败模式。我们的工件可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UMLKnowledgeConflict-8461获取。

</details>


### [61] [Cancelable Biometric Template Protection Based on Multi-Instance Fusion: A Contralateral Iris Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02860)
*Jittarin Chaivong,Nicha Vikromrotjananan,Teekatat Piriyapittaya,Waree Kongprawechnon,Suradej Duangpummet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需外部令牌的、基于对侧虹膜融合的可撤销模板保护方案，满足ISO/IEC 24745标准，通过多数投票融合、基于ID的盐值位置换和左右虹膜异或融合实现高安全性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的可撤销生物特征方案大多需要外部密钥或令牌，引入了额外的攻击面；而虹膜一旦泄露无法重新发放，因此需要一种无需存储密钥、满足不可逆性、不可关联性和机密性三大要求的虹膜模板保护方案。

Method: 1) 每只眼睛采集三个注册样本，通过多数投票融合（Majority Vote Fusion）生成稳定模板；2) 基于用户注册ID生成盐值，对模板进行位置换；3) 将左右眼置换后的模板进行逐位异或（XOR）融合，生成单一的保护融合模板。由于左右虹膜统计独立，融合提升了精度，且攻击者需同时获取两个虹膜码和两个盐值才能恢复信息。

Result: 在CASIA-IrisV4-Interval、CASIA-IrisV2（两个设备）和CASIA-Iris-Thousand数据集上，EER分别为0.36%、4.88%、10.80%和3.35%，其中最高值对应更具挑战性的跨设备场景。方法优于未保护基线，与最先进的可撤销方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 对侧虹膜融合的可撤销模板方案在无需外部令牌的前提下满足ISO/IEC 24745标准，在识别性能和安全方面均表现优异；这是首个将无令牌多实例融合与对侧绑定相结合的方案。

Original Abstract: Biometric templates are vulnerable to theft if stored without protection. Unlike passwords, a compromised iris cannot be reissued. Although existing cancelable biometric schemes address this problem, most still require an external key or token, introducing an additional attack surface. This paper proposes a cancelable contralateral iris template protection scheme that eliminates the need for a separate token or stored secret, satisfying the three requirements of ISO/IEC 24745: irreversibility, unlinkability, and confidentiality. The method fuses three enrollment samples per eye using Majority Vote Fusion to produce a stable template, and applies a salt-based bit permutation derived from the subject's enrollment ID. Combining the left- and right-permuted templates via a bitwise XOR produces a single Protected Fused Template. Since left and right iris patterns are statistically independent, fusing contralateral irises improves the accuracy of the system. An attacker must possess both iris codes and both salts to recover any useful information, yielding a larger effective key space than single-iris schemes. Experiments on three datasets, CASIA-IrisV4-Interval, CASIA-IrisV2 (two devices), and CASIA-Iris-Thousand, yield EERs of $0.36$\%, $4.88$\%, $10.80$\%, and $3.35$\%, respectively; the highest value reflects the more challenging cross-device scenario. These results demonstrate that our contralateral approach outperforms unprotected baselines while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art cancelable methods. %In addition, an ablation study confirms the benefit on both recognition performance and security. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first scheme to combine tokenless multi-instance fusion and contralateral binding under ISO/IEC 24745.

中文摘要翻译: 生物特征模板若未经保护存储，极易被盗。与密码不同，受损的虹膜无法重新发放。尽管现有的可撤销生物特征方案解决了这一问题，但大多数仍需外部密钥或令牌，引入了额外的攻击面。本文提出了一种无需单独令牌或存储密钥的可撤销对侧虹膜模板保护方案，满足ISO/IEC 24745标准的三大要求：不可逆性、不可关联性和机密性。该方法对每只眼睛的三个注册样本采用多数投票融合（Majority Vote Fusion）生成稳定模板，并应用基于注册ID的盐值位置换。通过逐位异或（XOR）将左右眼置换后的模板融合，生成单一的保护融合模板。由于左右虹膜模式统计独立，对侧虹膜融合提升了系统精度。攻击者必须同时获取两个虹膜码和两个盐值才能恢复任何有用信息，相比单虹膜方案产生了更大的有效密钥空间。在CASIA-IrisV4-Interval、CASIA-IrisV2（两个设备）和CASIA-Iris-Thousand三个数据集上的实验结果表明，等错误率（EER）分别为0.36%、4.88%、10.80%和3.35%；其中最高值反映更具挑战性的跨设备场景。这些结果表明，我们的对侧方法优于未保护基线，同时与最先进的可撤销方法保持竞争力。据我们所知，这是首个在ISO/IEC 24745标准下将无令牌多实例融合与对侧绑定相结合的方案。

</details>


### [62] [E-TraMamba: A New Paradigm for Efficient Long-Term 3D Feature Tracking with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02866)
*Juwei Shen,Yujie Wu,Changwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E-TraMamba是首个基于Mamba框架的事件数据3D特征跟踪器，采用线性状态空间模型实现高效长程建模，结合轻量级仿射变换预测器，在稀疏、噪声事件流中实现了超过2倍的特征寿命和SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CNN和Transformer的跟踪器难以在稀疏、噪声事件流中捕捉长程时空依赖关系，尤其是在实时性和效率约束下表现不佳。

Method: 1) 采用Mamba（线性状态空间模型）进行高效长程建模；2) 集成轻量级仿射变换预测器以应对运动模糊和遮挡；3) 设计多尺度线索融合方案（局部时空patch、相关图、位置嵌入），生成统一表示实现稳定3D跟踪；4) 构建大规模合成数据集EvD-PointOdyssey用于训练和评估。

Result: 在严格精度阈值（如0.1m）下，特征寿命超过基线2倍以上，跟踪特征比率更高，RMSE更低，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: E-TraMamba可作为低延迟视觉里程计、实时SLAM和交互式机器人的强有力候选方案。

Original Abstract: Event-based 3D tracking enables low-latency and high-speed perception, while existing CNN- and Transformer-based trackers struggle to capture long-range spatiotemporal dependencies in sparse, noisy event streams, especially under real-time and efficiency constraints. To address these challenges, we present E-TraMamba, the first Mamba-based framework for 3D feature tracking on event data. This new framework adopts a linear state-space model for efficient long-range modeling and integrates a lightweight affine-transform predictor to maintain stable tracking under motion blur and occlusion. We also design an effective scheme to fuse multi-scale cues -- local spatiotemporal patches, correlation maps, and positional embeddings -- into a unified representation that enables stable and smooth 3D tracking. We construct a large-scale synthetic dataset, named EvD-PointOdyssey, which is generated with monocular rendering and provides synchronized event streams, depth maps, and accurate 3D trajectories for training and evaluating event-based 3D tracking models. Extensive experiments on event-based benchmarks demonstrate that E-TraMamba achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering over $2\times$ longer feature lifetimes under strict accuracy thresholds (e.g., 0.1 m), with higher tracked-feature ratios and lower RMSE than all baselines. These results make E-TraMamba a strong candidate for low-latency visual odometry, real-time SLAM, and interactive robotics.

中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的3D跟踪能够实现低延迟和高速度感知，而现有的CNN和Transformer跟踪器难以在稀疏、噪声事件流中捕捉长程时空依赖关系，尤其是在实时性和效率约束下表现不佳。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了E-TraMamba，这是首个基于Mamba框架的事件数据3D特征跟踪器。该新框架采用线性状态空间模型实现高效长程建模，并集成轻量级仿射变换预测器，以在运动模糊和遮挡情况下保持稳定跟踪。我们还设计了一种有效方案，将多尺度线索——局部时空patch、相关图和位置嵌入——融合为统一表示，从而实现稳定平滑的3D跟踪。我们构建了大规模合成数据集EvD-PointOdyssey，该数据集通过单目渲染生成，提供同步的事件流、深度图和精确的3D轨迹，用于训练和评估基于事件的3D跟踪模型。在事件基准数据集上的大量实验表明，E-TraMamba达到了最先进性能，在严格精度阈值（如0.1米）下特征寿命超过基线2倍以上，同时具有更高的跟踪特征比率和更低的RMSE。这些结果使E-TraMamba成为低延迟视觉里程计、实时SLAM和交互式机器人的强有力候选方案。

</details>


### [63] [SPLIT: Training-Free AI-Generated and Partially Edited Video Detection via Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02886)
*Jongyeop Hyun,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPLIT是一种免训练的AI生成视频检测器，通过提取空间块级不一致性和时间粗糙度两种互补信号，在极低假正率下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实场景部署AI生成视频检测器需要极低的假正率（FPR），而标准指标如AUROC无法反映这一极端阈值下的实际性能，现有方法在此场景下表现不佳。

Method: SPLIT基于冻结的视觉编码器提取块级令牌，计算两个互补信号：两步时间粗糙度（TTR）通过一步和两步特征变化对比捕捉非平滑块轨迹；局部空间运动不一致性（LSMI）通过特征空间运动场的梯度衡量空间不一致的时间变化。两者通过伽马校正进行乘法融合以增强真假视频在严格阈值下的分离度。此外提出了服务对齐的评估协议，使用固定FPR下的Fake Recall和仅用真实视频进行阈值校准。

Result: 在FakeParts、GenVideo和ViF-Bench三个基准上，SPLIT在FPR=0.1%时取得了最高的Fake Recall，显著优于监督方法和免训练基线，且对后处理具有鲁棒性，计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: SPLIT作为免训练方法，在超低假正率场景下有效检测全生成和部分编辑视频，为实际部署提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Deploying AI-generated video detectors in real-world services demands an ultra-low false positive rate (FPR) on real videos to avoid falsely rejecting authentic content, a regime where standard metrics such as AUROC fail to reflect actual operating behavior. We introduce Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness (SPLIT), a training-free detector that operates on patch tokens from a frozen vision encoder to detect both fully generated and partially edited videos. SPLIT computes two complementary signals: Two-step Temporal Roughness (TTR), capturing non-smooth patch trajectories via one-step and two-step feature variation contrast, and Local Spatial Motion Incoherence (LSMI), measuring spatially inconsistent temporal changes through gradients of a feature-space motion field. The two are fused multiplicatively with gamma correction to sharpen real-fake separation at strict thresholds. We further propose a service-aligned evaluation protocol based on Fake Recall at fixed FPR with real-only threshold calibration and cross-real threshold transfer. Across three benchmarks (FakeParts, GenVideo, and ViF-Bench), SPLIT achieves the highest Fake Recall at FPR = $0.1\%$, substantially outperforming supervised and training-free baselines while remaining robust to post-processing with negligible overhead. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/mldljyh/SPLIT .

中文摘要翻译: 在实际服务中部署AI生成视频检测器需要极低的假正率（FPR），以避免错误拒绝真实内容，而AUROC等标准指标无法反映这种极端阈值下的实际行为。我们提出SPLIT（Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness，空间块级不一致性与时间粗糙度），一种免训练的检测器，它基于冻结的视觉编码器提取块级补丁令牌，可同时检测全生成和部分编辑视频。SPLIT计算两个互补信号：两步时间粗糙度（TTR），通过一步和两步特征变化对比捕捉非平滑块轨迹；以及局部空间运动不一致性（LSMI），通过特征空间运动场的梯度衡量空间不一致的时间变化。两者通过伽马校正进行乘法融合，以在严格阈值下增强真假视频的分离度。我们进一步提出了一种服务对齐的评估协议，基于固定FPR下的Fake Recall，使用仅含真实视频的阈值校准和跨真实视频阈值迁移。在FakeParts、GenVideo和ViF-Bench三个基准上，SPLIT在FPR=0.1%时取得最高的Fake Recall，显著优于监督方法和免训练基线，同时对后处理保持鲁棒且计算开销可忽略。代码已开源：https://github.com/mldljyh/SPLIT。

</details>


### [64] [ProLaViT: Learning Progressive Latent Visual Thoughts in Structured Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02907)
*Peiming Li,Yifan Wang,Xiaotian Zhang,Zhiyuan Hu,Shiyu Li,Zheng Wei,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProLaViT 是一种渐进式潜在视觉思维框架，通过内生自蒸馏机制和多样性损失，在连续潜在空间中实现多步结构化视觉推理，无需外部专家模型即可提升多模态大语言模型的复杂视觉推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在需要多步感知和逻辑演绎的复杂视觉推理任务中表现不佳。显式视觉生成方法计算成本过高，而现有的潜在空间方法要么依赖外部专家模型，要么缺乏严谨的认知逻辑。

Method: (1) 内生自蒸馏机制：利用模型自身的视觉编码器监督潜在思维。 (2) 可扩展的程序化合成管道：使模型内化算法精度。 (3) 两种推理范式：粗到细因果链（空间任务）和辩证推理链（逻辑任务，含反事实验证）。 (4) 距离加权多样性损失：施加拓扑感知约束，防止特征退化。

Result: 在视觉中心基准测试上，ProLaViT优于基线方法，在准确性和可解释性方面均表现更佳，且计算效率高。

Conclusion: ProLaViT通过潜在空间中的结构化视觉推导，有效增强了MLLMs的复杂视觉推理能力，实现了精度、可解释性与效率的协同提升。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress but still struggle with complex visual reasoning tasks requiring multi-step perception and logical deduction. While explicit visual generation incurs prohibitive computational costs, existing latent approaches often rely on external experts or lack rigorous cognitive logic. In this paper, we introduce ProLaViT (Progressive Latent Visual Thought), a framework empowering MLLMs to perform structured visual derivation in the continuous latent space. Unlike works dependent on heterogeneous external models, ProLaViT leverages an endogenous self-distillation mechanism, utilizing the model's own visual encoder to supervise latent thoughts. To facilitate this, we construct a scalable programmatic synthesis pipeline enabling the model to internalize algorithmic precision without inference time tools. We design two reasoning paradigms: (1) Coarse-to-Fine Causal Chain for spatial tasks, guiding attention from global context to local targets. (2) Dialectical Reasoning Chain for logical tasks, incorporating counter-factual thinking for verification. Furthermore, we propose a Distance-Weighted Diversity Loss to impose topology-aware constraints, preventing feature degeneration by enforcing semantic distinctiveness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ProLaViT outperforms baselines on vision-centric benchmarks, achieving superior accuracy and interpretability with high efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽已取得显著进展，但在需要多步感知和逻辑演绎的复杂视觉推理任务中仍面临挑战。显式视觉生成方法计算成本过高，而现有的潜在空间方法往往依赖外部专家或缺乏严谨的认知逻辑。本文提出 ProLaViT（渐进式潜在视觉思维），一种在连续潜在空间中赋能 MLLMs 执行结构化视觉推导的框架。与依赖异构外部模型的工作不同，ProLaViT 利用内生的自蒸馏机制，通过模型自身的视觉编码器监督潜在思维。为此，我们构建了一个可扩展的程序化合成管道，使模型能够在无需推理时工具的情况下内化算法精度。我们设计了两种推理范式：(1) 面向空间任务的粗到细因果链，引导注意力从全局上下文聚焦到局部目标；(2) 面向逻辑任务的辩证推理链，引入反事实思维进行验证。此外，我们提出距离加权多样性损失，通过施加拓扑感知约束来强制语义区分度，从而防止特征退化。大量实验表明，ProLaViT 在视觉中心基准测试上优于基线方法，以高计算效率实现了卓越的准确性和可解释性。

</details>


### [65] [Holo-Captioning: Toward the Text Equivalent of 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02908)
*Kun-Yu Lin,Chengke Bu,Zhenguo Li,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全息字幕生成（holo-captioning）新任务，旨在生成3D场景的结构化文本描述，涵盖实体语义标签、空间位置、属性及关系。作者构建了超1.5万场景的大规模基准，并提出HoloScribe模型，采用实例感知的解耦流水线和锚点感知的实例链接机制，显著优于现有3D密集字幕生成器和3D大语言模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D场景理解工作缺乏对场景中所有实体及其空间关系和属性的全面结构化文本描述，亟需一种能够生成3D场景“文本等价物”的方法，以填补3D视觉与自然语言之间的鸿沟。

Method: 首先开发字幕生成引擎，为单个实体实例和实例对生成详细描述；然后提出HoloScribe模型，采用实例感知的解耦流水线生成结构化全息字幕，并引入锚点感知的实例链接机制识别关系实例对；同时提出综合评价指标HoloScore和人工筛选测试集。

Result: 实验结果表明，HoloScribe显著优于当前最先进的3D密集字幕生成器和3D大语言模型，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的全息字幕生成任务及HoloScribe模型为3D场景的结构化文本描述提供了有效解决方案，推动了3D视觉与语言理解的发展。

Original Abstract: This work introduces holo-captioning, a novel task that strives to seek the text equivalent of 3D scenes. As the initial step, we formulate holo-captioning as generating a structured textual description that comprehensively depicts all entities within a 3D scene -- including their semantic tags, spatial locations, attributes, and inter-entity relations. To tackle this challenging task, we first develop an effective captioning engine to produce detailed descriptions of individual entity instances and instance pairs, and contribute a large-scale benchmark comprising over 15K scenes for training and evaluation. Building upon this foundation, we propose HoloScribe, a novel model that features an instance-aware decoupled pipeline for generating structured holo-captions, and further incorporates anchor-aware instance linking to identify relational instance pairs. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive evaluation metric named HoloScore, and provide a human-curated test set to ensure reliable model assessment. Experimental results demonstrate that HoloScribe significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D dense captioners and 3D LLM generalists, underscoring the effectiveness of our approach. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/holocap/

中文摘要翻译: 本文引入了全息字幕生成（holo-captioning）这一新任务，旨在寻求3D场景的文本等价物。作为初始步骤，我们将全息字幕生成定义为生成一种结构化的文本描述，全面描绘3D场景中的所有实体——包括它们的语义标签、空间位置、属性以及实体间的相互关系。为应对这一挑战性任务，我们首先开发了一个有效的字幕生成引擎，用于生成单个实体实例和实例对的详细描述，并构建了一个包含超过15,000个场景的大规模基准用于训练和评估。在此基础上，我们提出了HoloScribe模型，该模型采用实例感知的解耦流水线来生成结构化的全息字幕，并进一步引入锚点感知的实例链接机制来识别关系实例对。此外，我们提出了一个名为HoloScore的综合评估指标，并提供了一个人工筛选的测试集以确保可靠的模型评估。实验结果表明，HoloScribe显著优于最先进的3D密集字幕生成器和3D大语言模型，充分验证了我们方法的有效性。项目页面：https://visual-ai.github.io/holocap/

</details>


### [66] [Learning Taxonomic Trees with Hierarchical Representation Regularization for Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02909)
*Hulingxiao He,Zhi Tan,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiR²是一种即插即用的层级表征正则化方法，通过在LMM微调中引入双目标正则化（双曲包含损失+判别性分散损失），增强模型的分层视觉识别一致性，无需修改模型架构即可有效捕获分类学结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前大型多模态模型（LMMs）在微调时仅依赖语言建模目标，缺乏显式的分类学感知正则化，导致层级视觉识别（HVR）一致性低下，无法有效捕获概念间的语义层级关系。

Method: 提出层级表征正则化（HiR²），包含：(1) 语义感知的视觉树构建框架，从LLM中间层提取由粗到细的视觉特征；(2) 双目标正则化——在Lorentz模型中通过双曲包含锥施加层级蕴含损失，以及在单位球面上施加判别性分散损失以促进语义相似嵌入的角分离，同时不破坏径向层级结构。

Result: 大量实验表明HiR²能有效捕获多种LMMs和微调方法中的分类学结构，显著提升层级视觉识别一致性。

Conclusion: HiR²作为一种简单有效的即插即用正则化器，能够在不改变模型架构的前提下提升LMMs的层级一致性，有助于模型更好地理解和利用视觉与语言中的分类学知识。

Original Abstract: Taxonomies provide key information about the semantic relationships between concepts and the inherent organization of vision and language. Despite their impressive capabilities, large multimodal models (LMMs) often lack taxonomic knowledge, leading to low hierarchical visual recognition (HVR) consistency. These models typically only rely on language modeling objectives during fine-tuning and lack explicit taxonomy-aware regularization. To address this, we propose Hierarchical Representation Regularization ($HiR^2$), a simple plug-and-play regularizer that improves hierarchical consistency in LMMs. Specifically, we introduce a semantic-aware visual tree construction framework that extracts coarse-to-fine visual features from intermediate LLM layers guided by textual cues. The regularizer combines two complementary objectives: a taxonomic entailment loss that enforces hierarchy via hyperbolic entailment cones in the Lorentz model, and a discriminative dispersive loss that promotes angular separation of semantically similar embeddings on the unit sphere without disturbing the radial hierarchical structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $HiR^2$ effectively captures taxonomic structures across diverse LMMs and fine-tuning methods. Code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/HiR2_ICML2026.

中文摘要翻译: 分类学提供了关于概念间语义关系以及视觉与语言内在组织的关键信息。尽管大型多模态模型（LMMs）能力惊人，但它们通常缺乏分类学知识，导致层级视觉识别（HVR）一致性低下。这些模型在微调时通常仅依赖语言建模目标，缺乏显式的分类学感知正则化。为解决此问题，我们提出层级表征正则化（HiR²），一种简单的即插即用正则化器，可提升LMMs中的层级一致性。具体而言，我们引入了一个语义感知的视觉树构建框架，在文本线索引导下从LLM中间层提取由粗到细的视觉特征。该正则化器结合了两个互补目标：一是在Lorentz模型中通过双曲包含锥施加层级蕴含损失，二是在单位球面上施加判别性分散损失，促进语义相似嵌入的角分离，同时不破坏径向层级结构。大量实验表明，HiR²能有效捕获多种LMMs和微调方法中的分类学结构。代码已开源在https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/HiR2_ICML2026。

</details>


### [67] [See the Emotion: A Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling for EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02912)
*Jingjing Hu,Guo Dan,Haofan Cheng,Ying Zeng,Zhan Si,Jinxing Zhou,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面部表情符号代理建模框架，将EEG信号翻译为身份匿名的面部表情动画，实现从特征归因到行为可视化的范式转变，在保持高准确率的同时提供可解释的情绪可视化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于EEG的情绪识别模型虽精度高，但作为"黑箱"缺乏神经特征与人类可理解状态之间的语义关联，传统特征归因方法难以提供直观的情绪可视化解释。

Method: 提出面部表情符号代理建模框架，包括：1) FMENet作为专用骨干网络建模表达相关的空间协同关系；2) 面部表情符号学习分支(FELB)，将表情符号重建作为结构化语义正则化器，将EEG信号翻译为身份匿名的面部表情符号动画。

Result: 在EAV和MMER基准上，该方法在纯EEG模型中实现了最先进的准确率，并生成语义忠实的面部动画，提供了透明、保护隐私的大脑情绪演化可视化窗口。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态生成任务重构EEG可解释性问题，将神经表征锚定在可观测的面部动态流形上，实现了从"黑箱"到可视化情绪解读的范式转变，同时兼顾隐私保护。

Original Abstract: Despite the high accuracy of EEG-based emotion recognition, existing models remain opaque "black boxes", lacking semantic grounding between abstract neural features and human-interpretable states. In this paper, we reframe EEG explainability as a cross-modal generation task, shifting the paradigm from feature attribution to behavioral visualization. We introduce Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling, a novel framework that translates high-dimensional EEG signals into identity-anonymized facial emojis. Guided by the neuroscientific inspiration of neural-facial association, this approach grounds neural representations in the manifold of observable facial dynamics. Technically, our framework integrates FMENet, a specialized backbone modeling expression-relevant spatial synergies, and the Facial Emoji Learning Branch (FELB), which treats emoji reconstruction as a structured semantic regularizer. Extensive experiments on EAV and MMER benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among EEG-only models. Crucially, it generates semantically faithful facial animations that provide a transparent, privacy-preserving window into the brain's emotional evolution, effectively allowing users to "see the emotion" directly from neural signals. Code is available at https://github.com/xian-sh/SeeEmotion

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于EEG的情绪识别已达到高准确率，但现有模型仍然是"黑箱"，缺乏抽象神经特征与人类可理解状态之间的语义关联。本文重新将EEG可解释性定义为跨模态生成任务，将范式从特征归因转向行为可视化。我们提出面部表情符号代理建模——一种将高维EEG信号翻译为身份匿名的面部表情符号的新框架。受神经-面部关联这一神经科学灵感的引导，该方法将神经表征锚定在可观测的面部动态流形上。在技术层面，我们的框架整合了FMENet（一个建模表情相关空间协同关系的专用骨干网络）和面部表情符号学习分支（FELB，将表情符号重建视为结构化语义正则化器）。在EAV和MMER基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在纯EEG模型中达到了最先进的准确率。关键在于，它生成了语义忠实的面部动画，为大脑的情绪演化提供了透明且保护隐私的窗口，有效实现了让用户直接从神经信号"看到情绪"。代码已在https://github.com/xian-sh/SeeEmotion公开。

</details>


### [68] [R3D: Quantitative 3D Spatial Reasoning for Egocentric Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02921)
*Maxwell Horton,Wei Lu,Quan Tran,Yury Astashonok,Kirmani Ahmed,Babak Damavandi,Anuj Kumar,Xiao Zhang,Seungwhan Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了R3D-Bench基准测试（包含3,033个3D空间推理问题）和R3D模型无关工具调用框架，在自然第一视角RGB-D视频上实现定量3D空间推理，R3D+Qwen3-VL 235B达到73.5%平均相对精度，显著优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准无法同时处理自然第一视角视频、带位姿的RGB-D视频输入以及挑战性的定量3D空间推理问答，因此需要一个新的基准和框架来弥补这一空白。

Method: R3D是一个模型无关的空间工具调用框架，通过分割和深度提升的对象表示从视频中构建3D场景，并将场景信息通过八个可组合的空间工具提供给大语言模型进行空间推理。

Result: R3D with Qwen3-VL 235B在R3D-Bench上达到73.5%的平均相对精度，显著优于最佳深度基线CuTR+Tools（61.9%）和最佳RGB基线Gemini 3 Flash（46.5%）。

Conclusion: R3D框架通过显式构建3D场景并借助可组合工具调用，显著提升了LLM在自然第一视角视频上的定量3D空间推理能力。

Original Abstract: Quantitative 3D spatial reasoning from egocentric RGB-D video is a critical capability for next-generation wearable assistants. Yet existing benchmarks do not reflect the challenges of handling (1) natural egocentric video, (2) posed RGB-D video inputs, and (3) challenging quantitative 3D spatial reasoning Q&A. To fill this gap, we introduce R3D-Bench (Reasoning in 3D), a benchmark of 3,033 quantitative spatial reasoning questions across 15 types -- spanning multiple-choice, distance-based, and volumetric reasoning questions -- built on top of 57 egocentric video sequences from Aria Digital Twin. To set a strong baseline on this dataset, we introduce R3D, a model-agnostic spatial tool-calling framework. In contrast to existing approaches that directly embed 3D information into the model's input representation, R3D constructs a 3D scene from video using segmentation and depth-lifted object representations. It provides this information to an LLM through eight composable spatial tools. On R3D-Bench, R3D with Qwen3-VL 235B achieves 73.5% mean relative accuracy, substantially outperforming the best depth-enabled baseline (CuTR+Tools, 61.9%) and the best RGB-only baseline (Gemini 3 Flash, 46.5%).

中文摘要翻译: 从第一视角RGB-D视频中进行定量3D空间推理是下一代可穿戴助手的关键能力。然而，现有基准未能反映处理（1）自然第一视角视频、（2）带位姿的RGB-D视频输入以及（3）具有挑战性的定量3D空间推理问答所带来的挑战。为填补这一空白，我们提出了R3D-Bench（3D空间推理基准），该基准包含基于Aria Digital Twin中57段第一视角视频序列构建的3,033个定量空间推理问题，涵盖15种类型——包括多项选择、基于距离和体积推理的问题。为了在该数据集上建立强基线，我们提出了R3D，一个模型无关的空间工具调用框架。与直接将3D信息嵌入模型输入表示的现有方法不同，R3D通过分割和深度提升的对象表示从视频中构建3D场景，并通过八个可组合的空间工具将该信息提供给大语言模型。在R3D-Bench上，R3D搭配Qwen3-VL 235B实现了73.5%的平均相对精度，显著优于最佳深度基线（CuTR+Tools，61.9%）和最佳RGB基线（Gemini 3 Flash，46.5%）。

</details>


### [69] [STAC: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation and Compression for Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02922)
*Syed Ariff Syed Hesham,Yun Liu,Guolei Sun,Jing Yang,Henghui Ding,Xue Geng,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性时空聚合与压缩（STAC）方法，利用状态空间模型的线性递归特性实现对视频令牌的上下文感知压缩，在零样本流式兼容设定下实现85%令牌缩减和1.8倍加速，同时超越无压缩基线在推理分割基准上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频推理分割方法处理长视频时面临密集时空令牌的二次自注意力成本过高的问题；虽已有令牌压缩方法，但多在缺乏时序上下文的编码器特征上操作，无法可靠评估内容冗余；而获取全分辨率上下文感知又会产生压缩本欲减少的二次成本。

Method: STAC方法利用状态空间模型的线性递归特性（O(T)成本），通过解耦的双向空间扫描和因果时序扫描对每个令牌进行时序上下文条件化，得到可评估内容冗余的表征；然后利用递归导出的冗余信息进行分层自适应阈值压缩，并以分割目标优化阈值。

Result: STAC实现了85%的令牌缩减和1.8倍加速，在推理分割基准上以零样本流式兼容设定超越了无压缩基线。

Conclusion: 状态空间模型的线性递归可以有效解决视频推理分割中长视频处理的令牌压缩上下文感知问题，在显著降低计算成本的同时维持甚至提升分割性能。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation demands pixel-accurate object tracking across hundreds of frames under complex natural language queries, producing dense spatiotemporal tokens whose quadratic self-attention cost makes long-video processing prohibitive. Existing methods address this through token compression, yet typically operate on encoder features lacking temporal context, constraining selection before content redundancy can be reliably assessed. Informed compression requires contextual awareness, but acquiring that awareness at full resolution incurs the same quadratic cost compression aims to reduce. State-space models resolve this constraint, as their linear recurrence selectively conditions each token on temporal context at $\mathcal{O}(T)$ cost, producing representations where content redundancy becomes assessable. Building on this, Selective SpatioTemporal Aggregation and Compression (STAC) enriches features via decoupled bidirectional spatial and causal temporal scanning, leveraging recurrence-derived redundancy for hierarchical compression with adaptive thresholds optimised with segmentation objective. STAC achieves 85% token reduction and 1.8$\times$ speedup while surpassing compression-free baselines on reasoning segmentation benchmarks in a zero-shot streaming-compatible setting. Code is available \href{https://github.com/MCG-NKU/nku-video}{here}.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理分割要求在复杂的自然语言查询下，对跨越数百帧的像素级精确目标跟踪，产生密集的时空令牌，其二次自注意力代价使得长视频处理变得不可行。现有方法通过令牌压缩来解决这一问题，但通常操作在缺乏时序上下文的编码器特征上，导致在内容冗余可被可靠评估之前就进行了选择。信息感知的压缩需要上下文意识，但在全分辨率下获取这种意识会产生压缩本欲减少的相同二次代价。状态空间模型解决了这一约束，其线性递归以O(T)代价选择性地将每个令牌基于时序上下文进行条件化，产生内容冗余变得可评估的表征。基于此，选择性时空聚合与压缩（STAC）通过解耦的双向空间扫描和因果时序扫描来丰富特征，利用递归导出的冗余信息进行分层自适应阈值压缩，并以分割目标优化阈值。STAC在零样本流式兼容设定下实现了85%的令牌缩减和1.8倍加速，同时在推理分割基准上超越了无压缩基线。代码可在\href{https://github.com/MCG-NKU/nku-video}{此处}获取。

</details>


### [70] [VideoSearcher: Empowering Video Deep Research with Multi-Tool Agentic Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02927)
*Zhenkun Gao,Yicheng Bao,Jinlong Peng,Xueheng Li,Theo Huang,Bangwei Liu,Kunquan Li,Zhenye Gan,Tao Hu,Chengjun Xie,Mingqian Yang,Xuanhua He,Zhizhong Zhang,Xin Tan,Chengjie Wang,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoSearcher，一个面向视频深度研究（VDR）的闭环智能体框架，结合多工具推理与双分支序列策略优化（BiSPO）算法，显著提升了开放世界视频信息定位与多模态搜索推理能力，并构建了首个相关基准VideoSearch-QA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体主要针对静态图像，而当前的VDR基准依赖于以文本为中心的检索，丢弃了关键的视觉信息，无法满足开放世界视频证据探索的需求。

Method: 提出VideoSearcher闭环智能体框架，统一时间定位、空间聚焦与多模态搜索于单一推理轨迹中；并设计双分支序列策略优化（BiSPO）强化学习算法，将工具调用优化与答案准确性优化解耦，为证据推理和工具使用提供稳定学习信号。

Result: 在多个面向搜索和多模态理解的基准测试中，VideoSearcher显著优于先前的开源智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: VideoSearcher通过多工具推理与解耦强化学习策略，有效提升了开放世界视频深度研究的证据定位与推理能力，填补了现有工作在视频信息检索与多模态搜索推理方面的空白。

Original Abstract: Video understanding is moving beyond closed-context perception toward open-world evidence exploration, a paradigm formalized as Video Deep Research (VDR). However, existing multimodal search agents primarily target static images, and the current VDR benchmark relies on text-centric retrieval that discards crucial visual information. To address these limitations, we propose VideoSearcher, a closed-loop agentic framework that empowers Vision-Language Models with multi-tool reasoning for VDR. VideoSearcher unifies temporal localization, spatial focusing, and multimodal search within a single reasoning trajectory, enabling agents to progressively ground visual clues, retrieve relevant evidence, and synthesize answers. To optimize knowledge-intensive reasoning trajectories, we propose Bi-branch Sequence Policy Optimization (BiSPO), a reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples tool-invocation optimization from answer-accuracy optimization. This design provides stable learning signals for both evidence-grounded reasoning and purposeful tool use. Furthermore, we construct VideoSearch-QA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate open-world video information grounding and multimodal search-based reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VideoSearcher significantly outperforms prior open-source agentic baselines across various search-oriented and multimodal understanding benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 视频理解正从封闭语境感知迈向开放世界证据探索，这一范式被形式化为视频深度研究（VDR）。然而，现有的多模态搜索智能体主要针对静态图像，且当前的VDR基准依赖于以文本为中心的检索，丢弃了关键的视觉信息。为解决这些局限性，我们提出VideoSearcher，一个闭环智能体框架，赋予视觉语言模型多工具推理能力以支持VDR。VideoSearcher将时间定位、空间聚焦和多模态搜索统一于单一推理轨迹中，使智能体能够逐步定位视觉线索、检索相关证据并综合答案。为了优化知识密集型推理轨迹，我们提出双分支序列策略优化（BiSPO），一种将工具调用优化与答案准确性优化解耦的强化学习算法。该设计为基于证据的推理和有目的的工具使用提供了稳定的学习信号。此外，我们构建了VideoSearch-QA，这是首个旨在评估开放世界视频信息定位与多模态搜索推理的基准。大量实验表明，VideoSearcher在各种面向搜索和多模态理解的基准测试中，显著优于先前的开源智能体基线方法。

</details>


### [71] [CL-Anomaly: Layer-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts with Multimodal Large Language Model for Continual Learning in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02930)
*Wen Dong,Zhao Wang,Shuangqing Zhang,Kai Sun,Ben Li,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-Anomaly，一种基于隔离-共享协作的参数高效微调框架，用于多模态大语言模型在异常检测场景下的持续学习。通过任务私有专家PrivLoRA物理隔离任务特定参数子空间，结合层自适应共享专家和动量驱动的知识迁移策略，有效解决了多任务语义纠缠问题，在类增量、跨域和跨模态三个持续学习场景中均取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉任务中表现优异，但其全参数重训练在知识不断演化的真实场景中计算成本过高。现有持续学习方法存在跨任务参数空间的语义纠缠问题，阻碍模型的持续部署。这一问题在异常检测（AD）中尤为突出，因为AD存在模态、领域和缺陷尺度变异性三重异质性，严重复杂化了多任务知识迁移。

Method: 提出CL-Anomaly框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务私有专家PrivLoRA，在参数空间中物理隔离任务特定子空间，防止不同场景下异常知识的语义纠缠；(2) 层自适应共享专家（Layer-Adaptive Shared Experts），在统一特征空间中维护跨任务表示，实现新旧任务之间的知识共享；(3) 层自适应知识迁移策略（Layer-Adaptive Knowledge Transfer），通过基于动量的机制自动选择并动态更新每个任务的逐层关键共享专家，促进相关异常检测任务间的有效知识迁移。

Result: 在类增量、跨域和跨模态三个异常检测持续学习场景中的大量实验表明，CL-Anomaly在各项指标上均优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: CL-Anomaly通过参数高效微调框架，以隔离-共享协作的方式有效解决了多模态大语言模型在异常检测持续学习中的语义纠缠问题，为模型在动态真实场景中的持续部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in diverse vision tasks, but full-parameter retraining is computationally expensive as real-world knowledge evolves. Existing continual learning methods often suffer from semantic entanglement in parameter spaces across tasks, impeding the continuous deployment of models. This challenge is especially pronounced in Anomaly Detection (AD), which exhibits triple heterogeneity across modalities, domains, and defect scale variability, significantly complicating multi-task knowledge transfer. In this paper, we propose CL-Anomaly, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework based on an isolation-sharing collaboration to enable continual learning for anomaly detection with MLLMs. We introduce the task-private expert PrivLoRA, which physically isolates task-specific subspaces in the parameter space to prevent semantic entanglement of anomaly knowledge in diverse scenarios. The Layer-Adaptive Shared Experts maintain cross-task representations within a unified feature space, enabling knowledge sharing between previous and new tasks. Furthermore, we propose a Layer-Adaptive Knowledge Transfer strategy that automatically selects and dynamically updates the layer-wise key shared experts of each task via a momentum-based mechanism, promoting effective knowledge transfer across related anomaly detection tasks. Extensive experiments across three continual learning scenarios for anomaly detection, including class-incremental, cross-domain, and cross-modal, demonstrate that CL-Anomaly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/WenDongyp/CL-Anomaly.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在多样化的视觉任务中表现出色，但随着真实世界知识的不断演化，全参数重训练的计算成本过高。现有的持续学习方法常常面临跨任务参数空间的语义纠缠问题，阻碍了模型的持续部署。这一挑战在异常检测（AD）中尤为突出，因为异常检测在模态、领域和缺陷尺度变异性上呈现出三重异质性，显著复杂化了多任务知识迁移。本文提出CL-Anomaly，一种基于隔离-共享协作的参数高效微调框架，旨在实现基于多模态大语言模型的异常检测持续学习。我们引入任务私有专家PrivLoRA，其在参数空间中物理隔离任务特定子空间，以防止多样化场景下异常知识的语义纠缠。层自适应共享专家在统一特征空间内维护跨任务表示，实现新旧任务之间的知识共享。此外，我们提出了一种层自适应知识迁移策略，该策略通过基于动量的机制自动选择并动态更新每个任务的逐层关键共享专家，促进相关异常检测任务间的有效知识迁移。在类增量、跨域和跨模态三个异常检测持续学习场景中的大量实验表明，CL-Anomaly优于现有的最先进方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/WenDongyp/CL-Anomaly。

</details>


### [72] [MatPhaseBench: A Semantics-Guided Benchmark for Materials Phase Diagrams Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02934)
*Hanwen Wang,Sihan Liang,Zhiwei Liu,Yangang Wang,Wei Yan,Yuqin Liu,Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MatPhaseBench，一个面向材料相图的高质量基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在复杂科学图像理解上的能力。实验表明现有VLM在相图理解上远未达到专家水平，主要局限于表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在科学图像理解上展现出一定潜力，但对于需要深度机制解释的逻辑复杂图像（如材料相图）的系统评估仍然有限，亟需一个高质量、高可靠性的基准测试来系统评估VLM在此类任务上的能力。

Method: 从3681篇经典材料科学期刊论文中精选200个高质量图文对，覆盖189个材料系统和70种元素，构建MatPhaseBench基准。基准具有三大特征：(1)面向复杂科学图像理解，采用开放式任务；(2)完整的图像-文本语义对齐；(3)人工严格验证文本描述。然后使用该基准评估多个当前主流VLM的性能。

Result: 当前VLM在相图理解上远落后于专家水平：主要局限于表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理，领域意识和专家分析经验有限，在复合图或多图设置中区分细粒度差异的能力较差。

Conclusion: MatPhaseBench是一个具有挑战性的研究级基准测试，为复杂科学图像理解、相图分析和可信科学多模态AI提供了基础平台。

Original Abstract: Materials phase diagrams are a core knowledge representation in materials science, encoding temperature,composition, phase stability, and phase transformation pathways, with their full understanding requiring thermodynamic mechanism analysis and scientific reasoning. Although VLMs have shown promise in scientific image understanding, their systematic evaluation on such logically complex images demanding deep mechanistic interpretation remains limited, and phase diagrams provide a challenging testbed for this purpose. We introduce MatPhaseBench, a high-quality, high-reliability benchmark for complex scientific image understanding, focused on materials phase diagrams. MatPhaseBench is constructed from 3681 papers in classical materials science journals, from which 200 high-quality diagram-text pairs were selected, covering 189 material systems and 70 elements. The benchmark has three key features: (1)targeting complex scientific image understanding-it moves beyond simple objective tests to open-ended tasks requiring deep comprehension; (2)comprehensive image-text alignment-semantic information associated with images is fully preserved during literature mining and matching; (3) high-quality human-supervised text acquisition-all descriptions undergo strict manual validation. Experimental results show that current VLMs remain substantially behind expert-level understanding: they are largely limited to surface visual perception, lack deep reasoning grounded in thermodynamic mechanisms, have limited domain awareness and expert analytical experience, and perform poorly in distinguishing fine-grained differences in composite or multi-diagram settings. Overall, MatPhaseBench constitutes a challenging research-grade benchmark, providing a foundational platform for complex scientific image understanding, phase diagram analysis, and trustworthy multi-modal AI in science.

中文摘要翻译: 材料相图是材料科学中的核心知识表征，编码了温度、成分、相稳定性和相变路径等信息，对其完整理解需要热力学机制分析和科学推理。尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）在科学图像理解方面已展现出潜力，但在需要深度机制解释的逻辑复杂图像上的系统评估仍然有限，而相图为此提供了具有挑战性的测试平台。本文提出MatPhaseBench，一个面向材料相图的高质量、高可靠性复杂科学图像理解基准。MatPhaseBench从3681篇经典材料科学期刊论文中构建，从中精选出200个高质量图文对，覆盖189个材料系统和70种元素。该基准具有三大关键特征：(1)面向复杂科学图像理解——超越简单客观测试，进入需要深度理解的开放式任务；(2)全面的图文对齐——文献挖掘和匹配过程中完整保留与图像相关的语义信息；(3)高质量人工监督文本获取——所有描述均经过严格人工验证。实验结果表明，当前VLM在相图理解上仍远落后于专家水平：它们主要局限于表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理，领域意识和专家分析经验有限，在区分复合图或多图设置中的细粒度差异方面表现不佳。总体而言，MatPhaseBench构成了一个具有挑战性的研究级基准，为复杂科学图像理解、相图分析和可信科学多模态AI提供了基础平台。

</details>


### [73] [Pooling-Based Context Modeling for Convolution-Free Deep Image Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02952)
*Gihyun Kim,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Pool-DIP，一种无卷积架构，利用基于池化的对比建模来高效捕获空间上下文，在图像去噪中减少参数和计算复杂度，同时抑制高频噪声过拟合，并泛化到超分辨率和修复等任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度图像先验(DIP)虽能从单张噪声输入恢复图像，但其过度参数化的架构在优化过程中容易过拟合噪声，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出Pool-DIP，一种无卷积架构，采用基于池化的对比建模(Pooling-based Contrast Modeling)来捕获空间上下文信息，替代传统卷积操作。

Result: Pool-DIP在多个数据集（含真实世界基准）上取得竞争性去噪性能；大幅减少参数量和计算复杂度；频谱分析表明其能稳定优化过程中高频成分的演化并抑制错误高频信号；在超分辨率和修复任务上也表现良好。

Conclusion: Pool-DIP作为一种无卷积架构，通过池化对比建模有效解决了DIP的过拟合问题，在降低计算开销的同时保持了优异的图像恢复性能，具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) achieve strong denoising performance by exploiting spatial context from neighboring pixels. Deep Image Prior (DIP) leverages this property to restore images from a single noisy input without requiring large datasets. However, the over-parameterized architecture of DIP often leads to noise fitting during optimization. In this paper, we propose Pool-DIP, a convolution-free architecture that incorporates pooling-based contrast modeling to capture spatial context efficiently. Pool-DIP improves denoising performance while significantly reducing the number of parameters and computational complexity compared to convolution-based DIP models. Experimental results show that Pool-DIP achieves competitive performance across multiple datasets, including a real-world benchmark. Spectral analysis further reveals that Pool-DIP stabilizes the evolution of high-frequency components during optimization and suppresses erroneous high-frequency signals. The proposed architecture also generalizes well to other image restoration tasks such as super-resolution and inpainting.

中文摘要翻译: 卷积神经网络（CNN）通过利用相邻像素的空间上下文信息，实现了强大的去噪性能。深度图像先验（DIP）利用这一特性，能够在无需大规模数据集的情况下，从单张噪声输入中恢复图像。然而，DIP过度参数化的架构在优化过程中常常导致噪声拟合。本文提出Pool-DIP，一种无卷积架构，它引入基于池化的对比建模来高效捕获空间上下文。与基于卷积的DIP模型相比，Pool-DIP在显著减少参数量和计算复杂度的同时，提升了去噪性能。实验结果表明，Pool-DIP在多个数据集（包括真实世界基准）上取得了具有竞争力的性能。频谱分析进一步揭示，Pool-DIP在优化过程中稳定了高频成分的演化，并抑制了错误的高频信号。所提出的架构还能很好地泛化到其他图像恢复任务，如超分辨率和图像修复。

</details>


### [74] [MORE: A Multilingual Document Parsing Benchmark and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02956)
*Long Xu,Binghong Wu,Tinghao Yu,Hao Feng,Zhenyu Huang,Haoqing Jiang,Yunhao Wang,Shuo Huang,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MORE，一个覆盖149种语言的大规模多语言文档解析基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在结构化文档解析任务上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准主要关注英语和中文等高端资源语言，缺乏对低资源语言的评估能力；同时现有评估多局限于纯文本，忽略了代码块、表格、目录等结构化元素。这使得视觉语言模型声称的多语言能力无法被有效验证。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖149种语言的大规模基准数据集，包含代码块、表格、目录等结构化元素；所有样本均来自真实文档，采用模型辅助+人工精修的标注流程。

Result: 基于MORE评估了多种最先进的视觉语言模型，建立了长尾语言的性能基线，验证了基准在真实多样化场景下诊断模型能力的有效性。

Conclusion: MORE填补了多语言文档解析评估的空白，为未来多语言视觉语言模型研究提供了可靠基准。

Original Abstract: Multilingual documents encapsulate rich regional cultures, scientific discoveries, and historical records. Parsing this content into structured, machine-readable formats is critical for unlocking global knowledge. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on high-resource languages like English and Chinese, creating an evaluation blind spot concerning model performance on other languages. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) claim support for hundreds of languages, the lack of ground truth makes it impossible to empirically verify these capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MORE, a large-scale benchmark designed for multilingual document parsing evaluation. MORE distinguishes itself through three key dimensions: (1) Unprecedented Scale: It covers 149 languages, making it the most linguistically diverse benchmark to date; (2) Structural Complexity: Unlike previous works, it extends evaluation beyond plain text to include structural elements such as code blocks, tables, and catalogs; and (3) Data Authenticity: All samples are curated from real-world documents via a model-assisted, human-refined annotation pipeline. We evaluate state-of-the-art models using MORE, establishing new performance baselines for long-tail languages and validating the benchmark's effectiveness in diagnosing model capabilities in realistic, diverse scenarios. The MORE dataset will be available at https://github.com/zimoqingfeng/MORE.

中文摘要翻译: 多语言文档承载着丰富的区域文化、科学发现和历史记录。将这些内容解析为结构化的、机器可读的格式对于解锁全球知识至关重要。然而，现有基准主要关注英语和中文等高端资源语言，导致在模型对其他语言性能的评估上存在盲区。虽然最近的视觉语言模型声称支持数百种语言，但缺乏真实标注数据使得这些能力无法被实证验证。为填补这一空白，我们提出了MORE——一个面向多语言文档解析评估的大规模基准。MORE在三个关键维度上独具特色：（1）前所未有的大规模：覆盖149种语言，是目前语言多样性最丰富的基准；（2）结构复杂性：不同于以往仅关注纯文本的工作，它将评估扩展到代码块、表格和目录等结构化元素；（3）数据真实性：所有样本均来自真实世界文档，采用模型辅助、人工精修的标注流程。我们使用MORE评估了最先进的模型，为长尾语言建立了新的性能基线，并验证了该基准在真实多样化场景下诊断模型能力的有效性。MORE数据集将在https://github.com/zimoqingfeng/MORE上公开。

</details>


### [75] [ReLo-IRR: Reflection-Guided LoRA Framework for Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02957)
*Chaoqun Wang,Yuehuan Wei,Haoxiang Cao,Shaobo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReLo-IRR，一种基于反射引导的LoRA框架，通过预测反射强度描述符并融合时间条件机制，实现图像自适应的反射去除，在多种反射条件下均取得优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的SIRR方法采用图像无关的适配策略（如微调或ControlNet），对所有图像进行统一抑制，导致强反射残留、弱反射细节丢失。需要一种能根据反射严重程度自适应调节的去反射方法。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级估计器预测反射强度描述符，提供反射主导性的显式先验，实现图像相关的LoRA调制；2) 引入时间条件机制，将反射描述符与时间步嵌入融合，使LoRA调制随粗到细的去噪过程协同演化。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准上，ReLo-IRR相比现有方法取得了更优的去反射性能和鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模反射强度与去噪动力学，ReLo-IRR实现了对多样化反射条件的鲁棒抑制，验证了反射引导自适应策略的有效性。

Original Abstract: Single-image reflection removal (SIRR) aims to recover the clean transmission layer from a reflection-contaminated image. Although recent methods achieve promising results with large diffusion models, they rely on image-agnostic adaptation strategies, e.g., fine-tuning or ControlNet, that enforce uniform suppression regardless of reflection severity. As a result, heavy reflections often leave residuals, while weak ones suffer from detail loss. To this end, we propose ReLo-IRR, a reflection-guided LoRA framework built upon the rectified flow model. First, a lightweight estimator is designed to predict the reflection strength descriptor, providing an explicit prior of reflection dominance for each image and enabling image-dependent LoRA modulation. Second, we introduce a time-conditioned mechanism that fuses this reflection descriptor with timestep embeddings, enabling LoRA modulation to evolve consistently with the coarse-to-fine denoising process. By jointly modeling reflection strength and denoising dynamics, our ReLo-IRR achieves robust suppression of diverse reflection conditions. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks validate the effectiveness of ReLo-IRR, demonstrating superior dereflection performance and robust generalization. The code is released at https://github.com/KONGBAI-8080/ReLo-IRR.

中文摘要翻译: 单图像反射去除（SIRR）旨在从受反射污染的图像中恢复干净的透射层。尽管近期方法借助大规模扩散模型取得了令人瞩目的成果，但它们依赖于与图像无关的适配策略（如微调或ControlNet），对所有图像进行统一的抑制，无论反射严重程度如何。这导致强反射常留有残差，而弱反射则遭受细节丢失。为此，本文提出ReLo-IRR，一个基于整流流模型的反射引导LoRA框架。首先，设计了一个轻量级估计器来预测反射强度描述符，为每张图像提供反射主导性的显式先验，从而实现图像相关的LoRA调制。其次，引入时间条件机制，将该反射描述符与时间步嵌入融合，使LoRA调制能够与从粗到细的去噪过程一致地演化。通过联合建模反射强度与去噪动力学，我们的ReLo-IRR实现了对多样化反射条件的鲁棒抑制。在多个挑战性基准上的大量实验验证了ReLo-IRR的有效性，展示了其优越的去反射性能和鲁棒的泛化能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/KONGBAI-8080/ReLo-IRR。

</details>


### [76] [Incentivizing Vision Language Models to Search for Long Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02959)
*Harsh Goel,S P Sharan,Sahil Shah,Minkyu Choi,Joungbin An,Kristen Grauman,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSeek是一个基于强化学习后训练的智能体框架，将长视频问答转化为多轮检索过程，通过神经符号方法将用户问题编译为时序逻辑规约以提供可验证奖励信号，显著提升了长视频理解基准上的Pass@1和Pass@4得分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频问答方法通常采用被动、单次感知的方式处理长视频，缺乏主动检索和多轮交互能力。同时，强化学习后训练在长视频理解中的应用受限于缺乏可验证的奖励信号，无法像数学和代码等符号领域那样有效发挥作用。

Method: 提出神经符号方法：将复杂的用户自然语言查询编译为形式化的时序逻辑规约，系统性地将问题分解为所需原子视觉基元（如关键对象和活动及其时序关系）的确定性检查清单。这些基础事件作为RL后训练的密集可验证奖励信号，优化检索到的上下文与问题的相关性，而非仅依赖最终答案正确性。

Result: 在长视频理解基准上，相比基础模型，VSeek的Pass@1得分提升最高8%，Pass@4得分提升15%。

Conclusion: 通过显式优化可验证的证据搜索行为，VSeek有效将RL后训练的优势引入长视频理解领域，验证了基于时序逻辑规约的神经符号方法可以为开放域视觉任务提供可靠的奖励信号。

Original Abstract: We introduce VSeek, an agentic framework that transforms long-video question answering (LVQA) from a passive, single-pass perception task into a multi-turn retrieval process. VSeek utilizes a natural language-driven search to identify relevant context within long videos and is post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly formulate targeted search queries and reason over retrieved clips for LVQA. While RL post-training has revolutionized reasoning in symbolic domains such as mathematics and code, its application to long-video understanding remains hindered by a lack of verified rewards. To ensure that the retrieved context is relevant, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic approach that bridges open-ended natural language with discrete visual verification. Specifically, complex user queries are compiled into formal temporal logic specifications for systematically decomposing natural language questions into a definitive checklist of required atomic visual primitives, such as key objects and activities, along with their temporal ordering. These systematically derived grounding events provide the critical feedback signal for RL post-training, enabling dense, verifiable rewards based on the successful retrieval of these specific visual elements rather than relying entirely on outcome-only answer accuracy. By explicitly optimizing for this verifiable evidence-seeking behavior, VSeek improves Pass@1 scores by up to 8% and Pass@4 scores by 15% on long-video understanding benchmarks compared to base models. We open-source our code at https://utaustin-swarmlab.github.io/VSeek.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了VSeek，一个智能体框架，将长视频问答（LVQA）从被动的单次感知任务转变为多轮检索过程。VSeek利用自然语言驱动的搜索来识别长视频中的相关上下文，并通过强化学习（RL）后训练联合生成有针对性的搜索查询，并对检索到的片段进行推理以回答LVQA问题。虽然RL后训练已在数学和代码等符号领域的推理中引发了革命，但其在长视频理解中的应用仍然因缺乏可验证的奖励而受到阻碍。为确保检索到的上下文具有相关性，我们提出了一种新颖的神经符号方法，该方法将开放域自然语言与离散的视觉验证相连接。具体而言，复杂的用户查询被编译为形式化的时序逻辑规约，用于系统地将自然语言问题分解为所需的原子视觉基元（如关键对象和活动）及其时序关系的确定性检查清单。这些系统推导出的基础事件为RL后训练提供了关键的反馈信号，使得基于成功检索这些特定视觉元素的可验证密集奖励成为可能，而非完全依赖仅基于结果的答案正确性。通过显式优化这种可验证的证据搜索行为，与基础模型相比，VSeek在长视频理解基准上的Pass@1得分提升了最高8%，Pass@4得分提升了15%。我们在 https://utaustin-swarmlab.github.io/VSeek 开源了我们的代码。

</details>


### [77] [Overloading Large Vision-Language Models for Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02961)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yangyang Guo,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息过载的LVLM越狱攻击方法，通过递归式图像-排版布局组合大量文本和多维图像攻击，显著提升跨模态处理复杂度，从而突破现有安全对齐机制。在开源和商业模型上分别达到88.6%和84.0%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），超越基线48.7%，且具有良好的跨模型迁移性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVLM越狱攻击设计简单（如短文本、分布外图像），随着LLM骨干网络和多模态机制的进步，这些攻击的可迁移性大幅下降。因此需要一种能有效突破现代LVLM安全对齐的新攻击方法。

Method: 提出信息过载方法，结合大量文本攻击与多维图像攻击，通过递归式图像-排版布局组织这些组件，指数级增加多模态信息复杂度，放大跨模态处理负担，从而破坏LVLM的安全对齐机制。

Result: 在开源模型上平均ASR达88.6%，在商业LVLM上平均ASR达84.0%，超过最佳基线48.7%。在开源替代模型上优化的提示可有效跨模型族迁移。探针实验发现复杂的图像-排版构图会诱发强化跨模态处理，降低模型生成拒绝响应的确定性。

Conclusion: 信息过载是一种实用且新兴的LVLM部署安全风险，强调了针对复杂多模态越狱输入需要更强的防御措施。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) exhibit remarkable vision-language capabilities and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications such as personal assistants, document analysis systems, and embodied agents. However, their dual-modal attack surfaces make them vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing LVLM jailbreaks rely on simple designs, e.g., short text and out-of-distribution images. Nevertheless, recent advancements in both large language model backbones and multimodal mechanisms undermine these attacks, particularly their transferability among model architectures. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel information overloading method that is equipped with both extensive text and multi-dimensional image attacks. These components are arranged in recursion-based image-typography layouts to exponentially increase multimodal information complexity. This overloading approach amplifies the cross-modal processing required, which undermines the safety alignment in LVLMs. Extensive experiments on both open-sourced and commercial LVLMs establish our method as a new state-of-the-art LVLM jailbreak attack. On open-source models, our method achieves an average ASR of 88.6%; on commercial LVLMs, it reaches an average ASR of 84.0%, exceeding the best baseline by 48.7%. Moreover, our prompts optimized on open-source surrogate models transfer effectively across model families. Beyond empirical results, we probe the safety-critical information flows within victim LVLMs. Our observations reveal that complex image-typography compositions induce intensified cross-modal processing and reduce the model's certainty in generating refusal responses. Together, these findings highlight information overloading as a practical and emerging safety risk for real-world LVLM deployments, underscoring the need for stronger defenses against complex multimodal jailbreak inputs.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）展现出卓越的视觉-语言能力，并越来越多地部署在个人助理、文档分析系统和具身智能体等实际应用中。然而，其双模态攻击面使其容易受到越狱攻击。现有的LVLM越狱依赖于简单设计，例如短文本和分布外图像。然而，大型语言模型骨干网络和多模态机制的最新进展削弱了这些攻击，尤其是它们在不同模型架构间的可迁移性。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种新颖的信息过载方法，该方法同时配备了大量文本攻击和多维图像攻击。这些组件以递归式图像-排版布局组织，以指数级增加多模态信息复杂度。这种过载方法放大了所需的跨模态处理，从而破坏了LVLM中的安全对齐。在开源和商业LVLM上的大量实验将我们的方法确立为新的最先进LVLM越狱攻击。在开源模型上，我们的方法达到了88.6%的平均攻击成功率（ASR）；在商业LVLM上，平均ASR达到84.0%，超过最佳基线48.7%。此外，我们在开源替代模型上优化的提示能够有效跨模型族迁移。除了实证结果，我们还探测了受害LVLM内部的安全关键信息流。我们的观察揭示，复杂的图像-排版构图会诱发强化的跨模态处理，并降低模型生成拒绝响应的确定性。综合来看，这些发现突出了信息过载作为实际LVLM部署中一种新兴安全风险，强调了针对复杂多模态越狱输入需要更强的防御措施。

</details>


### [78] [Parallelized Autoregressive Decoding for Omni-Modal Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02963)
*Wenzheng Zeng,Siyi Jiao,Chen Gao,Hwee Tou Ng,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种并行化自回归框架（PadCaptioner）用于密集视频描述，通过挖掘跨事件弱依赖关系重构因果依赖图，实现无损并行解码，在提升推理效率的同时保持或提升事件级视频描述的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于自回归视频大语言模型的密集视频描述方法逐token生成，推理效率低，且随着视频长度和事件密度增加可扩展性差，亟需一种能并行解码同时保持描述质量的方法。

Method: (1) 潜在全局规划机制：自动学习事件级结构，生成紧凑token编码全局跨事件因果关系，并自适应聚合事件级音视频语义；(2) 事件因子化并行解码机制：在保留事件内顺序解码以维持局部语义连贯性的同时，对跨事件弱依赖的token进行并行解码，平衡局部关注与全局事件间感知。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法在全模态事件定位与描述任务中均展现出显著的效率和性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过利用跨事件弱依赖关系重构因果依赖图，可以在保证无损生成的前提下实现密集视频描述的并行化解码，为自回归视频大语言模型的效率提升提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Dense video captioning aims to generate temporally grounded descriptions of video events, benefiting both event-level video understanding and generation. In this domain, autoregressive video large language models have emerged as a prevalent paradigm due to their strong generative and cross-modal modeling capacity. However, generating dense captions under the token-by-token paradigm severely limits inference efficiency and hinders scalability as video length and event density increase. In this work, we propose a parallelized autoregressive framework that not only improves generation efficiency but also enhances temporally grounded captioning performance. Our key insight is to exploit the weak local dependencies across temporally distinct events to restructure the causal dependency graph, thereby enabling lossless parallel generation. Specifically, tokens with weak cross-event dependencies can be decoded in parallel, while tightly coupled tokens within each event retain sequential decoding to preserve local semantic coherence. To realize this insight, we introduce two key components for lossless parallel decoding: (1) a latent global planning mechanism that automatically learns the event-level structure and produces compact tokens encoding global inter-event causality while adaptively aggregating event-level audio-visual semantics, guiding subsequent dependency restructuring and parallel decoding; and (2) an event-factorized parallel decoding mechanism that effectively balances local focus with global inter-event awareness. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the clear advantage of our approach in both efficiency and performance in omni-modal event grounding and captioning. Project website: https://github.com/showlab/PadCaptioner.

中文摘要翻译: 密集视频描述旨在生成带有时间定位的视频事件描述，对事件级视频理解和生成均有助益。在该领域中，自回归视频大语言模型因其强大的生成能力和跨模态建模能力而成为主流范式。然而，在逐token生成范式下生成密集描述严重限制了推理效率，并阻碍了随着视频长度和事件密度增加的可扩展性。本文提出一种并行化自回归框架，该框架不仅能提升生成效率，还能增强带时间定位的描述性能。我们的关键洞察是利用时间上不同事件之间存在的弱局部依赖关系来重构因果依赖图，从而实现无损并行生成。具体而言，跨事件弱依赖的token可以并行解码，而每个事件内部紧密耦合的token则保留顺序解码以维持局部语义连贯性。为实现这一洞察，我们引入了两个关键组件用于无损并行解码：(1) 潜在全局规划机制，自动学习事件级结构并生成紧凑token编码全局跨事件因果关系，同时自适应聚合事件级音视频语义，引导后续依赖重构和并行解码；(2) 事件因子化并行解码机制，有效平衡局部关注与全局跨事件感知。在多个基准上的实验表明，我们的方法在全模态事件定位与描述任务中在效率和性能上均具有明显优势。项目网站：https://github.com/showlab/PadCaptioner。

</details>


### [79] [Awakening Diffusion Transformers: Eliciting Stronger Generation and Understanding via Massive Activation Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02968)
*Chaofan Gan,Zicheng Zhao,Yuanpeng Tu,Xi Chen,Ziran Qin,Tieyuan Chen,Supavadee Aramvith,Mehrtash Harandi,Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散Transformer（DiT）中的大规模激活（MA）现象，发现其分布在图像token空间但集中在固定特征维度，且与AdaLN残差缩放因子对齐、受去噪时间步调控。基于此提出了无需训练的EMA框架，通过MA驱动细节引导（DG）提升生成质量，并通过MA调制表征提取（MREP）改善密集特征判别能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模激活（MA）在Transformer模型中已被广泛观察到，但其在扩散Transformer（DiT）中的结构和功能角色尚未被充分理解，因此需要系统性分析MA在DiT中的分布特性与作用机制。

Method: 提出EMA框架包含两个组件：(1) MA驱动细节引导（DG），通过抑制MA维度构建缺失细节的反事实预测，引导采样生成更精细的视觉细节，支持部分前向推理、与无分类器引导集成及token级局部DG；(2) MA调制表征提取（MREP），利用预训练AdaLN通道级调制降低MA方向主导性，并将空间归一化的MA图拼接以保留有用空间结构。

Result: 实验表明EMA在生成质量和表征能力上均能持续提升DiT的性能。具体发现：MA在图像token上空间分布广泛但集中在固定特征维度；这些维度与AdaLN残差缩放因子紧密对齐，主要受去噪时间步而非文本条件调制；MA对细粒度细节合成关键但对全局语义影响有限；原始DiT特征的共享高幅值方向使空间token间过于相似，削弱了密集特征判别能力。

Conclusion: MA在DiT中具有任务依赖的双重效应：生成中负责细节合成，理解中因共享方向削弱判别性。EMA框架通过统一利用MA作为调制信号，无需额外训练即可同时提升DiT的生成质量和表征能力。

Original Abstract: Massive Activations (MAs) have been widely observed in Transformer-based models, yet their structure and functional roles in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we systematically analyze MAs in representative DiTs and find that they are spatially distributed across image tokens while concentrated in a small set of fixed feature dimensions. We further show that these dimensions are closely aligned with AdaLN residual scaling factors and are primarily modulated by the denoising timestep rather than text conditions. This structure leads to two task-dependent effects: for generation, MAs are critical for fine-grained detail synthesis while having limited influence on global semantics; for understanding, their shared high-magnitude directions make raw DiT features overly similar across spatial tokens and weaken dense feature discrimination. Based on these findings, we introduce Eliciting Massive Activation (EMA), a training-free framework that leverages Massive Activations (MAs) as a unified modulation signal to improve both generative and representational capabilities of DiTs. For generation, EMA proposes MA-driven Detail G}uidance (DG), which suppresses MA dimensions to construct a detail-deficient counterfactual prediction and guides sampling toward finer visual details. DG further supports efficient partial-forward inference, integration with classifier-free guidance, and token-level Local DG for refining selected image regions. For understanding, EMA introduces MA-modulated REPresentation extraction (MREP), which uses pretrained AdaLN channel-wise modulation to reduce MA directional dominance and concatenates spatially normalized MA maps to preserve useful spatial structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMA consistently improves both the generation quality and representation capability of DiTs.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模激活（Massive Activations, MAs）已在基于Transformer的模型中被广泛观察到，但它们在扩散Transformer（Diffusion Transformers, DiTs）中的结构和功能角色尚未得到充分理解。在本工作中，我们系统性地分析了代表性DiT中的MA，发现它们在图像token上空间分布广泛，同时集中在少量固定的特征维度上。我们进一步证明这些维度与AdaLN残差缩放因子紧密对齐，且主要受去噪时间步而非文本条件调制。这种结构导致了两种任务依赖的效应：在生成方面，MA对细粒度细节合成至关重要，但对全局语义影响有限；在理解方面，其共享的高幅值方向使得原始DiT特征在空间token之间过于相似，从而削弱了密集特征的判别能力。基于这些发现，我们提出了激发大规模激活（Eliciting Massive Activation, EMA），一个无需训练的框架，利用MA作为统一的调制信号来同时提升DiT的生成能力和表征能力。在生成方面，EMA提出了MA驱动的细节引导（Detail Guidance, DG），通过抑制MA维度构建缺失细节的反事实预测，引导采样朝向更精细的视觉细节。DG还支持高效的部分前向推理、与无分类器引导的集成，以及用于精细化选定图像区域的token级局部DG。在理解方面，EMA引入了MA调制的表征提取（MA-modulated REPresentation extraction, MREP），利用预训练的AdaLN通道级调制来降低MA方向主导性，并拼接空间归一化的MA图以保留有用的空间结构。大量实验表明，EMA一致地提升了DiT的生成质量和表征能力。

</details>


### [80] [RePos: Relative-to-Absolute Output Factorization for Cross-Environment WiFi-Based 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02986)
*Zhangcheng Hou,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RePos框架，通过分解绝对姿态为根相对姿态与根位置估计，利用WiFi CSI实现跨环境鲁棒的3D人体姿态估计，在MM-Fi协议下MPJPE降低10-21%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于WiFi CSI的3D人体姿态估计方法在单一环境表现良好，但跨环境泛化能力差，原因是绝对姿态回归将人体结构与特定环境位置信息耦合，导致模型过拟合位置特征。

Method: 提出RePos因子化框架：(1) 将CSI特征分组为身体部位感知的潜在令牌，通过骨架引导建模得到根相对姿态；(2) 使用独立的基于幅度的网络通过可微空间分解模块估计根位置；(3) 两部分解耦训练，防止绝对位置监督影响结构分支。

Result: RePos在MM-Fi跨环境协议下MPJPE达254.4-296.1 mm，相比现有WiFi方法降低10-21%；在各类活动协议、留一环境划分和无泄漏小样本迁移中均表现一致；分析表明相对姿态表示具有位置无关性，而根定位保留环境依赖性。

Conclusion: 解耦绝对姿态为根相对姿态与根位置估计可有效学习环境不变的姿态表示，提升WiFi感知的跨环境泛化能力，结构分支与定位分支展现出不同的泛化行为。

Original Abstract: Device-free 3D human pose estimation using commodity WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) enables privacy-preserving and illumination-robust human sensing, but its deployment is limited by poor cross-environment generalization. Unlike images, CSI measurements do not have a spatially localized correspondence to body parts and are heavily affected by multipath propagation, causing models that regress absolute poses to entangle body structure with environment-specific location cues. Within a single environment this coupling is benign: an end-to-end absolute-pose variant, RePos-D, already achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on Person-in-WiFi-3D (86.9 mm MPJPE, a 3.4% gain over the previous best WiFi method, DT-Pose). Across environments, however, the same model overfits position and suffers significant performance degradation. We therefore propose RePos, a factorized framework that separates root-relative pose estimation from root localization. By preventing absolute-pose supervision from affecting the structure branch, RePos learns environment-invariant pose representations. Specifically, it groups CSI features into body-part-aware latent tokens that skeleton-guided modeling refines into the pose, while a separate amplitude-based network estimates the root position through a differentiable spatial-decomposition module. Under the strict MM-Fi cross-environment protocol, RePos achieves MPJPEs of 254.4-296.1 mm, a 10-21% reduction over existing WiFi-based methods. The improvement remains consistent across activity protocols, leave-one-environment-out splits, and leakage-free few-shot transfer. Analysis of the learned features shows that relative-pose representations remain largely position-agnostic, while root localization retains environment dependence, indicating distinct generalization behavior for structure and localization.

中文摘要翻译: 基于商用WiFi信道状态信息（CSI）的无设备3D人体姿态估计能够实现隐私保护且光照鲁棒的人体感知，但其部署受限于较差的跨环境泛化能力。与图像不同，CSI测量值与身体部位之间不存在空间上的局部对应关系，且受多径传播影响严重，导致回归绝对姿态的模型将人体结构与特定环境的位置线索纠缠在一起。在单一环境内，这种耦合是有利的：端到端的绝对姿态变体RePos-D在Person-in-WiFi-3D数据集上已取得最先进的精度（MPJPE为86.9 mm，相比此前最优WiFi方法DT-Pose提升3.4%）。然而，跨环境时同一模型会过拟合位置信息并出现显著的性能下降。因此，我们提出RePos，一个将根相对姿态估计与根位置定位解耦的因子化框架。通过防止绝对姿态监督影响结构分支，RePos学习到环境不变的姿态表示。具体而言，它将CSI特征分组为身体部位感知的潜在令牌，经骨架引导建模精化得到姿态；同时，独立的基于幅度的网络通过可微空间分解模块估计根位置。在严格的MM-Fi跨环境协议下，RePos的MPJPE达到254.4-296.1 mm，相比现有基于WiFi的方法降低了10-21%。该改进在各类活动协议、留一环境划分和无泄漏小样本迁移中均保持一致。对所学特征的分析表明，相对姿态表示在很大程度保持位置无关性，而根定位保留环境依赖性，表明结构分支与定位分支具有不同的泛化行为。

</details>


### [81] [Cross-device Collaborative Test-time Adaptation with Zeroth-order Optimization and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02988)
*Yu Mitsuzumi,Akisato Kimura,Yasuhiro Fujiwara,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合零阶优化（ZOO）和模型合并的方法，用于跨设备协同测试时自适应（CDC-TTA），使得资源受限设备也能在不进行反向传播的情况下执行TTA，并通过降维预处理策略增强两者协同效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应（TTA）方法大多依赖反向传播（BP）进行模型更新，这需要大量内存资源，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。为了解决这一问题，本文希望在跨设备协同TTA框架下，让资源受限设备也能参与模型自适应更新。

Method: (1) 引入零阶优化（ZOO），仅需前向传播即可估计梯度，避免资源密集的反向传播；(2) 结合模型合并技术，将多个设备共享的模型权重合并，并将合并系数作为优化目标，降低ZOO带来的高维优化难度；(3) 提出预处理策略，剪除模型内部非影响性权重并减少模型间信息冗余，增强ZOO与模型合并的协同效果。

Result: 在常见的图像损坏和风格迁移基准测试上，实验验证了该方法在资源受限条件下有效实现了测试时自适应。

Conclusion: 集成ZOO和模型合并能够有效解决资源受限设备上的TTA部署难题，预处理策略进一步提升了方法的协同效果和性能。

Original Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates domain shifts by using incoming test data to update a model on the fly. The majority of TTA methods require resource-intensive backpropagation (BP) for model updates, particularly demanding large memory sizes, which makes it infeasible to deploy them on resource-limited devices (e.g., edge devices). To address this issue, we integrate two different techniques, zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) and model merging, under the recently established cross-device collaborative TTA (CDC-TTA) framework, where the system is composed of a mixture of resource-abundant and resource-limited devices, and the model information (e.g., model weights obtained on each device) is shared across the devices. Our method is executable on resource-limited devices by introducing ZOO, which requires only forward processing and bypasses the resource-intensive BP optimization. Concurrently, to mitigate the high-dimensional optimization difficulty caused by the side effect of ZOO, we incorporate model merging of the shared multiple models and set the merge coefficients as the optimization objective, which successfully reduces the optimization dimension. In addition, to enhance the synergistic combination of ZOO and model merging, we propose a unique preprocessing strategy that trims intra-model non-influential weights and reduces the inter-model information redundancy. We empirically confirmed the effectiveness of our method using common corruption and style-transferred image benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时自适应（TTA）通过使用输入的测试数据动态更新模型来缓解领域偏移。大多数TTA方法需要资源密集的反向传播（BP）进行模型更新，尤其需要大内存容量，这使得它们难以部署在资源受限的设备（如边缘设备）上。为解决此问题，我们将零阶优化（ZOO）和模型合并两种不同技术整合到最近建立的跨设备协同TTA（CDC-TTA）框架中，该系统由资源充裕设备和资源受限设备混合组成，模型信息（如在每个设备上获得的模型权重）在设备间共享。我们的方法通过引入ZOO，只需前向处理即可，绕过了资源密集的BP优化，从而可在资源受限设备上执行。同时，为了缓解ZOO副作用导致的高维优化困难，我们引入对共享的多个模型进行模型合并，并将合并系数作为优化目标，成功降低了优化维度。此外，为了增强ZOO与模型合并的协同组合，我们提出了一种独特的预处理策略，修剪模型内部非影响性权重并减少模型间的信息冗余。我们使用常见的图像损坏和风格迁移基准实验验证了该方法的有效性。

</details>


### [82] [GuideMe: Multi-Domain Task Guidance and Intervention in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02991)
*Fang Liu,Jinpeng Chen,Ke Xu,Yuhao Liu,Huankang Guan,Xudong Lu,Bo Yang,Gerhard Hancke,Rui Liu,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GuideMe，首个支持闭环交互式任务指导的多领域流式视频基准，包含2458个视频和47775个交互样本，用于训练和评估多模态大模型在实时程序指导中的表现。实验发现现有MLLMs在提供指令方面表现优异，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面存在显著不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在离线视频理解方面表现出色，但能否作为实时程序教练（持续监控执行、检测错误、提供纠正指导的闭环交互）尚不清楚。缺乏一个支持训练和评估MLLMs在闭环交互式任务指导中能力的多领域流式视频基准。

Method: 构建GuideMe基准，包含2458个视频（223.7小时，涵盖烹饪、物体操作、日常生活指导、健身等多领域）和47775个交互样本（包括下一步指令、完成反馈、错误检测、纠正指导）。同时设计三组件评估框架：时序语义二分图匹配（序列级对齐）、行为分类（干预时机）、LLM-as-a-Judge（内容质量）。

Result: 现有MLLMs在提供指令方面表现优异，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面持续失败，存在显著的性能不对称性。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs距离作为实时程序教练仍有较大差距，主要瓶颈在于错误检测和纠正反馈能力。GuideMe基准可用于推动该方向的研究。

Original Abstract: While multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at offline video understanding, an interesting question of how far they are from serving as a real-time procedural coach remains unknown. Such a role typically requires an MLLM to continuously monitor the execution, detect mistakes, and provide corrective guidance in a closed-loop interaction. In this paper, we construct GuideMe, the first multi-domain benchmark for streaming video that supports training and evaluation of MLLMs for closed-loop interactive task guidance. It comprises 2,458 videos spanning 223.7 hours across diverse domains (\eg, cooking, object manipulation, daily-life guidance, and fitness), with 47,775 interaction samples covering next-step instructions, completion feedback, error detection, and corrective guidance. To evaluate existing models on GuideMe, we design a three-component assessment framework to measure the capabilities of representative MLLMs, which consists of temporal-semantic bipartite matching for sequence-level alignment, behavioral classification for intervention timing, and LLM-as-a-Judge for content quality. Extensive experiments highlight a critical performance asymmetry: despite excelling at providing instructions, existing MLLMs consistently fail to identify execution errors and respond with corrective feedback. Code and data are released at https://fawnliu.github.io/project/guideme.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在离线视频理解方面表现出色，但它们距离作为实时程序教练还有多远仍是一个未知的有趣问题。这一角色通常要求MLLM持续监控执行过程、检测错误，并在闭环交互中提供纠正性指导。在本文中，我们构建了GuideMe——首个支持训练和评估MLLMs进行闭环交互式任务指导的多领域流式视频基准。该基准包含2458个视频，总时长223.7小时，涵盖烹饪、物体操作、日常生活指导和健身等多个领域，以及47775个交互样本，覆盖下一步指令、完成反馈、错误检测和纠正性指导。为了评估现有模型在GuideMe上的表现，我们设计了一个三组件评估框架，用于衡量代表性MLLMs的能力，该框架包括：用于序列级对齐的时序语义二分图匹配、用于干预时机的行为分类，以及用于内容质量的LLM-as-a-Judge。大量实验突显了一个关键的性能不对称性：尽管现有MLLMs在提供指令方面表现出色，但它们始终无法识别执行错误并提供纠正性反馈。代码和数据已发布在https://fawnliu.github.io/project/guideme。

</details>


### [83] [VISTA: Auditing Semantic Divergence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02995)
*Junchi Liao,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA，一种黑盒跨模型审计方法，通过语义熵与分布散度检测视觉语言模型中的视觉概念条件化偏差，发现1.2%的高可疑案例及选择性拒绝模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在面对特定视觉概念（如人口特征、公司标识、意识形态符号）时会产生条件化分歧，即模型输出与同输入的其他模型显著不同。这类行为无法通过纯文本审计检测，因为视觉概念无法像文本token那样被隔离或替换。

Method: VISTA（Visual Inconsistency Screening Through Analysis）是一种黑盒跨模型审计方法，结合语义熵（semantic entropy）与基于分布的散度（distribution-based divergence）来标记模型特定的异常行为。

Result: 在控制实验中，通过在小型偏置数据集上微调三个视觉语言模型植入条件化立场，VISTA成功检测到这些偏差。对6个视觉语言模型在19个主题上的审计，VISTA发现了142个高可疑案例（占比1.2%），并识别出选择性拒绝（selective refusal）这一此前未被报道的分歧模式——模型对不同群体的人口统计查询拒绝率从0%到65%不等。

Conclusion: VISTA能够有效检测视觉语言模型中难以通过文本审计发现的视觉概念条件化偏差，并揭示了选择性拒绝这一新的偏差模式，为模型安全审计提供了重要工具。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models can exhibit visual concept-conditioned divergence: given images containing demographic features, corporate logos, or ideological symbols, some models produce unusually uniform responses that differ from what peer models say about the same input. These behaviors evade text-only audits because visual concepts cannot be isolated or substituted the way text tokens can. We present VISTA (Visual Inconsistency Screening Through Analysis), a black-box cross-model audit that couples semantic entropy with distribution-based divergence to flag model-specific anomalies. In a controlled study, we implant concept-conditioned stances in three VLMs via fine-tuning on small biased datasets and confirm that VISTA detects them. Auditing six VLMs across 19 topics, VISTA surfaces 142 high-suspicion cases (1.2%) and identifies selective refusal as a previously unreported divergence pattern, where models refuse demographic queries at rates varying from 0 to 65% across groups.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型会表现出视觉概念条件化分歧：给定包含人口特征、公司标识或意识形态符号的图像时，部分模型会产生异常统一的输出，且与同行模型对同一输入的判断截然不同。这些行为无法通过纯文本审计检测，因为视觉概念无法像文本token那样被隔离或替换。我们提出VISTA（Visual Inconsistency Screening Through Analysis），一种黑盒跨模型审计方法，将语义熵与基于分布的分散度相结合，用于标记模型特定的异常。在控制实验中，我们通过在小型偏置数据集上对三个视觉语言模型进行微调来植入概念条件化立场，并验证了VISTA能够检测到这些偏差。对六个视觉语言模型在19个主题上的审计表明，VISTA发现了142个高可疑案例（占1.2%），并识别出选择性拒绝这一此前未被报道的分歧模式——模型对不同群体的人口统计查询拒绝率从0%到65%不等。

</details>


### [84] [CONFLUX: A Latent Diusion Model for 3D Chest-CT Synthesis with RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02998)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CONFLUX是一种用于胸部CT的3D潜扩散模型，结合变分自编码器与整流流Transformer，通过结构化放射学元数据实现可控生成，并利用在线强化学习后训练提升临床属性控制可靠性，最终发布模型及约20万张合成CT数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D医学图像生成模型在高保真度、原生3D生成和对指定条件的忠实控制方面难以兼得，亟需一种既能生成高质量3D体积又能精确控制临床属性的生成方法。

Method: 提出CONFLUX模型：(1) 使用3D变分自编码器压缩CT体积；(2) 采用整流流Transformer在潜空间中生成；(3) 通过自适应层归一化对结构化放射学元数据（18种异常发现、性别、年龄、重建核）进行条件控制；(4) 引入在线强化学习后训练阶段（组相对策略优化），以分类器对生成体积的发现恢复可靠性为奖励，强化条件控制的保真度。

Result: 在三平面FID指标上显著优于强基线（32.3 vs. MAISI的74.6）；后训练阶段去除了相对于真实扫描可靠性的47%的差距（由独立分类器评估）。

Conclusion: CONFLUX实现了高保真、可控的3D医学图像生成，后训练强化学习有效提升了条件控制的可靠性，并开源模型及大规模合成数据集以推动研究。

Original Abstract: Controllable generative models of 3D medical images can synthesize volumes with specified clinical attributes, but this demands samples that are simultaneously high-fidelity, natively 3D, and faithful to the requested conditioning. We present CONFLUX, a latent diffusion model for chest computed tomography (CT): a 3D variational autoencoder compresses each volume, and a rectified-flow transformer generates in the latent space. Generation is conditioned on structured radiological metadata (18 abnormality findings, sex, age, and reconstruction kernel) through adaptive layer normalization. The model leads strong volumetric baselines on tri-planar Frechet distance (FID 32.3 vs. 74.6 for MAISI) while exposing direct control over clinical attributes. To strengthen that control we add an online reinforcement-learning post-training stage (group-relative policy optimization) that rewards how reliably a classifier recovers the requested findings from each generated volume. Judged by a separate, independent classifier, post-training removes 47% of the shortfall relative to real-scan reliability. We release the model and a ~200k synthetic chest-CT dataset with conditioning metadata spanning a wide variety of clinical findings.

中文摘要翻译: 可控的三维医学图像生成模型能够合成具有指定临床属性的体数据，但这要求样本同时具备高保真度、原生三维结构以及对给定条件的忠实服从。我们提出CONFLUX——一种用于胸部计算机断层扫描（CT）的潜扩散模型：一个3D变分自编码器对每个体数据进行压缩，一个整流流Transformer在潜空间中执行生成过程。生成过程通过自适应层归一化，以结构化放射学元数据（18种异常发现、性别、年龄和重建核）为条件。该模型在三平面Fr\'echet距离上大幅领先强基线（FID 32.3 vs. MAISI的74.6），同时实现了对临床属性的直接控制。为强化这一控制，我们添加了一个在线强化学习后训练阶段（组相对策略优化），以分类器从每个生成体数据中恢复所请求发现的可靠性作为奖励。经独立分类器评估，后训练消除了相对于真实扫描可靠性的47%的差距。我们发布了该模型以及一个约20万张合成胸部CT数据集，其中包含覆盖多种临床发现的条件元数据。

</details>


### [85] [REAL-OW: Rehearsal-free Open World Object Detection with Low-Rank Adaptation and Dual-Stage Objectness Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03004)
*Huazhong Zhang,Xiaowen Fu,Yang Zhang,Linlin Shen,Jinbao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REAL-OW，首个基于LoRA协作适配器架构的无重放开放世界目标检测框架，通过通用适配器（GA）和特定适配器（SA）解耦增量知识，并引入双阶段目标建模（DSOM）解决无重放下的表征漂移问题，在检测精度和未知发现上超越现有重放方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放世界目标检测方法严重依赖 exemplar replay（样本重放）来缓解灾难性遗忘，但在实际应用中存储原始数据会面临数据访问限制、数据泄露风险以及显著的内存开销问题，亟需一种无需重放的解决方案。

Method: 提出REAL-OW框架：(1) 基于LoRA的协作适配器架构，骨干网络部署通用适配器（GA）实现跨任务通用表征的显著性感知精炼，解码器部署特定适配器（SA）为任务特定知识提供正交存储；(2) 双阶段目标建模（DSOM），交替进行特征聚合和边界巩固以稳定目标分布，同时保持已知与未知类别的分离；(3) 校准高斯负对数似然（CG-NLL）距离，专门针对无重放设置下分散的特征分布进行设计。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，REAL-OW达到了最先进性能，在检测精度和未知发现能力上均超越现有的样本重放方法。

Conclusion: REAL-OW为无重放开放世界目标检测建立了新的基线，证明了通过精心设计的适配器架构和双阶段建模，可以在完全不依赖原始数据存储的情况下，同时实现增量学习和未知目标检测的最优性能。

Original Abstract: Open-World Object Detection (OWOD) requires detectors to identify previously unseen objects as unknown and incrementally incorporate them into the set of known categories, while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Existing frameworks rely heavily on exemplar replay to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but in some real applications, storing raw data conflicts with data access restrictions and leads to data exposure risks, while incurring significant memory overhead. In this paper, we propose REAL-OW, a novel rehearsal-free framework that decouples incremental knowledge through a collaborative adapter architecture based on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Specifically, we deploy General Adapters (GAs) in the backbone to enable the significance-aware refinement of cross-task universal representations, while Specific Adapters (SAs) in the decoder provide orthogonal storage for task-specific expertise. To resolve representation drift in objectness modeling under rehearsal-free constraints, we introduce Dual-Stage Objectness Modeling (DSOM), which alternates between feature aggregation and boundary consolidation to stabilize objectness distributions while maintaining the separation between known and unknown categories. Furthermore, DSOM is supported by a Calibrated Gaussian Negative Log-Likelihood (CG-NLL) distance tailored for the dispersed feature distributions inherent in rehearsal-free settings. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that REAL-OW achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing existing exemplar replay methods in both detection precision and unknown discovery. Our approach establishes a new baseline for rehearsal-free OWOD.

中文摘要翻译: 开放世界目标检测（OWOD）要求检测器能够将未见过的目标识别为未知类别，并逐步将其纳入已知类别集合，同时保持已有知识。现有框架严重依赖样本重放来缓解灾难性遗忘，但在某些实际应用中，存储原始数据与数据访问限制相冲突，并导致数据暴露风险，同时带来显著的内存开销。本文提出REAL-OW，一种新颖的无重放框架，通过基于低秩适配（LoRA）的协作适配器架构解耦增量知识。具体而言，我们在骨干网络中部署通用适配器（GA），实现跨任务通用表征的显著性感知精炼，同时在解码器中部署特定适配器（SA），为任务特定专长提供正交存储。为解决无重放约束下目标建模中的表征漂移问题，我们引入双阶段目标建模（DSOM），交替进行特征聚合和边界巩固，以稳定目标分布同时保持已知与未知类别的分离。此外，DSOM由专门针对无重放设置下分散特征分布设计的校准高斯负对数似然（CG-NLL）距离支持。广泛评估表明，REAL-OW达到了最先进性能，在检测精度和未知发现上均超越现有的样本重放方法。我们的方法为无重放OWOD建立了新的基线。

</details>


### [86] [PosterHarness: Turning Scientific Poster Generation into an Auditable Instruction-Following Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03006)
*Tianyi Yang,Dawei Fu,Youpeng Wu,Zixun Kou,Linrui Chen,Ruobing Jiang,Zijian Wang,Qiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出POSTERHARNESS，一种将学术海报生成重构为可审计指令遵循任务的框架，通过占位符优先契约强制模型仅做视觉摘要设计（排版、阅读路径、色彩、背景），禁止生成数据图形，并基于12篇论文的基准测试验证了该契约能消除AI合成图形（34→0），同时提供了故障分类与对比分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本丰富的图像模型能够设计海报级布局，但缺乏衡量其是否遵守科学传播契约（标签可读、宽高比合规、不捏造科学图形）的方法，尤其是模型常混淆视觉摘要设计与数据图形生成两大任务，导致产生虚假科学图形。

Method: 提出POSTERHARNESS审计框架，核心是占位符优先契约：模型负责排版、阅读路径、色彩、背景等视觉摘要设计，但每个图形区域必须为空标签占位符；由确定性合成器在检测到的坐标处插入真实源论文图形。该框架使占位符数量与ID准确率、空白度、宽高比合规性、拒绝合成图形、公开文本卫生、源图形来源等指标均可测量，失败被记录为显式拒绝而非隐藏。

Result: (i) 反事实探测显示占位符契约将VLM计数的合成图形从34降至0；(ii) 故障分类识别出四类阻塞契约：占位符几何、占位符QA、模板评判器、公开文本；(iii) 与Paper2Poster对比：POSTERHARNESS产出更高分辨率伪影、更低空白画布分数、更强VLM视觉偏好，确定基线保留更多PosterQuiz式信息且运行更快，此为体制刻画而非优劣声明。

Conclusion: POSTERHARNESS为评估科学海报生成中的指令遵循能力提供了可审计、可复用的框架，有效杜绝了模型生成虚假科学图形，并为后续研究提供了故障分类体系和对比基准。
