### [141] [Modeling the Impact of Visual Brand Language on Attention, Object Recognition, and Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02929)
*Rachel F. Heaton,John E. Hummel*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文基于JIM物体识别模型和LISA类比推理模型，模拟研究了视觉品牌语言对物体识别、注意力分配和功能信息检索的影响，发现品牌信息会吸引注意力并减慢对物体功能类别的识别，品牌化程度越大影响越显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究视觉品牌语言如何影响用户识别和理解物体功能身份的能力，以及这种影响对产品可用性和用户体验的潜在意义。

Method: 基于JIM物体识别模型和LISA类比推理模型构建经验支持的建模框架，模拟视觉品牌语言对物体识别、注意力分配和功能信息检索的影响。

Result: 模拟结果表明，品牌信息会捕获注意力，并可能减慢使用者对物体功能类别的识别速度，品牌化程度越大，这种效应越显著。

Conclusion: 视觉品牌语言会影响用户对物体的认知加工，这对设计物体的可用性和用户体验具有潜在影响。

Original Abstract: Visual brand language is the set of visual properties that convey brand identity for a product. What is the impact of visual brand language on a person's ability to recognize and understand the functional identity of an object? Using an empirically supported modeling framework based on the JIM model of object recognition and the LISA model of analogical inference, we simulated the impact of visual brand language on object recognition, the allocation of attention, and retrieval of functional information about objects. Our simulations predict that brand information captures attention and can slow recognition of an object's functional category, with greater degrees of branding causing larger effects. These results have potential implications for the usability and experience of designed objects.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉品牌语言是传达产品品牌标识的一系列视觉属性。视觉品牌语言如何影响人们识别和理解物体功能身份的能力？本文基于JIM物体识别模型和LISA类比推理模型构建了一个经验支持的建模框架，模拟了视觉品牌语言对物体识别、注意力分配以及物体功能信息检索的影响。我们的模拟预测表明，品牌信息会捕获注意力，并可能减慢对物体功能类别的识别速度，品牌化程度越大，影响越显著。这些结果对设计物体的可用性和用户体验具有潜在启示意义。

</details>


### [142] [Shunting Inhibition and Dendritic Branching Shape Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03556)
*Houman Safaai,Maceo Richards,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于电导的树突网络中的局部学习机制，揭示了突触可塑性中梯度分解为局部eligibility × 路径特定误差项的因子化形式，并检验了在受限体细胞反馈下，分流抑制是否能重塑区室误差场以改善信用分配。实验表明，尽管分流抑制有一定帮助，但反馈场保真度仍是主要瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索生物神经元中树突分支结构下的信用分配机制，特别是当体细胞反馈受限于局部信息时，分流抑制能否帮助改善区室级别的误差信号压缩与传播。

Method: 构建基于电导的树突网络模型（含E/I突触库、分流抑制、树状分支-体细胞耦合），理论推导梯度的因子化分解（局部eligibility × 路径特定误差），并设计多种反馈模式（全局标量、每体细胞、低秩、路径结构）进行实验验证，结合精确梯度重建、路径增益等诊断工具分析分流抑制的作用机制。

Result: 1) 精确梯度重建验证了因子化分解的正确性；2) 分流抑制能重塑区室误差场以匹配特定反馈模式；3) 在非负电导和每体细胞5因子反馈条件下，shunting LocalCA在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和figure-ground MNIST上比匹配的反向传播低5-6个百分点，表明反馈场保真度仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: E/I电导、分流抑制和树突分支能够重塑局部学习中的信用信号几何结构，但受限的体细胞反馈导致反馈场保真度不足，限制了局部学习与反向传播之间的性能差距。

Original Abstract: Biological neurons assign credit across branching dendrites, where synaptic drive, dendritic conductance, local voltage, and somatic teaching signals interact to shape synaptic plasticity. We study conductance-based dendritic networks with E/I synapse banks, shunting inhibition, and tree-structured branch-to-soma coupling, and examine when restricted somatic feedback can approximate compartment-specific backpropagated errors. Exact gradients factor into local eligibility x compartment error terms: the eligibility uses presynaptic activity, driving force, and input resistance, whereas the fast non-local term is a path-specific error obtained by transporting a soma error through dendritic gains. This factorization turns local learning into a credit-signal compression problem. We test the hypothesis that shunting inhibition benefits learning under these constraints when it reshapes the compartment-error field to better match global scalar, per-soma, low-rank, or path-structured feedback. Exact-gradient reconstruction verifies the factorization; path-gain, rank, broadcast-fidelity, inhibition-intervention, and transported-error-oracle diagnostics support the proposed mechanism. Under nonnegative conductances and per-soma 5-factor (5F) feedback, shunting LocalCA remains 5--6 percentage points below matched backpropagation on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and figure-ground MNIST, indicating that feedback-field fidelity remains a major bottleneck. These results show how E/I conductance, shunting inhibition, and dendritic branching can reshape credit-signal geometry in restricted local learning.

中文摘要翻译: 生物神经元在分支树突上分配信用，突触驱动、树突电导、局部电压和体细胞教学信号相互作用以塑造突触可塑性。我们研究了具有E/I突触库、分流抑制和树状分支-体细胞耦合的基于电导的树突网络，并检验了受限体细胞反馈何时能近似区室特异性反向传播误差。精确梯度可分解为局部资格因子×区室误差项：资格因子利用突触前活动、驱动力和输入电阻，而快速非局部项是通过树突增益传输体细胞误差获得的路径特定误差。这种因子化将局部学习转化为信用信号压缩问题。我们检验了以下假设：当分流抑制重塑区室误差场以更好地匹配全局标量、每体细胞、低秩或路径结构化反馈时，它能在这些约束下有益于学习。精确梯度重建验证了因子化分解；路径增益、秩、广播保真度、抑制干预和传输误差预言诊断支持了所提出的机制。在非负电导和每体细胞五因子（5F）反馈条件下，shunting LocalCA在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和figure-ground MNIST上仍比匹配的反向传播低5—6个百分点，表明反馈场保真度仍然是主要瓶颈。这些结果展示了E/I电导、分流抑制和树突分支如何重塑受限局部学习中的信用信号几何结构。

</details>


### [143] [Microsecond-precision sound localization emerges from slow equilibrium dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03890)
*Toshio Irino*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经群体动力学稳定平衡态的ITD编码模型，替代了经典的Jeffress场所编码理论，通过跨频率通道的兴奋-抑制相互作用实现微秒级精度，而不依赖于精确的延迟线或定时抑制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决听觉系统中双耳感知对动态声学线索响应迟缓，却又能实现微秒级ITD敏感性的共存矛盾，弥补经典Jeffress场所编码理论无法解释这一现象的不足。

Method: 将ITD表示为神经群体动力学的稳定平衡态，通过跨频率通道的兴奋性和抑制性相互作用生成群体信号，驱动动力系统收敛到对应估计ITD的平衡点。

Result: 模型在依赖相对缓慢的时序动力学条件下实现了微秒级精度，并复现了频率依赖的最佳延迟分布等关键生理观测结果，且无需显式延迟线或精确计时的抑制机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为慢速神经动力学如何产生精确的ITD敏感性提供了一种潜在解释，挑战了经典的Jeffress场所编码框架。

Original Abstract: Precise sound localization relies on microsecond sensitivity to interaural time differences (ITDs), yet binaural perception exhibits sluggish tracking of dynamic acoustic cues. How these properties coexist remains unresolved. Here, ITD is represented as a stable equilibrium of neural population dynamics rather than by the classical place-coding framework originally proposed by Jeffress in 1948. In this framework, excitatory and inhibitory interactions across frequency channels generate a population signal that drives a dynamical system toward an equilibrium corresponding to the estimated ITD. Despite relying on relatively slow temporal dynamics, the model achieves microsecond-level precision and reproduces key physiological observations, including frequency-dependent best-delay distributions, without requiring explicit delay lines or precisely timed inhibition. These findings provide a potential explanation for how precise ITD sensitivity can arise from slow neural dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的声源定位依赖于对双耳时间差（ITD）的微秒级灵敏度，然而双耳感知对动态声学线索的响应却显得迟缓。这两种特性如何共存至今仍未解决。本文提出，ITD由神经群体动力学的稳定平衡态表示，而非经典Jeffress在1948年提出的场所编码框架。在该框架中，跨频率通道的兴奋性和抑制性相互作用生成群体信号，驱动动力系统收敛到对应估计ITD的平衡点。尽管依赖相对缓慢的时序动力学，该模型仍能实现微秒级精度，并复现了频率依赖的最佳延迟分布等关键生理观测结果，且无需显式延迟线或精确计时的抑制机制。这些发现为慢速神经动力学如何产生精确的ITD敏感性提供了潜在解释。

</details>


### [144] [Learning Biophysical Models of Large-Scale Multineuronal Data to Enable Precise Neurostimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04063)
*Amrith Lotlikar,Ian Christopher Tanoh,Praful Vasireddy,Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Ramandeep Vilkhu,Michael Sommeling,A. J. Phillips,Alexander Sher,Alan Litke,Scott W. Linderman,E. J. Chichilnisky,Subhasish Mitra*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可微分生物物理仿真和仿真推断的框架，能够从高密度微电极阵列（MEA）的细胞外记录中快速推断霍奇金-赫胥黎（HH）模型参数，从而预测神经对电刺激的响应，在猕猴视网膜实验中以仅几分钟记录数据实现了90.6%的刺激响应预测准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统HH模型参数拟合依赖细胞内记录，侵入性强且通量低，难以大规模捕获神经元的几何和细胞特异性属性；而MEA虽可高通量记录细胞外信号，但HH模型复杂度过高，此前无法仅从细胞外数据中可靠地进行生物物理参数推断。

Method: 利用可微分生物物理仿真（differentiable biophysical simulation）结合基于仿真的推断（simulation-based inference）框架，从MEA细胞外记录中提取设计的特征，快速推断HH模型参数。

Result: 在30微米间距512电极阵列的离体猕猴视网膜实验中，收集了数百小时的刺激与记录数据；该框架使用仅几分钟记录拟合的HH模型，预测未见过的多电极刺激响应，准确率达到90.6%，替代了数小时的刺激测试。

Conclusion: 该框架可快速从细胞外MEA数据中推断HH参数，实现高精度神经刺激响应预测，为转化神经工程中神经刺激模式的个性化预测提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multi-compartment Hodgkin-Huxley (HH) models provide a principled framework for predicting neural dynamics and responses to electrical stimulation. However, fitting HH biophysical parameters typically requires intracellular recordings, which are invasive and low-throughput, limiting the ability to capture the geometry and cell-specific properties of many neurons in a given neural circuit. Multi-electrode arrays (MEAs) offer a scalable alternative - high-density extracellular measurements from full neural populations, but HH model complexity has so far precluded reliable biophysical inference from extracellular data alone. Here, we introduce a framework to rapidly infer HH parameters from designed features of extracellular MEA measurements by leveraging differentiable biophysical simulation and simulation-based inference, unlocking a wide range of downstream applications. In this work, we focus on a central goal of translational neuroengineering: predicting neural spiking responses to candidate neurostimulation patterns that would take hours to measure clinically. To validate our approach, we collected hundreds of hours of stimulation and recording data from isolated macaque retina with a 30 um-pitch 512-electrode array. Our framework predicted previously unseen multi-electrode stimulation responses with 90.6% accuracy using HH models fit from only a few minutes of recording, replacing hours of stimulus testing.

中文摘要翻译: 多隔室霍奇金-赫胥黎（HH）模型为预测神经动力学及对电刺激的响应提供了原理性框架。然而，拟合HH生物物理参数通常需要细胞内记录，这种方法具有侵入性且通量低，限制了捕获给定神经回路中许多神经元的几何和细胞特异性属性的能力。微电极阵列（MEA）提供了一种可扩展的替代方案——对完整神经群体进行高密度细胞外测量，但HH模型的复杂性迄今阻碍了仅从细胞外数据进行可靠的生物物理推断。本文引入了一个框架，通过利用可微分生物物理仿真和基于仿真的推断，从MEA细胞外测量的设计特征中快速推断HH参数，从而解锁广泛的下游应用。在本工作中，我们聚焦于转化神经工程的一个核心目标：预测神经对候选神经刺激模式的尖峰响应，这在临床上需要数小时的测量。为验证该方法，我们使用30微米间距512电极阵列从离体猕猴视网膜收集了数百小时的刺激和记录数据。我们的框架利用仅从几分钟记录中拟合的HH模型，预测了未见过的多电极刺激响应，准确率达到90.6%，替代了数小时的刺激测试。

</details>


### [145] [Beyond DSA: Conjugacy-based Comparison of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04493)
*Prakhar Godara,Pang Shiang Tay,Marcelo G. Mattar*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文指出DSA方法中正交对齐既非拓扑共轭的必要条件也非充分条件，并提出了基于共轭的相似性分析（CSA），通过对齐由候选状态空间双射诱导的变换（而非任意正交矩阵）来更准确地判断动力系统是否实现相同计算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DSA方法使用正交相似变换来对齐Koopman算子以判断动力系统是否计算等价，但正交对齐与拓扑共轭之间存在根本性偏差，需要更准确的相似性度量方法。

Method: 提出共轭相似性分析（CSA），将对齐限制为由候选状态空间双射诱导的变换矩阵，而非任意正交矩阵；并证明CSA拟合的对齐是候选双射对应的组合算子在有限数据下的投影。

Result: 理论上证明了正交对齐既不必要也不充分用于拓扑共轭；通过控制实验展示了当观测字典显式或隐式从数据中选取时，CSA与DSA的关键差异。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的相似性度量必须满足特定条件才能可靠地识别计算系统之间的共轭关系，CSA为此提供了更严格的框架。

Original Abstract: Comparing whether two dynamical systems implement the same computation despite differences in coordinates or measurements is a central problem in neuroscience and machine learning. Dynamical Similarity Analysis [DSA; Ostrow et al., 2023] addresses this problem by aligning finite-dimensional Koopman approximations through an orthogonal similarity transformation. Here we show that orthogonal alignment is neither necessary nor sufficient for topological conjugacy: conjugate systems may require a non-orthogonal basis-transfer matrix that DSA cannot capture, while non-conjugate systems may have orthogonally equivalent Koopman operators that DSA fails to distinguish. We use this observation to formulate Conjugacy-based Similarity Analysis (CSA), which restricts alignments to those induced by candidate state-space bijections rather than arbitrary orthogonal matrices. We prove that CSA's fitted alignment is the finite-data projection of the composition operator associated with the candidate bijection, and use controlled examples to show why this distinction matters when observable dictionaries are chosen explicitly or implicitly from data. These results clarify what Koopman-based similarity measures must ensure to support claims of identifying conjugacies between computational systems.

中文摘要翻译: 比较两个动力系统是否在坐标或测量方式不同的情况下实现相同计算，是神经科学和机器学习中的一个核心问题。动力相似性分析[DSA; Ostrow et al., 2023]通过正交相似变换对齐有限维Koopman近似来解决这一问题。本文证明，正交对齐对于拓扑共轭既非必要条件也非充分条件：共轭系统可能需要DSA无法捕捉的非正交基转移矩阵，而非共轭系统可能拥有DSA无法区分的正交等价Koopman算子。基于这一发现，我们提出了基于共轭的相似性分析（CSA），将对齐限制为由候选状态空间双射诱导的变换，而非任意正交矩阵。我们证明CSA拟合的对齐是候选双射对应组合算子在有限数据下的投影，并通过受控实例展示当观测字典显式或隐式从数据中选取时，这一区分的重要性。这些结果阐明了基于Koopman算子的相似性度量必须满足何种条件才能支持识别计算系统之间共轭关系的论断。

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Auditing the Audit: Five Failure Modes in Benchmark-Validity Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02586)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出基于扰动的构念效度审计本身是脆弱的，其结论可能被实现细节无声地操纵。作者定义了五类流水线故障（F1-F5），并通过一个两模型、五基准的自我审计案例展示所有单元均未通过统一六点尽职调查门控，无法达到确认性结论。该门控被定位为担保级证据的扣留与披露协议，而非基准效度裁决的途径。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有治理框架要求AI提供者和审计者提供文档化的评估证据，基于扰动的构念效度审计是常见形式。但作者认为这类审计本身很脆弱——读者无法从报告数字中看到的实现细节可能无声地制造出审计结论，存在可信度危机。

Method: 提出五类流水线故障分类（F1-F5）作为非穷尽性的起始分类法，并在两个开放权重指令微调模型上使用五个安全基准进行自我审计。设计统一的六点尽职调查门控作为评估框架，检查每个实验单元是否达到确认性标准。

Result: 在统一六点尽职调查门控下，所有实验单元均落入非确认性桶中，无一达到确认性水平。这表明即使是在标准安全基准上的自我审计，也存在大量未被报告的实现细节问题。

Conclusion: 作者将六点门控定位为担保级证据的扣留与披露协议，作为经典构念效度证据的补充而非替代。该框架旨在提高审计透明度和可信度，而非提供基准效度裁决的途径。

Original Abstract: Governance frameworks ask AI providers and auditors for documented evaluation evidence, and perturbation-based construct-validity audits are a common form of that evidence. We argue the audits are themselves fragile: their conclusions can be silently manufactured by implementation details that readers cannot see in the reported numbers. We name five classes of pipeline failure and demonstrate each in a self-audit over safety benchmarks and open-weight instruction-tuned models. Under a unified six-point due-diligence gate, every cell lands in a non-confirmatory bucket, and no cell reaches confirmatory. The evidence here is a single two-model, five-benchmark case study, and F1--F5 is an illustrative, deliberately non-exhaustive starting taxonomy -- not a comprehensive partition of audit failures. We position the gate as a withholding and disclosure protocol for assurance-grade evidence, supplementary to (not a replacement for) classical construct-validity evidence, and not as a route to benchmark-validity verdicts.

中文摘要翻译: 治理框架要求AI提供者和审计者提供文档化的评估证据，基于扰动的构念效度审计是这类证据的常见形式。我们认为审计本身是脆弱的：它们的结论可能被读者在报告数字中无法看到的实现细节无声地制造出来。我们命名了五类流水线故障，并在一个自我审计中通过安全基准和开放权重指令微调模型展示了每一种故障。在统一的六点尽职调查门控下，每个单元都落入非确认性桶中，且没有任何单元达到确认性。这里的证据是一个单一的两模型、五基准案例研究，F1-F5是一个说明性的、刻意非穷尽性的起始分类法——并非审计故障的全面划分。我们将该门控定位为担保级证据的扣留与披露协议，作为经典构念效度证据的补充（而非替代），并且不作为通往基准效度裁决的途径。

</details>


### [147] [Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models for Electricity Price Forecasting: Contamination Risk, Distributional Shifts, and Covariate Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02623)
*Zhenghua Pan,Ahmed Aziz Ezzat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在协变量驱动的非平稳场景（以电力价格预测为测试平台）中的泛化能力，发现TSFMs竞争力强但依赖协变量支持，且与领域专用方法具有互补性，简单集成效果显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在零样本预测中表现出色，但在协变量驱动、非平稳环境下的泛化能力尚未被充分探索。电力价格预测因其复杂的时间依赖、分布偏移以及对结构和上下文信息的强依赖，成为评估TSFMs的理想测试平台。

Method: 提出一个双数据集基准评估框架，用于电力价格预测，以降低数据污染风险并实现对TSFMs的公平评估。从点预测、概率预测、尾部行为、价格尖峰等多个维度进行评估，并与领域专用方法进行对比。

Result: TSFMs具有很强的竞争力，通常优于通用基线模型；但其性能严重依赖于协变量支持，且并不总能一致地超越专门为EPF设计的领域专用方法。有趣的是，TSFMs与领域专用方法的简单集成表现出显著潜力，表明两种方法捕捉了互补的预测信息。

Conclusion: TSFMs在电力价格预测中具有竞争力但存在局限，与领域专用方法互补，简单集成策略值得进一步探索。

Original Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have shown strong zero-shot forecasting performance, but their generalization in covariate-driven, non-stationary settings is underexplored. Electricity price forecasting (EPF) presents a challenging testbed due to complex temporal dependencies, distributional shifts, and strong reliance on structural and contextual information. We propose a two-dataset-benchmarking framework for EPF to mitigate contamination risk and enable fair evaluation of TSFMs. We examine key aspects of EPF including point and probabilistic forecasting performance, tail behavior, price spikes, and comparisons against domain-specific methods. We find that TSFMs are highly competitive and often outperform general-purpose baselines. Yet, their performance depends critically on covariate support, and they do not consistently surpass domain-specific methods tailored to EPF. Interestingly, simple ensembles of TSFMs and domain-specific methods appear to have significant potential, suggesting that the two approaches capture complementary predictive information.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在零样本预测中展现了强大的性能，但其在协变量驱动的非平稳环境中的泛化能力尚未得到充分探索。电力价格预测（EPF）由于存在复杂的时间依赖性、分布偏移以及对结构性和上下文信息的强依赖，构成了一个具有挑战性的测试平台。我们提出了一个面向EPF的双数据集基准评估框架，以降低数据污染风险并实现对TSFMs的公平评估。我们从点预测与概率预测性能、尾部行为、价格尖峰以及与领域专用方法的对比等多个维度考察了EPF的关键方面。研究发现，TSFMs具有很强的竞争力，通常优于通用基线模型。然而，它们的性能严重依赖于协变量支持，并且并不总能一致地超越专门为EPF设计的领域专用方法。有趣的是，TSFMs与领域专用方法的简单集成似乎具有显著潜力，表明这两种方法捕捉了互补的预测信息。

</details>


### [148] [QuantFlow: A Federated Mamba-Based Post-Transformer Foundation Model for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02632)
*Shah Nawaz Haider,Steve Austin,Arnab Barua,Sarowar Morshed Shawon,Hadaate Ullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantFlow是一个结合倒置序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器、分位数回归和联邦学习的概率预测框架，在多个时间序列数据集上取得良好结果，并能通过联邦学习保护隐私。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型依赖集中式数据和Transformer注意力机制，限制了其在长序列、高维度和隐私敏感信号上的应用。

Method: 将每个变量在整个观测窗口上嵌入，通过双向Mamba状态空间解码器进行正向和反向处理，投影到五个条件分位数；结合TSMixup通过Dirichlet加权插值增强时间多样性，并采用联邦学习实现隐私保护。

Result: 在ETTm1和Weather数据集上分别获得0.2834和0.2218的均方误差；20客户端非独立同分布联邦部署在三轮通信后仍保持有效精度，无需集中原始数据。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模是可扩展、不确定性感知且隐私意识的时间序列预测的有前途的基础，但在不规则流行病学信号和长时域泛化方面存在局限。

Original Abstract: Time-series forecasting supports decisions in finance, en-ergy, transportation, public health, and industrial monitoring. Recent foundation models improve transfer across forecast-ing tasks, but many depend on centralized data and Trans-former attention, which restricts their use for long, high-di-mensional, and privacy-sensitive signals. This paper presents QuantFlow, a probabilistic forecasting framework that com-bines inverted sequence embedding, bidirectional Mamba state-space decoders, quantile regression, and federated learning. Each variable is embedded over the complete ob-servation window, processed in forward and reverse direc-tions, and projected to five conditional quantiles. TSMixup expands temporal diversity through Dirichlet-weighted inter-polation while preserving sequence structure. Experiments cover cryptocurrency, traffic, electricity, Electricity Trans-former Temperature, influenza, and weather data. QuantFlow obtains mean squared errors of 0.2834 on ETTm1 and 0.2218 on Weather, and a 20-client non-IID deployment retains use-ful accuracy after three communication rounds without cen-tralizing raw records. The results indicate that selective state-space modelling is a promising basis for scalable, uncer-tainty-aware, and privacy-conscious time-series prediction, while also revealing limitations on irregular epidemiological signals and long-horizon generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测支持金融、能源、交通、公共卫生和工业监控中的决策制定。近期的基础模型改善了跨预测任务的迁移能力，但许多模型依赖集中式数据和Transformer注意力机制，限制了其在长序列、高维度和隐私敏感信号上的使用。本文提出QuantFlow，一个结合倒置序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器、分位数回归和联邦学习的概率预测框架。每个变量在整个观测窗口上嵌入，经过正向和反向处理，并投影到五个条件分位数。TSMixup通过Dirichlet加权插值扩展时间多样性，同时保留序列结构。实验涵盖加密货币、交通、电力、电力变压器温度、流感和天气数据。QuantFlow在ETTm1和Weather数据集上分别获得0.2834和0.2218的均方误差，20客户端非独立同分布部署在三轮通信后无需集中原始记录即可保持有效精度。结果表明选择性状态空间建模是可扩展、不确定性感知且隐私意识的时间序列预测的有前途的基础，同时也揭示了在不规则流行病学信号和长时域泛化方面的局限性。

</details>


### [149] [GRAFT: Grafted Reference Audio for Fine-grained Pronunciation in Zero-shot Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02633)
*Antonis Asonitis,Francesco Verdini,Aref Farhadipour,Vijeta Avijeet,Pierre-Edouard Honnet,Marzieh Razavi,Juan Pablo Zuluaga Gomez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种针对文本到语音神经编解码语言模型的逐词发音控制机制，通过从短语音样本中提取发音提示并绑定到目标词位置，显著提升了罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语的发音准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到语音系统虽能达到高自然度和可理解度，但继承了文本的歧义性，在罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语上容易发音错误。即使基于音素的条件模型也无法提供直接的逐词发音控制手段。

Method: GRAFT通过从短语音样本中提取目标词的发音提示（使用模型自身的语音分词器编码），将该提示绑定到提示文本中该词的位置上。在训练数据构建过程中使用语音转换技术，解耦提示音的说话人与目标说话人，使提示音可来自任意声音而输出保持目标说话人的音色。

Result: 在盲听英语研究中，人类评估者以明显优势将GRAFT评为第一，认为其对困难词汇的发音最接近参考录音。在五项语言客观基准上，GRAFT将目标词音素错误率降低了22-39%，在目标词发音上优于竞争性的开源零样本系统（包括基于音素和文本的条件系统），同时保持了说话人相似度和自然度。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过逐词发音控制机制有效解决了文本到语音系统中罕见词汇发音不准确的问题，在保持说话人相似度和自然度的前提下显著提升了发音准确率。

Original Abstract: We present GRAFT, a per-word pronunciation conditioning mechanism for text-to-speech neural codec language modeling. Existing systems reach high intelligibility and naturalness but inherit the ambiguity of text and mispronounce rare proper nouns, loanwords and technical terms. Even phoneme-conditioned models offer no direct acoustic handle for per-word pronunciation. GRAFT controls the pronunciation of a chosen word from a short spoken sample of it, encoded with the model's own speech tokenizer and bound to the word's position in the prompt. Voice conversion during training-data construction disentangles the hint speaker from the target speaker, so the hint may come from any voice while the output stays in the target voice. In a blind English listening study, human raters rank GRAFT first by a clear margin, judging its rendering of the difficult word closest to a reference recording of that word. On a five-language objective benchmark, GRAFT reduces target-word phoneme error rate by 22-39% over the identical text-only backbone and outperforms competitive open-source zero-shot systems, both phoneme- and text-conditioned, on target-word pronunciation, while preserving speaker similarity and naturalness.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了GRAFT，一种用于文本到语音神经编解码语言建模的逐词发音调节机制。现有系统达到了高可理解度和自然度，但继承了文本的歧义性，会在罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语上发音错误。即使是基于音素调节的模型也无法提供直接的逐词发音声学控制手段。GRAFT通过从一段简短的语音样本中提取选定词的发音控制信息，该样本使用模型自身的语音分词器进行编码，并绑定到该词在提示文本中的位置。在训练数据构建过程中使用语音转换技术，将提示音说话人与目标说话人解耦，因此提示音可来自任意声音，而输出仍保持目标说话人的音色。在一项盲听英语研究中，人类评估者以明显优势将GRAFT评为第一，认为其对困难词汇的发音最接近该词的参考录音。在五项语言的客观基准测试中，GRAFT将目标词的音素错误率相较纯文本骨干模型降低了22-39%，并在目标词发音上优于竞争性的开源零样本系统（包括基于音素和基于文本的调节系统），同时保持了说话人相似度和自然度。

</details>


### [150] [Federated Learning for Object Detection: Enabling Collaborative Drone Learning Without Centralizing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02636)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将联邦学习（FL）应用于无人机目标检测，在保护数据隐私的同时实现高性能检测，轻量级模型YOLO26 nano相比单机训练在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别提升52.89%和67.80%，性能接近集中式训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 安全关键的无人机和边缘视觉系统（如灾难响应、安保监控、基础设施巡检等）需要大量持续更新的数据集来训练高性能目标检测模型，但集中式收集航拍图像会引发隐私、法规、存储和带宽等问题，尤其分布式无人机部署中数据就地生成，传输到集中设施不切实际或不可取。

Method: 使用Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台，在KIIT-MiTA数据集上实现联邦目标检测流程，让多架无人机在保持图像数据本地化和私有化的同时，协作改进共享的目标检测模型，并与单无人机训练和集中式训练基线进行对比。

Result: 联邦学习方法的性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单无人机训练。最佳轻量级模型YOLO26 nano（适用于资源受限的边缘设备）在mAP@0.50上相对提升52.89%，在mAP@0.50:0.95上相对提升67.80%。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在无需数据集中化的情况下，实现跨分布式无人机机群的可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的目标检测。

Original Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental capability for AI-driven perception in safety-critical drone and edge-vision systems, including disaster response, operational security environments, infrastructure monitoring and defense applications. Robust model performance in such environments depends on large, continuously updated datasets. However, training high-performing detectors typically requires centralizing aerial imagery, which raises privacy, regulatory, storage, and bandwidth challenges. This is especially problematic in distributed drone deployments, where visual data is generated onboard and is often impractical or undesirable to transfer to a centralized infrastructure.
  In this work, we apply Federated Learning (FL) for object detection, enabling drones to improve a shared model while keeping image data local and private. We implement a federated object detection pipeline using the Sherpa.ai FL platform on the KIIT-MiTA dataset, and compare it with Single-drone and Centralized baselines using mean Average Precision (mAP) at IoU thresholds of 0.50 and 0.50-0.95. In our experiments, the proposed FL approach remains close to Centralized training while dramatically improving over Single-drone training, with the best lightweight model (YOLO26 nano), suitable for deployment even on very limited edge infrastructure, achieving relative gains of 52.89% and 67.80% in mAP@0.50 and mAP@0.50:0.95, respectively. These results show that FL enables scalable, high-performing, and privacy-preserving object detection across distributed drone fleets without data centralization.

中文摘要翻译: 目标检测是安全关键的无人机和边缘视觉系统中AI驱动感知的基础能力，这些系统包括灾难响应、作战安全环境、基础设施监控和国防应用。在此类环境中，鲁棒的模型性能依赖于大规模、持续更新的数据集。然而，训练高精度检测器通常需要集中化航拍图像，这带来了隐私、法规、存储和带宽方面的挑战。这在分布式无人机部署中尤为突出，因为视觉数据在机上生成，传输到集中式基础设施往往不切实际或不可取。在本工作中，我们将联邦学习（FL）应用于目标检测，使无人机能够在保持图像数据本地化和私有化的同时改进共享模型。我们使用Sherpa.ai FL平台在KIIT-MiTA数据集上实现了联邦目标检测流程，并在IoU阈值为0.50和0.50-0.95下，将其与单无人机和集中式基线以平均精度（mAP）进行比较。在我们的实验中，所提出的FL方法性能接近集中式训练，同时显著优于单无人机训练。最佳轻量级模型（YOLO26 nano）适用于甚至在非常有限的边缘基础设施上部署，其在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别实现了52.89%和67.80%的相对提升。这些结果表明，联邦学习能够在无需数据集中化的情况下，实现跨分布式无人机机群的可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的目标检测。

</details>


### [151] [Post-Generation Curation of Synthetic Images via Homogeneous-Heterogeneous Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02637)
*Disheng Liu,Tuo Liang,Chaoda Song,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从固定生成图像池中通过智能子集选择提升下游任务效用，提出一种与生成器无关、无需重训练的选择方法，通过保真度-多样性准则对抗生成模型的模态偏差，显著优于现有数据选择基线，甚至用更少样本匹配真实数据性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有利用生成模型数据的方法要么需要训练/微调生成器，要么需要提示工程或推理时引导等轻量级后验适配，这些方法都依赖于特定生成器且需要专业知识。本文研究一个互补问题：给定一个固定的生成图像池，能否仅通过选择信息性子集来提升下游效用？

Method: 将每个真实类别划分为规范同质子集（HO）和非冗余异质子集（HE），然后基于保真度-多样性准则对合成图像评分，奖励语义对齐同时惩罚规范冗余。该方法与生成器无关，无需重训练。

Result: 在多个基准上持续优于最先进的数据选择基线；用少40%的合成样本即可匹配真实数据性能；在更强的任务调优生成器上仍有效；在分类和分割任务上均有增益。

Conclusion: 生成后选择不是更好生成器的替代方案，而是提升合成数据效用的互补机制。

Original Abstract: Recent generative models can produce high-quality synthetic images, offering scalable training training data for data-hungry models. Existing approaches to exploiting this potential typically involve 1) training or fine-tuning generators, or 2) using lightweight post-hoc adaptation like prompt engineering or inference-time guidance, making them generator-specific and expertise-intensive. We study a complementary question: given a fixed pool of generated images, can downstream utility be improved purely by selecting an informative subset? The answer is yes. We show that effective selection must counter a structural bias of modern generators: they tend to over-produce canonical modes of each class while underrepresenting intra-class variation. Building on this insight, we split each real class into a canonical Homogeneous (HO) subset and a non-redundant Heterogeneous (HE) subset, then score synthetic images by a fidelity-diversity criterion that rewards semantic alignment while penalizing canonical redundancy. The method is generator-agnostic and requires no retraining. Across multiple benchmarks, it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data selection baselines and matches the real-data performance with up to 40% fewer synthetic samples. The same criterion remains effective when applied on top of stronger task-tuned generators, with gains on both classification and segmentation tasks. Post-generation selection is therefore not a substitute for better generators, but a complementary mechanism for improving the utility of synthetic data.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的生成模型能够产生高质量合成图像，为数据饥渴型模型提供可扩展的训练数据。利用这一潜力的现有方法通常涉及：1）训练或微调生成器，或2）使用轻量级后验适配（如提示工程或推理时引导），这使得它们依赖于特定生成器且需要专业知识。我们研究一个互补问题：给定一个固定的生成图像池，能否仅通过选择信息性子集来提升下游效用？答案是肯定的。我们表明，有效选择必须对抗现代生成器的一个结构性偏差：它们倾向于过度生成每个类别的规范模式，而低估类内变异。基于这一洞察，我们将每个真实类别划分为一个规范同质（HO）子集和一个非冗余异质（HE）子集，然后通过一个保真度-多样性准则对合成图像评分，该准则奖励语义对齐同时惩罚规范冗余。该方法与生成器无关，无需重训练。在多个基准上，它持续优于最先进的数据选择基线，并且用少40%的合成样本即可匹配真实数据性能。同样的准则在更强的任务调优生成器上仍然有效，在分类和分割任务上均有增益。因此，生成后选择不是更好生成器的替代方案，而是提升合成数据效用的互补机制。

</details>


### [152] [A Granularity-Aware EEG Feature Framework for Psychopathology Dimension Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02670)
*Haofan Cheng,Jingjing Hu,Jingrong Pei,Shuaiqi Fu,Meilun Shen,Shuai Fang,Meng Wang,Dan Guo,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种粒度感知的EEG特征管道，将多尺度描述符组织为全局、区域和通道三个层次，并在HBN队列中评估了其对四种精神病理学维度的预测能力。结果显示，基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择在特定条件下优于传统方法，多尺度EEG特征包含与维度精神病理学相关的微弱但可检测的信号。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑电图（EEG）为研究维度精神病理学的神经生理学关联提供了非侵入性手段，但跨EEG范式和特征粒度的系统证据仍然有限。现有研究缺乏对多尺度特征进行系统组织与评估的统一框架。

Method: 提出一种粒度感知的EEG特征管道，将多尺度描述符组织为全局、区域和通道三个层级；使用Healthy Brain Network（HBN）队列，评估四种EEG范式下对p因子、内化、外化和注意问题四个精神病理学维度的预测；采用基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择策略。

Result: 基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择在选定条件下比传统方法有改进，但效应量仍然适中；可视化显示维度特异性的空间和频谱模式与现有神经生理学知识大致一致；在独立PEARL队列上的探索性交叉数据集检查表明该选择原则在协议变化下技术上可行。

Conclusion: 多尺度EEG特征包含与维度精神病理学相关的微弱但可检测的信号，粒度感知的选择策略可作为未来基于EEG的表型研究中有用的特征降维策略。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a noninvasive approach for examining neurophysiological correlates of dimensional psychopathology, yet systematic evidence across EEG paradigms and feature granularities remains limited. Here, we develop a granularity-aware EEG feature pipeline that organizes multi-scale descriptors into global, regional, and channel levels. Using the Healthy Brain Network (HBN) cohort, we evaluate the prediction of four psychopathology dimensions: p-factor, internalizing, externalizing, and attention problems, across four EEG paradigms. Given the heterogeneity of pediatric psychopathology and the moderate reliability of questionnaire-derived scores, this setting represents a challenging feasibility test rather than a clinical screening scenario. Tree-based models and granularity-balanced feature selection showed promising improvements over conventional approaches in selected conditions, although effect sizes remained modest. Visualization of selected markers revealed dimension-specific spatial and spectral patterns that were broadly aligned with existing neurophysiological knowledge. An exploratory cross-dataset sanity check on the independent PEARL cohort suggested that the proposed selection principle remains technically feasible under protocol shifts, without claiming cross-dataset generalizability. Overall, multi-scale EEG features contain weak but detectable signals related to dimensional psychopathology, and granularity-aware selection may serve as a useful feature-reduction strategy for future EEG-based phenotyping studies.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）提供了一种非侵入性手段来研究维度精神病理学的神经生理学关联，然而跨EEG范式和特征粒度的系统证据仍然有限。在此，我们开发了一种粒度感知的EEG特征管道，将多尺度描述符组织为全局、区域和通道三个层级。利用健康大脑网络（HBN）队列，我们评估了在四种EEG范式下对四种精神病理学维度（p因子、内化、外化及注意问题）的预测能力。鉴于儿科精神病理学的异质性以及问卷得分的适度可靠性，该设定更多代表了一项具有挑战性的可行性测试，而非临床筛查场景。基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择在选定条件下相比传统方法显示出有希望的改进，尽管效应量仍然适中。对选定标记的可视化揭示了维度特异性的空间和频谱模式，这些模式与现有的神经生理学知识大致一致。在独立PEARL队列上的探索性交叉数据集合理性检查表明，所提出的选择原则在协议变化下仍保持技术可行性，但未声称跨数据集的泛化能力。总体而言，多尺度EEG特征包含与维度精神病理学相关的微弱但可检测的信号，粒度感知的选择策略可作为未来基于EEG的表型研究中一种有用的特征降维策略。

</details>


### [153] [LiNO: Lifting based multiresolution neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02715)
*Himanshu Pandey,Subham Patel,Ratikanta Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于二代小波提升方案的提升神经算子（LiNO），一种多分辨率算子学习框架，通过可逆的自适应提升变换实现多尺度动力学建模，在多种物理基准测试中表现优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经算子在同时捕捉全局动力学和精细尺度结构方面存在不足，需要一种能够表征多尺度特征的分层多尺度分解框架。

Method: 基于二代小波提升方案构建多分辨率算子LiNO，通过参数化提升变换直接从数据学习多分辨率分解，该变换是自适应且完全可逆的，在提升后的多分辨率空间中分别演算粗尺度和方向细节系数，实现尺度感知的物理建模。

Result: 在Darcy流、泊松方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程及Gray-Scott反应扩散系统等基准测试中，LiNO相比现有最先进神经算子展现出强劲性能，涵盖多尺度现象、输运主导动力学和混沌系统。

Conclusion: 自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Recently, neural operators have shown promising outcomes for learning solution operators of differential equations directly from data. This framework learns a functional mapping from the parameter field to the solution field, enabling the prediction of an entire class of solutions rather than a specific instance. However, existing operators often struggle to capture both global dynamics and fine-scale structure simultaneously. To design an effective operator capable of representing multiscale features, a hierarchical multiscale decomposition framework is required. In this study, we develop the Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO), a multiresolution operator built on the second-generation wavelet lifting scheme. LiNO learns a multiresolution decomposition directly from data by parameterizing the lifting transform. This lifting transformation is adaptive to the underlying solution function and exactly invertible by construction, enabling information-preserving multiscale operator learning. In the lifted multiresolution space, the operator evolves coarse and directional detail coefficients separately, resulting in scale-aware modeling of the underlying physics. We evaluate LiNO on several benchmarks, including Darcy flow, the Poisson equation, the Allen-Cahn equation, the compressible Navier-Stokes equation, and the Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion system. Together, these benchmarks cover a wide range of physical behaviors, including multiscale phenomena, transport-dominated dynamics, and chaotic systems. LiNO demonstrates strong performance on these challenging benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art neural operators. These results suggest that adaptive multiresolution operators provide a promising direction for scientific machine learning.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，神经算子在直接从数据中学习微分方程解算子方面展现了令人瞩目的成果。该框架学习从参数场到解场的函数映射，使得能够预测整类解而非特定实例。然而，现有算子通常难以同时捕捉全局动力学和精细尺度结构。为了设计能够表征多尺度特征的有效算子，需要一种分层多尺度分解框架。在本研究中，我们提出了提升神经算子（LiNO），一种基于第二代小波提升方案构建的多分辨率算子。LiNO通过参数化提升变换直接从数据中学习多分辨率分解。该提升变换对底层解函数具有自适应性，并且通过构造保证严格可逆，从而实现了信息无损的多尺度算子学习。在提升后的多分辨率空间中，算子分别演算粗尺度系数和方向细节系数，实现了对底层物理的尺度感知建模。我们在多个基准测试上评估了LiNO，包括Darcy流、泊松方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程以及Gray-Scott反应扩散系统。这些基准测试共同涵盖了多尺度现象、输运主导动力学和混沌系统等广泛的物理行为。与最先进的神经算子相比，LiNO在这些具有挑战性的基准测试中展现了强劲的性能。这些结果表明，自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了一条有前景的方向。

</details>


### [154] [Weighted Conformal Prediction for Lab-to-Track Thermal Transfer in EV Motorsport Powertrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02722)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用共形预测（Conformal Prediction）解决电动汽车动力电池热预测中实验室到赛道的分布迁移问题。在真实锂离子电池数据集上，标准EnbPI方法在分布内覆盖率达到95%，但在真实协变量偏移下降至70.13%；引入加权EnbPI（密度比加权）后仅恢复至72.42%，改进有限。在F1真实遥测数据上作为无监督OOD诊断，异常标记率远高于分布内基线，表明共形域适应是部分有效但远未成熟的解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高性能电动汽车动力电池的内部温度在实验室外几乎无法观测，基于实验室标准工况（如FUDS）校准的热预测模型在部署到真实驾驶工况（如US06高速驾驶）时因分布偏移导致预测失效，需要一种能够提供分布自由不确定性量化的域适应方法。

Method: 采用EnbPI（集成批预测区间）作为基础的共形预测方法，针对自相关时间序列提供留一法bootstrap集成分位数区间。在此基础上提出加权EnbPI，将EnbPI的集成残差与密度比加权（Tibshirani et al., 2019）相结合，通过概率域分类器估计密度比，从而对协变量偏移进行校准。

Result: 1) 在CALCE锂离子数据集上，标准EnbPI在分布内（FUDS）达到95.00%的覆盖率的完美校准；2) 在真实协变量偏移（US06, 45°C）下，覆盖率骤降至70.13%；3) 加权EnbPI将覆盖率恢复至72.42%，改进微小且如实报告；4) 在F1真实遥测数据（Monza和Silverstone）上，无监督异常标记率分别为65.6%和58.0%，远高于分布内基线5%，但与刹车/DRS区域关联不一致。

Conclusion: 共形域适应是预测热波动的一个有前景但仅部分有效的工具。加权EnbPI在真实协变量偏移下仅带来微小改进，远未完全解决问题。论文诚实地指出了方法的局限性，并建议未来需要更强大的域适应策略。

Original Abstract: Predicting thermal volatility in high-performance EV powertrains is difficult as internal temperatures are rarely observable outside the lab, and models calibrated on lab drive cycles fail when deployed against real-world loads. We study this lab-to-track transfer problem using conformal prediction, offering distribution-free uncertainty bounds. We implement Ensemble Batch Prediction Intervals (EnbPI; Xu & Xie, 2021), a leave-one-out bootstrap-ensemble conformal method for autocorrelated time series, and calibrate it on real CALCE lithium-ion cycler data (A123 SP20 cells, FUDS profile). We evaluate it under a genuine, measured covariate shift: a second real CALCE test condition (US06 Highway Driving Schedule at 45°C). The unweighted EnbPI bound, achieving its nominal 95% coverage in-distribution (measured: 95.00%), degrades to 70.13% empirical coverage under this real shift. We introduce a weighted EnbPI procedure combining EnbPI's ensemble residuals with density-ratio weighting (Tibshirani et al., 2019), estimating the density ratio via a probabilistic domain classifier. This recovers coverage to 72.42%, a modest, honestly-reported improvement, not a complete fix. We additionally apply the calibrated model to real 2023 Formula 1 telemetry (Monza and Silverstone, driver VER) as an unsupervised out-of-distribution diagnostic. Because no internal thermal channel exists in public trackside telemetry, we report only unsupervised flag rates (65.6% at Monza, 58.0% at Silverstone, well above the 5% in-distribution base rate) and note inconsistent associations between flags and braking/DRS zones. We conclude that conformal domain adaptation is a promising but only partially solved tool for this problem, detailing exactly where it falls short.

中文摘要翻译: 在高性能电动汽车动力总成中预测热波动十分困难，因为内部温度在实验室之外几乎无法观测，且基于实验室驾驶循环校准的模型在面对真实负载时失效。本文利用共形预测方法研究这种实验室到赛道的迁移问题，提供分布自由的不确定性边界。我们实现了EnbPI（集成批预测区间；Xu & Xie, 2021），一种针对自相关时间序列的留一法bootstrap集成共形方法，并在真实CALCE锂离子循环数据集（A123 SP20电池，FUDS剖面）上进行校准。我们在一个真实且经实际测量的协变量偏移条件下进行评估：第二个真实CALCE测试条件（US06高速驾驶调度，45°C）。未加权的EnbPI边界在分布内达到名义上的95%覆盖率（实测：95.00%），但在该真实偏移下降至70.13%的经验覆盖率。我们引入一种加权EnbPI流程，将EnbPI的集成残差与密度比加权（Tibshirani等，2019）相结合，通过概率域分类器估计密度比。该方案将覆盖率恢复至72.42%，这是一个微小且诚实报告的改进，而非完全的修复。我们还将校准后的模型应用于真实的2023年F1遥测数据（蒙扎和银石赛道，车手VER），作为无监督的分布外诊断。由于公共赛道遥测数据中不存在内部热通道，我们仅报告无监督的异常标记率（蒙扎65.6%，银石58.0%，远高于5%的分布内基线），并注意到标记与刹车/DRS区域之间的不一致关联。我们得出的结论是，共形域适应是一个有前景但仅部分解决问题的工具，并详细说明了其不足之处。

</details>


### [155] [Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Risk Aversion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02755)
*Kristina Zhang,Junior Chinomso Okoroafor,Benjamin Maltbie,Andrew Lin,Abhitej Bokka,Elliott Thornley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否通过低 stakes 训练让 AI 学会风险厌恶，并使其泛化到天文高 stakes 场景。实验表明风险厌恶可部分泛化（跨越98个数量级），但尚不足以作为可靠的保险机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为防止对齐失败的 AI 采取高风险策略（如叛乱），训练 AI 具备资源风险厌恶是一种潜在保险机制。关键在于低 stakes 下学到的风险厌恶是否能泛化到天文高 stakes 场景。

Method: 构建 RiskAverseOOD 基准，使用 SFT、tie training、DPO 和 activation steering 等方法训练 Qwen3-8B 等模型在低 stakes 下选择风险厌恶行为，然后测试其在天文高 stakes 下的泛化能力。同时训练奖励模型评估风险厌恶推理。

Result: SFT 和 tie training 达到约70%合作率，DPO 约52%，activation steering 约39%（基线为2%）；奖励模型在风险厌恶推理上达99.6%成对准确率；效果在多种模型规模和家族上复现。

Conclusion: 低 stakes 下学到的风险厌恶可以部分泛化到天文高 stakes（跨越98个数量级），但尚不足以作为可靠的保险机制，实现完全一致的泛化仍是开放问题。

Original Abstract: Training AIs to be risk-averse in resources could offer a failsafe in the event that AIs turn out misaligned. Misaligned but risk-averse AIs would tend to prefer low-risk, low-reward strategies like cooperation over high-risk, high-reward strategies like rebellion, limiting the downsides of any misalignment. But we can only feasibly train AIs to be risk-averse on low-stakes gambles, and we will only be safe if their risk aversion generalizes to astronomically-high-stakes gambles. Will it? To shed light on this question, we introduce RiskAverseOOD: a benchmark for measuring how well risk aversion generalizes out of distribution. We then offer some initial results. Using a variety of methods to make Qwen3-8B choose risk-aversely when the stakes are low, we find that we can induce substantial risk aversion when the stakes are astronomically high. Our models' learned risk aversion generalizes at least partially across 98 orders of magnitude. From a baseline 2% rate of choosing a safe `Cooperate' option, we see rates around 70% (SFT and tie training), 52% (DPO), and 39% (activation steering). In another experiment, our fine-tuned reward model reliably scores risk-averse reasoning above risk-neutral or excessively risk-averse alternatives (99.6% pairwise accuracy). We replicate these effects at different scales (Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-14B) and across model families (Gemma-3-12B-IT and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct). Overall, we find that risk aversion learned at low stakes can generalize OOD to astronomically high stakes, though not yet consistently enough to serve as a reliable failsafe. Achieving that level of consistency is an open problem.

中文摘要翻译: 训练人工智能在资源上保持风险厌恶，可以在人工智能出现对齐失败时提供一种保险机制。对齐失败但风险厌恶的 AI 倾向于选择低风险、低回报的策略（如合作），而非高风险、高回报的策略（如叛乱），从而限制对齐失败的负面影响。但我们只能在低 stakes 赌局中实际训练 AI 保持风险厌恶，而只有当其风险厌恶泛化到天文高 stakes 赌局时，我们才是安全的。这种泛化能否实现？为揭示这一问题，我们引入了 RiskAverseOOD：一个衡量风险厌恶泛化到分布外场景能力的基准。我们随后提供了一些初步结果。使用多种方法让 Qwen3-8B 在低 stakes 时做出风险厌恶的选择，我们发现当 stakes 达到天文级别时，可以诱导出显著的风险厌恶行为。模型学到的风险厌恶至少能部分泛化跨越98个数量级。从基线仅2%选择安全"合作"选项的比例，我们观察到 SFT 和 tie training 下约70%、DPO 下约52%、activation steering 下约39%的合作率。在另一项实验中，我们微调的奖励模型能够可靠地将风险厌恶推理排在风险中性或过度风险厌恶的替代方案之上（99.6%的成对准确率）。我们在不同规模（Qwen3-1.7B 和 Qwen3-14B）以及不同模型家族（Gemma-3-12B-IT 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）上复现了这些效果。总体而言，我们发现低 stakes 下学到的风险厌恶可以泛化到分布外的天文高 stakes 场景，但尚不足以具备足够的稳定性来充当可靠的保险机制。实现这一水平的稳定性仍是开放问题。

</details>


### [156] [Safe Inference-Time Alignment via Lagrangian Reward Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02781)
*Yaswanth Chittepu,Ativ Joshi,Sohini Chintala,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LARA框架，通过在推理时对奖励信号进行拉格朗日对偶校准，在安全约束下实现帮助性与无害性之间的更好权衡，无需重新训练模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法仅优化单一标量分数，显式安全约束要么被忽略，要么通过手动调参的惩罚项编码，缺乏原则性的约束处理方式。

Method: 基于KL正则化的约束优化目标（包含奖励模型和成本模型），通过对偶化将约束优化问题转化为关于非负对偶变量的一维凸优化问题，在小型校准集上估计对偶变量，得到增强奖励信号，可直接嵌入现有推理时对齐方法。

Result: LARA在序列级和词元级推理时对齐方法上均改进了帮助性-无害性权衡，其中Best-of-N方法性能最佳，接近基于微调的直接对齐基线。

Conclusion: LARA提供了一种通用的、原则性的推理时安全约束对齐框架，在序列级方法上可得到精确的约束策略保证，在词元级方法上则提供对偶校准的启发式解。

Original Abstract: Inference-time alignment steers a frozen language model during decoding using auxiliary reward signals, avoiding the cost of repeated weight updates. However, existing inference-time alignment methods typically optimize a single scalar score, so explicit safety constraints must either be ignored or encoded through manually tuned penalties. We propose Lagrangian Reward Augmentation (LARA), a general inference-time alignment framework under safety constraints. Starting from a KL-regularized constrained objective with a reward model and a cost model, LARA dualizes the constraint and reduces the optimization problem to a one-dimensional convex problem over a nonnegative dual variable. Estimated on a small calibration set, this dual variable defines an augmented reward that can be used as a drop-in scoring signal within existing inference-time alignment methods. For sequence-level sampling methods, such as Best-of-N reranking, the calibrated dual variable corresponds to the solution of the expected-cost constrained problem. For token-level reward-guided decoding methods, the same construction yields a principled dual-calibrated heuristic rather than an exact constrained-policy guarantee. We evaluate LARA on both sequence-level and token-level inference-time alignment methods, and find that LARA improves the helpfulness-harmlessness tradeoff, with Best-of-N achieving the best performance among inference-time methods, approaching finetuning-based direct alignment baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 推理时对齐利用辅助奖励信号在解码过程中引导冻结的语言模型，避免了重复更新权重的代价。然而，现有的推理时对齐方法通常优化单一标量分数，因此显式的安全约束要么被忽略，要么通过手动调参的惩罚项来编码。本文提出拉格朗日奖励增强（LARA），一种在安全约束下的通用推理时对齐框架。从包含奖励模型和成本模型的KL正则化约束目标出发，LARA对约束进行对偶化，将优化问题简化为关于非负对偶变量的一维凸问题。在小型校准集上估计得到该对偶变量后，可定义一种增强奖励，作为即插即用的评分信号嵌入现有推理时对齐方法中。对于序列级采样方法（如Best-of-N重排序），校准后的对偶变量对应期望成本约束问题的解。对于词元级奖励引导解码方法，相同的构造提供了一种原则性的对偶校准启发式解，而非精确的约束策略保证。我们在序列级和词元级推理时对齐方法上评估LARA，发现LARA改进了帮助性-无害性权衡，其中Best-of-N在推理时方法中表现最佳，接近基于微调的直接对齐基线。

</details>


### [157] [Induction Heads Interpolate N-Grams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02800)
*Francesco D'Angelo,Oguz Kaan Yuksel,Swathi Shree Narashiman,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变压器中的归纳头（induction heads）在上下文学习（in-context learning）中实现的统计估计器，发现其通过两种平滑机制（软上下文匹配和BOS伪计数）实现类似Jelinek-Mercer平滑和Dirichlet平滑的正则化效果，而非简单的计数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 虽然归纳头被认为是变压器中实现上下文学习的关键注意力回路，但其具体实现的统计估计器尚未得到精确刻画。

Method: 在阶为k的马尔可夫链上训练变压器，识别两种互补的平滑机制：（1）有限注意力权重尺度下的软上下文匹配估计器；（2）序列起始（BOS）token引入的加性伪计数。构建了解耦变压器实现这两种机制，并与经典计数基线进行比较。

Result: 训练后的变压器恢复出了预测的注意力模式；在伪计数平滑最优或低阶上下文提供结构化证据的场景中，变压器匹配或超越了经典计数基线。

Conclusion: 归纳头与经典统计平滑之间存在桥梁关系，变压器学习的是对上下文估计进行正则化（平滑），而非简单计数。

Original Abstract: Induction heads are attention circuits believed to underlie in-context learning in transformers, yet a precise characterization of the estimators they implement remains elusive. We study transformers trained on order-$k$ Markov chains and identify two complementary smoothing mechanisms. First, at finite attention-weight scale, the circuit implements a soft context-matching estimator: it aggregates contributions from exact and partial context matches, weighted exponentially by their overlap, and induces a data-dependent interpolation across context orders analogous to Jelinek-Mercer smoothing. Second, a beginning-of-sequence (BOS) token induces additive pseudo-counts, recovering Dirichlet-style smoothing. We construct a disentangled transformer implementing both mechanisms and show that trained transformers recover the predicted attention patterns. Across settings where pseudo-count smoothing is optimal or lower-order contexts provide structured evidence, trained transformers match or outperform classical count-based baselines. Our results bridge mechanistic interpretability of induction heads with classical statistical smoothing, revealing that transformers learn to regularize in-context estimation rather than simply count.

中文摘要翻译: 归纳头是被认为支撑变压器中上下文学习的注意力回路，然而对其所实现估计器的精确刻画仍然难以捉摸。我们在阶为k的马尔可夫链上训练变压器，并识别出两种互补的平滑机制。首先，在有限注意力权重尺度下，该回路实现了一个软上下文匹配估计器：它聚合精确和部分上下文匹配的贡献，按其重叠指数加权，并引入一种类似于Jelinek-Mercer平滑的、跨上下文阶的数据相关插值。其次，序列起始（BOS）token引入加性伪计数，恢复了Dirichlet风格的平滑。我们构建了一个解耦变压器实现这两种机制，并展示训练后的变压器恢复出预测的注意力模式。在伪计数平滑最优或低阶上下文提供结构化证据的场景中，训练后的变压器匹配或超越了经典计数基线。我们的结果在归纳头的机械可解释性与经典统计平滑之间架起了桥梁，揭示了变压器学习的是对上下文估计进行正则化（平滑），而非简单计数。

</details>


### [158] [Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805)
*Pranshu Chaturvedi,Parth Shroff,Tarun Suresh,Hangoo Kang,Kaiyue Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BDLM Mamba-H混合模型，通过限制反向Mamba扫描至活跃去噪块实现精确缓存，在长上下文生成中兼顾高效注意力与块级并行扩散，显著提升吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高吞吐长上下文生成是LLM的核心挑战，传统自回归解码受限于内存带宽。现有两类方法——高效注意力/线性时序混合器（如Mamba）和块级并行生成——在结合时存在技术困难：双向Mamba扩散模型的反向扫描需扫描整个序列，其状态无法被精确缓存重用。

Method: 提出BDLM Mamba-H混合架构，将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃的去噪块内，使得跨块间可实现精确的KV缓存重用，从而在块级并行扩散中同时利用Mamba的高效状态传递和注意力机制。

Result: 在87M参数DCLM实验中，BDLM Mamba-H的C4-en验证困惑度优于BDLM Attention和全序列基线；350M参数时与BDLM Attention竞争力相当。长上下文推理中，65K tokens下吞吐量达到全序列DiffuMamba-H的19.7倍，262K tokens下达到BDLM Attention的3.7倍。

Conclusion: Mamba与注意力混合的架构是长上下文扩散模型的有潜力的方案，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升吞吐量。

Original Abstract: High-throughput long-context generation is one of the central challenges for large language models. Generation is typically memory-bandwidth-bound rather than compute-bound: each decoding step must stream the accumulated key/value (KV) cache from memory, so bandwidth demand grows with context length while only one token is emitted. Two parallel approaches have therefore emerged: reducing memory access with efficient attention variants and linear-time mixers such as Mamba, or increasing parallel computation by generating blocks of tokens at once. However, technical challenges arise when combining these two ideas. Earlier hybrid diffusion models such as DiffuMamba use bidirectional Mamba mixing, including a reverse-direction scan relative to causal generation. This reverse scan needs to scan the entire sequence, so its states are not prefix-only and cannot be precisely reused as a cache even when diffusion is performed block by block. We propose a BDLM Mamba--attention hybrid that addresses this challenge by restricting the reverse Mamba scan to the active denoising block, which enables exact caching across blocks. In an 87M-parameter DCLM sweep, BDLM Mamba-H achieves the best C4-en validation perplexity compared to BDLM attention and full-sequence baselines. At 350M parameters, it remains competitive with BDLM attention. For long-context inference, BDLM Mamba-H reaches 19.7x the throughput of full-sequence DiffuMamba-H at 65K tokens and 3.7x the throughput of BDLM attention at 262K, showing that Mamba hybrids are a potential long-context diffusion architecture.

中文摘要翻译: 高吞吐长上下文生成是大语言模型的核心挑战之一。生成过程通常受内存带宽限制而非计算限制：每个解码步骤必须从内存中流式读取累积的键/值（KV）缓存，因此带宽需求随上下文长度增长，但每次仅生成一个token。为此，两类并行方法应运而生：通过高效注意力变体和线性时序混合器（如Mamba）减少内存访问，或通过一次性生成token块来增加并行计算。然而，将这两种思路结合时面临技术挑战。早期的混合扩散模型如DiffuMamba使用双向Mamba混合，其中包含与因果生成方向相反的反向扫描。该反向扫描需扫描整个序列，因此其状态不是仅前缀的，即使在逐块扩散时也无法被精确地作为缓存重用。我们提出BDLM Mamba-H混合模型，通过将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃的去噪块内来解决这一挑战，从而实现了跨块的精确缓存。在87M参数的DCLM实验中，BDLM Mamba-H的C4-en验证困惑度优于BDLM Attention和全序列基线。在350M参数下，它仍与BDLM Attention保持竞争力。在长上下文推理中，BDLM Mamba-H在65K tokens下达到全序列DiffuMamba-H吞吐量的19.7倍，在262K tokens下达到BDLM Attention吞吐量的3.7倍，表明Mamba混合架构是一种有潜力的长上下文扩散模型架构。

</details>


### [159] [Less Tokens, Better Forecasts: Sparse Residual Routing for Efficient Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02829)
*Janet Wang,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Xi Xiao,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse-Reslim，一种无参数即插即用的路由模块，通过仅让25%的空间token经过昂贵的中间Transformer块，并以残差更新方式将变化量散射回完整序列，在保持固定网格稠密预测要求的同时，大幅提升天气预测模型的效率与精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于ViT的天气预测模型对所有空间token进行统一计算，但邻近网格点数值相似、大范围区域随时间平滑演化，导致大量中间逐token计算冗余。标准的token效率方法（如剪枝或融合）会移除或融合token，但天气预测是时空稠密预测问题，每个网格单元必须保留物理有意义的表征，尤其在自回归滚动预测中。因此需要一种兼容固定网格要求的稀疏token处理方法。

Method: Sparse-Reslim是一个无参数即插即用的路由模块。它仅将25%的空间token路由通过昂贵的中间Transformer块，并将这些块视为残差更新：计算路由token产生的变化量（delta），然后将此变化量散射回完整序列。未选中的token保持路由前的表征不变，不丢弃任何网格单元，也不使用掩码token替代，无需融合层或额外参数。

Result: 在ERA5数据上（分辨率最高达0.25°操作标准）和两种模型家族（确定性Transformer和扩散模型）上，Sparse-Reslim在评估的每个变量上均提升了预测精度，同时大幅降低计算成本：主要设置下训练速度约提升2.5倍，在0.25°分辨率下达到3.18倍加速，峰值内存降低超过2.2倍。控制分解实验表明，精度提升主要来自稀疏路由本身，随机token选择在不产生选择器开销的情况下提供了额外的正则化收益。

Conclusion: Sparse-Reslim有效地将稀疏token处理与天气预测的固定网格要求兼容，通过残差更新策略实现了计算效率和预测精度的双重提升，且无需额外参数，是一种简洁高效的方法。

Original Abstract: Existing ViT-based weather forecasting models apply uniform computation across all spatial tokens, even though nearby atmospheric grid points often contain similar values and large regions evolve smoothly over time. This makes much of the intermediate per-token computation redundant. Standard token-efficiency methods, such as pruning or merging, reduce cost by removing or fusing tokens. However, weather forecasting is a spatiotemporal dense prediction problem in which a history of atmospheric states must be mapped to future values on the original latitude-longitude grid. Thus, every grid cell must retain a physically meaningful representation, especially under autoregressive rollout. We introduce Sparse-Reslim, a parameter-free plug-in routing module that makes sparse token processing compatible with this fixed-grid requirement. Sparse-Reslim routes only 25% of spatial tokens through the expensive middle transformer blocks and treats those blocks as residual updates: it computes the change produced for the routed tokens and scatters only this delta back to the full sequence. Unselected tokens keep their pre-routing representations exactly, so no grid cell is dropped or replaced by a mask token, and no fusion layer or additional parameters are introduced. Across ERA5 resolutions up to the operational 0.25\textdegree{} standard and two model families, a deterministic Transformer and a diffusion model, Sparse-Reslim improves forecast accuracy on every evaluated variable while substantially reducing cost: training is about 2.5x faster in the main settings and reaches 3.18x speedup at 0.25\textdegree{}, with over 2.2x lower peak memory. A controlled decomposition shows that the accuracy gain comes primarily from sparse routing itself, while random token selection provides an additional regularization benefit without selector overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的基于ViT（Vision Transformer）的天气预报模型对所有空间token采用统一计算，尽管邻近大气网格点常包含相似数值且大范围区域随时间平滑演化，这使得中间逐token计算存在大量冗余。标准的token效率方法（如剪枝或融合）通过移除或融合token来降低计算成本。然而，天气预报是一个时空稠密预测问题，需要将历史大气状态映射到原始经纬度网格上的未来值。因此，每个网格单元都必须保留物理有意义的表征，尤其在自回归滚动预测中。我们提出Sparse-Reslim——一种无参数的即插即用路由模块，使稀疏token处理与此固定网格要求兼容。Sparse-Reslim仅将25%的空间token路由通过昂贵的中间Transformer块，并将这些块视为残差更新：计算路由token产生的变化量（delta），然后仅将此变化量散射回完整序列。未选中的token保持路由前的表征不变，因此没有网格单元被丢弃或被掩码token替代，也无需引入融合层或额外参数。在ERA5数据上（分辨率最高达运行标准的0.25°）和两种模型家族（确定性Transformer和扩散模型）上，Sparse-Reslim在评估的每个变量上均提升了预测精度，同时大幅降低计算成本：主要设置下训练速度约提升2.5倍，在0.25°分辨率下达到3.18倍加速，峰值内存降低超过2.2倍。控制分解实验表明，精度提升主要来自稀疏路由本身，而随机token选择在不产生选择器开销的情况下提供了额外的正则化收益。

</details>


### [160] [On the Design Space of Discrete Diffusion Online Adaptation for Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02834)
*Trevor Chen,Ariel Dai,Jason Yang,Riccardo De Santi,Daniel Khalil,Wenda Chu,Nate Gruver,Pranav Murugan,Alexander F. G. Goldberg,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散扩散模型在分子优化中的在线微调问题，系统分析了采样、奖励塑造、去偏、回放和有效性控制等组件在完整在线循环中的相互作用，并提出了一个实用的反馈高效分子优化方案，该方案在多种小分子和蛋白质任务上优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分子优化方法通常从预训练生成模型出发，但测试阶段的目标是从有限的黑盒评估预算中最大化任务特定高奖励分子，而非从先验分布采样。然而，采样策略、奖励如何转换为模型更新、反馈如何重用等关键选择大多被孤立研究，缺乏对它们如何在完整的在线自适应循环中相互补充、冗余或干扰的系统理解。

Method: 针对离散扩散模型的在线微调问题，本文系统研究了五个关键组件：1）采样策略（acquisition，即哪些候选分子被评估）；2）奖励塑造（reward shaping，奖励如何转换为训练信号）；3）模型去偏（model debiasing，缓解分布偏移）；4）经验回放（replay，复用历史反馈）；5）有效性控制（validity penalties，保持分子处于有效流形）。通过控制实验，在6个小分子结合亲和力任务和3个蛋白质适应度任务上评估各组件的贡献与交互效应。

Result: 1）采样、奖励塑造和模型去偏是互补的提升奖励的途径，尤其在药物小分子场景下效果显著；2）经验回放能稳定学习过程；3）有效性惩罚有助于将小分子探索约束在有效分子流形上；4）完整方案在等量的黑盒评估预算和GPU时间消耗下，优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线；5）当高奖励候选分子需要更大程度偏离预训练先验时，增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 联合使用采样策略、奖励塑造、去偏、回放和有效性控制的在线微调方案是一种反馈高效的分子优化实用方案，其在离散扩散模型场景下显著优于离线微调和推理时搜索方法。

Original Abstract: Molecular optimization often starts from a pretrained generative model that captures a broad prior over valid molecular structures. At test time, however, the goal is not to sample from this prior, but to use a limited oracle budget to shift generation toward task-specific high-reward molecules. We study this adaptation problem for discrete diffusion models. Each online round couples several choices. The loop must decide which candidates to evaluate, how rewards become model updates, which feedback to reuse, and how far to move beyond the pretrained prior. These choices have mostly been studied in isolation, leaving open whether they complement one another, become redundant, or interfere inside a full online adaptation loop. We conduct controlled studies across six small-molecule binding-affinity tasks and three protein-fitness tasks. We find that acquisition, reward shaping, and model debiasing provide complementary routes to higher reward, especially for small molecules. Replay further stabilizes learning, while validity penalties keep small-molecule exploration on the valid molecular manifold. Together, these findings point to a practical recipe for feedback-efficient molecular optimization: online fine-tuning with acquisition, reward shaping, debiasing, replay, and validity control. This recipe outperforms offline fine-tuning and inference-time search baselines under matched oracle-call budgets and GPU-hour accounting. The gains are largest when high-reward candidates require larger shifts from the pretrained prior.

中文摘要翻译: 分子优化通常从一个预训练的生成模型开始，该模型捕获了有效分子结构的广泛先验知识。然而在测试阶段，目标并非从该先验中采样，而是利用有限的评估预算将生成过程转向任务特定的高奖励分子。本文针对离散扩散模型研究这一自适应问题。每一轮在线循环耦合了多个决策：循环必须决定哪些候选分子被评估、奖励如何转化为模型更新、哪些反馈数据被重用，以及在多大程度上偏离预训练先验。这些选择大多被孤立研究，尚未明确它们在完整的在线自适应循环中是互补、冗余还是相互干扰。我们在六项小分子结合亲和力任务和三项蛋白质适应度任务上进行了控制实验。研究发现，采样策略、奖励塑造和模型去偏是提升奖励的互补途径，尤其对于小分子而言。经验回放进一步稳定了学习过程，而有效性惩罚则将小分子探索约束在有效的分子流形上。综合来看，这些发现指向了一个反馈高效的分子优化实用方案：结合采样、奖励塑造、去偏、回放和有效性控制的在线微调。在匹配的评估预算和GPU时间消耗下，该方案优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线。当高奖励候选分子需要更大程度地偏离预训练先验时，增益最为显著。

</details>


### [161] [Labeled-Data-Free Meta-Learning: Efficient Task Generation Using Pre-trained Models and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02850)
*Lei Sun,Yusuke Tanaka,Tomoharu Iwata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需模型反转的数据无关元学习新方法，通过利用预训练模型和未标注数据生成元训练任务，并引入任务加权机制，显著降低计算成本并提升少样本分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据无关元学习（DFML）方法依赖模型反转生成训练数据，该过程计算昂贵且难以生成高维匹配原始分布的数据；实际应用中获取标注数据成本高或受隐私限制，因此需要一种更高效、免于模型反转的元学习方案。

Method: 提出一种新的元学习设置：联合利用预训练模型和未标注数据，通过将预训练模型生成的软标签分配给未标注数据来构造元训练任务；并引入基于任务置信度和类别分布平衡的任务加权机制，以提升元学习效果。

Result: 该方法显著降低了计算成本并提升了泛化能力，相比现有最优DFML方法，实现了最高104倍的加速，少样本分类准确率提升8.4%至36.4%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的免模型反转的数据无关元学习方法在计算效率和分类性能上均显著优于现有方法，为实际应用中缺乏标注数据时的元学习提供了更实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Meta-learning without labeled data is crucial for real-world applications, where obtaining labeled datasets can be expensive or restricted due to privacy concerns. Data-Free Meta-Learning (DFML) addresses this challenge by leveraging pre-trained models without access to training data. However, existing DFML methods rely on model inversion to generate training data, a process that is generally difficult and computationally expensive due to the need to generate high-dimensional data matching the original distribution. To address this limitation, we propose a novel meta-learning setting that avoids model inversion by jointly leveraging pre-trained models and unlabeled data. Our method generates meta-training tasks by assigning soft labels from pre-trained models to unlabeled data. Since the quality of these tasks can vary, we introduce a task-weighting mechanism based on task confidence and class distribution balance to ensure effective meta-learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach substantially reduces computational cost and improves generalization, achieving up to 104-fold speedup and 8.4 percent to 36.4 percent improvements in few-shot classification accuracy compared to state-of-the-art DFML methods.

中文摘要翻译: 无标签数据的元学习对于现实应用至关重要，因为获取标注数据集可能成本高昂或因隐私问题而受限。数据无关元学习（DFML）通过利用预训练模型且无需访问训练数据来解决这一挑战。然而，现有的DFML方法依赖模型反转来生成训练数据，这一过程通常困难且计算开销大，因为需要生成匹配原始分布的高维数据。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种新的元学习设置，通过联合利用预训练模型和未标注数据来避免模型反转。我们的方法将预训练模型生成的软标签分配给未标注数据，从而构造元训练任务。由于这些任务的质量可能参差不齐，我们引入了一种基于任务置信度和类别分布平衡的任务加权机制，以确保有效的元学习。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著降低了计算成本并提升了泛化能力，相比现有最优DFML方法，在少样本分类准确率上实现了最高104倍的加速和8.4%至36.4%的提升。

</details>


### [162] [Trading Confidence: Comprehensive Uncertainty Estimation in Algorithmic Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02864)
*Lin Li,Li Rong Wang,Hsuan Fu,Xiuyi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个融合分布性、认知性和偶然性不确定性估计的强化学习框架，通过SHAP加权重建不确定性、MC Dropout和基于LSTM的技术指标共识机制，提升金融交易在高度不确定市场中的表现。在五大美国股指上的实验表明，该方法在收益和风险管理上显著优于传统模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 金融市场具有高度不确定性（随机波动、模型局限、机制转换），传统RL模型在动态环境中难以适应突发的市场干扰，导致次优交易决策。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知的RL框架，整合三种不确定性估计（分布性、认知性、偶然性），具体包括：SHAP加权重建不确定性、MC Dropout、基于LSTM的技术指标共识机制。

Result: 在五大美国股票指数上的实验结果表明，配备不确定性估计的RL智能体在收益和风险管理方面显著优于传统模型。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了基于RL的金融交易中的不确定性估计，未来工作将扩展到其他资产类别和替代RL架构以增强适应性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach in financial trading, enabling agents to learn optimal strategies through direct market interaction. However, financial markets are highly uncertain, with price fluctuations driven by stochastic volatility, model limitations, and regime shifts. Traditional RL models struggle in dynamic environments, often failing to adapt to sudden market disruptions, leading to suboptimal trading decisions. To address this challenge, we propose an uncertainty-aware RL framework that integrates distributional, epistemic, and aleatoric uncertainty estimations. Our approach enhances uncertainty estimation using SHAP-weighted reconstruction uncertainty, MC Dropout, and an LSTM-based technical indicator consensus mechanism. Experimental results on five major U.S. stock indices demonstrate that RL agents equipped with uncertainty estimation significantly outperform traditional models in return and risk management. This study advances uncertainty estimation in RL-based financial trading, with future research extending its application to other asset classes and alternative RL architectures for greater adaptability.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已成为金融交易中的一种强大方法，使得智能体能够通过与市场的直接交互来学习最优策略。然而，金融市场具有高度不确定性，价格波动受随机波动、模型局限和机制转换的驱动。传统RL模型在动态环境中表现不佳，往往无法适应突发的市场干扰，导致次优的交易决策。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了一种不确定性感知的RL框架，该框架整合了分布性、认知性和偶然性三种不确定性估计。我们的方法通过SHAP加权重建不确定性、MC Dropout和基于LSTM的技术指标共识机制来增强不确定性估计。在五大美国股指上的实验结果表明，配备不确定性估计的RL智能体在收益和风险管理方面显著优于传统模型。本研究推进了基于RL的金融交易中的不确定性估计，未来研究将把其应用扩展到其他资产类别和替代RL架构，以增强适应性。

</details>


### [163] [Reward Granularity in RLVR: Comparing Process and Outcome Reward Structures for Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02869)
*Anagha Radhakrishna Palandye,Rebecca Glick,Osheen Kaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种奖励条件（过程-only、结果-only及三种混合权重）对使用GRPO微调Qwen2.5-0.5B模型在GSM8K任务上的影响，发现过程级监督比结果级监督准确率高出近10个百分点（63.73% vs 53.75%），并能生成步骤有效性更高、链长偏差更小的推理轨迹。混合奖励与过程权重正相关，但低过程/高结果配置（λ=0.1）表现弱于纯结果监督，表明存在冲突的优化信号。误差分析揭示了两种失败模式的不同分布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR工作大多仅对最终答案进行奖励（基于结果的奖励），而步骤级过程监督（过程奖励）的影响在小模型上尚未得到充分探索，小模型缺乏在稀疏反馈下自我纠正的能力，因此需要系统研究奖励粒度对推理性能的影响。

Method: 在GSM8K数据集上，使用GRPO算法对Qwen2.5-0.5B模型进行微调，系统比较了五种奖励条件：无RL基线、纯过程监督、纯结果监督以及三种混合权重（λ分别为0.9、0.5、0.1的过程权重）。

Result: 纯过程监督准确率达63.73%，比纯结果监督（53.75%）高出近10个百分点，且生成的推理轨迹步骤有效性更高、与真实链长偏差更小。混合奖励与过程权重正相关，但λ=0.1配置表现弱于纯结果监督。误差分析显示过程模型产生结构不一致但算术正确的轨迹，结果模型产生简洁但易出推导错误的链。

Conclusion: 奖励粒度是RLVR的一阶设计决策，过程级监督能显著提升小语言模型的准确性和推理轨迹保真度。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving mathematical reasoning in language models. Yet most RLVR work rewards only the final answer (outcome-based rewards), leaving the impact of step-level process supervision (process rewards) underexplored especially for small models that lack the capacity to self-correct under sparse feedback. We systematically compare five reward conditions applied to Qwen2.5-0.5B fine-tuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on GSM8K: a no-RL baseline, process-only, outcome-only, and three hybrid weightings ($λ\in \{0.9, 0.5, 0.1\}$ process weight). Process-only supervision achieves 63.73% test accuracy versus 53.75% for outcome-only, a nearly 10-percentage point gap while yielding reasoning traces with higher step validity and lower deviation from ground-truth chain length. Hybrid rewards generally correlate positively with process weight, with one notable anomaly: the low-process / high-outcome configuration ($λ=0.1$) underperforms pure outcome supervision, suggesting conflicting optimization signals. Error analysis using GPT-4o as a judge reveals distinct failure mode distributions: process models generate structurally inconsistent but arithmetically grounded traces, while outcome models produce concise but derivation-error-prone chains. Our results demonstrate that reward granularity is a first-order design decision for RLVR, with process-level supervision substantially improving both accuracy and trace fidelity in small language models.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升语言模型数学推理能力的一种有前景的范式。然而，大多数RLVR工作仅奖励最终答案（基于结果的奖励），步骤级过程监督（过程奖励）的影响仍未得到充分探索，尤其是在小模型上——这些模型缺乏在稀疏反馈下进行自我纠正的能力。我们系统比较了五种奖励条件，这些条件应用于使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）在GSM8K上微调的Qwen2.5-0.5B模型：无RL基线、纯过程监督、纯结果监督以及三种混合权重（λ∈{0.9, 0.5, 0.1}的过程权重）。纯过程监督的测试准确率达到63.73%，而纯结果监督为53.75%，两者相差近10个百分点，同时生成的推理轨迹具有更高的步骤有效性和更小的与真实链长的偏差。混合奖励通常与过程权重呈正相关，但有一个显著的异常：低过程/高结果配置（λ=0.1）的表现弱于纯结果监督，这表明存在冲突的优化信号。使用GPT-4o作为评判者的误差分析揭示了不同的失败模式分布：过程模型生成结构不一致但算术基础的轨迹，而结果模型生成简洁但易产生推导错误的链。我们的结果表明，奖励粒度是RLVR的一阶设计决策，过程级监督能显著提升小语言模型的准确性和轨迹保真度。

</details>


### [164] [Poisson-Gamma Modeling of Inter-Relational Dependencies in Dynamic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02872)
*Nan Fang,Yijun Wang,Hao Liao,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGRE（泊松-伽马关系演化）概率模型，用于建模动态知识图谱中的跨关系依赖关系。该模型采用泊松-伯努利公式表示多关系时间链接，引入伽马分布潜变量捕获实体-因子关联和跨关系依赖，并通过伽马马尔可夫过程建模潜变量的时间演化，在链接预测任务中取得竞争性能，尤其在稀疏场景下表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动态知识图谱随时间演化且常存在噪声和不完整问题，而现有方法对时间依赖和关系依赖的联合建模能力不足，亟需一种能够有效捕获多关系动态知识图谱中跨关系依赖关系和时间演化模式的概率模型。

Method: PGRE模型采用以下方法：(1) 通过泊松-伯努利公式对多关系时间链接进行建模；(2) 引入伽马分布潜变量捕获实体-因子关联以及由共享潜在社区介导的跨关系依赖；(3) 利用伽马马尔可夫过程对这些潜变量的时间演化进行建模，从而以原则性方式刻画关系动态。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，PGRE在链接预测任务中取得竞争性性能，尤其在稀疏设置下表现突出，同时能够揭示动态知识图谱中有意义的关系演化模式。

Conclusion: PGRE作为一种概率模型，能够有效建模动态知识图谱中的跨关系依赖和时间演化，在链接预测任务中表现优异，特别是处理稀疏数据时优势明显，并能提供可解释的关系演化模式。

Original Abstract: Dynamic knowledge graphs are ubiquitous in today's AI applications, as we represent molecular structures, social relationships, and language information using these graph models. As knowledge graphs evolve over time and are often noisy and incomplete, modeling their temporal and relational dependencies becomes crucial for downstream tasks. To address these challenges, this paper proposes PGRE (Poisson-Gamma Relational Evolution), a probabilistic model for modeling inter-relational dependencies in dynamic knowledge graphs. PGRE represents multi-relational temporal links via a Poisson-Bernoulli formulation. It introduces Gamma-distributed latent variables to capture entity-factor associations and cross-relation dependencies mediated by shared latent communities. A Gamma Markov process further models the temporal evolution of these latent variables, enabling principled characterization of relational dynamics. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that PGRE achieves competitive performance in link prediction, particularly in sparse settings, while revealing meaningful relational evolution patterns in dynamic knowledge graphs.

中文摘要翻译: 动态知识图谱在当今的人工智能应用中无处不在，因为我们使用这些图模型来表示分子结构、社会关系和语言信息。随着知识图谱随时间演化且常常存在噪声和不完整性，对其时间和关系依赖性的建模对于下游任务变得至关重要。为应对这些挑战，本文提出PGRE（泊松-伽马关系演化），一种用于建模动态知识图谱中跨关系依赖关系的概率模型。PGRE通过泊松-伯努利公式表示多关系时间链接，引入伽马分布潜变量捕获实体-因子关联以及由共享潜在社区介导的跨关系依赖。伽马马尔可夫过程进一步对这些潜变量的时间演化进行建模，从而能够以原则性方式刻画关系动态。在基准数据集上的实验表明，PGRE在链接预测任务中取得了竞争性性能，尤其在稀疏场景下表现突出，同时能够揭示动态知识图谱中有意义的关系演化模式。

</details>


### [165] [Dynamic Regret for Non-Stationary Linear Bandits via Misspecification Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02891)
*Zihao Hu,Yuan Yao,Jiheng Zhang,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将非平稳线性老虎机问题转化为误设定递减视角，在处理随时间变化的可行决策集和漂移动奖励模型的在线决策问题时，实现了最优的 \(\widetilde O(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3})\) 动态遗憾界，且不依赖于正交结构假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在解决非平稳线性老虎机问题时，虽然能达到最优的遗憾界，但强加了轮次特定决策集的正交结构假设，这在上下文相关的应用中具有限制性。本文旨在消除这一限制，处理具有一般紧致决策集的线性老虎机和K臂上下文线性老虎机。

Method: 提出一种统一的误设定递减视角：将时间范围划分为多个块，将每个块的动态遗憾与固定参数线性老虎机基准的遗憾相关联，其中块内参数漂移被视为有界误设定。利用具有误设定相关遗憾保证的重启算法，实现最优动态遗憾界。

Result: 对于一般紧致决策集的线性老虎机和K臂上下文线性老虎机，均实现了最优的 \(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3}\) 动态遗憾依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法成功去除了正交结构假设，在更一般的设定下达到了与现有最优方法相同的动态遗憾界，填补了非平稳线性老虎机领域的重要理论空白。

Original Abstract: Many online decision-making problems involve both round-specific feasible actions and drifting reward models: eligible ad impressions, feasible prices, and available treatments can change over time, while user preferences, demand curves, and patient responses may evolve. Motivated by these applications, we study non-stationary linear bandits with round-specific feasible decision sets. Existing methods that obtain the optimal \(\widetilde O(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3})\) dependence, where \(P_T\) is the path length of the reward-parameter sequence, impose an orthogonal-structure assumption on round-specific decision sets, which can be restrictive in contextual applications. We address this gap through a unified misspecification-reduction viewpoint: after partitioning the horizon into blocks, we relate each block's dynamic regret to regret against a fixed-parameter linear bandit benchmark, with the within-block parameter drift entering as bounded misspecification. Restarting algorithms with misspecification-dependent regret guarantees then yields the optimal \(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3}\) dynamic-regret dependence for both linear bandits with general compact decision sets and \(K\)-armed contextual linear bandits.

中文摘要翻译: 许多在线决策问题同时涉及轮次特定的可行动作和漂移动奖励模型：合格的广告展示、可行的价格和可用的治疗可能随时间变化，而用户偏好、需求曲线和患者反应也可能演变。受这些应用的启发，我们研究了具有轮次特定可行决策集的非平稳线性老虎机。现有方法获得最优的 \(\widetilde O(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3})\) 依赖关系（其中 \(P_T\) 是奖励参数序列的路径长度）时，对轮次特定决策集施加了正交结构假设，这在上下文应用中可能具有限制性。我们通过统一的误设定递减视角来填补这一空白：在将时间范围划分为块后，我们将每个块的动态遗憾与针对固定参数线性老虎机基准的遗憾相关联，其中块内参数漂移作为有界误设定进入。结合具有误设定相关遗憾保证的重启算法，对于具有一般紧致决策集的线性老虎机和K臂上下文线性老虎机，都能得到最优的 \(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3}\) 动态遗憾依赖关系。

</details>


### [166] [Variable Bit-width Quantization: Learning Per-Group Precision for "Bigger-but-Smaller" Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02893)
*Hamish Ogilvy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变比特量化(VBQ)，让每个连续64个权重的分组通过Gumbel-Softmax学习自己的精度{1,2,4,8}比特，发现高度异构的分配模式，实现了“更大但更小”的效果：相同存储下参数更多、困惑度更低，且加速推理。深层会自修复浅层的量化误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统低比特量化将精度视为全局超参数，所有权重使用相同位宽，无法灵活分配比特预算。本文旨在让精度成为可学习的非均匀资源，在固定比特预算下通过异质分配超越均匀分配的性能。

Method: 提出可变比特量化(VBQ)：将模型每连续64个权重分为一组，每组通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛从{1,2,4,8}比特中学习自己的分辨率，采用交替优化联合训练精度logits和权重，使精度信号干净且与任务对齐。

Result: (1) 69%分组坍缩到1比特，LM头平均1.09比特，第一MLP块约2.5比特，模式稳定可固化复用；(2) 131M模型1.82平均比特在TinyStories上PPL 4.2，超越55M FP16模型(PPL 4.4)，存储减少3.8倍；(3) 1.46B模型在FineWeb-Edu上匹配593M FP16基线，存储减少~3.7倍，参数多2.5倍；(4) 质量每字节效率比FP16高3.9-8.4倍；(5) 推理加速：131M持平，1.0B快1.9倍，9B快4.7倍；(6) 深层逐步自修复浅层量化误差。

Conclusion: VBQ将精度重塑为可学习的非均匀资源，证明在固定比特预算下不均匀分配优于均匀分配，实现了“更大但更小”的模型范式，且存在深层自修复量化误差的机制。从零训练的搜索在1.5B参数内经济有效，更大规模仍有待探索。

Original Abstract: Low-bit quantization shrinks language models but treats precision as a single global hyper-parameter: every weight uses the same bit-width. We introduce Variable Bit-width Quantization (VBQ), a training-time method in which each contiguous group of 64 weights learns its own resolution from {1,2,4,8} bits via a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation, trained jointly by an alternating optimization that gives the precision logits a clean, task-aligned signal. VBQ discovers a consistent, strongly heterogeneous allocation within individual projection types, not merely across layers, impossible to express with per-layer methods: 69% of groups collapse to 1 bit, the LM head averages 1.09 bits, while the first MLP block keeps ~2.5 bits. This pattern is stable enough to freeze into a fixed recipe and reuse without further search. The recipe yields a "bigger-but-smaller" regime: a 131M model at 1.82 mean bits reaches perplexity 4.2 on TinyStories, beating a 55M FP16 model (PPL 4.4) at 3.8x less storage, and lets a 1.46B model on FineWeb-Edu match a 593M FP16 control at ~3.7x less storage with 2.5x more parameters. As quality-per-byte, VBQ is 3.9-8.4x more efficient than FP16. The recipe maps directly to packed low-bit storage, so it also accelerates inference: with custom fused dequantize-and-multiply kernels, memory-bandwidth-bound autoregressive decode is faster at equal output, and the speedup grows with scale (parity at 131M, 1.9x at 1.0B, 4.7x at 9B on Apple silicon). A distributional analysis (KL divergence and argmax-flip rate) reveals a striking mechanism: deeper layers progressively self-heal the quantization error injected by early layers. The win is a from-scratch, train-time phenomenon; scaling the search economically beyond 1.5B parameters remains open. VBQ reframes precision as a learnable, non-uniform resource and shows that spending a fixed bit budget unevenly beats spending it uniformly.

中文摘要翻译: 低比特量化压缩了语言模型，但将精度视为单一的全局超参数：每个权重使用相同的位宽。我们提出可变比特量化(VBQ)，一种训练时方法，其中每连续64个权重的分组通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛从{1,2,4,8}比特中学习自己的分辨率，并通过交替优化联合训练，使精度logits获得干净且与任务对齐的信号。VBQ发现了投影类型内部（而不仅仅是跨层）一致的、强烈异构的分配模式，这是逐层方法无法表达的：69%的分组坍缩到1比特，语言模型头平均1.09比特，而第一个MLP块保持约2.5比特。该模式足够稳定，可以固化为固定方案并无需进一步搜索即可复用。该方案产生了一种“更大但更小”的范式：一个平均1.82比特的131M模型在TinyStories上达到困惑度4.2，超越了55M FP16模型（PPL 4.4），存储减少3.8倍；同时让FineWeb-Edu上的1.46B模型匹配593M FP16对照模型，存储减少约3.7倍，参数多2.5倍。就每字节质量而言，VBQ的效率是FP16的3.9-8.4倍。该方案直接映射到打包的低比特存储，因此也加速了推理：使用自定义的融合反量化-乘核，在相同输出下，内存带宽受限的自回归解码更快，且加速比随规模增长（在Apple芯片上，131M持平，1.0B为1.9倍，9B为4.7倍）。分布分析（KL散度和argmax翻转率）揭示了一个引人注目的机制：深层逐步自修复浅层注入的量化误差。这一优势是从零开始的训练时现象；将搜索经济地扩展到1.5B参数以上仍有待探索。VBQ将精度重新定义为可学习的非均匀资源，并证明在固定比特预算下，不均匀分配优于均匀分配。

</details>


### [167] [Bootstrap Flow-Map Tree Sampling Enables Online Feedback Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02915)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Jens Sjölund,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bootstrap Flow-Map-Tree (BFMT)，一种在有限采样预算下进行历史感知全局搜索与对齐的高效采样框架，通过单次函数评估实现全树路径构建，并动态调度时间步，从全局探索平滑过渡到局部精细化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型在奖励对齐时通常只擅长局部搜索，无法处理先验未知、仅通过序列反馈逐步揭示偏好的场景，缺乏在有限采样预算下进行广泛全局探索以发现高效用区域的能力。

Method: BFMT通过单次函数评估实现任意树深度的全树路径构建，大幅降低计算开销并提供序列采样的前瞻性；同时引入动态过渡时间步调度，在探索过程中从广泛的全局探索平滑过渡到已发现高效用模式的精细局部优化。

Result: 在多种搜索与对齐任务上的大量实验和消融研究表明，BFMT显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: BFMT是一种高效、历史感知的全局搜索与对齐框架，能有效应对有限采样预算下偏好未知且通过序列反馈逐步揭示的挑战，显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: In many scientific and engineering domains, maximizing discovery within a limited sampling budget demands strategic, observation-guided exploration. While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local searches within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we introduce Bootstrap Flow-Map-Tree (a.k.a BFMT), a novel computationally efficient sampling framework designed for history-aware global search and alignment under sampling budget constraints. BFMT enables full tree-path construction from any tree depth using a single function evaluation, drastically reducing computational overhead while providing critical foresight for sequential sampling. By enabling dynamic transition time steps scheduling, BFMT efficiently allocates its sampling budget, smoothly transitioning from broad global exploration to fine-grained local refinement of high-utility modes discovered through exploration. Extensive experiments and ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks demonstrate that BFMT substantially outperforms baseline approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多科学和工程领域中，在有限的采样预算内最大化发现成果需要具有策略性、观测引导的探索。虽然生成模型已经实现了无需训练的奖励对齐，但当前方法通常擅长在底层分布的狭窄区域内进行局部搜索。当偏好先验未知且仅通过序列反馈逐步揭示时——这种情况需要广泛的探索以发现高效用区域——这些方法就会失效。为解决此问题，我们引入了Bootstrap Flow-Map-Tree（简称BFMT），一种新颖的高效采样框架，专为有限采样预算约束下的历史感知全局搜索与对齐而设计。BFMT能够通过单次函数评估实现任意树深度的全树路径构建，大幅降低计算开销，同时为序列采样提供关键的前瞻性。通过支持动态过渡时间步调度，BFMT高效分配其采样预算，从广泛的全局探索平滑过渡到对探索中发现的高效用模式进行精细局部优化。在多种搜索与对齐任务上的大量实验和消融研究表明，BFMT显著优于基线方法。

</details>


### [168] [CoFEND: A Cross-Modal Fusion End-to-End Network for Cold-Start Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02928)
*Di Wu,Hongyi Sun,Haichao Xu,Jia Chen,Zhong Chen,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨模态融合端到端学习网络（CMF-ELN），用于解决冷启动药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测问题。该方法通过构建多模态知识图谱、设计四通道图自编码器进行端到端的跨模态相似度融合，并引入两阶段可解释性方案，显著提升了预测精度和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有冷启动DDI预测方法存在三个主要局限：（1）仅考虑部分关系和机制，忽略跨模态信息，导致相似度建模不完整或有偏；（2）相似度计算与DDI预测分离执行，导致两者不对齐；（3）可解释性分析局限于单模态且仅关注肇事药物，忽视受害药物的易感性因素。因此需要一种能够融合多模态信息、端到端学习并实现双向可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出CMF-ELN，包含三个组件：（1）利用多样化多模态信息构建四种以药物为中心的知识图谱，在重构监督下实现全面的相似度建模；（2）设计四通道图自编码器，在端到端学习框架中融合跨模态相似度；（3）设计两阶段可解释性方案，精确定位肇事药物和受害药物的关键因素。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，CMF-ELN在预测精度上显著优于现有方法，并能提供更全面的机制可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态融合和端到端学习，CMF-ELN有效解决了冷启动DDI预测中的相似度建模不完整、计算与预测不对齐以及可解释性不足的问题，在预测性能和可解释性方面均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Cold-start drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction for new drugs is critical for minimizing unexpected adverse drug reactions. The key challenge is to capture similarity between new and known drugs. However, such similarity is closely associated with complex relationships and mechanisms among drugs, enzymes, transporters, molecular structures, and other biomedical entities. Existing methods have three limitations in capturing such similarity: (1) only partial relationships and mechanisms are considered, which overlooks cross-modal information and yields incomplete or biased similarity modeling; (2) similarity computation between new and known drugs is conducted separately across modalities and performed offline for cold-start DDI prediction, leading to misalignment between similarity computation and DDI prediction; and (3) existing interpretability analyses are typically single-modality and focus primarily on key determinants of the perpetrator drug, while the underlying causes of susceptibility for the victim drug are seldom investigated. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel Cross-Modal-Fused End-to-End Learning Network (CMF-ELN) with three components. First, diverse multimodal information is leveraged to construct four types of drug-centered knowledge graphs, enabling comprehensive similarity modeling under reconstruction-based supervision. Second, a four-channel graph autoencoder is designed to fuse cross-modal similarity within an end-to-end learning framework. Finally, a two-stage interpretability scheme is devised to precisely localize key factors for both perpetrator and victim drugs. Extensive experiments on two real datasets demonstrate that CMF-ELN achieves significantly higher prediction accuracy and more comprehensive interpretability of mechanisms than its peers.

中文摘要翻译: 新药的冷启动药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测对于最小化意外不良反应至关重要。关键挑战在于捕捉新药与已知药物之间的相似性。然而，这种相似性与药物、酶、转运体、分子结构及其他生物医学实体之间的复杂关系和机制密切相关。现有方法在捕捉这种相似性时存在三个局限性：（1）仅考虑部分关系和机制，忽略了跨模态信息，导致相似性建模不完整或有偏；（2）新药与已知药物之间的相似性计算在各模态中独立进行且离线执行，导致相似性计算与DDI预测之间的不对齐；（3）现有的可解释性分析通常是单模态的，且主要关注肇事药物的关键决定因素，而受害药物易感性的潜在原因很少被研究。为解决这些问题，本文提出了一种新颖的跨模态融合端到端学习网络（CMF-ELN），包含三个组件。首先，利用多样化的多模态信息构建四种以药物为中心的知识图谱，在基于重构的监督下实现全面的相似性建模。其次，设计四通道图自编码器，在端到端学习框架内融合跨模态相似性。最后，设计两阶段可解释性方案，精确定位肇事药物和受害药物的关键因素。在两个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，CMF-ELN在预测精度和机制可解释性方面均显著优于现有方法。

</details>


### [169] [In-span learning: adapting reduced-order models using their own predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02937)
*Amirpasha Hedayat,Laura Balzano,Karthik Duraisamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种新的“跨度内自适应”通道，通过在降阶模型的现有子空间内利用模型自身预测轨迹进行增量式奇异值分解与遗忘机制，实现对基的重新加权和对齐，从而提升模型对未来跨度外修正的吸收能力。该方法在三维螺旋、粘性Burgers和Fisher-KPP动力学上得到验证，并被类比为动力系统版本的上下文学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统降阶模型在在线动力学偏离训练数据时精度下降，现有自适应方法仅依赖外部跨度外信息（如全阶修正或传感器快照）更新子空间，忽略了降阶子空间内部尚未被利用的跨度内信息通道。

Method: 提出“跨度内学习”方法：在现有降阶子空间内，将模型自身的预测轨迹流通过增量式奇异值分解（带有遗忘因子）进行处理，得到一个轨迹感知的谱预处理器。该过程不改变子空间本身，而是对基向量进行重新加权和重新对齐，使其朝向动力学实际访问的模式。

Result: 在三维螺旋系统上暴露了该机制的内在特性，并在粘性Burgers方程和Fisher-KPP动力学上验证了其有效性。结果表明，跨度内学习能使模型更有效地吸收未来的跨度外修正信息。

Conclusion: 跨度内学习揭示了模型生成的轨迹中包含的可用信息比此前认知的更多，可视为动力系统版本的上下文学习。该发现为计算科学提出了新的原则。

Original Abstract: Reduced-order models compress high-dimensional dynamics into low-dimensional representations that can be evaluated rapidly, but they lose accuracy when online dynamics drift beyond the training data. Adaptive methods address this by updating the subspace online with external, out-of-span information, such as full-order corrections or sensor snapshots. We discovered that a complementary and previously unexploited in-span adaptation channel exists within the current reduced subspace. By streaming the model's own predictions through an incremental singular-value decomposition with forgetting, we obtain a trajectory-informed spectral preconditioner, in which the subspace is unchanged but the basis is reweighted and realigned toward the modes visited by the dynamics. This enables the model to absorb future out-of-span corrections more effectively. We expose aspects of this mechanism on a three-dimensional spiral and confirm it on viscous Burgers and Fisher-KPP dynamics. We also discuss how in-span learning can be viewed as a dynamical-systems analogue of in-context learning. More broadly, in-span learning suggests a new principle for computational science, revealing that model-generated trajectories contain more usable information than previously recognized.

中文摘要翻译: 降阶模型将高维动力学压缩为可快速评估的低维表示，但当在线动力学偏离训练数据时，其精度会下降。自适应方法通过在线更新子空间并引入外部跨度外信息（如全阶修正或传感器快照）来解决这一问题。我们发现，在当前的降阶子空间内存在一种互补且此前未被利用的跨度内自适应通道。通过将模型自身的预测流经带遗忘因子的增量式奇异值分解处理，我们获得了一个轨迹感知的谱预处理器，其中子空间不变，但基向量被重新加权并重新对齐至动力学实际访问的模式。这使得模型能够更有效地吸收未来的跨度外修正信息。我们在三维螺旋系统上揭示了该机制的内在特性，并在粘性Burgers和Fisher-KPP动力学上验证了其有效性。我们还讨论了跨度内学习如何被视为动力系统版本的上下文学习。更广泛地说，跨度内学习为计算科学提出了一个新原则，揭示了模型生成的轨迹中包含的可用信息比此前认知的更多。

</details>


### [170] [Missingness as Signal: Channel-Independent Spectrogram Learning for Clinical Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02938)
*Soyeon Park,Charmgil Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CISM是一个用于ICU临床时间序列预测的通道独立频谱图框架，通过显式建模缺失模式作为预测信号，在MIMIC-IV数据集上的院内死亡率预测任务中取得了最佳性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ICU临床时间序列预测面临生理变量异质性和信息性缺失的挑战，缺失模式本身反映了临床决策和患者严重程度，可作为预测信号而非简单数据瑕疵，现有方法未能充分利用这一结构化信号。

Method: CISM框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将每个临床变量转换为变量独立的时间-频率频谱图；2) 通过变量对齐编码保留变量身份；3) 将显式的缺失流与频谱图表示对齐融合，利用像素级掩码注入增强对观测模式的建模能力。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV院内死亡率预测任务上，CISM取得了最高的平均AUROC（0.7225）、AUPRC（0.3308）和F1（0.3808），优于时间序列、缺失感知、视觉和时频基线方法。消融实验表明观测模式提供有意义的预测信号，对齐的缺失流在AUROC和AUPRC上带来互补性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 临床时间序列预测中，将观测模式作为结构化信号进行建模至关重要，缺失模式携带的预测信息可通过通道独立频谱图与缺失流对齐的方式有效利用。

Original Abstract: Clinical time series prediction in intensive care units remains challenging due to heterogeneous physiological variables and informative missingness. The presence or absence of a measurement can reflect clinical decisions and patient severity, and thus missingness can serve as a predictive signal rather than a simple data artifact. This work presents CISM, a Channel-Independent Spectrogram framework with a Missingness stream for clinical multivariate time series prediction.
