CISM converts each clinical variable into a variable-wise time-frequency spectrogram, preserves variable identity through variable-aligned encoding, and aligns an explicit missingness stream with the spectrogram representation. Experiments on an in-hospital mortality task derived from MIMIC-IV show that CISM achieves the highest mean AUROC (0.7225), AUPRC (0.3308), and F1 (0.3808) among the compared time series, missingness-aware, vision, and time-frequency baselines. Ablation studies further show that observation patterns provide a meaningful informative signal. Pixel-level mask injection improves performance over plain spectrogram inputs and recovers much of this predictive value. The aligned missingness stream contributes a further, complementary gain in both AUROC and AUPRC. These results highlight the importance of modeling observation patterns as structured signals in clinical time series prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 重症监护病房中的临床时间序列预测由于异质性生理变量和信息性缺失而仍具挑战性。测量值的存在与否能够反映临床决策和患者严重程度，因此缺失可以作为预测信号而非简单的数据瑕疵。本工作提出CISM，一种面向临床多变量时间序列预测的、带有缺失流的通道独立频谱图框架。CISM将每个临床变量转换为变量独立的时间-频率频谱图，通过变量对齐编码保留变量身份，并将显式的缺失流与频谱图表示对齐。在MIMIC-IV数据集上进行的院内死亡率预测任务实验表明，CISM在相比的时间序列基线、缺失感知基线、视觉基线和时频基线中取得了最高的平均AUROC（0.7225）、AUPRC（0.3308）和F1（0.3808）。消融研究进一步表明，观测模式提供了有意义的预测信号。像素级掩码注入相比纯频谱图输入提升了性能，并恢复了大部分预测价值。对齐的缺失流在AUROC和AUPRC上均带来了额外的互补性增益。这些结果突显了在临床时间序列预测中将观测模式作为结构化信号进行建模的重要性。

</details>


### [171] [A Precedent-Guided Co-Scientist for Side-Effect-Aware Drug Redesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02944)
*Yujin Kim,Charmgil Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECEDE 是一个基于先例引导的 AI 共科学家系统，通过 LLM 协调器集成药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱和安全优化先例，在有监督的人类审查流程下实现保留疗效的副作用导向药物重新设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统药物重新设计往往孤立地生成分子，缺乏对现有药理证据和先例的系统性利用，且难以保证在减轻指定副作用的同时保留治疗功能。因此，作者提出一种以证据为基础、受先例引导的自动化工作流来弥补这一空白。

Method: PRECEDE 框架将药物重新设计构建为基于证据的推理过程，利用 LLM 协调器整合三类知识源：药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱以及安全性驱动的优化先例，并辅以显式策略和人工审查检查点，实现可审计、可证伪的假设生成。

Result: 论文未提供具体实验结果，但提出了一个完整的框架设计，将 PRECEDE 定位为人类监督的 AI for Science 工作流，强调假设的可审计性、可证伪性和药理学边界约束。

Conclusion: PRECEDE 通过先例引导和人类监督结合的方式，为副作用感知的药物重新设计提供了一种系统化、可归因的 AI 辅助解决方案，有望提升药物优化的安全性和可信度。

Original Abstract: We propose PRECEDE, a precedent-guided co-scientist for side-effect-aware drug redesign that revises a parent compound to mitigate a specified side effect while preserving therapeutic function. Rather than isolated molecular generation, PRECEDE frames redesign as evidence-grounded reasoning over drug--side-effect associations, biomedical knowledge graphs, and precedents of safety-driven optimization, coordinated by an LLM orchestrator with explicit policies and human-review checkpoints. We position PRECEDE as a human-supervised AI-for-science workflow in which hypotheses remain auditable, falsifiable, and bounded by prior pharmacology.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出 PRECEDE，一个基于先例引导的共科学家系统，用于副作用感知的药物重新设计。该系统在保留治疗功能的前提下，对母体化合物进行修正以减轻特定副作用。与孤立的分子生成方式不同，PRECEDE 将重新设计构建为基于证据的推理过程，涵盖药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱以及安全性驱动的优化先例，并由一个具备显式策略和人工审查检查点的 LLM 协调器统一调度。我们将 PRECEDE 定位为一个人类监督的 AI for Science 工作流，其中的假设保持可审计、可证伪，并受限于已有的药理学知识边界。

</details>


### [172] [Individual Parameters in Weight-Sparse Transformers Appear Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02964)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化LLM流水线，用于描述权重何时对模型预测重要，并在稀疏和稠密Transformer上评估权重的可解释性，发现稀疏Transformer中更高比例的权重具有可解释性（12-31%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 mechanistic interpretability 方法通常聚焦于特定行为反向工程组件角色，但组件在不同输入子集上可能具有不同功能；本文旨在理解单个权重在整个训练分布上的全局作用，而非仅局限于特定子分布。

Method: 提出一个自动化LLM流水线，自动生成关于权重何时重要的简短可读描述，并在保留文本上验证该描述是否泛化，只有描述能泛化的权重才被认为可解释。

Result: 在两类稀疏和两类稠密Transformer上，稀疏Transformer中可解释权重的比例高于稠密Transformer；且剔除不可靠描述后差距进一步扩大。12%至31%的稀疏Transformer权重具有单一简短描述可解释其用途。

Conclusion: 相当一部分稀疏Transformer模型的权重确实可以被全局性地解释，表明自动化可解释性方法在理解神经网络内部机制方面具有潜力。

Original Abstract: A central goal of mechanistic interpretability is to understand how neural networks work and what each individual component does. Dominant circuit-finding approaches focus on a specific behavior and reverse-engineer the role of components on the associated sub-distribution. However, past work has shown that components can have different functions that are active on different subsets of the input distribution. In this work we ask whether a single weight can be understood globally across the full training distribution by characterizing when it matters (the inputs on which ablating it changes the model's predictions). We introduce an automated LLM pipeline that writes a short, human-readable description of when a weight matters and verifies it on held-out text, crediting a weight only if its description generalizes. Across two sparse and two dense transformers, the fraction of weights that are interpretable (in this sense) is higher in sparse transformers than in dense ones, a gap that widens once unreliable descriptions are discarded. Our results show that a meaningful fraction of a sparse transformer model's weights can be interpreted: 12 to 31% of weights have a single short description that identifies what the weight is used for.

中文摘要翻译: 机制可解释性的一个核心目标是理解神经网络如何工作以及每个独立组件的作用。主流的电路发现方法聚焦于特定行为，并对相关子分布上的组件角色进行逆向工程。然而，以往研究表明，组件在不同输入子集上可能具有不同的活跃功能。本文提出的问题是：能否通过刻画单个权重何时重要（即移除它会改变模型预测的那些输入）来全局性地理解它在整个训练分布上的作用。我们引入了一个自动化LLM流水线，该流水线生成关于权重何时重要的简短可读描述，并在保留文本上进行验证，仅当描述能够泛化时才给予该权重可解释性认定。在两类稀疏和两类稠密Transformer上，稀疏Transformer中可解释（在此意义上）的权重比例高于稠密Transformer，且剔除不可靠描述后这一差距进一步扩大。我们的结果表明，稀疏Transformer模型中相当一部分权重是可以被解释的：12%至31%的权重具有单一的简短描述，能够识别该权重被用于什么任务。

</details>


### [173] [Rank-Order N-of-M Codes for Sparse Distributed Memory: Disentangling Representation and Learning Effects in Noise Robustness Against Contemporary Neuromorphic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02967)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了秩阶N-of-M编码作为SDM的替代编码器，通过重实验证、容量实验和鲁棒性实验，揭示了秩阶编码与MAX-Hebbian学习的交互作用带来了主要鲁棒性增益，并分析了编码能耗优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CALM模型将阈值二进制编码器识别为开放设计问题，本文旨在探索更优的编码方案以提升SDM作为在线情节记忆的性能。

Method: 采用秩阶N-of-M编码替代阈值二进制编码，通过忠实重实现验证架构等价性，进行多种子容量实验和BER鲁棒性实验，在GloVe-100和句子嵌入上评估，并分析编码能耗。

Result: 1) 重实验证了WheelSDM与RankOrderSDM的精确等价性（余弦相似度1.0000）；2) 在缩放配置中RankOrderSDM比StandardSDM饱和容量高13.4个百分点；3) 鲁棒性增益主要来自秩阶编码与MAX-Hebbian学习的交互，而非编码器本身；4) 理想化秩阶编码在4位精度下能耗仅为SpikingMamba SI-LIF神经元的一半。

Conclusion: 秩阶编码为SDM提供了可衡量的性能提升，尤其是与MAX-Hebbian学习结合时，其编码能耗优势显著，为CALM等记忆增强架构提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Large language models remain limited as continual learning systems, motivating renewed interest in Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM) as an explicit online episodic memory. CALM (Nechesov and Ruponen, 2025) identifies its threshold-binary encoder as an open design question. This paper evaluates rank-order N-of-M encoding (Furber et al., 2007) as an alternative. We make three contributions. First, a faithful reimplementation validates the published architecture by confirming exact equivalence between WheelSDM and RankOrderSDM (cosine similarity 1.0000 across 10 seeds) and reproducing the documented divergence of RDLIF neurons under interference. Second, multi-seed capacity experiments show RankOrderSDM outperforming StandardSDM by 13.4 percentage points at saturation in the scaled configuration and by 0.8 percentage points at the published architecture scale. Third, BER robustness experiments disentangle representation and learning effects, showing that the large robustness gain arises primarily from the interaction of rank-order encoding with MAX-Hebbian learning, while the encoder alone provides only a small advantage under matched learning conditions. Experiments on GloVe-100 embeddings confirm this small but consistent encoding benefit on real structured data, whereas sentence embeddings exhibit a ceiling effect at low memory load. A secondary analysis shows that idealized rank-order encoding requires half the component-level encoding energy of SpikingMamba's SI-LIF neurons at four-bit precision, although decoder costs dominate overall system energy. These results identify which components of the original rank-order SDM architecture provide measurable benefits for contemporary memory-augmented AI systems, offering practical guidance for architectures such as CALM.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型在持续学习系统中仍然存在局限性，这激发了人们对稀疏分布式记忆（SDM）作为显式在线情节记忆的新兴趣。CALM（Nechesov和Ruponen, 2025）将其阈值二进制编码器识别为一个开放的设计问题。本文评估了秩阶N-of-M编码（Furber等人, 2007）作为替代方案。我们做出三项贡献。首先，一项忠实的重实现通过确认WheelSDM与RankOrderSDM之间的精确等价性（10个种子下余弦相似度为1.0000）并复现已报道的RDLIF神经元在干扰下的发散行为，验证了已发表的架构。其次，多种子容量实验表明，在缩放配置中，RankOrderSDM在饱和时的性能比StandardSDM高出13.4个百分点，在已发表架构规模下高出0.8个百分点。第三，误码率（BER）鲁棒性实验分离了表示效应和学习效应，表明较大的鲁棒性增益主要来源于秩阶编码与MAX-Hebbian学习的交互作用，而在匹配学习条件下单独的编码器仅提供微小优势。对GloVe-100嵌入的实验证实了这种微小但一致的编码优势在真实结构化数据上成立，而句子嵌入在低记忆负载下表现出天花板效应。一项辅助分析表明，在四比特精度下，理想化的秩阶编码所需的分量级编码能耗仅为SpikingMamba的SI-LIF神经元的一半，尽管解码器成本主导了系统总能耗。这些结果揭示了原始秩阶SDM架构中哪些组件为当代记忆增强AI系统提供了可衡量的收益，为CALM等架构提供了实用指导。

</details>


### [174] [MABLE: Masked Autoencoding with Bi-Lipschitz Decoding for Embeddings and Graph Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02990)
*Yaniv Shulman,Shaghayegh Akbarpour,Jack B. Muir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MABLE，一种自监督图嵌入框架，通过掩码重建、双Lipschitz解码和对比对齐，从大规模异构图（地理空间矿产数据）中学习稳定且有判别力的节点和图嵌入，无需判别器或难负样本选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图自监督方法在处理大规模、异构图时面临嵌入不稳定、负样本选择困难等问题，尤其是在地理空间矿产勘探这类复杂场景中缺乏有效的无监督表示学习方案。

Method: MABLE结合掩码自编码重建与固定余弦相似度损失对齐增强视图；双Lipschitz特征解码器将低维重建分量与特征相似性绑定；匹配节点一致性约束用于图池化的上下文；Lipschitz控制的池化稳定图级表示；对齐增强通过掩码、节点丢弃和采样变化训练鲁棒性。

Result: 在铜矿和阿拉伯地盾两个地理空间数据集上，MABLE嵌入提供了互补的下游信号，生成了连贯的嵌入层用于假设生成，无需判别器或难负样本选择。

Conclusion: MABLE是一种有效的大规模异构图自监督学习框架，能够生成稳定、有判别力的嵌入，适用于地理空间矿产勘探等复杂应用场景。

Original Abstract: We propose MABLE (Masked Autoencoding with Bi-Lipschitz Decoding for Embeddings and Graph Metric Learning), a self-supervised framework for learning node and graph embeddings from large, heterogeneous graphs, demonstrated here on geospatial mineral-exploration data. MABLE combines masked reconstruction with fixed cosine-similarity losses that align matched augmented views while keeping unpaired embeddings well spread. A bi-Lipschitz feature decoder ties a low-dimensional reconstruction component of each node embedding to feature similarity, while matched-node consistency shapes the remaining context used by graph pooling. Lipschitz-controlled pooling helps stabilize graph-level representations under perturbations of retained node embeddings, while augmentation alignment trains robustness to masking, node dropping, and sampling variation. Across local copper and regional Arabian Shield studies, MABLE embeddings provide complementary downstream signal and produce coherent embedding-derived layers for hypothesis generation without learned discriminators or hard-negative selection.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出MABLE（用于嵌入和图度量学习的双Lipschitz解码掩码自编码框架），一种从大规模异构图（在地理空间矿产勘探数据上进行演示）中学习节点和图嵌入的自监督框架。MABLE将掩码重建与固定余弦相似度损失相结合，对齐匹配的增强视图，同时保持未配对嵌入的良好分布。双Lipschitz特征解码器将每个节点嵌入的低维重建分量与特征相似性绑定，而匹配节点一致性约束则塑造图池化所使用的剩余上下文。Lipschitz控制的池化有助于在保留节点嵌入受到扰动时稳定图级表示，而对齐增强则训练模型对掩码、节点丢弃和采样变化的鲁棒性。在局部铜矿和阿拉伯地盾区域研究中，MABLE嵌入提供了互补的下游信号，并生成了连贯的嵌入层以用于假设生成，无需学习判别器或难负样本选择。

</details>


### [175] [Transfer Learning in High-dimensional Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03005)
*Joonho Kim,Seyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Trans-Ising 迁移学习方法，用于高维 Ising 模型估计中利用辅助二值数据集，通过损失驱动的源筛选规则和两阶段估计过程，实现比仅用目标数据和简单数据池化更低的估计误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高维 Ising 模型估计中目标样本量通常有限，而如何有效利用相关性未知的辅助二值数据集仍是一个挑战性问题。

Method: 提出 Trans-Ising 方法，包含两部分：(1) 基于留出目标伪似然的损失驱动源筛选规则，用于识别信息性辅助源，防止负迁移；(2) 两阶段估计过程：先通过合并数据的逐节点 ℓ1 正则化逻辑回归得到初始估计，再使用折叠凹惩罚进行仅基于目标数据的修正步骤。

Result: 理论上建立了固定节点 ℓ2 和 ℓ1 误差界、精确图选择一致性以及筛选规则的条件一致性；实验上通过大量模拟和真实数据分析表明，Trans-Ising 比仅用目标数据和简单数据池化方法取得了更低的估计误差。

Conclusion: Trans-Ising 通过有选择地利用辅助数据并结合两阶段估计，有效解决了高维 Ising 模型中小目标样本量下的迁移学习问题，验证了方法的有效性和理论可靠性。

Original Abstract: In high-dimensional Ising model estimation, target sample sizes are often limited, and effectively using auxiliary binary datasets of unknown relevance remains challenging. To address this, we propose Trans-Ising, a transfer learning method that combines a loss-based source screening rule with a two-stage estimation procedure. The method first identifies informative auxiliary sources using held-out target pseudolikelihood to prevent negative transfer. It then computes an initial estimator via pooled nodewise $\ell_1$-regularized logistic regression, followed by a target-only correction step using a folded-concave penalty. Theoretically, we establish fixed-node $\ell_2$ and $\ell_1$ error bounds, exact graph selection consistency, and the conditional consistency of the screening rule. Through extensive simulations and real-data analyses, we demonstrate that Trans-Ising achieves lower estimation errors than both target-only estimation and naive data pooling.

中文摘要翻译: 在高维 Ising 模型估计中，目标样本量通常有限，而如何有效利用相关性未知的辅助二值数据集仍然是一个挑战性问题。为此，我们提出 Trans-Ising，一种结合基于损失的源筛选规则与两阶段估计过程的迁移学习方法。该方法首先使用留出目标伪似然来识别信息性辅助源，以防止负迁移；然后通过合并数据的逐节点 ℓ1 正则化逻辑回归计算初始估计量，随后使用折叠凹惩罚进行仅基于目标数据的修正步骤。在理论上，我们建立了固定节点 ℓ2 和 ℓ1 误差界、精确图选择一致性以及筛选规则的条件一致性。通过大量模拟和真实数据分析，我们证明 Trans-Ising 比仅使用目标数据的估计和简单的数据池化方法取得了更低的估计误差。

</details>


### [176] [Back to Basics: Improving Molecular Understanding in LLMs via SMILES-Graph Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03007)
*Wenda Wang,Jinjia Feng,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolBasic 是一个结构优先的分子大语言模型框架，通过 SMILES-图双向转换和对齐来增强分子结构理解，并在下游任务中取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分子大语言模型在下游任务上表现良好，但缺乏可靠的结构基础，对基本结构识别任务表现不佳，违背了"结构决定功能"的化学基本原则。

Method: 提出 MolBasic 框架，核心是构建多级结构感知基准，以双向 SMILES-图转换作为核心任务来对齐序列表示和拓扑表示；并采用渐进式学习方案，结合标准化的思维链（CoT）引导模型从结构获取逐步过渡到高阶分子推理。

Result: 实验表明 MolBasic 显著提升了分子结构理解能力，并在属性预测和目标优化等下游任务上取得了稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了"结构优先"范式的有效性，证明增强结构理解是提升分子大语言模型整体性能的关键路径。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in molecular large language models have led to strong performance on molecular understanding and generation tasks, yet these gains often come without reliable structural grounding. In particular, existing approaches conflict with the chemistry principle that structure determines function: despite their downstream success, current molecular LLMs perform poorly on basic structure recognition, suggesting that they fail to capture molecular graphs from canonical SMILES. To remedy this, we propose MolBasic, a structure-first framework that strengthens structural comprehension via SMILES-Graph translation. MolBasic is built around a multi-level structure perception benchmark, where bidirectional SMILES-Graph conversion serves as the core task to align sequential and topological representations. On top of this foundation, we employ a progressive learning scheme with a standardized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to steer models from structure acquisition toward higher-level molecular reasoning. Experiments show that MolBasic substantially improves structural understanding and yields robust gains on downstream tasks, including property prediction and objective optimization, supporting our structure-first paradigm.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，分子大语言模型的进展在分子理解和生成任务上取得了优异性能，但这些提升往往缺乏可靠的结构基础。具体而言，现有方法与"结构决定功能"的化学原则相冲突：尽管在下游任务上表现成功，当前的分子大语言模型在基本结构识别上表现不佳，表明它们未能从规范 SMILES 中捕获分子图。为解决这一问题，我们提出 MolBasic，一个结构优先的框架，通过 SMILES-图转换来增强结构理解。MolBasic 围绕一个多级结构感知基准构建，其中双向 SMILES-图转换作为对齐序列表示和拓扑表示的核心任务。在此基础之上，我们采用渐进式学习方案和标准化的思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT），引导模型从结构获取进阶到更高层次的分子推理。实验表明，MolBasic 显著提升了结构理解能力，并在属性预测和目标优化等下游任务上取得了稳定的性能提升，支持了我们"结构优先"的范式。

</details>


### [177] [Do ECG Foundation Models Transfer to Rare Cardiac Diseases? Evidence from Brugada Syndrome Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03009)
*Beatrice Zanchi,Giuliana Monachino,Alvise Dei Rossi,Luigi Fiorillo,Georgia Sarquella-Brugada,Giulio Conte,Francesca Dalia Faraci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了九种公开的心电图基础模型在Brugada综合征检测中的表现，发现预训练的优势主要体现在优化稳定性而非可迁移的临床知识，当模型架构较紧凑时预训练没有显著增益，零样本跨站点迁移时所有方法均接近随机水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究基础模型在生理信号上的大规模预训练是否真正提供了超越优化改进的临床泛化能力，尤其是在罕见心电图表型检测中。

Method: 在BrSwiss队列（294名患者，87例病例）和外部独立HUCA队列（363名患者，76例病例）上，系统评估了九种公开的心电图基础模型在Brugada综合征检测中的表现，采用三种训练策略（从头训练、线性探测、全微调），并设置了多种配置，包括3%数据消融实验和零样本跨站点迁移。

Result: 预训练对无法从头收敛的高容量架构是必要的（AUC提升最高0.411，p<0.05），但对已在标注数据上收敛的紧凑架构无显著增益；在完整BrSwiss上，最佳微调FM（ECG-CPC，AUC=0.962）仅略优于最强监督基线（ECG-CPC从头训练，AUC=0.932；p=0.091）；数据效率优势在BrSwiss-3%上存在（AUC增益=0.055，p<0.01）但在HUCA上未复现；零样本跨站点迁移下，基于FM的管线并不优于监督基线，均接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 对于Brugada综合征检测，基础模型预训练是机械性的而非语义性的，主要提供优化稳定性而非可迁移的临床知识，这挑战了大规模预训练天然编码有临床意义表示的假设，强调了模型架构和数据域对齐的核心作用。

Original Abstract: Background: Foundation models (FMs) trained on large-scale unlabeled physiological data have emerged as a promising paradigm for medical artificial intelligence. Their ability to capture clinically meaningful, transferable representations for rare diseases remains largely unproven. This study investigates whether FM pre-training provides genuine clinical generalization benefits beyond improved optimization for rare electrocardiographic (ECG) phenotypes. Methods: We systematically evaluated nine publicly available ECG FMs for Brugada syndrome detection on the BrSwiss cohort (294 patients, 87 cases) and the independent external HUCA cohort (363 patients, 76 cases), under three strategies (from-scratch training, linear probing, full fine-tuning) across several configurations, including a 3% data ablation and zero-shot cross-site transfers. Results: Pre-training was necessary for high-capacity architectures unable to converge from scratch (AUC gain up to 0.411, p < 0.05), but gave no significant gain for compact architectures already converged on labeled data alone. On full BrSwiss, the best fine-tuned FM (ECG-CPC, AUC = 0.962) only marginally exceeded the strongest supervised baseline (ECG-CPC from scratch, AUC = 0.932; p = 0.091). At matched training-set size, the data-efficiency advantage on BrSwiss-3% (AUC gain = 0.055, p < 0.01) did not replicate on HUCA. Under zero-shot cross-site transfer, FM-based pipelines did not generalize better than supervised baselines, all approaching chance-level performance. Conclusion: For Brugada syndrome detection, FM pre-training is mechanical rather than semantic, providing optimization stability rather than transferable clinical knowledge. These findings challenge the assumption that large-scale pre-training inherently encodes clinically meaningful representations, highlighting the central role of model architecture and data-domain alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 背景：在大规模无标签生理数据上训练的基础模型（FMs）已成为医学人工智能的一种有前景范式。其捕获临床上有意义且可迁移的罕见疾病表征的能力在很大程度上尚未得到证实。本研究探讨FM预训练是否在罕见心电图表型的优化改进之外提供了真正的临床泛化优势。方法：我们在BrSwiss队列（294名患者，87例病例）和独立外部HUCA队列（363名患者，76例病例）上系统评估了九种公开的心电图FM用于Brugada综合征检测的表现，采用三种策略（从头训练、线性探测、全微调），涵盖多种配置，包括3%数据消融实验和零样本跨站点迁移。结果：预训练对于无法从头收敛的高容量架构是必要的（AUC提升高达0.411，p<0.05），但对于已在标注数据上收敛的紧凑架构未带来显著增益。在完整BrSwiss数据集上，最佳微调FM（ECG-CPC，AUC=0.962）仅以微弱优势超过最强的监督基线（ECG-CPC从头训练，AUC=0.932；p=0.091）。在匹配训练集规模的情况下，BrSwiss-3%上的数据效率优势（AUC增益=0.055，p<0.01）在HUCA上未得到复现。在零样本跨站点迁移下，基于FM的管线并未比监督基线表现出更好的泛化能力，所有方法均接近随机水平。结论：对于Brugada综合征检测，FM预训练是机械性的而非语义性的，提供的是优化稳定性而非可迁移的临床知识。这些发现挑战了大规模预训练天然编码有临床意义表征的假设，突显了模型架构和数据域对齐的核心作用。

</details>


### [178] [Can Model Merging Improve Aggregation in DiLoCo?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03011)
*Stefan Horoi,Benjamin Thérien,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了局部SGD/DiLoCo中的伪梯度聚合与基于任务算术的模型合并之间的显式类比，并利用这一联系将合并方法引入分布式优化。实验发现Iso-C方法优于简单平均和动量DiLoCo，并据此提出IsoLoCo（将Iso-C与Nesterov动量结合），在语言模型预训练中显著优于DiLoCo，且优势随worker数量增加而扩大。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通信高效的分布式学习方法（如局部SGD、DiLoCo）在独立局部模型数量增多或局部训练步数增大时，性能相对于FLOP匹配的数据并行方法会下降。同时，模型合并技术在独立微调模型聚合方面取得了显著进展。作者希望建立这两种方法之间的联系，利用模型合并技术来改进分布式优化中的聚合步骤，从而提升低通信分布式训练的性能。

Method: 1) 建立局部SGD/DiLoCo中伪梯度聚合与任务算术模型合并之间的显式类比，将合并方法引入分布式优化；2) 评估多种先进模型合并方法，发现Iso-C最具潜力；3) 基于此提出IsoLoCo，将Iso-C与Nesterov动量结合，适配分布式训练场景。

Result: 1) DiLoCo SGD + Iso-C聚合不仅优于简单伪梯度平均，甚至优于基于动量的DiLoCo；2) IsoLoCo在不同worker数量、模型大小和内部步数设置下均显著优于DiLoCo，且优势随worker数量增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 受合并启发的聚合策略是低通信分布式训练的一种有效方法，IsoLoCo为提升通信高效分布式学习提供了新的思路。

Original Abstract: Model merging techniques, which aggregate independently finetuned models into one to combine their capabilities, have become a topic of significant interest in recent years, with a broad array of methods having been proposed to tackle this problem. Simultaneously, an emerging trend in distributed learning has been the use of methods such as local SGD and DiLoCo, which greatly reduce communication costs by periodically aggregating the independently trained local models. However, these communication-efficient methods have been shown to degrade in performance relative to the FLOP-matched data-parallel gold standard as the number of independent local models grows and as the number of local training steps before global communication is increased. In this work, we draw an explicit analogy between the pseudo-gradient aggregation step in local SGD/DiLoCo and task arithmetic-based model merging, establishing a straightforward way to utilize merging methods in the context of distributed optimization. We then evaluate multiple state-of-the-art model merging methods in this setting and identify one method in particular, Iso-C, as a promising approach for improving DiLoCo. We find that DiLoCo SGD with Iso-C aggregation outperforms not only simple pseudo-gradient averaging but even the momentum-based DiLoCo, despite lacking a momentum mechanism itself. Building on this finding, we propose IsoLoCo, which adapts Iso-C for distributed training by equipping it with Nesterov momentum. Our empirical evaluations on language model pre-training across varying numbers of local workers show that IsoLoCo significantly outperforms DiLoCo, with the gap between them widening as the number of workers increases. This advantage remains present across model sizes and inner step counts, confirming that merging-inspired aggregation is an effective strategy for low-communication distributed training.

中文摘要翻译: 模型合并技术旨在将独立微调的模型聚合成一个以融合其能力，近年来引起了广泛关注，已有大量方法被提出以解决该问题。与此同时，分布式学习中的一个新兴趋势是使用局部SGD和DiLoCo等方法，这些方法通过周期性聚合独立训练的局部模型来大幅降低通信成本。然而，研究表明，随着独立局部模型数量的增加以及全局通信前局部训练步数的增大，这些通信高效方法的性能相对于FLOP匹配的数据并行黄金标准会下降。在本工作中，我们在局部SGD/DiLoCo中的伪梯度聚合步骤与基于任务算术的模型合并之间建立了显式类比，从而为在分布式优化中利用合并方法提供了一条直接的途径。在此框架下，我们评估了多种最先进的模型合并方法，并发现其中一种方法——Iso-C——是改进DiLoCo的有前景的方案。我们发现，采用Iso-C聚合的DiLoCo SGD不仅优于简单的伪梯度平均，甚至优于基于动量的DiLoCo，尽管其本身并不具备动量机制。基于这一发现，我们提出了IsoLoCo，该方法通过为Iso-C配备Nesterov动量来使其适配分布式训练。我们在不同本地worker数量下的语言模型预训练实验表明，IsoLoCo显著优于DiLoCo，且两者之间的差距随worker数量的增加而扩大。该优势在不同模型规模和内部步数下均保持一致，从而证实了受合并启发的聚合是低通信分布式训练的一种有效策略。

</details>


### [179] [Out-of-distribution Neural Inference in Dynamical Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03039)
*Yuan-Bin Zhu,Shuang Qiao,Shi-Ju Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经网络在动力学逆问题中的分布外泛化能力，发现Transformer倾向于保持训练集的链路密度，而卷积模型会利用多数类（无连边）的统计优势走向稀疏预测，揭示了高分布内精度和表面鲁棒性背后并非真正的物理规则学习，而是架构依赖的统计先验。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管神经网络被广泛用于从动力学观测中推断隐藏的物理结构，但其分布外性能是否反映可迁移的物理规则学习仍不清楚。作者希望在一个可控的逆问题（从Glauber磁化轨迹重建动力学Ising模型的交互图）中系统检验这一问题。

Method: 在动力学Ising模型的逆问题中，使用卷积、图、Transformer及混合架构对Glauber磁化轨迹进行训练，通过边群体诊断方法分析不同架构在拓扑和温度偏移下的重建策略，并与真实物理规则进行对比。

Result: 1）数据驱动的训练在不同架构下会产生截然不同且可复现的统计策略；2）Transformer模型倾向于保持训练集的链路密度，而卷积模型会因利用多数类（无连边）的统计优势而坍缩为稀疏甚至全无连边的预测，且这种预测在分布外看似稳定；3）高分布内精度和表观分布外鲁棒性不一定意味着学到了从动力学到结构的物理规则。

Conclusion: 神经重建可能受架构依赖的统计先验支配，而非真正的物理规则学习。这揭示了标准数据驱动学习在物理逆问题中的一个具体失效模式，并激励在机器学习辅助科学发现中引入规则引导的原则。

Original Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly used to infer hidden physical structure from dynamical observations, yet it remains unclear whether their out-of-distribution performance reflects transferable physical rule learning. We address this question in a controlled inverse problem: reconstructing interaction graphs of a kinetic Ising model from Glauber magnetization trajectories. Across convolutional, graph, Transformer, and hybrid architectures, we find that data-driven training produces distinct and reproducible statistical strategies under topology and temperature shifts. Edge-population diagnostics reveal that Transformer-based models tend to preserve the link density of the training ensemble, whereas convolutional models can collapse toward sparse- or no-link predictions that appear out-of-distribution stable by exploiting the majority no-link class. Thus, high in-distribution accuracy and apparent out-of-distribution robustness do not necessarily imply a learned dynamics-to-structure rule. Instead, neural reconstruction can be governed by architecture-dependent statistical priors. Our results identify a concrete failure mode of standard data-driven learning in physical inverse problems and motivate rule-guided principles for machine-learning-assisted scientific discovery.

中文摘要翻译: 神经网络越来越多地被用于从动力学观测中推断隐藏的物理结构，但其分布外性能是否反映可迁移的物理规则学习仍不清楚。本文在一个可控的逆问题中探讨该问题：从Glauber磁化轨迹重建动力学Ising模型的交互图。在卷积、图、Transformer及混合架构上，我们发现数据驱动训练在拓扑和温度偏移下会产生截然不同且可复现的统计策略。边群体诊断揭示，基于Transformer的模型倾向于保持训练集的链路密度，而卷积模型可能坍缩为稀疏或无连边的预测——这种预测通过利用多数无连边类别而在分布外看似稳定。因此，高分布内精度和表观分布外鲁棒性并不一定意味着学到了从动力学到结构的规则。相反，神经重建可能受架构依赖的统计先验支配。我们的结果识别了标准数据驱动学习在物理逆问题中的一个具体失效模式，并激励在机器学习辅助科学发现中引入规则引导的原则。

</details>


### [180] [OmniFocus: Query-Guided Modality-Balanced Token Compression for Omni-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03050)
*Shijie Cao,Qingyu Zhang,Boxi Yu,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniFocus是一种无需训练的查询引导式令牌压缩方法，用于全模态大语言模型，通过对视频和音频进行独立重要性估计，实现模态对称的压缩设计，在低令牌保留率下保持强压缩性能，并在准确率和效率间取得良好平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音视频令牌压缩方法通常依赖单模态引导，忽视了查询相关证据在音视频输入中的时间局部性，并隐含假设两种模态具有时间对齐的信息密度分布，导致模态偏差问题。

Method: 提出OmniFocus，一种无需训练的查询引导式令牌压缩方法。它对视频和音频分别进行独立的重要性估计，采用模态对称的压缩设计，保留各模态特有的显著证据，同时维持音视频对齐，从而缓解单模态引导压缩可能引发的模态偏差问题。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni模型家族上的四个音视频基准实验中，OmniFocus在低令牌保留率下保持强压缩性能；在25%令牌保留率下多个主要基准得分优于现有基线。在DailyOmni上使用Qwen2.5-Omni-7B时，保持59.40准确率，同时相比全令牌基线实现了高达1.38倍的预填充加速。

Conclusion: OmniFocus作为一种无需训练的音视频令牌压缩方法，通过模态对称的独立重要性估计，有效缓解了模态偏差问题，在压缩性能和推理效率之间取得了优越的平衡。

Original Abstract: Omni modal large language models (OmniLLMs) have attracted wide attention for their ability to jointly process audio and video, but they generate large token sequences under audio-visual inputs, leading to substantial inference cost. Existing audio-visual token compression methods often rely on unimodal guidance, overlooking the temporal locality of query-relevant evidence in audio-visual inputs and implicitly assuming that the two modalities share a temporally aligned information density distribution. We propose \textbf{OmniFocus}, a training-free query-guided token compression method for OmniLLMs that performs independent importance estimation for video and audio, enabling a modality-symmetric compression design that preserves modality-specific salient evidence while maintaining audio-visual alignment, thereby mitigating the modality bias issue that can arise from unimodal-guided compression. Experiments on the Qwen2.5-Omni model family across four audio-visual benchmarks show that OmniFocus maintains strong compressed performance at low token retention ratios and outperforms existing baselines on several major benchmark scores at 25\% token retention. On DailyOmni with Qwen2.5-Omni-7B at 25\% token retention, OmniFocus maintains 59.40 accuracy while delivering up to 1.38$\times$ prefill speedup relative to the full-token baseline, highlighting a favorable practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 全模态大语言模型（OmniLLMs）因其联合处理音频和视频的能力而受到广泛关注，但在音视频输入下会生成大量令牌序列，导致高昂的推理成本。现有的音视频令牌压缩方法通常依赖单模态引导，忽视了查询相关证据在音视频输入中的时间局部性，并隐含假设两种模态具有时间对齐的信息密度分布。我们提出\textbf{OmniFocus}，一种无需训练的查询引导式令牌压缩方法，用于OmniLLMs。该方法对视频和音频分别进行独立的重要性估计，实现模态对称的压缩设计，在保留各模态特有显著证据的同时维持音视频对齐，从而缓解单模态引导压缩可能引发的模态偏差问题。在Qwen2.5-Omni模型家族上进行的四个音视频基准实验表明，OmniFocus在低令牌保留率下保持强压缩性能，且在25\%令牌保留率下多个主要基准得分优于现有基线。在DailyOmni上使用Qwen2.5-Omni-7B时，OmniFocus在25\%令牌保留率下保持59.40的准确率，同时相比全令牌基线实现了高达1.38倍的预填充加速，展示了良好的实际准确率-效率平衡。

</details>


### [181] [SHiPPO: Recurrent Memory with Transported Polynomial Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03055)
*Tomoya Mizuguchi,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHiPPO通过将HiPPO的固定通道记忆提升为可移动通道框架，引入了一种传输投影记忆先验，使状态空间模型能够实现与token相关的记忆控制，并通过Sylvester系数动力学保留在线记忆算子，同时支持精确的块仿射扫描和循环解码。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: HiPPO在固定通道坐标中提供递归状态记忆，但现代选择性SSM依赖于token相关的控制和通道交互，现有方法无法在移动通道框架中保持HiPPO的投影记忆特性。

Method: 提出SHiPPO框架，通过任意固定或实现的右传输路径，同时传输近似族和通道度量；状态在绑定的移动框架中遵循Sylvester系数动力学，保留左在线记忆算子同时添加右作用传输。为实现选择性SSM执行，推导了受限制的群局部实现，包括控制器兼容的右作用、指数调整更新、精确块仿射扫描和循环解码。

Result: 更大的当前token写入秩能改善普通预测误差，但无法恢复已写入记忆的顺序敏感变化；传输记忆变体能恢复该信号，移除传输路径后信号消失。有限域联想召回诊断提供了互补的自回归证据。

Conclusion: SHiPPO是一种机制上可解释的传输记忆先验，其证据集中于记忆机制而非广泛的序列建模优势。

Original Abstract: HiPPO gives recurrent states memory semantics as coefficients of online polynomial projections, but in fixed channel coordinates. Modern selective SSMs, by contrast, rely on token-dependent control and channel interaction. We introduce SHiPPO (Sylvester HiPPO), a transported projection-memory prior that lifts HiPPO coefficient memories into a moving channel frame. For any fixed or realized right-transport path, SHiPPO transports the approximation family and channel metric together; conditional on that path, the state is ordinary HiPPO in a tied moving frame and follows Sylvester coefficient dynamics, preserving the left online-memory operator while adding right-action transport. For selective-SSM execution, we derive a restricted group-local realization with controller-compatible right actions, exponential-adjusted updates, exact block-affine scan, and recurrent decoding. We also give a simultaneous-reducibility criterion identifying when right transports collapse to static mixing plus independent scalar or blockwise banks. Controlled diagnostics show that larger current-token write rank improves ordinary prediction error but cannot recover order-sensitive changes to already-written memory; transported-memory variants recover this signal, which disappears when the transport pathway is removed. A finite-field associative-recall diagnostic with interleaved bindings, operations, and queries provides complementary autoregressive evidence while leaving the preferred right-action realization open. Taken together, these results support SHiPPO as a mechanistically grounded transported-memory prior, with evidence focused on memory mechanisms rather than broad sequence-modeling dominance.

中文摘要翻译: HiPPO为递归状态赋予记忆语义，将其作为在线多项式投影的系数，但局限于固定通道坐标。相比之下，现代选择性状态空间模型依赖于与token相关的控制和通道交互。本文提出SHiPPO（Sylvester HiPPO），一种传输投影记忆先验，将HiPPO系数记忆提升到移动通道框架中。对于任意固定或已实现的右传输路径，SHiPPO同时传输近似族和通道度量；在该路径条件下，状态在绑定的移动框架中表现为普通HiPPO，并遵循Sylvester系数动力学，在保留左在线记忆算子的同时添加右作用传输。为实现选择性SSM执行，我们推导了一种受限制的群局部实现，包含与控制器兼容的右作用、指数调整更新、精确块仿射扫描和循环解码。我们还给出了一个同时可约化准则，用于识别右传输何时退化为静态混合加上独立标量或分块存储库。受控诊断表明，更大的当前token写入秩能改善普通预测误差，但无法恢复已写入记忆的顺序敏感变化；传输记忆变体能恢复这一信号，而移除传输路径后该信号消失。一个包含交错绑定、操作和查询的有限域联想召回诊断提供了互补的自回归证据，同时将偏好的右作用实现留待进一步探索。综合来看，这些结果支持SHiPPO作为一种机制上可解释的传输记忆先验，其证据集中于记忆机制而非广泛的序列建模优势。

</details>


### [182] [LACE-SVD: Loss-Aware SVD with Cumulative Error Correction for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03057)
*Zhuowen Liu,Longkun Hao,Shiyu Feng,Xiaowen Chang,Ruiqun Li,Changqun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LACE-SVD框架，通过损失感知的秩预算分配和累积误差校正机制，解决低秩压缩中忽视层间损失敏感性和局部误差传播的问题，显著提升大模型压缩效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SVD压缩方法主要基于局部重构目标分配秩预算，未考虑层间损失敏感性差异，且局部近似误差会沿残差流传播累积，导致全局偏差放大。

Method: 首先估计候选压缩比引起的校准负对数似然增量，求解预算约束分配问题来分配秩预算；然后通过闭式解局部更新精炼压缩模型，并引入传播感知校正以减少残差流输出模块的层输出差异，作为累积误差传播的代理。

Result: 在0.6高压缩比下，LLaMA-7B模型上WikiText-2困惑度（32.57）显著优于Dobi-SVD（46.18）。

Conclusion: LACE-SVD通过显式建模层间损失敏感性和误差传播，显著提升了低秩压缩在高压缩比下的性能，为LLM高效压缩提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: The rapid growth in the parameter scale of large language models (LLMs) has created a strong demand for efficient compression techniques. As a hardware-agnostic and highly compatible approach, low-rank compression has been widely adopted to reduce both memory footprint and computational cost. However, existing SVD-based methods are still largely driven by local reconstruction objectives, overlooking two critical limitations: rank budgets are often allocated without explicitly considering layer-wise loss sensitivity, and local approximation errors can propagate and accumulate through the residual stream, leading to amplified global deviations from the original model. To address these issues, we propose LACE-SVD, a Loss-Aware SVD framework with Cumulative Error correction for LLM compression. LACE-SVD first estimates the calibration negative-log-likelihood increase induced by candidate layer-wise compression ratios and solves a budget-constrained allocation problem to assign rank budgets. It then refines the compressed model with closed-form local updates and introduces a propagation-aware correction for residual-stream output modules, reducing layer-output discrepancy as a proxy for cumulative error propagation. Experimental results demonstrate that at a high compression ratio (0.6), the WikiText-2 PPL of our method on LLaMA-7B (32.57) is significantly better than that of Dobi-SVD (46.18).

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）参数规模的快速增长催生了对高效压缩技术的强烈需求。作为一种硬件无关且高度兼容的方法，低秩压缩已被广泛采用以减少内存占用和计算成本。然而，现有的基于SVD的方法仍然主要由局部重构目标驱动，忽略了两个关键局限性：秩预算的分配通常没有显式考虑层间损失敏感性，且局部近似误差会沿残差流传播和累积，导致与原始模型的全局偏差被放大。为解决这些问题，我们提出了LACE-SVD，一种用于LLM压缩的具有累积误差校正的损失感知SVD框架。LACE-SVD首先估计由候选层间压缩比引起的校准负对数似然增量，并通过求解一个预算约束分配问题来分配秩预算。随后，它通过闭式局部更新来精炼压缩模型，并引入一种传播感知校正用于残差流输出模块，减少层输出差异作为累积误差传播的代理。实验结果表明，在高压缩比（0.6）下，我们的方法在LLaMA-7B上取得的WikiText-2困惑度（32.57）显著优于Dobi-SVD（46.18）。

</details>


### [183] [Spectral Rewiring for Exploration, Purification, and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03065)
*Zhilong Zhang,Hongli Yu,Huan-ang Gao,Hanlin Wu,Yuxuan Song,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAR是一种基于谱空间的后训练编辑方法，通过保留模型更新中的推理有效成分（约0.58%参数）并过滤正交方向，在不重新训练的情况下提升推理性能、减少多领域干扰，并支持专家模型合并实现跨域泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 密集全参数更新会导致推理性能抑制（测试时扩展过早饱和）和多能力整合时的干扰问题，需要一种无需重新训练的方法来提取推理有效成分。

Method: 提出子空间对齐重连（SAR），这是一种后训练编辑方法。它识别基模型谱空间中集中了推理有效更新成分，保留该谱核心并移除正交分量，从而在保留推理增益的同时过滤抑制性能或放大跨域干扰的更新方向。

Result: 仅使用约0.58%总参数即可保留超过99%的后训练性能；提升数学推理中的高k探索；在内部模型的7个智能体编码基准中改进6个；释放被抑制的编码能力的同时保持数学推理和指令遵循；实现跨专家模型合并，超越之前的合并基线和最佳单领域专家。

Conclusion: 从参数几何中提取推理有效更新可以作为一种无需训练的机制，有效提升推理和多领域性能。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a standard post-training recipe for large language models, but dense full-parameter updates create two deployment-relevant bottlenecks: suppressed reasoning performance, often reflected by premature saturation of test-time scaling, and interference when consolidating multiple capabilities through multi-domain training or model merging. We show that the reasoning-effective component of these updates is largely concentrated in the base model's spectral space, motivating Subspace-Aligned Rewiring (SAR), a post-hoc editing method that retains this spectral core while removing orthogonal components. SAR therefore preserves reasoning gains and filters residual update directions that suppress performance or amplify cross-domain interference. Across several model families and scales, SAR extracts compact reasoning cores using as little as approximately 0.58% of total parameters: it preserves over 99% of post-training performance and improves high-k exploration in mathematical reasoning, and generalizes to agentic coding by improving six of seven open benchmarks on an in-house model. SAR also purifies mixed-domain training updates by releasing suppressed coding capability while maintaining math reasoning and instruction following. It further enables model merging across experts, yielding cross-domain generalization that surpasses previous merging baselines and even the best single-domain experts. Overall, SAR shows that extracting reasoning-effective updates from parameter geometry can serve as a training-free mechanism to improve reasoning and multi-domain performance.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习已成为大型语言模型标准后训练流程的一部分，但密集的全参数更新带来了两个与部署相关的瓶颈：推理性能受到抑制（通常表现为测试时扩展的过早饱和），以及通过多领域训练或模型合并来整合多种能力时产生的干扰。我们发现，这些更新中推理有效的成分主要集中基模型的谱空间中，这启发我们提出子空间对齐重连（SAR），一种后训练编辑方法，它在保留这一谱核心的同时移除正交分量。因此，SAR保留了推理增益，并过滤了那些抑制性能或放大跨领域干扰的残余更新方向。在多个模型家族和规模上，SAR仅使用约0.58%的总参数即可提取紧凑的推理核心：它保留了超过99%的后训练性能，提升了数学推理中的高k探索性能，并在内部模型的智能体编程任务上改进了七个开放基准中的六个。SAR还通过释放被抑制的编码能力（同时保持数学推理和指令遵循）来净化混合领域训练更新。此外，SAR实现了跨专家模型合并，产生的跨领域泛化能力超越了之前的合并基线，甚至超过了最佳的单领域专家。总体而言，SAR表明，从参数几何中提取推理有效的更新可以作为一种无需训练的机制，用于提升推理和多领域性能。

</details>


### [184] [Robustness Meets Uncertainty: Evidential Adversarial Training for Robust Selective Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03075)
*Nicolas Sournac,Ahmed Baha Ben Jmaa,Bertrand Braeckeveldt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗训练对预测不确定性可靠性的影响，发现许多方法在提升鲁棒精度的同时恶化了不确定性排序，导致选择性分类性能下降。为此提出Evidential Adversarial Training (EV-AT)，通过在狄利克雷参数空间中对齐干净与对抗预测，改善了鲁棒性与不确定性的权衡，在多个指标上超越了现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对抗训练被广泛用于提升深度神经网络的鲁棒性，但其对预测不确定性可靠性的影响尚不清楚。现有工作很少系统性地同时分析选择性分类与对抗鲁棒性，作者试图填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Evidential Adversarial Training (EV-AT)：基于狄利克雷分布对不确定性建模，包含两个损失项——(i) 基于证据的损失，保证干净样本的准确性与可靠不确定性；(ii) 鲁棒证据对齐损失，在log狄利克雷参数空间中匹配干净预测与对抗预测。

Result: 广泛实验表明EV-AT在鲁棒性与不确定性的帕累托前沿上超越了多种最先进的对抗训练方法；同时发现多种现有方法在提升鲁棒精度时反而恶化了不确定性排序，导致选择性分类行为更差。

Conclusion: EV-AT通过显式对齐干净与对抗预测的不确定性分布，有效缓解了鲁棒性与不确定性可靠性之间的冲突，为安全关键场景提供了更优的权衡方案。

Original Abstract: Safety-critical applications require classifiers that are both robust and reliable. Adversarial training is a widely adopted defense for improving robustness in deep neural networks; however, its effect on the reliability of predictive uncertainty remains underexplored. We investigate this gap through the lens of selective classification, which has rarely been systematically analyzed alongside adversarial robustness. We introduce a unified benchmark for the robustness-uncertainty trade-off. It standardizes architectures, augmentations, threat models, and evaluation metrics across clean, adversarial, and common-corruption settings. Across a wide range of state-of-the-art adversarial training methods, we uncover a recurring failure mode: several approaches improve robust accuracy while degrading uncertainty ranking, leading to poorer selective behavior. To address this, we propose Evidential Adversarial Training (EV-AT), which models uncertainty through a Dirichlet distribution and combines (i) an evidence-based loss promoting clean accuracy and reliable uncertainty with (ii) a robust evidence-alignment loss matching clean and adversarial predictions in log Dirichlet-parameter space. Extensive experiments show that EV-AT shifts the Pareto frontier of robustness-uncertainty trade-offs beyond prior state-of-the-art adversarial training methods. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/NicolasSournac/Robustness_Meets_Uncertainty.EV-AT.

中文摘要翻译: 安全关键应用要求分类器兼具鲁棒性和可靠性。对抗训练是提升深度神经网络鲁棒性广泛采用的防御手段，但其对预测不确定性可靠性的影响仍鲜有探索。我们通过选择性分类的视角来研究这一空白，该视角此前很少与对抗鲁棒性一同被系统分析。我们引入了一个面向鲁棒性-不确定性权衡的统一基准，该基准在干净、对抗和常见损坏场景下标准化了架构、数据增强、威胁模型和评估指标。在多种最先进的对抗训练方法中，我们发现了一个反复出现的失效模式：若干方法在提升鲁棒精度的同时恶化了不确定性排序，导致选择性分类行为更差。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于证据的对抗训练方法（Evidential Adversarial Training, EV-AT），该方法通过狄利克雷分布对不确定性建模，并结合了(i) 促进干净准确率和可靠不确定性的基于证据的损失，以及(ii) 在log狄利克雷参数空间中匹配干净预测与对抗预测的鲁棒证据对齐损失。大量实验表明，EV-AT在鲁棒性-不确定性权衡的帕累托前沿上超越了先前最先进的对抗训练方法。我们的源代码已公开在 https://github.com/NicolasSournac/Robustness_Meets_Uncertainty.EV-AT。

</details>


### [185] [STELLA: Efficient Sensor-to-LLM Translation for On-Device Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03089)
*Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Albert Zomaya,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STELLA提出了一种高效的传感器到LLM翻译框架，通过轻量级分层分词器将多通道惯性传感器数据压缩为紧凑潜变量令牌，在冻结的预训练LLM上实现边缘设备上的实时、准确、隐私保护且可个性化的人体活动识别，在七个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的HAR方法难以部署在边缘设备上：原始传感器提示过长、云端推理引入延迟和隐私风险、微调LLM管道将通用模型变成特定任务分类器。需要一种既能利用LLM的泛化能力又适合边缘部署的解决方案。

Method: 提出STELLA框架，核心是轻量级分层分词器将整个多通道惯性窗口压缩为固定数量的紧凑潜变量传感器令牌，再投影到冻结预训练LLM的嵌入空间，与自然语言提示结合进行标签评分。支持仅更新分词器的设备端个性化，并引入本地检索上下文增强推理。

Result: 在7个公开HAR数据集和8个基准测试上达到SOTA，相比先前方法F1提升高达11.83%；设备端个性化在用户数据积累后进一步带来最高21.91%的F1提升；在边缘设备上实现实时推理。

Conclusion: 高效的传感器分词是将准确、隐私保护且个性化的基于LLM的HAR部署到边缘设备的实用路径。

Original Abstract: HAR is increasingly expected to run continuously on edge devices, yet recent LLM-based methods remain hard to deploy: raw sensor prompts are long, cloud inference adds latency and privacy risk, and fine-tuned LLM pipelines turn general-purpose models into task-specific classifiers. We present STELLA, an efficient sensor-to-LLM translation framework for on-device HAR that shifts the burden from LLM adaptation to sensor tokenization. A lightweight hierarchical tokenizer compresses an entire multi-channel inertial window into a fixed set of compact latent sensor tokens, which are projected into the embedding space of a frozen pretrained LLM and combined with a natural-language prompt for label scoring. This preserves activity-relevant temporal and cross-channel structure while keeping LLM-side computation predictable across sensor configurations. STELLA also supports on-device personalization, adapting only the lightweight tokenizer on small amounts of user-specific labelled data and augmenting inference with a local retrieval context, keeping the LLM, user data, and retrieval on device. Across seven public HAR datasets and eight benchmark settings, STELLA achieves new state-of-the-art performance, improving over prior methods by up to 11.83% F1; on-device personalization yields up to a further 21.91% F1 as user data accumulates after deployment. STELLA also outperforms representative time-series tokenizers under the same LLM pipeline and achieves real-time inference under practical mobile and edge budgets, showing that efficient sensor tokenization is a practical path toward accurate, private, and personalized LLM-based HAR on edge devices.

中文摘要翻译: 人体活动识别（HAR）越来越需要持续运行在边缘设备上，然而近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法仍难以部署：原始传感器提示过长，云端推理增加了延迟和隐私风险，且微调的LLM管道将通用模型转变为特定任务的分类器。本文提出STELLA，一种面向设备端HAR的高效传感器到LLM翻译框架，将负担从LLM适配转移到传感器分词化。一个轻量级分层分词器将整个多通道惯性窗口压缩为一组固定数量的紧凑潜变量传感器令牌，这些令牌被投影到冻结的预训练LLM的嵌入空间中，并与自然语言提示结合进行标签评分。这保留了与活动相关的时间维度和跨通道结构，同时使LLM端的计算在不同传感器配置下保持可预测。STELLA还支持设备端个性化，仅在小量用户标注数据上适配轻量级分词器，并通过本地检索上下文增强推理，使LLM、用户数据和检索均在设备端进行。在七个公开HAR数据集和八个基准设置中，STELLA达到了新的最优性能，F1值相比先前方法最高提升11.83%；设备端个性化在部署后随着用户数据积累，F1值最高可进一步提升21.91%。STELLA在同一LLM管道下也优于具有代表性的时间序列分词器，并在实际移动端和边缘预算下实现了实时推理，表明高效的传感器分词化是将准确、隐私保护且个性化的基于LLM的HAR部署到边缘设备的实用路径。

</details>


### [186] [Stacked LoRA for Subject-Adaptive EEG Foundation Models in Motor Imagery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03094)
*Aymen Sarhane,Fouad Lbakali,Mouad Souissi,Jonathan Lys,Giulia Lioi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stacked LoRA框架，通过结构化解耦全局共享知识与个体特异性神经特征，有效提升EEG基础模型在跨被试运动意念分类中的泛化性能，在多个基准上取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑机接口中EEG解码面临显著的跨被试差异问题，现有基础模型即使经过大规模预训练，仍无法直接作为固定特征提取器用于下游任务，需要额外的适配策略。现有方法缺乏对主体不变知识与个体特异性特征的结构化解耦。

Method: 提出Stacked LoRA框架，在每个适配层将低秩更新分解为两部分：全局适配器（Global adapter，跨所有被试联合训练）和个体特异性适配器（Subject-Specific adapters，各自吸收个体变异性）。对比了三种策略：个体LoRA、全局LoRA、Stacked LoRA（两者组合）。

Result: 在BCI Competition IV-2a、PhysioNet Motor Imagery和临床Zuo2025基准上，Stacked LoRA在大多数骨干网络与数据集组合中取得最佳准确率。分析表明：对于大规模多样化群体，全局适配器已足够；对于临床记录中高跨会话变异性的场景，个体特异性适配器起决定性作用。

Conclusion: Stacked LoRA通过结构化解耦全局与个体知识，有效缓解了跨被试变异性问题；最优的全局-个体平衡取决于目标人群特征，为EEG基础模型的下游适配提供了自适应指导原则。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) decoding for brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) faces a major challenge: substantial inter-subject variability limits effective cross-subject generalization. Consequently, practical systems still rely largely on subject-specific models trained from scratch and requiring individual recalibration. EEG foundation models have recently emerged as a promising alternative; however, even large pretrained models cannot simply be used as fixed feature extractors and still require additional adaptation before they can be reliably applied to downstream tasks. In this work, we address this challenge through targeted adaptation strategies. Building on recent EEG foundation models such as REVE, LaBraM, and LUNA, we examine the impact of different low-rank adaptation strategies on motor imagery classification. We propose a framework that structurally decouples subject-invariant knowledge from subject-specific neural signatures: the low-rank update at each adapted layer is split into a Global adapter, trained jointly across all subjects, and Subject-Specific adapters, each absorbing individual variability. To assess the contribution of each path, we compare three adaptation strategies: (i) subject-specific LoRA (ii) global LoRA and (iii) stacked LoRA, combining both Global and Subject Specific adapters. Experiments on BCI Competition IV-2a, PhysioNet Motor Imagery, and the clinical Zuo2025 benchmark show that Stacked LoRA effectively mitigates inter-subject variability, achieving the best accuracy in the large majority of backbone and dataset combinations. Our analysis further reveals that the optimal balance between the global and subject-specific paths depends on the target population: a shared adapter is sufficient for large, diverse cohorts, whereas subject-specific adaptation is decisive under the high inter-session variability of clinical recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）解码在脑机接口（BCI）中面临一个重大挑战：显著的跨被试差异限制了有效的跨被试泛化能力。因此，实际系统仍然严重依赖从零开始训练且需要个体重新校准的专属于被试的模型。EEG基础模型最近成为一种有前景的替代方案；然而，即使是大型预训练模型也无法简单地用作固定特征提取器，在可靠地应用于下游任务之前仍需额外的适配。在本工作中，我们通过针对性的适配策略来应对这一挑战。基于REVE、LaBraM和LUNA等最新的EEG基础模型，我们考察了不同低秩适配策略对运动意念分类的影响。我们提出一个框架，从结构上将被试不变知识与个体特异性神经特征解耦：每个适配层的低秩更新被拆分为一个全局适配器（在所有被试上联合训练）和若干个体特异性适配器（各自吸收个体变异性）。为评估每条路径的贡献，我们比较了三种适配策略：（i）个体特异性LoRA，（ii）全局LoRA，以及（iii）Stacked LoRA，即同时结合全局适配器和个体特异性适配器。在BCI Competition IV-2a、PhysioNet Motor Imagery以及临床Zuo2025基准上的实验表明，Stacked LoRA有效缓解了跨被试变异性，在绝大多数骨干网络与数据集组合中取得了最佳准确率。我们的分析进一步揭示，全局路径与个体特异性路径之间的最优平衡取决于目标人群：对于大规模、多样化的群体，共享适配器已足够；而对于临床记录中高跨会话变异性的场景，个体特异性适配器起决定性作用。

</details>


### [187] [Heterogeneous Graph Condensation via Role-Aware Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03097)
*Fuyan Ou,Yulin Hu,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HGC-RC提出了一种角色感知的异构图压缩框架，通过轻量传播、角色感知混合聚类和紧凑图重构，在保持任务性能的同时大幅加速大规模异构图上的HGNN训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有异构图神经网络在大规模图上训练计算成本过高，而现有的图压缩方法主要针对同构图设计，依赖计算昂贵的梯度匹配或双层优化范式，难以直接应用于异构场景。

Method: 首先通过轻量传播提取语义增强的节点嵌入；然后引入角色感知混合聚类策略，包括对带标签目标节点进行类划分聚类以保持类别分布，对非目标节点进行无监督类型聚类以保留关键跨类型连接；最后基于聚类分配高效重构紧凑异构图。

Result: 大量实验表明HGC-RC超越了现有最先进基线方法，能够在不牺牲任务性能的前提下有效加速大规模异构图上HGNN的训练。

Conclusion: HGC-RC提供了一种实用且高效的异构图压缩路径，解决了大规模异构图训练的计算瓶颈问题。

Original Abstract: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) have exhibited remarkable efficacy in modeling complex systems with multiple types of nodes and relations, yet their training on large-scale heterogeneous graphs remains computationally prohibitive. Although graph condensation methods can effectively improve learning efficiency on large-scale graphs, existing condensation processes are mainly designed for homogeneous graphs and typically rely on computationally expensive gradient matching or bilevel optimization paradigms, rendering them impractical for heterogeneous settings. To address these limitations, we propose HGC-RC, a simple yet effective role-aware heterogeneous graph condensation framework. Specifically, HGC-RC first extracts semantically enhanced node embeddings via lightweight propagation. It then introduces a role-aware hybrid clustering strategy consisting of class-partitioned clustering for labeled target nodes to preserve class distributions and unsupervised type-wise clustering for non-target nodes to retain critical cross-type connectivity. Finally, a compact heterogeneous graph is efficiently reconstructed based on the resulting cluster assignments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HGC-RC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, offering a practical pathway to accelerate HGNN training on large-scale heterogeneous graphs without sacrificing task performance

中文摘要翻译: 异构图神经网络（HGNNs）在建模包含多种节点和关系类型的复杂系统方面展现出了卓越的效果，然而在大规模异构图上训练HGNN仍面临计算成本高昂的问题。尽管图压缩方法可以有效提升大规模图上的学习效率，但现有压缩过程主要针对同构图设计，且通常依赖计算代价昂贵的梯度匹配或双层优化范式，使其难以适用于异构场景。为解决这些局限，我们提出了HGC-RC，一种简单而有效的角色感知异构图压缩框架。具体而言，HGC-RC首先通过轻量传播提取语义增强的节点嵌入；然后引入角色感知混合聚类策略，包括对带标签目标节点进行类划分聚类以保持类别分布，以及对非目标节点进行无监督类型聚类以保留关键的跨类型连接；最后基于聚类分配高效重构出紧凑的异构图。大量实验表明，HGC-RC优于现有最先进基线方法，为在不牺牲任务性能的前提下加速大规模异构图上HGNN的训练提供了一条实用路径。

</details>


### [188] [Observable- and Positional-Encoding-Dependent Symmetry Readout from Neural Network Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03108)
*Naoya Chiba,Satoshi Sugiyama,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于配备位置编码（PE）的神经场网络，权重中可见的对称性并非真实的对称群本身，而是一个由训练参数、位置编码和读出可观测量共同决定的可观对称集。通过精确可观测性层级结构，揭示了PE设计不仅影响逼近行为，还决定了哪些几何结构可以通过事后权重级可观测性被读取。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的事后神经网络权重分析常试图从参数中直接恢复几何结构，但忽略了位置编码对对称性可观测性的影响，导致某些真实对称性在权重层面被结构性地隐藏。

Method: 提出了精确可观测性层级 $G_{\mathrm{obs}}^{\mathrm{exact}} \subseteq G_{\mathrm{lift}}^{\mathrm{exact}}(φ) \cap G_{\mathrm{true}}$，并使用MLP在二维有符号距离函数上进行了多对称群、多PE（DyadicAxisPE, TriAxisPE, 随机傅里叶特征）和基于Gram矩阵的可观测量实验验证。

Result: 实验结果呈现一致的PE依赖模式：DyadicAxisPE支持$D_4$敏感读出但结构性地抑制$D_3$旋转；TriAxisPE通过将坐标轴替换为三个120度分离的轴降低了$D_3/D_6$读出分数；随机傅里叶特征主要表现出$\pi$旋转响应。

Conclusion: PE设计不仅影响逼近行为，还决定哪些结构可以被事后权重级可观测性读取，为基于原理的可观测性依赖的对称性读出提供了基础。

Original Abstract: Post-hoc analysis of trained neural network weights often seeks to recover geometric structure directly from the parameters. We show that, for positional-encoding-equipped neural fields, the symmetry visible from weights is not the true symmetry group itself, but an observable symmetry set determined by the trained parameters, the positional encoding (PE), and readout observable. We formulate this dependence through an exact observability hierarchy, $G_{\mathrm{obs}}^{\mathrm{exact}} \subseteq G_{\mathrm{lift}}^{\mathrm{exact}}(φ) \cap G_{\mathrm{true}}$, where $G_{\mathrm{lift}}^{\mathrm{exact}}(φ)$ is the set of input transformations that the PE can exactly lift to the feature space. The hierarchy implies that even when a target function has a geometric symmetry, that symmetry may be structurally invisible to weight-level observables if the PE does not represent the corresponding transformation. We test this prediction using MLPs trained on two-dimensional signed distance functions with multiple shape symmetry groups, positional encodings, and Gram-based observables. The results show a consistent PE-dependent pattern: DyadicAxisPE supports $D_4$-sensitive readout but structurally suppresses $D_3$ rotations, TriAxisPE yields lower $D_3$ / $D_6$ readout scores under the tested Gram observables by replacing coordinate axes with three 120-degree-separated axes, and random Fourier features mainly exhibit a $π$-rotation response under these readouts. These findings show that PE design affects not only approximation behavior but also which structures are accessible to post-hoc weight-level readouts. This provides a basis for a principled observable-dependent symmetry readout.

中文摘要翻译: 对训练后神经网络权重的分析常试图直接从参数中恢复几何结构。我们证明，对于配备位置编码（PE）的神经场网络，权重中可见的对称性并非真实的对称群本身，而是由训练参数、位置编码和读出可观测量共同决定的可观对称集。我们通过一个精确的可观测性层级结构 $G_{\mathrm{obs}}^{\mathrm{exact}} \subseteq G_{\mathrm{lift}}^{\mathrm{exact}}(φ) \cap G_{\mathrm{true}}$ 来形式化这一依赖关系，其中 $G_{\mathrm{lift}}^{\mathrm{exact}}(φ)$ 是PE能够精确提升到特征空间的输入变换集合。该层级结构表明，即使目标函数具有几何对称性，如果PE无法表示相应的变换，该对称性在权重级可观测性下可能结构性地不可见。我们使用在二维有符号距离函数上训练的MLP进行了实验验证，涉及多种形状对称群、位置编码和基于Gram矩阵的可观测量。结果呈现一致的PE依赖模式：DyadicAxisPE支持$D_4$敏感读出但结构性地抑制$D_3$旋转；TriAxisPE通过将坐标轴替换为三个120度分离的轴，在所测试的Gram可观测量下获得更低的$D_3/D_6$读出分数；随机傅里叶特征在这些读出下主要表现出$\pi$旋转响应。这些发现表明，PE设计不仅影响逼近行为，还决定了哪些结构可以通过事后权重级可观测性被访问。这为基于原理的可观测量依赖的对称性读出提供了基础。

</details>


### [189] [Integrating Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Safe Reinforcement Learning in a 1-DoF Helicopter System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03125)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可微物理模型直接嵌入PPO Actor损失函数中，通过在训练时模拟短时未来轨迹来惩罚预期安全违规，从而在不依赖复杂奖励塑形的情况下保障工业信息物理系统的硬件安全约束。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度强化学习在工业信息物理系统中面临"黑箱"探索可能违反严格硬件安全限制的问题，而传统方法依赖复杂的奖励塑形来管理这些约束，效率较低且设计困难。

Method: 将可微物理模型直接嵌入近端策略优化（PPO）的Actor损失函数中，在训练过程中模拟短时域的未来轨迹，使策略因预期的安全违规而受到惩罚，且该惩罚独立于任务奖励信号。

Result: 在具有严格俯仰约束的模拟1自由度直升机测试平台上，基于物理信息的软正则化方法显著减少了约束违规，同时保持了可靠的目标跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 物理信息嵌入的软正则化方法能够在不依赖复杂奖励塑形的前提下，有效降低深度强化学习策略在工业信息物理系统中的安全违规行为，兼顾安全性与任务性能。

Original Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) offers powerful control for industrial cyber-physical systems (ICPSs), but its "black-box" exploration risks violating strict hardware safety limits. Typically, these constraints are managed through complex reward shaping. In this work-in-progress paper, we embed a differentiable physics model directly into the proximal policy optimization (PPO) actor loss function. By simulating short-horizon future trajectories during training, the policy is penalized for anticipated safety violations independent of the task-reward signal. Evaluated on a simulated 1-degree-of-freedom helicopter testbed with strict pitch constraints, our physics-informed soft regularizations substantially reduce constraint violations while maintaining reliable target tracking.

中文摘要翻译: 深度强化学习（DRL）为工业信息物理系统（ICPS）提供了强大的控制能力，但其"黑箱"式探索可能违反严格的硬件安全限制。通常，这些约束通过复杂的奖励塑形来管理。在这项进行中的工作中，我们将一个可微物理模型直接嵌入到近端策略优化（PPO）的Actor损失函数中。通过在训练过程中模拟短时域的未来轨迹，策略会因预期的安全违规而受到惩罚，且此惩罚独立于任务奖励信号。在具有严格俯仰约束的模拟1自由度直升机测试平台上进行评估，我们的物理信息软正则化方法在保持可靠目标跟踪的同时，显著减少了约束违规行为。

</details>


### [190] [ACPO: Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization via Fine-Grained Surrogate Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03126)
*Zijun Xie,Yuyang You,Yongzhi Li,Enlei Gong,Zeyu Chen,Quan Chen,Yanhua Cheng,Peng Jiang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了自适应信用分配策略优化（ACPO），一种基于模态局部替代熵的令牌级信用分配框架，通过非对称调节策略更新来改进大语言模型的强化学习推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RL方法在LLM推理中面临稀疏结果奖励导致的令牌级信用分配困难，轨迹级奖励均匀分配无法区分每个令牌贡献，而熵感知方法存在与采样令牌策略更新不对齐的梯度问题。

Method: 提出ACPO框架，基于模态局部替代熵非对称地调节策略更新：在成功轨迹中强调不确定决策，在失败轨迹中强调过度自信令牌。该替代熵具有确定性熵界，在模态对齐和近端更新下保持策略梯度方向至主导阶。

Result: 在数学推理（AIME 2025）和代码生成（HumanEvalPro）基准上，ACPO一致优于DAPO、GTPO和SAPO等强RL基线方法。

Conclusion: ACPO通过令牌级信用分配和非对称策略更新有效提升了LLM的推理能力，在多个基准上取得一致改进。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), but sparse outcome rewards still make token-level credit assignment difficult. Existing scalable RL methods typically assign trajectory-level rewards uniformly across tokens, while recent entropy-aware approaches either rely on coarse detached heuristics or directly optimize true entropy, which can introduce non-local gradient components misaligned with sampled-token policy updates. We propose Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization (ACPO), a token-level credit assignment framework based on a mode-local surrogate entropy. ACPO asymmetrically modulates policy updates by emphasizing uncertain decisions in successful rollouts and overconfident tokens in failed rollouts. We show that the surrogate admits deterministic entropy bounds and, under modal alignment and proximal updates, preserves the policy-gradient direction to leading order. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, including AIME 2025 and HumanEvalPro, show that ACPO consistently improves over strong RL baselines such as DAPO, GTPO, and SAPO.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已显著提升了大语言模型（LLMs）的推理能力，但稀疏的结果奖励仍然使得令牌级信用分配变得困难。现有的可扩展RL方法通常将轨迹级奖励均匀分配给所有令牌，而最近的熵感知方法要么依赖粗糙的分离启发式，要么直接优化真实熵，这可能会引入与采样令牌策略更新不对齐的非局部梯度分量。我们提出了自适应信用分配策略优化（ACPO），一种基于模态局部替代熵的令牌级信用分配框架。ACPO通过强调成功轨迹中的不确定决策和失败轨迹中的过度自信令牌，非对称地调节策略更新。我们证明了该替代熵具有确定性熵界，且在模态对齐和近端更新下，能保持策略梯度方向至主导阶。在数学推理和编程基准（包括AIME 2025和HumanEvalPro）上的实验表明，ACPO一致优于DAPO、GTPO和SAPO等强RL基线方法。

</details>


### [191] [Anticipatory Reinforcement Learning for Trajectory Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03132)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测性状态增强方法，将目标速度和未来参考视界引入DRL状态空间，以解决工业控制中的滞后和超调问题。在1-DoF直升机平台上，仿真误差降低9倍（MAD从2.73°降至0.31°），但零样本迁移到真实硬件存在仿真到现实的差距。有趣的是，使用单一较长前瞻视界的简单配置在真实世界达到了与最复杂模型相当的顶级性能（1.11°），表明物理迁移不一定需要高度细粒度的预测数据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业控制中纯反应式DRL（基于当前跟踪误差）会导致滞后和超调问题，需要在不引入过高计算开销的前提下实现前瞻性控制。

Method: 提出一种预测性公式，通过将目标速度和未来参考视界（prediction horizons）增强到DRL状态空间中，使用PPO算法在1-DoF直升机测试平台上评估了八种配置。

Result: 仿真中误差降低9倍（MAD从2.73°降至0.31°）；零样本迁移到真实硬件存在仿真到现实的差距；使用单一较长前瞻视界的简单配置在真实世界达到最复杂模型同等性能（1.11°）；高度细粒度的预测数据并非物理迁移的必要条件。

Conclusion: 预测性状态增强能显著提升仿真性能，但物理迁移时简单配置即可达到最优性能，表明面向实际部署时应优先考虑简洁的预测设计而非高度细粒度的预测数据。

Original Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) in industrial control often suffers from lag and overshoot due to purely reactive control based on the current tracking error. To achieve anticipatory control without high computational overhead, we introduce a predictive formulation that augments the DRL state space with target velocities and future reference horizons. Evaluating eight configurations using proximal policy optimization (PPO) on a 1-degree-of-freedom (1-DoF) helicopter testbed, simulation results showed a 9-fold error reduction, lowering the mean absolute deviation from 2.73° to 0.31°. However, zero-shot transfer to physical hardware revealed a sim-to-real gap. Interestingly, a simpler configuration using a single, further look-ahead horizon matched the real-world top performance of the most complex model (1.11°). Overall, evaluating various combinations of prediction horizons and target velocities demonstrated that highly granular predictive data is not necessarily required for physical transfer.

中文摘要翻译: 深度强化学习（DRL）在工业控制中常因基于当前跟踪误差的纯反应式控制而出现滞后和超调问题。为在不引入过高计算开销的前提下实现前瞻性控制，我们提出一种预测性公式，通过将目标速度和未来参考视界增强到DRL状态空间中。使用近端策略优化（PPO）在1自由度（1-DoF）直升机测试平台上评估了八种配置，仿真结果显示误差降低了9倍，平均绝对偏差从2.73°降至0.31°。然而，零样本迁移到物理硬件暴露了仿真到现实的差距。有趣的是，使用单一较长前瞻视界的简单配置在真实世界中达到了与最复杂模型相当的顶级性能（1.11°）。总体而言，对各种预测视界和目标速度组合的评估表明，物理迁移不一定需要高度细粒度的预测数据。

</details>


### [192] [Sample-Efficient Pareto Front Modeling for Energy-Aware Reinforcement Learning Using Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03140)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将多目标贝叶斯优化(MOBO)应用于强化学习奖励函数权重的自动选择，以高效发现Pareto最优折中策略，在Quanser Aero 2平台上验证了相较于均匀网格搜索的优越性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业自动化中多目标强化学习通常依赖人工手动加权奖励函数，这种方法耗时、依赖直觉、易引入偏差，且难以发现最优折中解，亟需自动化的权重选择方法。

Method: 将权重选择过程建模为多目标贝叶斯优化(MOBO)问题，采用期望超体积改进(qEHVI)采集函数，在1-DoF俯仰控制Quanser Aero 2实验平台上进行验证。

Result: MOBO方法在超体积和最大展宽两个指标上均优于均匀网格采样，能够以更少的评估预算发现高质量且多样化的折中策略。

Conclusion: MOBO方法能够高效地自动发现Pareto最优折中策略，实现复杂机电系统中的能量感知控制，显著提升样本效率。

Original Abstract: Industrial automation increasingly demands control strategies that balance operational performance with strict energy efficiency requirements. A common approach to solving this multi-objective problem, particularly within the framework of reinforcement learning (RL), is to formulate a single, scalar reward function that linearly combines the competing objectives. However, the manual weighting of these different objectives is heavily reliant on domain intuition, incredibly time-consuming, prone to human bias, and frequently fails to uncover optimal trade-off solutions. This work addresses the critical challenge of automating the weight selection process to systematically and efficiently discover the Pareto front of optimal trade-off policies. We formulate the weight selection process as a multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) problem and evaluate its sample efficiency against a standard uniform grid search baseline. Using a physical Quanser Aero 2 testbed configured for 1-DoF pitch control, our results demonstrate that the MOBO approach, utilizing the expected hypervolume improvement (qEHVI) acquisition function, consistently outperforms uniform grid sampling. MOBO achieves superior hypervolume and maximum spread, successfully identifying high-quality, diverse trade-off policies with a reduced evaluation budget, thereby enabling highly efficient energy-aware control in complex mechatronic systems.

中文摘要翻译: 工业自动化日益需要能够在运行性能与严格的能效要求之间取得平衡的控制策略。解决这一多目标问题的一种常见方法，特别是在强化学习(RL)框架内，是构建一个线性组合竞争目标的单一标量奖励函数。然而，对这些不同目标进行手动加权严重依赖领域直觉、极其耗时、容易引入人为偏差，且常常无法发现最优折中解。本文针对自动选择权重以系统且高效地发现Pareto最优折中策略这一关键挑战。我们将权重选择过程建模为多目标贝叶斯优化(MOBO)问题，并在标准均匀网格搜索基线之上评估其样本效率。使用配置为1-DoF俯仰控制的物理Quanser Aero 2实验平台，我们的结果表明，采用期望超体积改进(qEHVI)采集函数的MOBO方法始终优于均匀网格采样。MOBO在超体积和最大展宽指标上表现更优，能够以更少的评估预算成功识别出高质量、多样化的折中策略，从而在复杂机电系统中实现高效的能量感知控制。

</details>


### [193] [CuBAS: Information Geometric Curvature-Based Adaptive Sampling for Supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03145)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuBAS是一种基于信息几何的自适应数据采样框架，利用Potts MRF模型的曲率估计来选择高信息量的训练子集，在60多个基准数据集上显著优于随机采样和不确定性基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有采样策略大多忽视数据分布的内在几何结构，而训练集的信息量与其规模同样重要，因此需要一种能捕捉数据几何复杂性的自适应采样方法。

Method: 将标注数据集视为统计流形，通过Potts MRF模型的充分统计量计算每个顶点处二阶与一阶观测Fisher信息之比作为局部曲率；构建k-NN图，将节点分为低曲率（平滑同质簇）和高曲率（决策边界附近）两个互补区域，从中联合采样以构建信息量最大的紧凑子集。

Result: 在超过60个基准数据集上的实验表明，CuBAS在多种标注预算和分类器架构下，相比随机采样和基于不确定性的基线方法取得了一致且统计显著的改进，且计算效率高（与k-NN图边数呈线性关系）。

Conclusion: CuBAS是一个计算高效、理论扎实且可解释的自适应数据选择框架，通过统计流形的微分几何为数据采样提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Original Abstract: The informativeness of a training set is as consequential as its size, yet most sampling strategies remain agnostic to the intrinsic geometry of the data distribution. We introduce CuBAS (Curvature-Based Adaptive Sampling), an information-geometric framework for adaptive data selection in supervised classification, grounded in the q-state Potts Markov random field (MRF) model. The central insight is that a labeled dataset can be viewed as a statistical manifold, on which local curvature, estimated via the ratio of second to first-order observed Fisher information, faithfully encodes the geometric complexity of the data distribution. We construct a k-nearest-neighbor graph over the labeled data and derive a closed-form curvature score at each vertex from the Potts sufficient statistics. This curvature signal partitions the graph into two complementary regimes: low-curvature regions, corresponding to smooth, homogeneous clusters, and high-curvature regions, concentrated around decision boundaries that are disproportionately informative for classification. By selecting nodes from both regimes, CuBAS constructs compact yet maximally informative training subsets. Empirical evaluation across more than 60 benchmark datasets demonstrates consistent and statistically significant improvements over random sampling and uncertainty-based baselines, across a wide range of labeling budgets and classifier architectures. CuBAS is computationally efficient (linear in the number of k-NN graph edges), theoretically grounded in the differential geometry of statistical manifolds, and interpretable in terms of the local shape operator of the data manifold.

中文摘要翻译: 训练集的信息量与其规模同等重要，然而大多数采样策略仍忽视数据分布的内在几何结构。我们提出CuBAS（基于曲率的自适应采样），这是一个基于q态Potts马尔可夫随机场（MRF）模型的信息几何框架，用于监督分类中的自适应数据选择。核心洞见在于：标注数据集可被视为一个统计流形，其局部曲率通过对二阶与一阶观测Fisher信息之比进行估计，能够忠实编码数据分布的几何复杂度。我们在标注数据上构建k近邻图，并从Potts充分统计量推导出每个顶点处曲率的闭式解。该曲率信号将图划分为两个互补区域：低曲率区域对应平滑、同质的簇，高曲率区域则集中于对分类具有不成比例信息量的决策边界附近。通过从两个区域联合选取节点，CuBAS能够构建紧凑且信息量最大的训练子集。在超过60个基准数据集上的实验表明，在广泛的标注预算和分类器架构下，CuBAS相比随机采样和基于不确定性的基线方法取得了一致且统计显著的改进。CuBAS计算高效（与k-NN图边数呈线性关系），在统计流形的微分几何上具有扎实的理论基础，并且可基于数据流形的局部形状算子进行解释。

</details>


### [194] [Rethinking Neural Nonlinearity as Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03148)
*Muhammad Sabih,Frank Hannig,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出阈值门控（Threshold Gating, TG）作为一种通用非线性原语，证明标准激活函数（ReLU、SiLU、Softmax等）均为TG的特例，并在多种网络架构上验证了其等价性、可训练性及硬件实现优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统观点认为激活函数是实现神经网络非线性逼近能力的必要原语。本文旨在证明非线性也可以通过输入条件化的分支阈值门控实现，从而统一各类激活函数，并带来训练、压缩和硬件实现方面的收益。

Method: 提出阈值门控（TG）原语，通过输入条件化的阈值分支实现非线性；证明ReLU、PReLU、Hardtanh、SiLU、Sigmoid、Tanh、GELU均为TG的实例；利用Softmax的逐元素Sigmoid形式实现其精确TG转换；提出“最小分支定理”关联分支数量与深度网络的可训练性。

Result: 在CNN、Transformer、循环架构上对预训练网络进行等价转换，无需重新训练即可保持性能；从零训练时TG可超越简单替换激活函数，在模型压缩、性能和训练时间上取得增益；在模拟存内计算系统中，TG可实现统一硬件实现，减少ADC/DAC瓶颈。

Conclusion: 阈值门控是一个统一的非线性原语，能够等价替代现有各类激活函数，同时支持从零训练，并利于硬件高效实现，为神经网络非线性机制提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Activation functions are considered an essential primitive for neural nonlinearity, i.e., they enable neural networks to serve as universal approximators. In this paper, we show that this nonlinearity can also be achieved by input-conditioned threshold gating through branches as a universal primitive. We demonstrate that standard activations -- whether piecewise-linear (ReLU, PReLU, Hardtanh) or smooth (SiLU, Sigmoid, Tanh, GELU) -- are in fact instances of a single Threshold Gating (TG) primitive. For softmax, we show that it admits an exact TG conversion via its equivalent per-element Sigmoid form. We then validate these equivalences by converting pretrained networks across CNNs, transformer-based models, and recurrent architectures, preserving model performance without requiring retraining. Threshold Gating also enables training from scratch that goes beyond replacing existing activations, enabling gains in model compression, performance, and shorter training. We also propose a 'Minimal Branch Theorem' which relates the minimum number of required branches in our primitive to the trainability of general deep neural networks. In terms of hardware implementation, TG maps to a unified implementation in the case of analog in-memory systems, addressing the bottleneck of analog-to-digital and digital-to-analog converters (ADC/DAC) that is known to significantly impact power consumption and on-chip area.

中文摘要翻译: 激活函数被认为是实现神经网络非线性的基本原语，即它们使神经网络能够作为通用逼近器。在本文中，我们表明这种非线性也可以通过输入条件化的分支阈值门控作为一种通用原语来实现。我们证明标准激活函数——无论是分段线性函数（ReLU、PReLU、Hardtanh）还是平滑函数（SiLU、Sigmoid、Tanh、GELU）——实际上都是单一阈值门控（Threshold Gating，TG）原语的实例。对于Softmax，我们证明它可以通过其等价的逐元素Sigmoid形式进行精确的TG转换。然后，我们通过在CNN、基于Transformer的模型以及循环架构上转换预训练网络来验证这些等价性，无需重新训练即可保持模型性能。阈值门控还可以支持从零开始训练，并且不仅限于替换现有激活函数，还能在模型压缩、性能和缩短训练时间方面带来收益。我们还提出了一个“最小分支定理”，该定理将原语所需的最小分支数量与通用深度神经网络的训练能力联系起来。在硬件实现方面，TG在模拟存内计算系统中映射为统一的实现，解决了模数转换器和数模转换器（ADC/DAC）的瓶颈问题，而这一问题已知会显著影响功耗和芯片面积。

</details>


### [195] [Decentralised Federated Learning over Temporal Networks: The Role of Heterogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03171)
*Arash Badie-Modiri,Chiara Boldrini,Lorenzo Valerio,János Kertész,Márton Karsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了去中心化联邦学习在时间网络上的收敛过程等价于惰性随机游走扩散过程，并指出当前实验设置因忽略通信网络的结构和时间不均匀性而高估了收敛速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习研究对通信网络中结构和时间不均匀性的影响理解不足，需要系统分析这些因素如何影响模型聚合与收敛。

Method: 将去中心化联邦学习中的局部平均聚合过程映射为时间网络上的惰性随机游走扩散过程，通过理论分析和真实世界时间网络数据验证。

Result: 1) 去中心化联邦学习的早期和稳态极限阶段均与惰性随机游走扩散动力学一致；2) 典型实验场景因忽略时间和结构不均匀性导致收敛速度被不切实际地高估；3) 真实世界时间网络中的不均匀性大多会显著减缓扩散和收敛过程。

Conclusion: 去中心化联邦学习必须考虑通信网络的结构与时间异质性，否则实验结论不可靠；不均匀性通常会大幅拖慢模型收敛。

Original Abstract: Decentralised federated learning, based on peer-to-peer communication, is increasingly proposed for on-device training of machine learning models, promising a privacy-preserving, communication-efficient training process with no risk of single-point failure. However, the role of structural and temporal inhomogeneities in such fully decentralised settings remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate their effects when model parameters are locally averaged during aggregation. We show that the decentralised federated learning process is governed, both in the early phase and the late, stationary limit, by the same dynamics as a lazy random-walk diffusion process on temporal networks. Based on this mapping, we demonstrate that the typical experimental scenario used in decentralised federated learning leads to unrealistically rapid convergence because of ignoring the temporal and structural inhomogeneities inherent in the communication network. We analyse real-world temporal networks and find that inhomogeneities most often dramatically slow down diffusion, hence the convergence process.

中文摘要翻译: 基于点对点通信的去中心化联邦学习越来越多地被用于设备上的机器学习模型训练，其具有隐私保护、通信高效且无单点故障风险等优势。然而，在这种完全去中心化的设置中，结构和时间不均匀性的作用仍不清晰。本文研究了模型参数在聚合过程中进行局部平均时这些因素的影响。我们表明，去中心化联邦学习过程在早期阶段和稳态极限阶段均受与时间网络上的惰性随机游走扩散过程相同的动力学所支配。基于这一映射，我们证明了去中心化联邦学习中使用的典型实验场景由于忽略了通信网络中固有的时间和结构不均匀性，会导致不切实际的快速收敛。我们分析了真实世界的时间网络，发现不均匀性大多会显著减慢扩散过程，从而减慢收敛过程。

</details>


### [196] [Lyapunov-Guided Training for Hardware-Safe Neural Networks Under Fixed-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04531)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于李雅普诺夫稳定性的量化框架，通过单调投影约束隐藏状态能量，解决低精度神经网络在硬件包装算术下的溢出崩溃问题，在6-16位定点量化中有效抑制激活溢出并恢复稳定训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低精度神经网络在资源受限硬件中具有吸引力，但定点算术的二进制补码溢出包装会改变隐藏激活的符号和幅度，导致数值误差不稳定传播和严重精度下降，现有理想量化模型无法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出李雅普诺夫稳定量化框架：通过逐层李雅普诺夫函数监控隐藏状态能量，施加单调投影约束以确保状态演化在深度上有界且非递增，从而抑制溢出包装带来的数值不稳定。

Result: 在MNIST上使用紧凑补丁变换器评估，在4-16位定点量化下：无约束包装量化感知训练在6-16位下精度几乎退化为随机猜测，激活溢出率超过11%；而单调李雅普诺夫投影将激活溢出率抑制至0.012%以下，在12位时达到86.55%的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于李雅普诺夫的状态控制可以作为硬件感知的稳定机制，实现可靠的定点神经网络推理与训练，有效解决包装算术带来的数值不稳定问题。

Original Abstract: Low-precision neural networks are attractive for resource-constrained hardware, but fixed-point arithmetic introduces failure modes that are often hidden by idealised quantisation models. In particular, two's-complement overflow wrapping can corrupt hidden activations by changing both their magnitude and sign, leading to unstable numerical error propagation and severe accuracy degradation. This paper proposes a Lyapunov-stabilised quantisation framework for low-precision neural networks operating under hardware-style wrapping arithmetic. The hidden-state energy is monitored through a layerwise Lyapunov function, and a monotone projection is applied to enforce bounded and non-increasing state evolution across depth. The method is evaluated on MNIST using a compact patch-based transformer under post-training quantisation and quantisation-aware training with fixed-point bit-widths from 4 to 16 bits. Monte Carlo results show that unconstrained wrapped quantisation-aware training collapses to near-chance accuracy across 6-16 bits, with activation overflow rates exceeding 11%. In contrast, the proposed monotone Lyapunov projection suppresses activation overflow to below 0.012% and restores stable low-precision learning, achieving 86.55% accuracy at 12 bits. These results demonstrate that Lyapunov-based state control can act as a hardware-aware stabilisation mechanism for reliable fixed-point neural inference and training.

中文摘要翻译: 低精度神经网络对资源受限的硬件具有吸引力，但定点算术引入了一些通常被理想化量化模型所隐藏的故障模式。特别是，二进制补码溢出包装会通过改变隐藏激活的幅度和符号来破坏它们，导致不稳定的数值误差传播和严重的精度下降。本文提出了一种李雅普诺夫稳定量化框架，适用于在硬件风格包装算术下运行的低精度神经网络。通过逐层李雅普诺夫函数监控隐藏状态能量，并施加单调投影以强制状态在深度上演化时保持有界且非递增。该方法在MNIST数据集上使用紧凑补丁变换器进行评估，涵盖训练后量化和量化感知训练，定点位宽从4位到16位。蒙特卡洛结果表明，无约束的包装量化感知训练在6-16位下精度几乎退化为随机猜测，激活溢出率超过11%。相比之下，所提出的单调李雅普诺夫投影将激活溢出抑制到0.012%以下，恢复了稳定的低精度学习，在12位时达到86.55%的准确率。这些结果表明，基于李雅普诺夫的状态控制可以作为一种硬件感知的稳定机制，用于可靠的定点神经网络推理和训练。

</details>


### [197] [Understanding electricity consumption behaviour through Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03176)
*Enrico Cofler,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Matteo Giuliani,Andrea Castelletti,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用逆强化学习（IRL）将家庭用电消费行为建模为奖励函数，研究意大利家庭在2021-2023年夏季经历能源危机和热浪冲击后，不同消费者群体的制冷行为变化。研究发现冲击对消费行为的影响存在异质性：部分为暂时调整，部分为持久性改变，部分无明显变化；且消费时间维度是行为异质性的重要来源。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解家庭用电消费对 socioeconomic 和气候驱动因素的非线性响应，为决策者在气候变化和地缘政治紧张背景下设计能源政策提供依据。现有方法大多过度简化了消费者的非线性行为。

Method: 将家庭视为与复杂环境交互的智能体，采用逆强化学习（Inverse Reinforcement Learning）将消费行为表示为模型隐含的奖励函数，并观察这些奖励函数在受到社会经济和气候冲击时的变化。该方法在意大利不同电力消费聚类群上进行了测试。

Result: 1) 2021-2023年夏季的冲击在不同消费者群体间产生了异质性响应：部分为暂时调整（冲击缓解后消退），部分为持久性改变（持续到2023年），部分群体变化不显著。2) 在社会经济和环境背景相似但日间消费时间不同的群体中，响应存在显著差异，表明消费时间维度是行为异质性的独立维度。

Conclusion: 能源政策和需求响应方案不仅要考虑消费者的身份特征和居住环境，还应考虑其消费时间特征以及冲击响应的持久性。

Original Abstract: Understanding how households consume electricity in response to socioeconomic and climatic drivers is important for decision-makers designing energy policies in a changing climate and under geopolitical tensions. Consumers respond differently to thermal stress depending on income, consumption habits and the surrounding built environment, a nonlinear behaviour that most approaches oversimplify. In this study, households are treated as agents interacting with complex environments, and Inverse Reinforcement Learning is used to represent their consumption behaviour as model implied reward functions. Specifically, we observe how these reward functions change when households undergo socioeconomic and climatic shocks. The framework is tested on different clusters of electricity consumption profiles in Italy. Clusters' reward functions are retrieved and used to understand how cooling behaviour changes from summer 2021 to summer 2022 and 2023, before, during and after the energy crisis and a heatwave. We find that these shocks reshaped cooling behaviour heterogeneously across consumer groups, in directions conditioned by their prior habits and built environment. Across the 2021 to 2023 summers, we identify a spectrum of responses: transient adjustments that receded as the shocks eased, durable shifts persisting into 2023, and consumers exhibiting negligible change. At the intradaily scale, groups comparable in socioeconomic and environmental context but differing in their daily timing of consumption responded distinctly, identifying time of use as a separate dimension of behavioural heterogeneity. Energy policies and demand-response schemes should therefore account not only for who consumers are and where they live, but for when they consume and whether their response to a shock persists.

中文摘要翻译: 理解家庭如何响应社会经济和气候驱动因素而消耗电力，对于决策者在气候变化和地缘政治紧张背景下设计能源政策至关重要。消费者对热应力的响应取决于收入、消费习惯和周边建筑环境，这种非线性行为被大多数方法过度简化了。本研究将家庭视为与复杂环境交互的智能体，采用逆强化学习将其消费行为表示为模型隐含的奖励函数。具体而言，我们观察当家庭遭受社会经济和气候冲击时这些奖励函数如何变化。该框架在意大利不同电力消费谱聚类群上进行了测试。我们提取各聚类的奖励函数，用于理解从2021年夏季到2022年和2023年夏季（即能源危机和热浪之前、期间和之后）的制冷行为变化。我们发现，这些冲击在不同消费者群体间异质地重塑了制冷行为，其变化方向受其先前习惯和建筑环境的制约。在2021至2023年的夏季中，我们识别出一系列响应谱：随着冲击缓解而消退的暂时性调整、持续到2023年的持久性变化，以及几乎无变化的消费者。在日内时间尺度上，社会经济和环境背景相似但日间消费时间不同的群体表现出显著差异，揭示了消费时间作为行为异质性的一个独立维度。因此，能源政策和需求响应方案不仅应考虑消费者是谁及其居住地点，还应考虑其消费时间以及其对冲击的响应是否具有持久性。

</details>


### [198] [A Bayesian Framework for Evaluating Scenario Compatibility in Generative Population Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03190)
*Zhenlin Qin,Leizhen Wang,Yancheng Ling,Zhenliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集成贝叶斯更新的框架，用于量化情景级约束在生成式人口综合模型中的兼容性，通过有效样本量（ESS）度量条件化对结构不确定性的压缩，并发现情景影响取决于其与模型学习到的联合结构的对齐程度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前在情景分析中，将宏观目标施加于生成式人口综合模型时采用确定性边际校准，隐含假设目标与模型的结构支撑兼容，但缺乏对情景约束是否位于生成支撑内以及如何影响结构不确定性的系统性检验。

Method: 提出人口感知的条件变分自编码器（population-aware conditional VAE）学习合理的群体结构分布；通过集成采样近似先验结构不确定性；将情景目标视为聚合统计量的概率证据，通过贝叶斯更新计算后验权重；利用有效样本量（ESS）量化情景兼容性和结构不确定性压缩程度。

Result: 实验表明情景影响不仅取决于目标大小，还取决于与学习到的联合结构的对齐程度；当目标落在先验集成支撑外时，会暴露出结构性失效模式。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估下游交通分析中情景可行性和结构一致性提供了一种概率诊断工具。

Original Abstract: Scenario-based transportation analysis specifies future assumptions through aggregate population targets, whereas generative population synthesis models produce detailed individual-level realizations. When scenario targets are imposed on generative models, current practice relies on deterministic marginal calibration, implicitly assuming that the targets are compatible with the model's learned structural support. However, whether scenario-level constraints lie within the generative support--and how strongly they distort structural uncertainty--remains largely unexamined. We propose an ensemble-based Bayesian updating framework to quantify scenario compatibility in conditional population synthesis. A population-aware conditional variational autoencoder is developed to learn a distribution over plausible population structures while preserving aggregate fidelity. An ensemble of realizations sampled from the learned prior provides an empirical approximation of structural uncertainty. Scenario targets are treated as probabilistic evidence over aggregate statistics, and posterior weights are obtained through Bayesian updating across the ensemble. Scenario compatibility is quantified using effective sample size (ESS), which measures posterior concentration and the compression of structural uncertainty induced by conditioning. Experiments demonstrate that scenario impact depends not only on target magnitude but also on alignment with the learned joint structure, and reveal structural failure modes when targets fall outside prior ensemble support. The proposed framework provides a probabilistic diagnostic model for evaluating scenario feasibility and structural consistency before downstream projection and transportation planning.

中文摘要翻译: 基于情景的交通分析通过聚合人口目标来指定未来假设，而生成式人口综合模型则产生详细的个体级别实现。当情景目标被施加于生成模型时，当前实践依赖于确定性边际校准，隐含假设目标与模型学到的结构支撑是兼容的。然而，情景级约束是否位于生成支撑内——以及它们会多强地扭曲结构不确定性——在很大程度上仍未得到检验。本文提出了一种基于集成的贝叶斯更新框架，用于量化条件人口综合中的情景兼容性。我们开发了一种具有人口感知的条件变分自编码器，以学习合理的群体结构分布，同时保持聚合保真度。从学习的先验中采样的集成实现提供了结构不确定性的经验近似。情景目标被视为聚合统计量的概率证据，并通过跨集成的贝叶斯更新获得后验权重。情景兼容性通过有效样本量（ESS）进行量化，该指标衡量后验集中度以及条件化所诱导的结构不确定性压缩。实验表明，情景影响不仅取决于目标大小，还取决于与学习到的联合结构的对齐程度，并且当目标落在先验集成支撑之外时会揭示结构性失效模式。所提出的框架为在下游预测和交通规划前评估情景可行性和结构一致性提供了一种概率诊断模型。

</details>


### [199] [Reduced-Order Models: The Mother of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03198)
*Rajat Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了世界模型的神经解剖学结构早在现代自监督学习之前就已在模型降阶(MOR)和控制文献中独立发展，通过梳理三个社区的工作揭示了两条技术路线的历史渊源，并指出将世界模型部署到高可靠性系统的关键障碍不是预测保真度而是可验证性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示世界模型与现代自监督学习之外更早的模型降阶和控制理论之间的历史联系，指出两者互补的优势与不足，为构建可部署于关键系统的可验证世界模型铺平道路。

Method: 通过跨社区溯源分析，比较三个领域（基于POD的湍流降阶模型、早期计算机视觉的特征脸方法、基于测量的POD设施热控制框架）中独立发展的世界模型功能组件，并对比传统MOR方法与学习型世界模型各自的优势与缺失。

Result: MOR贡献了可验证性、物理基础和极端数据效率；学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表征、可迁移性和长时域推理。当前关键障碍是可验证性而非预测精度。

Conclusion: 提出了一项研究议程：融合两条技术谱系，构建物理基础扎实且可验证的世界模型，以实现在电力、热工、过程控制等不可失败系统中的可靠部署。

Original Abstract: World models -- compressed latent representations of an environment that support action-conditioned prediction and planning -- are typically presented as a product of modern self-supervised learning. This paper argues that the functional anatomy of a world model was independently developed, deployed, and formally analyzed decades earlier in the model-order-reduction (MOR) and control literature, under different names and for a different purpose: the real-time operation of physical systems. We trace the anatomy across three communities. Low-dimensional models of turbulence built on proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) supplied latent dynamics learned from data of a chaotic environment; eigenface methods in early computer vision supplied the encoder-decoder half, including a primitive runtime validity check; and measurement-based POD frameworks for facility thermal control assembled the complete loop -- POD coefficients as latent state, parametric dependence on actuator setpoints as action conditioning, modal reconstruction as decoding, and, critically, a priori analytical error bounds as a verification layer that certified when the model's predictions could be trusted in closed loop. We then examine what each tradition possesses that the other lacks: MOR contributes verification, physical grounding, and extreme data efficiency; learned world models contribute nonlinear representation, transferability, and horizon. We argue that the outstanding obstacle to deploying world models in systems that cannot fail -- power, thermal, process control -- is not predictive fidelity but verifiability, and we outline a research agenda for physics-grounded, verifiable world models that unifies the two lineages.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型——即支撑动作条件预测与规划的环境压缩潜在表征——通常被视为现代自监督学习的产物。本文论证，世界模型的功能解剖结构早在数十年前的模型降阶（MOR）与控制文献中就已独立发展、部署并被形式化分析，只是以不同名称、服务于不同目的：即物理系统的实时运行。我们跨三个社区追溯其解剖结构。基于本征正交分解（POD）构建的湍流低维模型提供了从混沌环境数据中学习的潜在动力学；早期计算机视觉中的特征脸方法提供了编码器-解码器部分，包括一种原始的运行时可有效性检查；而基于测量的POD框架用于设施热控制则组装了完整闭环——以POD系数作为潜在状态、以对执行器设定点的参数依赖作为动作条件、以模态重构作为解码，并且关键地，以先验分析误差界作为验证层，用于认证模型的预测在闭环中何时可被信任。随后我们审视每个传统所拥有而对方所欠缺之处：MOR贡献了可验证性、物理基础和极端数据效率；学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表征、可迁移性和长时域推理。我们指出，将世界模型部署到不可失败系统（电力、热工、过程控制）中的突出障碍并非预测保真度，而是可验证性，并据此提出了一项融合两条谱系的、基于物理的可验证世界模型的研究议程。

</details>


### [200] [OpFlow: Learning Opportunity-Conditioned Choice Potentials for Robust OD Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03200)
*Changjian Liu,Yong Gao,Yuqing Wang,Leyi Su,Honglei Guo,Zhiyang Wang,Xiaoyu Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OpFlow框架，通过解耦OD流为“暴露-选择”机制（可迁移的行中心选择势+可单独标定的源规模），实现分布偏移下OD流预测的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始OD计数监督无法区分可迁移的选择机制与环境特定的捷径，导致深度模型在分布偏移下脆弱。核心在于原始OD计数混合了源产生需求量和需求分配两个对象，需要解耦出真正可迁移的“暴露-选择”规律。

Method: 提出机制约束框架OpFlow，学习行中心的选择势（choice potentials），将流重建分为两步：由选择势诱导出分配矩阵，再与单独标定的源规模（origin scale）结合。分布偏移下，空间暴露和诱导分配可以变化，真正迁移的是从暴露状态到相对选择势的条件映射。
