中文摘要翻译: 目的：表面肌电图（sEMG）能够直接测量肌肉活动，为神经和肌肉骨骼疾病患者的康复评估提供支持。然而，由于sEMG对电极位置在不同会话间变化的敏感性，其更广泛的应用受到限制。为应对这一挑战并实现多会话sEMG，本研究开发了一种新型高密度sEMG（HDsEMG）算法，用以量化电极位置的变化并减轻其对常见时域和频域sEMG特征的影响。方法：11名健康受试者在四块下肢肌肉上进行等长和动态运动，并使用HDsEMG记录。实验重复四次，每次以偏移的位置重新贴放电极阵列。通过最小化空间映射的HDsEMG指标误差来估计阵列位置变化，并将结果与3D扫描的真实值进行对比。最后，在选定电极上计算相对特征差异，评估跨会话sEMG效应被减轻的程度。结果：在81.7%的案例中，电极位置估计优于假设位置不变的假设，其中37.6%的估计误差在1厘米以内。基于算法估计和真实值计算出的各位置间最近电极对的特征差异在统计上相似。相反，同一电极在不同位置间的特征差异显著更大，等长最大包络幅值的平均差异从使用算法时的15.9%增加到未使用算法时的21.1%。结论：该算法的应用减少了因电极位置变化引起的跨会话特征差异。这有助于实现更直接的跨会话特征比较，代表了向稳健的sEMG测量迈出的有希望的一步，可用于肌肉骨骼和神经康复的追踪。

</details>


### [290] [Two-dimensional Fourier compressed sensing under a fixed readout budget per channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03611)
*Nitin Jonathan Myers*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对每通道采样预算受限的二维稀疏信号重建问题，推导了互相关下界（高于经典Welch界），并构造了能达到该界的确定性欠采样模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在通信、雷达和成像等领域，需要从部分傅里叶测量中恢复稀疏信号，但实际中每个通道（如频域的行或列）只能采样固定数量的条目，现有方法未充分考虑这种每通道读出的束对重建性能的影响。

Method: 推导了在每通道读出预算约束下压缩感知矩阵互相关的下界；构造了能达到该下界的确定性欠采样模式，并通过仿真与随机欠采样进行对比。

Result: 证明该下界大于经典Welch界，说明了有限读出预算带来的性能损失；构造的确定性采样模式能达到该下界，并在仿真中优于随机采样。

Conclusion: 每通道读出预算限制了稀疏信号重建性能，所提出的确定性采样模式能有效缓解这一限制。

Original Abstract: Recovering sparse signals from their subsampled Fourier representation is an important problem in communications, radar, and imaging. In this letter, we focus on reconstructing sparse 2D signals (matrices) under the constraint that only a fixed number of entries can be sampled from each channel, e.g., a row or a column in the Fourier domain. For a specified per-channel readout budget, we derive a lower bound on the mutual coherence of the corresponding compressed sensing matrix. We show that our bound is larger than the classical Welch bound, due to a limited readout budget. We also construct deterministic subsampling patterns that attain this bound for a class of matrix dimensions and readout budgets, and benchmark them against random subsampling through simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 从欠采样的傅里叶表示中恢复稀疏信号是通信、雷达和成像领域中的一个重要问题。本文聚焦于在每通道（例如频域中的行或列）只能采样固定数量条目的约束下重建稀疏二维信号（矩阵）。针对给定的每通道读出预算，我们推导了相应压缩感知矩阵互相关的一个下界。我们证明，由于读出预算有限，该下界大于经典的Welch界。我们还构造了能够在一类矩阵维度和读出预算下达到该下界的确定性欠采样模式，并通过仿真将其与随机欠采样进行了对比分析。

</details>


### [291] [STAR-RIS-Assisted Integrated Sensing, Secure Communication, and Power Transfer: A Transmit Power Minimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03637)
*Ling He,Vaibhav Kumar,Yingyang Chen,Miaowen Wen,Christina Pöpper,Marwa Chafii*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于STAR-RIS的集成感知、安全通信和无线能量传输（ISSCPT）系统，通过联合优化波束赋形与STAR-RIS配置以最小化发射功率，仿真验证其相比传统RIS和无RIS基线具有更优性能，并揭示了安全-感知-能量之间的权衡关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究分别验证了集成感知与通信（ISAC）以及无线信息与能量同时传输（SWIPT）的双功能方案，但集成感知、安全通信和功率传输（ISSCPT）三者协同的设计尚未充分探索，主要难点在于设计变量之间存在紧密耦合。为了解耦并扩展空间自由度，作者引入智能超表面，特别是STAR-RIS能够实现全空间波束控制，为高能效ISSCPT提供了天然载体。

Method: 构建STAR-RIS辅助的ISSCPT系统模型，建立发射功率最小化问题，联合优化发射/接收波束赋形和STAR-RIS配置参数；采用交替优化（AO）框架，结合连续凸近似（SCA）、二阶锥规划（SOCP）和特征值分解（EVD）进行求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提出的STAR-RIS辅助设计方案在发射功率需求上优于传统RIS（cRIS）和无RIS基线；同时量化了ISSCPT相比ISAC和安全SWIPT所需的额外发射功率，明确了元表面辅助系统中的安全-感知-能量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: STAR-RIS能够有效支撑ISSCPT系统的全空间波束控制，实现更低的发射功率需求；该研究为未来无线网络中感知、安全通信与无线能量传输的统一架构设计提供了理论基础和性能参考。

Original Abstract: Evolving wireless networks call for architectures that unify sensing, communication, and wireless power transfer. Although integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) and simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) have validated dual-function transmission, the combination of integrated sensing, secure communication, and power transfer (ISSCPT) remains largely unexplored, in part due to the tight coupling among design variables. To address this coupling and expand spatial degrees of freedom, we turn to intelligent metasurfaces: while a conventional reconfigurable intelligent surface (cRIS) reflects only to one side and thus limits coverage and flexibility, a simultaneously transmitting and reflecting RIS (STAR-RIS) enables full-space wave control, making it a natural vehicle for power-efficient ISSCPT. We study a STAR-RIS-assisted ISSCPT system and pose a central question: How much transmit power is required to operate such a system? We formulate a transmit-power minimization problem that jointly optimizes transmit and receive beamforming and the STAR-RIS configuration, and solve it via alternating optimization with successive convex approximation, second-order cone programming, and eigenvalue decomposition. Simulations show that the proposed STAR-RIS-assisted design outperforms cRIS and no-RIS baselines, and quantify the additional transmit power required by ISSCPT relative to ISAC and secure SWIPT, clarifying security-sensing-power tradeoffs in metasurface-assisted systems.

中文摘要翻译: 不断演进的无线网络需要能够统一感知、通信和无线能量传输的架构。尽管集成感知与通信（ISAC）以及无线信息与能量同时传输（SWIPT）已经验证了双功能传输的可行性，但集成感知、安全通信与功率传输（ISSCPT）三者结合的研究仍相对空白，部分原因在于设计变量之间存在紧密耦合。为应对这种耦合并扩展空间自由度，我们转向智能超表面：传统的可重构智能表面（cRIS）仅能向一侧反射，限制了覆盖范围和灵活性，而同时透射和反射的RIS（STAR-RIS）则能够实现全空间波束控制，从而成为高能效ISSCPT的自然载体。本文研究了一个STAR-RIS辅助的ISSCPT系统，并提出一个核心问题：运行该系统需要多少发射功率？我们构建了一个发射功率最小化问题，联合优化发射波束赋形、接收波束赋形以及STAR-RIS配置，并通过交替优化结合连续凸近似、二阶锥规划和特征值分解的方法进行求解。仿真结果表明，所提出的STAR-RIS辅助设计方案优于cRIS和无RIS基线，并量化了ISSCPT相对于ISAC和安全SWIPT所需的额外发射功率，从而阐明了元表面辅助系统中的安全-感知-能量权衡关系。

</details>


### [292] [Virtual Reality-Simulated Interaction Between Micro-Mobility Vehicles and Pedestrians: A Biomechanical Analysis of Human Gait and Movement Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03705)
*Sahan Siriwardena,Omar Aboelrous,Anas Madkoor,Mohamed Elansari,Aiman Alhetari,Jassim Al Jufairi,Mohammed Al-Kuwari,Qinaat Hussain,Charitha Dias,Ajith Sominanda Herath*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究利用沉浸式虚拟现实（VR）与无标记姿态估计技术，分析了行人在与电动滑板车交互时的步态适应特征，发现交叉口和近碰撞场景显著缩短了步长，并引发了反射性步态调整，表明该方法可有效量化行人对微移动交通工具的 Biomechanical 响应，为智能交通安全提供敏感指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究多依赖基于轨迹的观测方法，缺乏对行人在与电动滑板车交互过程中 Biomechanical 步态响应的深入理解，难以准确量化碰撞风险。

Method: 招募12名健康男性大学生，在沉浸式VR环境中完成四种步行场景（正常行走、电动滑板车迎面而来、交叉口相遇、近碰撞相遇），使用OpenPose 25点模型分析矢状面视频，通过Kinovea和自定义JSON工具提取步长、步态周期时间、步行速度、站立相与摆动相、下肢关节轨迹等参数，并采用ANOVA、MANOVA和非参数检验进行统计分析。

Result: 交叉口和近碰撞场景显著缩短了步长（p<0.001），从正常行走的226.5 cm降至近碰撞场景的204.7 cm；步态速度和时序未受显著影响，但参与者在压力场景中表现出更短的站立相、更长的摆动相以及受限的膝关节运动。

Conclusion: 沉浸式VR结合无标记姿态估计能有效量化行人对微移动交互的 Biomechanical 步态响应，所识别的步态适应特征可作为碰撞风险的敏感指标，支持开发主动式行人安全措施和智能微移动控制系统。

Original Abstract: Pedestrian walking is a fundamental activity of daily living and a key component of first and last-mile urban mobility. The rapid adoption of e-scooters has increased pedestrian-vehicle interactions on shared sidewalks and crossings, raising collision risks. However, most previous studies have relied on trajectory-based observations, providing limited insight into biomechanical gait responses. This study investigated pedestrian gait adaptations during simulated e-scooter interactions using immersive virtual reality (VR) and markerless pose estimation. Twelve healthy male university students (21-23 years) completed four VR walking scenarios: normal walking, e-scooter encounters at 10-25 km/h, crossing encounters, and near-crash encounters. Sagittal-plane videos were analyzed using the OpenPose 25-point model. Step length, gait cycle time, walking velocity, stance and swing phases, and lower-limb joint trajectories were extracted using Kinovea and custom JSON-based analysis tools. Statistical analyses included ANOVA, MANOVA, and non-parametric tests Crossing and near-crash scenarios significantly reduced step length (p<0.001), from 226.5 cm during normal walking to 204.7 cm during near-crash simulations. Although gait velocity and timing were not significantly affected, participants consistently exhibited shorter stance phases, longer swing phases, and restricted knee motion during stressful encounters, indicating reflexive gait adaptations to perceived collision risk. These findings demonstrate that immersive VR combined with markerless pose estimation effectively quantifies pedestrian biomechanical responses to micro-mobility interactions. Gait adaptations identified in this study may serve as sensitive indicators of collision risk and support the development of proactive pedestrian safety measures and intelligent micro-mobility control systems.

中文摘要翻译: 行人步行是日常生活的基本活动，也是城市交通“第一公里”和“最后一公里”出行的关键组成部分。电动滑板车的快速普及增加了共享人行道和交叉口上行人与车辆的交互，从而提高了碰撞风险。然而，以往大多数研究依赖于基于轨迹的观测方法，对 Biomechanical 步态响应的洞察十分有限。本研究利用沉浸式虚拟现实（VR）和无标记姿态估计技术，探究了行人在模拟电动滑板车交互过程中的步态适应特征。12名健康男性大学生（21-23岁）完成了四种VR步行场景：正常行走、电动滑板车以10-25 km/h速度迎面而来、交叉口相遇以及近碰撞相遇。使用OpenPose 25点模型分析矢状面视频，通过Kinovea和自定义基于JSON的分析工具提取步长、步态周期时间、步行速度、站立相与摆动相以及下肢关节轨迹。统计分析包括方差分析（ANOVA）、多元方差分析（MANOVA）和非参数检验。交叉口和近碰撞场景显著缩短了步长（p<0.001），从正常行走时的226.5 cm降至近碰撞模拟时的204.7 cm。尽管步态速度和时序未受到显著影响，但参与者在压力场景中一致表现出更短的站立相、更长的摆动相以及受限的膝关节运动，这表明行人对感知到的碰撞风险产生了反射性步态适应。这些发现表明，沉浸式VR结合无标记姿态估计可有效量化行人对微移动交互的 Biomechanical 响应。本研究所识别的步态适应特征可作为碰撞风险的敏感指标，并支持开发主动式行人安全措施和智能微移动控制系统。

</details>


### [293] [LAMBDA: A Low-Altitude Multimodal Base Dataset for UAV Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03826)
*Lin Zhou,Peichuan Rao,Chenshuo Zhang,Jianhua Mo,Shu Sun,Zhiyong Chen,Meixia Tao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: LAMBDA是一个面向低空通感一体化(ISAC)研究的高保真多模态基础数据集，通过数字孪生管线生成，包含RGB、深度图、LiDAR点云、IMU、UAV位姿、CSI和雷达数据，覆盖多种场景与天气条件，已通过质量控制和两项UAV ISAC用例验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低空通感一体化(ISAC)研究需要对齐的多模态数据，能够联合描述无线传播、视觉外观、UAV运动、LiDAR感知和雷达感知在相同轨迹和时间戳下的对应关系，现有数据集难以满足这一需求。

Method: 通过高保真数字孪生管线生成数据集，包含精细场景几何、材质分配和UAV电磁建模，提供同步的多模态数据，支持用户自定义天线阵列尺寸、带宽、子载波间隔、线性调频参数及平面波/球面波信道合成。

Result: 数据集覆盖城市、郊区和校园场景，多UAV/多基站设置，夜间条件及晴/雨/雪/雾天气变化；通过质量控制、天气与多模态可视化以及两项用例（RGB辅助波束预测和RGB-LiDAR联合UAV定位）验证了数据集的可靠性和可用性。

Conclusion: LAMBDA数据集具备高保真度、模态多样性、场景丰富性和配置灵活性，能够有效支撑低空ISAC研究中的数据需求。

Original Abstract: Research on low-altitude integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) requires aligned multimodal data that jointly describe wireless propagation, visual appearance, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) motion, light detection and ranging (LiDAR) perception, and radar sensing under common trajectories and timestamps. To address this need, a low-altitude multimodal base dataset, named LAMBDA, is introduced. LAMBDA is characterized by high fidelity, modality diversity, scenario richness, and configuration flexibility. It is generated through a high-fidelity digital-twin pipeline with detailed scene geometry, refined material assignment, and electromagnetic modeling of UAVs. LAMBDA provides synchronized RGB images, depth maps, LiDAR point clouds, inertial measurement unit states, UAV poses, channel state information (CSI), and radar-synthesis resources across matched low-altitude operating conditions, shared coordinate systems, and synchronized frame indices. The dataset covers urban, suburban, and campus scenes, multi-UAV/multi-base-station settings, nighttime conditions, and sunny, rainy, snowy, and foggy weather variations. Its CSI and radar resources support user-defined antenna-array sizes, bandwidths, subcarrier spacings, chirp parameters, and plane-wave or spherical-wavefront channel synthesis. The reliability and usability of LAMBDA are assessed through quality control, weather and multimodal visualization, and two UAV ISAC-related use cases: RGB-aided beam prediction and RGB-LiDAR-based UAV localization.

中文摘要翻译: 低空通感一体化(ISAC)研究需要对齐的多模态数据，这些数据需在共同轨迹和时间戳下联合描述无线传播、视觉外观、无人机(UAV)运动、激光雷达(LiDAR)感知和雷达感知。为满足这一需求，本文引入了一个低空多模态基础数据集，命名为LAMBDA。LAMBDA具有高保真度、模态多样性、场景丰富性和配置灵活性的特点。它通过高保真数字孪生管线生成，包含精细的场景几何、精细化的材质分配以及无人机的电磁建模。LAMBDA在匹配的低空运行条件、共享坐标系和同步帧索引下，提供同步的RGB图像、深度图、LiDAR点云、惯性测量单元状态、UAV位姿、信道状态信息(CSI)和雷达合成资源。该数据集涵盖城市、郊区和校园场景，多无人机/多基站设置，夜间条件，以及晴、雨、雪、雾等天气变化。其CSI和雷达资源支持用户自定义的天线阵列尺寸、带宽、子载波间隔、线性调频参数，以及平面波或球面波前信道合成。通过质量控制、天气与多模态可视化，以及两个UAV ISAC相关用例（RGB辅助波束预测和RGB-LiDAR联合UAV定位），对LAMBDA的可靠性和可用性进行了评估。

</details>


### [294] [Task-Oriented Multimodal Edge Intelligence via Integrated Sensing-Communication-Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03907)
*Weiwei Chen,Yinghui He,Zhong Ye,Dingzhu Wen,Guanding Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向任务的多模态集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）框架，利用最大编码率缩减（MCR²）准则实现设备端紧凑判别特征提取与边缘端联合推理，在有限资源下显著优于单模态及基线方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISCC设计依赖单模态感知，易受遮挡、环境不确定性和模态故障影响，鲁棒性差；多模态ISCC虽更具潜力，但面临数据维度高、通信/计算/能耗预算紧张、模态间关联复杂等挑战，目前研究尚不充分。

Method: 提出任务导向的多模态ISCC框架，包含设备端特征提取与边缘端联合多模态推理；采用MCR²准则学习紧凑且具判别性的任务相关特征，并以此作为边缘端感知评估的度量；将感知精度最大化问题建模为含时延和资源约束的优化问题，通过等价变换后采用块坐标下降（BCD）算法求解。

Result: 在人类活动识别任务上，基于公开数据集的实验表明，所提方法在有限资源条件下始终优于三种基线方案。

Conclusion: MCR²准则在特征提取与感知评估方面相较于传统交叉熵方法具有明显优势，所提多模态ISCC框架为资源受限场景下的鲁棒边缘智能提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing, communication, and computation (ISCC) has recently emerged as a unified framework for enabling edge intelligence. However, existing ISCC designs predominantly rely on single-modal sensing, which is inherently vulnerable to occlusions, environmental uncertainties, and modality-specific failures, leading to degraded robustness in real-world deployments. This motivates the need for multi-modal ISCC, yet its design remains insufficiently explored. Compared with the single-modal case, multi-modal ISCC is more challenging because heterogeneous modalities enlarge data dimensionality and tighten communication/computation/energy budgets, while inter-modal correlations further complicate performance characterization. To address these challenges, we propose a task-oriented multi-modal ISCC framework that integrates device-side feature extraction with edge-side joint multi-modal inference. A central component of our approach is the maximal coding rate reduction (MCR^2) criterion, which enables each device to learn compact and discriminative task-relevant features, offering clear advantages over conventional cross-entropy-based extractors. We further leverage MCR^2 as a principled metric for edge-side sensing evaluation. On this basis, we formulate a sensing accuracy maximization problem under delay and resource constraints and develop an efficient block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm after transforming the problem into a more tractable equivalent form. Focusing on a human activity recognition task, we conduct extensive experiments on publicly available datasets to evaluate the performance of the proposed ISCC framework. The results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms three baseline schemes under limited resource conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）最近作为一种实现边缘智能的统一框架而出现。然而，现有ISCC设计主要依赖单模态感知，这本质上容易受到遮挡、环境不确定性以及模态特定故障的影响，导致实际部署中的鲁棒性下降。这激发了多模态ISCC的需求，但其设计尚未得到充分探索。与单模态情况相比，多模态ISCC更具挑战性，因为异质模态扩大了数据维度并压缩了通信/计算/能耗预算，而模态间的相关性进一步复杂化了性能表征。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种任务导向的多模态ISCC框架，该框架整合了设备端特征提取与边缘端联合多模态推理。我们方法的核心组件是最大编码率缩减（MCR²）准则，它使每个设备能够学习紧凑且具判别性的任务相关特征，相较于传统的基于交叉熵的特征提取器具有明显优势。我们进一步利用MCR²作为边缘端感知评估的原则性度量。在此基础上，我们在时延和资源约束下构建了一个感知精度最大化问题，并在将问题转化为更易处理的等价形式后，开发了一种高效的块坐标下降（BCD）算法。以人类活动识别任务为例，我们在公开数据集上进行了广泛实验，以评估所提ISCC框架的性能。结果表明，在有限资源条件下，我们的方法始终优于三种基线方案。

</details>


### [295] [A Triple-Band Bandpass Filter Using Square Open-Loop Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03921)
*Rashmi Ravi,Bhaskarareddy N C,Augustine O. Nwajana*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了多频带滤波器设计技术，并设计了一款工作于2.1/2.2/2.3 GHz的三频带通滤波器原型，采用九个方形开口环谐振器，在Rogers RT/Duroid 6010LM基板上实现，仿真结果显示插损低至0.674 dB，回损良好，验证了开口环谐振器变换技术的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多频段无线通信系统的快速发展需要紧凑、高性能的多频带通滤波器来隔离宽频谱中的特定频段，现有设计技术需要进一步验证和优化。

Method: 采用九个方形开口环谐振器（每个通带三个），特征阻抗50 Ω，分数带宽6%，在Rogers RT/Duroid 6010LM基板（介电常数10.7，损耗角正切0.0023，厚度1.27 mm）上利用Keysight ADS软件进行设计与仿真。

Result: 三个通带（2.1/2.2/2.3 GHz）的插入损耗分别为0.674 dB、0.976 dB和1.314 dB；回波损耗分别为16.785 dB、33.609 dB和17.162 dB。2.2 GHz中心频率实现高精度。

Conclusion: 方形开口环谐振器变换技术可有效用于现代无线通信系统中的紧凑型多频带滤波器设计，仿真结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of multi-band wireless communication systems has driven demand for compact, high-performance multi-band bandpass filters (BPFs) capable of isolating specific frequency bands within a wider spectrum. This paper reviews the current state-of-the-art in multi-band filter design and implementation techniques and presents the design, simulation, and characterisation of a prototype triple-band bandpass filter to validate one of the investigated techniques. A triple-band BPF operating at 2.1 GHz, 2.2 GHz, and 2.3 GHz is designed using Keysight ADS software and implemented on Rogers RT/Duroid 6010LM substrate (dielectric constant = 10.7, loss tangent = 0.0023, thickness = 1.27 mm). The design employs nine square open-loop resonators three per passband with a characteristic impedance of 50 Ω and a fractional bandwidth of 6%. Simulation results demonstrate insertion losses of 0.674 dB, 0.976 dB, and 1.314 dB, and return losses of 16.785 dB, 33.609 dB, and 17.162 dB across the three passbands respectively. The 2.2 GHz centre frequency is achieved with high precision, confirming the effectiveness of the square open-loop resonator transformation technique for compact multi-band filter design in modern wireless communication systems.

中文摘要翻译: 多频段无线通信系统的快速发展推动了对紧凑、高性能多频带通滤波器的需求，这类滤波器能够在较宽频谱内隔离特定频段。本文综述了当前多频带滤波器设计与实现技术的最新进展，并展示了一款原型三频带通滤波器的设计、仿真与特性表征，以验证所研究技术之一的有效性。该三频带通滤波器工作于2.1 GHz、2.2 GHz和2.3 GHz，采用Keysight ADS软件设计，并在Rogers RT/Duroid 6010LM基板（介电常数=10.7，损耗角正切=0.0023，厚度=1.27 mm）上实现。设计采用九个方形开口环谐振器——每个通带三个——特征阻抗为50 Ω，分数带宽为6%。仿真结果表明，三个通带的插入损耗分别为0.674 dB、0.976 dB和1.314 dB，回波损耗分别为16.785 dB、33.609 dB和17.162 dB。2.2 GHz中心频率实现高精度，验证了方形开口环谐振器变换技术在现代无线通信系统中设计紧凑型多频带滤波器的有效性。

</details>


### [296] [Optimal Uplink Pinching-Antenna Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04159)
*Zhenqiao Cheng,Chongjun Ouyang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究上行链路多用户捏合天线系统（PASS），提出三种搜索算法（贪婪搜索、波束搜索、分支定界搜索）来优化有限预配置位置下的和速率，数值结果显示所提算法优于传统固定阵列，且贪婪搜索和中宽度波束搜索能以更低计算成本达到近最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对实际部署中每个捏合天线（PA）只能被限制在有限数量的预配置位置上这一实际约束，作者需要解决由此带来的上行链路和速率最大化问题。

Method: 将有约束的和速率最大化问题建模为分层树搜索，分别提出三种算法：1) 贪婪搜索（GS），通过高效矩阵逆更新选择局部最优分支；2) 波束搜索（BeS），保留若干有前景的局部路径以提供可调的性能-复杂度权衡；3) 分支定界搜索（BnB），通过单调变换后的目标函数剪枝非竞争子树且不损失最优性。

Result: 数值结果表明所提算法相比传统固定阵列有显著性能提升；贪婪搜索和中宽度波束搜索能够以低于分支定界搜索的计算成本实现近最优性能。

Conclusion: 贪婪搜索和波束搜索在实际部署中能够在性能与计算复杂度之间取得良好平衡，是实现上行链路多用户捏合天线系统的有效方案。

Original Abstract: An uplink multiuser pinching-antenna system (PASS) is considered, where multiple dielectric waveguides are deployed at the base station and one pinching antenna (PA) is activated on each waveguide. For practical implementation, each PA is restricted to a finite number of preconfigured locations. The resulting uplink sum-rate maximization problem is represented as a layered tree search. Three algorithms are then developed: a greedy search (GS), a beam search (BeS), and an optimal branch-and-bound (BnB) search. In GS, the locally best branch is selected through efficient matrix-inverse updates. In BeS, several promising partial paths are retained to provide a tunable performance-complexity tradeoff. In BnB, noncompetitive subtrees are pruned through a monotonic transformed objective without loss of optimality. Substantial gains over a conventional fixed array are demonstrated by numerical results. Near-optimal performance is also achieved by GS and moderate-width BeS at a lower computational co t than BnB.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种上行链路多用户捏合天线系统（PASS），其中基站的多个介电波导上各激活一个捏合天线（PA）。在实际部署中，每个PA被限制在有限数量的预配置位置上。由此产生的上行链路和速率最大化问题被建模为分层树搜索。本文开发了三种算法：贪婪搜索（GS）、波束搜索（BeS）和最优分支定界搜索（BnB）。在GS中，通过高效的矩阵逆更新选择局部最优分支。在BeS中，保留若干有前景的局部路径以提供可调的性能-复杂度权衡。在BnB中，通过单调变换后的目标函数剪枝非竞争子树，且不损失最优性。数值结果表明，所提算法相比传统固定阵列具有显著增益。贪婪搜索和中宽度波束搜索能够以低于BnB的计算成本实现近最优性能。

</details>


### [297] [Light Coils: MRI with Fully Optical Data and Power Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04211)
*Zining Liu,Morteza Teymoori,Jakob Gerlach,Reza Aghabagheri,Henning Helmers,Michael Bock,Caglar Ataman,Ali Oezen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Light Coils概念，一种全光学的MRI接收架构，利用光纤替代传统电缆传输数据、供电和实现线圈失谐。通过Mach-Zehnder调制器将MR信号编码到C波段光载波，采用光纤供能（PoF）系统驱动前置放大器，并利用脉冲序列触发的光路控制主动失谐。在3T MRI系统上验证了单通道和四通道阵列，实现了与常规同轴读出的可比信噪比，并展示了密集波分复用下超过28dB的通道间光隔离和并行成像能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统高密度接收线圈阵列受限于电缆和电子元件的电磁耦合、集成复杂度和安全性约束，增加线圈数量面临瓶颈。需要一种轻量化、模块化且可扩展的新型接收架构来突破这些限制。

Method: 1) 采用Mach-Zehnder调制器将MR信号从每个线圈转换到C波段光载波进行信号编码；2) 使用光纤供能（PoF）系统配合高效光伏电池实现光-电功率转换，驱动前置放大器；3) 利用脉冲序列触发的光学路径控制主动失谐；4) 联合优化调制器偏置、光功率和前端增益使其与常规RF线圈系统信噪比匹配；5) 在四通道阵列中使用密集波分复用实现单纤多通道传输。

Result: 1) Light Coils在MZM输入功率5-10mW和光电转换器输入80-100mW时可匹配传统电缆RF线圈系统的信噪比；2) 在临床3T MRI系统上，单通道活体人脑成像质量和SNR与常规同轴读出相当；3) 四通道阵列通过密集波分复用实现单纤传输，通道间光隔离>28dB；4) 相比传统电缆参考系统，噪声相关性降低，并实现了并行成像。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一条基于集成光子学和光纤供能的可扩展路径，有望实现轻量化、模块化且潜在超密集的MRI接收阵列，为突破传统线圈阵列的电缆数量限制提供了全光学解决方案。

Original Abstract: In MRI, dense receiver coil arrays with a high number of coil elements are used to efficiently detect and encode the signal. Further increasing the number of coils is hampered by electrical cabling and massive electronics that introduce electromagnetic coupling, integration complexity and even safety constraints. Here we introduce the novel Light Coils concept, a fully optical MRI receive architecture in which data transmission, front-end power delivery, and coil detuning are all implemented optically, thereby reducing the massive galvanic cabling to a few optical fibers. For signal encoding, Mach-Zehnder modulators (MZM) are used to convert the MR signal from each coil onto a C-band optical carrier. The preamplifiers are driven via a power-over-fiber (PoF) system that uses a high-efficiency photovoltaic (PV) cell for optical-to-electrical power conversion. A pulse-sequence-triggered optical path controls active detuning. Jointly optimizing modulator bias, optical power and front-end gain under realistic receiver chain conditions, Light Coils can match the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of conventional RF coil systems with galvanic cables at MZM input powers of 5-10mW and photonic power converter inputs of 80-100mW. At a clinical 3T MRI system, we show in vivo human brain imaging with a single-channel Light Coil element with an image quality and SNR comparable to a conventional coaxial readout using the identical coil element. Extending the concept to a four-channel array using dense wavelength-division multiplexing over a single fiber, we demonstrate wavelength-selective routing with inter-channel optical isolation exceeding 28dB, reduced noise correlation compared with the galvanic reference, and parallel imaging. These results establish a scalable route towards lightweight, modular, and potentially ultra-dense MRI receive arrays based on integrated photonics and power-over-fiber.

中文摘要翻译: 在磁共振成像中，采用具有大量线圈单元的高密度接收线圈阵列来高效检测和编码信号。进一步增加线圈数量受限于电缆和大量电子元件，它们会引入电磁耦合、集成复杂性甚至安全性约束。本文提出了新型Light Coils概念，一种全光学MRI接收架构，其中数据传输、前端供电和线圈失谐均通过光学方式实现，从而将大量电缆减少为几根光纤。在信号编码方面，采用Mach-Zehnder调制器（MZM）将来自每个线圈的MR信号转换到C波段光载波上。前置放大器通过光纤供能（PoF）系统驱动，该系统使用高效光伏（PV）电池进行光电功率转换。脉冲序列触发的光路控制主动失谐。通过联合优化调制器偏置、光功率和前端增益，在 realistic 接收链条件下，Light Coils可在MZM输入功率5-10mW和光子功率转换器输入80-100mW时匹配传统电缆RF线圈系统的信噪比（SNR）。在临床3T MRI系统上，我们展示了使用单通道Light Coils元件的活体人脑成像，其图像质量和信噪比与使用相同线圈元件的常规同轴读出相当。将该概念扩展到使用单纤密集波分复用的四通道阵列，我们展示了波长选择性路由，通道间光隔离超过28dB，与电缆参考相比降低了噪声相关性，并实现了并行成像。这些结果为基于集成光子学和光纤供能的轻量化、模块化且潜在超密集MRI接收阵列建立了一条可扩展的路径。

</details>


### [298] [AI-RAN on NPUs: Baseband Processing Without Baseband Chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04224)
*Shilong Zhang,Luping Xiang,Jienan Chen,Kun Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次证明NPU也能高效完成无线基带处理，通过将通信算法重构为AI计算原语，解决NPU架构与基带负载之间的根本失配问题，并在Ascend 310B1边缘NPU上验证了完整的OFDM收发机。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI-RAN范式目前主要基于GPU实现，但NPU作为专为推理优化的AI加速器，能否支持无线基带处理尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 通过揭示计算同构性——NPU的矩阵与向量引擎天然覆盖物理层计算，但存在架构失配：传统基带追求最小化算术运算，NPU性能取决于引擎利用率最大化。因此，将通信算法重构为AI计算原语，优先优化引擎利用率而非算术操作数。

Result: 在Ascend 310B1边缘NPU上实现了完整的OFDM收发机，并通过USRP X300在3.0 GHz频段上验证了端到端的空中传输。

Conclusion: NPU能够有效支持无线基带处理，为AI-RAN在NPU平台上的部署提供了首个实证依据。

Original Abstract: AI-RAN aims to unify artificial intelligence and radio access network workloads on a shared compute substrate. While this paradigm has so far been demonstrated primarily on Graphics Processing Units (GPUs), it remains unclear whether Neural Processing Units (NPUs), which are AI accelerators optimized for inference, can also support wireless baseband processing. Here, we provide the first affirmative answer by resolving the fundamental mismatch between baseband workloads and NPU architecture. A computational isomorphism exists: matrix and vector engines NPUs dedicate to inference inherently cover physical-layer operations. Yet NPU architectures are natively shaped for dense-tensor AI inference, not baseband. This architectural mismatch surfaces as opposing optimization objectives: traditional baseband minimizes arithmetic operations, whereas NPU performance demands maximizing engine utilization. We close this gap by reconstructing communication algorithms onto AI compute primitives, prioritizing engine utilization over arithmetic count. We validate this with a complete OFDM transceiver on an Ascend 310B1 edge NPU, demonstrating end-to-end over-the-air transmission via USRP X300 at 3.0 GHz.

中文摘要翻译: AI-RAN旨在将人工智能与无线接入网工作负载统一到共享计算基底上。尽管该范式目前主要在图形处理单元（GPU）上得到验证，但尚不清楚神经处理单元（NPU）——一种专为推理优化的AI加速器——能否同样支持无线基带处理。本文首次给出了肯定答案，通过解决基带负载与NPU架构之间的根本失配问题。双方存在计算同构性：NPU用于推理的矩阵与向量引擎天然覆盖物理层运算。然而，NPU架构本质上是为密集张量AI推理而非基带处理设计的。这种架构失配体现为相反的优化目标：传统基带追求最小化算术操作数，而NPU性能取决于最大化引擎利用率。我们通过将通信算法重构为AI计算原语来弥合这一差距，优先考虑引擎利用率而非算术操作数。我们在Ascend 310B1边缘NPU上验证了完整的OFDM收发机，通过USRP X300在3.0 GHz频段上实现了端到端空中传输。

</details>


### [299] [Cognitive Digital Twins for Self-Aware Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04299)
*Afan Ali,Ali Arshad Nasir,Daniel Benevides da Costa*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的认知数字孪生框架，结合变分自编码器和潜层激活监控，检测信道估计中的分布漂移并自主执行生命周期管理，无需真实信道标签，在中等和严重漂移下优于传统离线训练方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于AI/ML的信道估计器在传播条件偏离其训练分布时性能会无声退化，现有方法缺乏自主检测和应对分布漂移的能力。

Method: 提出模型无关的认知数字孪生（CDT）框架，结合变分自编码器（VAE）与潜层激活监控，检测分布漂移并自主执行continue、update、retire三种生命周期动作，无需真实信道知识。

Result: 在多种信道上的仿真表明，该框架能够准确检测漂移并实现鲁棒的信道估计，在中等和严重信道漂移下持续优于传统离线训练的深度学习估计器。

Conclusion: 该CDT框架与3GPP AI原生生命周期管理完全兼容，为通信系统中AI模型的自适应维护提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML)-based channel estimators silently degrade when propagation conditions drift from their training distributions. This letter proposes a model-agnostic cognitive digital twin (CDT) framework that combines a variational autoencoder (VAE) with latent activation monitoring to detect distribution drift and autonomously execute \textsc{continue}, \textsc{update}, or \textsc{retire} lifecycle actions without requiring ground-truth channel knowledge. The proposed framework is fully compatible with the AI-native lifecycle management envisioned in 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP). Simulations over various channels demonstrate accurate drift detection and robust channel estimation, consistently outperforming conventional offline-trained deep learning estimators under moderate and severe channel drift.

中文摘要翻译: 基于人工智能（AI）和机器学习（ML）的信道估计器在传播条件偏离其训练分布时会无声地退化。本文提出了一种模型无关的认知数字孪生（CDT）框架，该框架结合了变分自编码器（VAE）与潜层激活监控，用于检测分布漂移并自主执行continue（继续）、update（更新）或retire（退役）生命周期动作，无需依赖真实信道知识。所提框架与第三代合作伙伴计划（3GPP）设想的AI原生生命周期管理完全兼容。在多种信道上的仿真结果表明，该方法能够准确检测漂移并实现鲁棒的信道估计，在中等和严重信道漂移下持续优于传统离线训练的深度学习估计器。

</details>


### [300] [Deadline-Bound Finite-Object Delivery over Intermittent LEO Satellite Contact Plans under Residual-Service Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04405)
*Houtianfu Wang,O. Tansel Baydas,Hanlin Cai,Haofan Dong,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星中继网络在间歇性星间/星地通信计划下传输完整数据对象的问题，提出了一种残差服务感知的交付层，解决了路径私有评估中重复计数和漏计问题，并通过有界双向条带化方法将平均和中位交付间隙缩小约40%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在低轨卫星中继网络中，数据对象（如感知瓦片、遥测数据、模型更新和检查点）需要在间歇性的星间和星地通信计划中传输。当数据对象被分割到多条候选路径上传输时，路径私有的评估方法可能会重复计算同一条链路的服务量，或遗漏部分完成情况，导致无法准确判断完整对象是否能在截止时间前交付。因此，需要一种能够感知残差服务并准确评估交付可行性的方法。

Method: 提出了一种残差服务感知的交付层，该层从接触计划路由生成中消费候选路径，并在每条链路先入先出（FIFO）残差服务条件下测试完整对象能否在截止时间前交付。对于边不相交的互补接触，该层退化为固定路径服务；推导了充分的服务预算条件，在该条件下双向条带化严格扩大可行载荷区域。最终采用有界双向条带化方法进行优化。

Result: 在受控的共享接触争用下，路径私有评估会导致最多154秒的交付完成低估，且可能对无残差服务完成的固定计划报告有限完成时间。在20-180颗卫星的程序化接触模型上，有界双向条带化方法将平均和中位交付间隙相较受限参考基线缩小约40%，而P90和最差情况间隙保持不变。

Conclusion: 残差服务感知的交付层能够有效解决路径私有评估中的重复计数和漏计问题，有界双向条带化策略在保持最坏情况性能不变的前提下，显著改善了平均和中位交付性能，为LEO中继网络中的可靠数据交付提供了理论依据和实用方法。

Original Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) relay networks deliver finite objects -- sensing tiles, telemetry blocks, model updates, and checkpoints -- over intermittent inter-satellite and space-to-ground contact plans. Partial delivery is insufficient when the complete object misses its deadline. When an object is split across candidate paths, a path-private evaluation can count the same contact service more than once and silently under-count completion. We develop a residual-service-aware delivery layer that consumes candidate paths from contact-plan route generation and tests whether the complete object can be delivered before its deadline under per-edge first-in-first-out residual service. Under controlled shared-contact contention, path-private evaluation under-counts completion by up to 154 s and can report finite completion for a fixed plan with no residual-service completion. For edge-disjoint complementary contacts, the layer reduces to fixed-path service; we derive a sufficient service-budget condition under which two-way striping strictly enlarges the feasible payload region. We verify a restricted exhaustive reference, characterize runtime over a 20-180-satellite procedural contact model, and show that bounded two-way striping reduces mean and median gaps to the restricted reference by about 40%, while P90 and worst-case gaps remain unchanged.

中文摘要翻译: 低地球轨道（LEO）中继网络在间歇性的星间和星地接触计划上传递有限的数据对象——感知瓦片、遥测数据块、模型更新和检查点。当完整对象错过截止时间时，部分交付是不够的。当一个对象被分割到多条候选路径上时，路径私有的评估可能会重复计算同一条链路的服务量，或在无意中漏计完成情况。我们开发了一个残差服务感知的交付层，该层消耗来自接触计划路由生成的候选路径，并在每条链路先入先出（FIFO）残差服务条件下测试完整对象能否在截止时间前交付。在受控的共享接触争用下，路径私有评估会导致最多154秒的完成低估，并且可能对没有残差服务完成的固定计划报告有限完成时间。对于边不相交的互补接触，该层退化为固定路径服务；我们推导了一个充分的服务预算条件，在该条件下双向条带化严格扩大可行载荷区域。我们验证了一个受限的穷举参考，在20-180颗卫星的程序化接触模型上表征了运行时特性，并证明有界双向条带化将平均和中位交付间隙相较受限参考缩小约40%，而P90和最差情况间隙保持不变。

</details>


### [301] [Non-invasive Blood Glucose Estimation from Wearable Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04414)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Yilong Wang,Kewei Yang,Tianyang Lei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习的动态增量学习（DIL）框架，结合MERDC模块和PGBA模块，用于无创血糖估计，在纵向PPG数据上实现了0.64 mmol/L的MAE，并公开了一个包含183名受试者285天数据的纵向基准数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纵向PPG数据存在分布漂移，且参考毛细血管血糖标签稀疏且获取成本高，现有方法难以在无标签的PPG流上进行标签高效的自适应学习。

Method: 提出动态增量学习（DIL）框架，包含两个核心模块：1）基于互熵优化的动态聚类模块（MERDC），采用回放策略；2）不确定性量化的代理梯度桥接智能体（PGBA），用于标签高效地适应未标记的PPG数据流。

Result: 在5折独立于受试者的验证下，MAE为0.64±0.01 mmol/L，RMSE为1.29±0.10 mmol/L，97.69±1.63%的估计值落在Clarke A+B区域，聚合级别分析进一步验证了误差分布的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该结果为可穿戴生理传感中的自适应无创血糖估计提供了概念验证，并建立了纵向基准数据集以支持后续研究。

Original Abstract: Non-invasive blood glucose estimation from wearable physiological signals remains difficult because longitudinal photoplethysmography (PPG) data are subject to distribution drift, whereas reference capillary blood glucose labels are sparse and costly to acquire. We propose a \rev{deep-learning-based} dynamic incremental learning (DIL) framework that combines a mutual entropy-optimized replay-based dynamic clustering module (MERDC) with an uncertainty-quantified proxy gradient bridging agent (PGBA) for label-efficient adaptation to unlabeled PPG streams. To support this setting, we further establish a longitudinal benchmark dataset comprising PPG, reference capillary blood glucose, and cuff blood pressure measurements from 183 participants collected over 285 days, and we make this resource available to the research community. Under 5-fold subject-independent validation, the proposed method achieves a mean absolute error (MAE) of $0.64 \pm 0.01$ millimoles per liter (mmol/L) and a root mean square error (RMSE) of $1.29 \pm 0.10$ mmol/L, with $97.69 \pm 1.63\%$ of estimates falling within Clarke zones A+B. Aggregation-level analyses further support the robustness of the observed error distribution beyond window-level evaluation. \rev{These results provide a proof-of-concept for adaptive non-invasive glucose estimation in wearable physiological sensing and establish a longitudinal benchmark for subsequent research.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可穿戴生理信号的无创血糖估计仍然困难，原因在于纵向光电容积描记（PPG）数据存在分布漂移，而参考毛细血管血糖标签稀疏且获取成本高昂。我们提出了一个基于深度学习的动态增量学习（DIL）框架，该框架结合了基于互熵优化的回放式动态聚类模块（MERDC）和不确定性量化的代理梯度桥接智能体（PGBA），用于标签高效地适应未标记的PPG数据流。为支持这一设定，我们进一步建立了一个纵向基准数据集，包含183名受试者在285天内采集的PPG、参考毛细血管血糖和袖带血压测量值，并将该资源公开提供给研究社区。在5折独立于受试者的验证下，所提方法实现了0.64±0.01毫摩尔/升（mmol/L）的平均绝对误差（MAE）和1.29±0.10 mmol/L的均方根误差（RMSE），97.69±1.63%的估计值落在Clarke A+B区域。聚合级别分析进一步支持了观察到的误差分布超出窗口级别评估的稳健性。这些结果为可穿戴生理传感中的自适应无创血糖估计提供了概念验证，并建立了用于后续研究的纵向基准。

</details>


### [302] [A Simultaneous Clustering and Tracking Algorithm for Capturing Cluster-Level Spatial Consistency in 6G Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04664)
*Jiaxin Lin,Pan Tang,Jianhua Zhang,Zhaowei Chang,Peijie Liu,Yufeng Qin,Ke Chen,Huixin Xu,Byonghyo Shim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于马氏距离的联合聚类与跟踪算法(MD-SCT)，利用多径分量在时延、角度和空间域的联合分布，实现6G亚太赫兹频段下信道簇演化的平滑跟踪，有效提升空间一致性建模的可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有聚类与跟踪方法未能充分利用多径分量的空间相关性：跟踪后聚类方法将聚类与跟踪解耦，而联合聚类与跟踪方法主要依赖前一快照的簇中心，导致簇级空间一致性不足，难以满足6G大规模MIMO、通感一体化和太赫兹通信等场景对真实簇基信道模型的需求。

Method: 提出基于马氏距离的同步聚类与跟踪(MD-SCT)算法。该算法在时延、角度和空间域上捕捉聚类多径分量的联合分布，利用马氏距离将连续快照中的多径分量与已有簇关联，从而在聚类的同时实现固有跟踪。

Result: 采用均方连续差和梯度变化率评估性能，结果表明所提算法能够产生更平滑的簇演化轨迹，提升了6G空间一致性建模中簇信道的可靠性。

Conclusion: MD-SCT算法通过马氏距离联合建模多径分量的多维分布，实现了聚类与跟踪的一体化，在亚太赫兹频段下获得了更平滑的簇演化，为6G簇级空间一致性信道建模提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Spatial consistency is a fundamental physical property of wireless channels that reflects the smooth evolution of the channel between spatial locations. At the cluster level, it requires similar multipath components (MPCs) remain grouped into the same clusters as the transceivers move, enabling consistent cluster tracking. Cluster-level spatial consistency is essential for realistic cluster-based channel models, especially for potential 6G techniques such as massive MIMO, integrated sensing and communication, and terahertz (THz) communication. However, existing clustering and tracking methods do not fully exploit spatial correlations of MPCs. In tracking-after-clustering, clustering and tracking are decoupled, while joint clustering-and-tracking mainly relies on cluster centers from the previous snapshot. In this work, we propose a Mahalanobis-distance-based simultaneous clustering and tracking (MD-SCT) algorithm to capture the joint distribution of clustered MPCs in the delay, angular and spatial domains. Under Mahalanobis distance, MPCs in successive snapshots are associated with existing clusters, thereby inherently tracking while clustering. The algorithm is further applied in the sub-THz band. Performance is evaluated using mean square successive difference and gradient change rate. The results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm yields smoother cluster evolution. This improves the reliability of clustered channels for spatial consistency modeling in 6G.

中文摘要翻译: 空间一致性是无线信道的基本物理属性，反映了信道在空间位置之间的平滑演化。在簇层面上，它要求随着收发机移动，相似的多径分量(MPCs)仍归为同一簇，从而实现一致的簇跟踪。簇级空间一致性对于基于簇的真实信道模型至关重要，特别是对于潜在6G技术如大规模MIMO、通感一体化和太赫兹(THz)通信。然而，现有的聚类与跟踪方法未能充分利用多径分量的空间相关性。在跟踪后聚类方法中，聚类与跟踪是解耦的，而联合聚类与跟踪主要依赖前一快照的簇中心。本文提出一种基于马氏距离的同步聚类与跟踪(MD-SCT)算法，以捕捉聚类多径分量在时延、角度和空间域上的联合分布。在马氏距离下，连续快照中的多径分量与已有簇相关联，从而在聚类的同时实现固有跟踪。该算法进一步应用于亚太赫兹频段。采用均方连续差和梯度变化率评估性能。结果表明，所提算法能产生更平滑的簇演化，这提升了6G空间一致性建模中簇信道的可靠性。

</details>


### [303] [Multi-Target ISAR Imaging of UAV Swarms Using Fast Reweighted Atomic Norm Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04703)
*Mohammad Roueinfar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出将快速重加权原子范数去噪（FRAND）算法扩展至无人机集群ISAR成像中，通过2DADMM优化解决多目标稀疏孔径下的信号分离与高分辨成像问题，在低SNR条件下优于MUSIC和Cadzow方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无人机集群中多个紧密间隔目标的后向散射信号发生相干叠加，导致传统ISAR成像方法难以有效分离和分辨各目标，亟需一种能够处理多目标稀疏孔径且抗噪声能力强的成像算法。

Method: 将原子范数最小化问题重新公式化以适应集群成像场景，引入加权正则化，并采用二维交替方向乘子法（2DADMM）进行高效优化求解，从而实现复合回波中单个无人机信号的分离与高分辨距离-多普勒成像。

Result: FRAND算法能够有效解混复合信号，生成单个无人机的高分辨距离-多普勒图像；在低信噪比条件下，其性能优于MUSIC和Cadzow等传统方法；均方误差（MSE）定量评估证实了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了原子范数最小化在复杂多目标雷达成像应用中的强大潜力，为无人机集群ISAR成像提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Inverse Synthetic Aperture Radar (ISAR) imaging of UAV swarms presents significant challenges due to the coherent superposition of backscattered signals from multiple closely spaced targets. This work explores the extension of the Fast Reweighted Atomic Norm Denoising (FRAND) algorithm to this multi-target scenario. We develop a comprehensive mathematical framework that reformulates the atomic norm minimization problem for swarm imaging, incorporating weighted regularization and efficient optimization via the TwoDimensional Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (2DADMM). The proposed method handles both sparse aperture conditions and additive white Gaussian noise while maintaining computational efficiency. We simulate an ISAR system receiving composite echoes from UAV swarms, each modeled with distinct scattering centers. The results demonstrate that FRAND effectively disentangles the mixed signals and generates high-resolution range-Doppler profiles for individual UAVs, outperforming traditional methods like Multiple Signal Classification (MUSIC) and Cadzow in low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) conditions. Quantitative evaluation using MeanSquare Error (MSE) criteria confirms the superiority of the proposed approach. This study establishes the strong potential of atomic norm minimization for complex multi-target radar imaging applications.

中文摘要翻译: 逆合成孔径雷达（ISAR）对无人机集群成像面临重大挑战，因为多个紧密间隔目标的后向散射信号会发生相干叠加。本文探索了将快速重加权原子范数去噪（FRAND）算法扩展至这一多目标场景。我们建立了一个全面的数学框架，重新表述了面向集群成像的原子范数最小化问题，融入了加权正则化并通过二维交替方向乘子法（2DADMM）实现高效优化。所提方法既能处理稀疏孔径条件，又能应对加性高斯白噪声，同时保持计算效率。我们模拟了一个接收无人机集群复合回波的ISAR系统，每个无人机均以不同的散射中心建模。结果表明，FRAND能够有效解混复合信号，生成单个无人机的高分辨距离-多普勒图像，在低信噪比（SNR）条件下优于传统方法如多信号分类（MUSIC）和Cadzow。基于均方误差（MSE）准则的定量评估证实了所提方法的优越性。本研究确立了原子范数最小化在复杂多目标雷达成像应用中的强大潜力。

</details>


### [304] [Dual Fluid Antenna-Assisted UAV MU-MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04748)
*Runke Fan,Tianheng Xu,Pei Peng,Xianfu Chen,Celimuge Wu,Kai-Kit Wong,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对无人机网络动态信道和多用户干扰问题，提出双流体天线辅助MU-MIMO下行通信方案，通过联合优化无人机轨迹、收发FA位置和波束成形，实现平均可达速率提升120%（vs FPA）和110%（vs现有FA方案）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 克服固定位置天线（FPA）在动态无人机信道和多用户干扰场景下的性能局限性，利用流体天线（FA）的位置自适应性和灵活波束成形能力来提升系统性能。

Method: 建立双FA辅助的无人机网络MU-MIMO下行通信模型，以最大化平均可达速率为目标，联合优化无人机轨迹、收发两端FA位置和波束成形；针对高度耦合的非凸问题，设计基于交替优化（AO）的算法，将问题分解为若干子问题分别求解，得到次优解。

Result: 所提方案较传统FPA基线获得120%的性能增益，较现有FA基线获得110%的性能增益。

Conclusion: FA辅助的无人机网络通过位置自适应性和灵活波束成形，能显著提升MU-MIMO下行通信的平均可达速率，优于传统FPA和现有FA方案。

Original Abstract: Fluid Antennas (FAs)-assisted Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) networks leverage the FA position adaptivity and flexible beamforming to overcome the limitations of Fixed-Positioned Antennas (FPAs) in dynamic UAV channels and Multi-User (MU) interference. This letter investigates a dual FA-assisted UAV network for MU-Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output (MIMO) downlink communications, aiming to maximize the average achievable rate through the joint optimization of UAV trajectory, the transmit/receive FA positions, and beamforming. The formulated problem is highly coupled and non-convex. Accordingly, an efficient Alternating Optimization (AO)-based algorithm is developed for decomposed subproblems, yielding a suboptimal solution. Numerical results demonstrate significant performance gains of 120% and 110% over conventional FPA-based and existing FA-based baselines, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 流体天线辅助的无人机网络利用FA的位置自适应性和灵活波束成形，克服了固定位置天线在动态无人机信道和多用户干扰中的局限性。本文研究了一种双FA辅助的无人机网络用于多用户多输入多输出下行通信，旨在通过联合优化无人机轨迹、收发端FA位置和波束成形来最大化平均可达速率。所构建的问题高度耦合且非凸。为此，我们开发了一种基于交替优化的高效算法，将问题分解为若干子问题并求得次优解。数值结果表明，与传统FPA基线和现有FA基线相比，所提方案分别获得了120%和110%的显著性能增益。

</details>


### [305] [Shift-MoE-Based DJSCC for CSI Feedback in Multi-User Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04795)
*Jian Zou,Yifan Lian,Yongsheng Liang,Fanyang Meng,Wenwu Xie,Liang Yang,Jian Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对频分双工系统中钉螺天线系统的CSI反馈难题，提出了一种基于深度联合源信道编码的端到端CSI反馈方案Shift-MoE，通过通道分组移位操作和门控MLP混合专家模块，有效捕获网格依赖并适应异质用户统计特性，在归一化均方误差上优于现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在频分双工系统中，钉螺天线系统的性能提升严重依赖于基站端精确的信道状态信息。然而，PASS的CSI在波导-天线网格上呈现结构化相关性，且不同用户间存在显著异质性，导致传统固定反馈映射难以泛化。

Method: 提出Shift-MoE方案：1）采用通道分组的一步移位操作（channel-grouped one-step shift operations）来捕获网格依赖关系，避免全局注意力机制的高复杂度；2）引入门控多层感知机混合专家模块（gated MLP mixture-of-experts），自适应地建模不同用户的异质CSI统计特性。

Result: 数值结果表明，Shift-MoE在归一化均方误差指标上持续优于代表性基于学习的CSI反馈基线方法，并在不同系统参数设置下保持有效性。

Conclusion: Shift-MoE方案能够有效解决PASS中CSI的结构化相关性和用户异质性挑战，在不同系统参数下均表现稳健，为频分双工钉螺天线系统提供了一种高效的端到端CSI反馈方案。

Original Abstract: In frequency-division duplexing systems, the performance gains of pinching-antenna systems (PASS) critically depend on accurate channel state information (CSI) at the base station. However, PASS CSI exhibits structured correlations over the waveguide-antenna grid and pronounced heterogeneity across users, making conventional fixed feedback mappings difficult to generalize. To address this challenge, this letter proposes an end-to-end CSI feedback scheme over a noisy uplink feedback link based on deep joint source-channel coding, termed Shift-based Mixture-of-Experts (Shift-MoE). Specifically, Shift-MoE leverages channel-grouped one-step shift operations to capture grid dependencies without global attention, and employs a gated multilayer perceptron mixture-of-experts module to adapt to heterogeneous CSI statistics across users. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed Shift-MoE consistently outperforms representative learning-based CSI feedback baselines in normalized mean squared error and remains effective under different system parameter settings.

中文摘要翻译: 在频分双工系统中，钉螺天线系统的性能提升严重依赖于基站端精确的信道状态信息。然而，PASS的CSI在波导-天线网格上呈现结构化相关性，且不同用户间存在显著异质性，使得传统的固定反馈映射难以泛化。为应对这一挑战，本文提出了一种基于深度联合源信道编码的端到端CSI反馈方案，称为Shift-based Mixture-of-Experts（Shift-MoE），用于存在噪声的上行反馈链路。具体而言，Shift-MoE利用通道分组的一步移位操作来捕获网格依赖关系而无需全局注意力，并采用门控多层感知机混合专家模块来适应不同用户间的异质CSI统计特性。数值结果表明，所提出的Shift-MoE在归一化均方误差上持续优于代表性的基于学习的CSI反馈基线方法，并在不同系统参数设置下保持有效性。

</details>


### [306] [Amplitude-Independent Robust Snapshot 6-D Radio SLAM via a Uniffed Angle-Delay Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04847)
*Shengqiang Shen,Aoyun Hao,Weihao Geng,Lei Yang,Shiyin Li,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖信号幅度的鲁棒无线SLAM方法，基于统一的角度-时延公式，在单快拍多径信道下对未知6-D位姿和时钟偏置的UE进行定位并重建环境地标，无需幅度预分类或路径损耗模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有鲁棒快拍无线SLAM方法主要在平面/2D场景下开发，依赖路径幅度或路径损耗信息处理LoS，对校准误差和传播模型失配敏感，缺乏适用于3D/6D且与幅度无关的鲁棒方法。

Method: (1) 粗阶段：基于角度-时延统一公式，直接从测量值中选择几何一致的内点，无需幅度预分类或路径潜在变量；扩展到3D/6D位姿估计，采用twist-swing两阶段遍历初始化和SO(3)局部优化。(2) 精细阶段：Jacobian行均衡的迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)优化，结合准Akaike信息准则(QAIC)模型比较，检测LoS路径并联合优化UE状态与散射点。

Result: 仿真表明，所提方法与经校准的幅度依赖基线方法相比具有竞争力，且对路径损耗模型失配具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的幅度无关鲁棒无线SLAM方法在3D/6D场景下有效，摆脱了对幅度信息的依赖，提升了模型失配下的鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了更可靠的方案。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses bistatic snapshot radio SLAM, in which a user equipment (UE) with unknown 6-D pose and clock bias is localized and environmental landmarks are reconstructed from a single multipath channel snapshot. Under mixed line-of-sight (LoS)/non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation, existing robust snapshot SLAM methods are mainly developed or validated in planar/2-D settings and often use path-amplitude or path-loss information for LoS handling, which makes them sensitive to calibration errors and propagation-model mismatch. We propose an amplitude-independent robust radio SLAM method built on a uniffed angle-delay formulation for LoS and single-bounce NLoS inlier paths. In the coarse stage, the method estimates the UE state and selects geometrically consistent inliers directly from angle-delay measurements, without amplitudebased LoS preclassiffcation or path-wise latent variables; the formulation is further extended to general 3-D/6-D pose estimation through twist-swing two-stage traversal initialization and local reffnement on SO(3). A subsequent Jacobian-row-equilibrated iteratively reweighted least-squares (IRLS) reffnement, combined with quasi-Akaike information criterion (QAIC) model comparison, detects the LoS path and jointly reffnes the UE state and scattering points. We also analyze formulation-speciffc local-rank properties and their minimal-set implications under unknown path identity. Simulations show that the proposed method remains competitive with calibrated amplitude-dependent baselines and is more robust to path-loss-model mismatch.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究双站快拍无线SLAM问题，其中具有未知6维位姿和时钟偏置的用户设备(UE)通过单个多径信道快照实现定位与环境地标重建。在混合视距(LoS)/非视距(NLoS)传播条件下，现有鲁棒快拍SLAM方法主要在平面/二维场景下开发或验证，且通常利用路径幅度或路径损耗信息处理LoS，这使得它们对校准误差和传播模型失配敏感。本文提出一种与幅度无关的鲁棒无线SLAM方法，其基于LoS和单跳NLoS内点路径的统一角度-时延公式。在粗阶段，该方法直接从角度-时延测量中估计UE状态并选择几何一致的内点，无需基于幅度的LoS预分类或路径级隐变量；该公式进一步通过twist-swing两阶段遍历初始化和SO(3)局部优化扩展到通用的三维/六维位姿估计。随后的Jacobian行均衡迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)优化，结合准Akaike信息准则(QAIC)模型比较，检测LoS路径并联合优化UE状态与散射点。我们还分析了公式特定的局部秩性质及其在未知路径身份下的最小集含义。仿真表明，所提方法与经校准的幅度依赖基线方法相比具有竞争力，且对路径损耗模型失配具有更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [307] [Antenna Optimization for Decode-and-Forward Relay in Magnetic Induction Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04888)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Rui Wang,Xuefeng Yin,Zhibing Xu,Xinyu Qu,Bofeng Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对矿井隧道中采用磁感应（MI）通信的应急救援车辆，提出了一种基于几何建模与随机搜索算法的中继天线位置与姿态（PO）优化方法，以提升系统数据速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MI通信在矿井隧道中受天线位置和姿态影响极大，导致不可达空间增加、天线部署复杂，亟需优化中继天线的位置与姿态以实现更高数据速率。

Method: 建立中继MI天线PO优化问题，引入隧道约束；利用几何建模消除隧道约束，设计具有快速收敛和良好全局搜索能力的随机搜索算法进行求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法能够快速收敛到一个最优解，显著提升了弱信号场景下车载MI系统的数据速率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的几何建模结合随机搜索算法，能有效解决隧道中MI中继天线的PO优化问题，实现数据速率的明显改善。

Original Abstract: Magnetic Induction (MI) communication is effective in underground tunnels for emergency rescue vehicle due to the small-size antenna. It can highly benefit from a cooperative decode-and-forward (DF) relay to achieve a higher data rate. However, its channel gain is extremely position-and-orientation-selective. The unreachable space increases the complexity of the antenna deployment. To find the best antenna position and orientation (PO) of the relay achieving the higher data rate, this paper formulates the optimization problem of the relay MI antenna PO with tunnel constraints. To solve the problem more quickly, we propose to use geometric modeling to eliminate the tunnel constraints and develop a random-search algorithm achieving a fast convergence and excellent global search ability. Simulations show that the proposed algorithm can quickly converge to one optimum which signifies a noticeable improvement of data rate for vehicle MI systems with weak signals.

中文摘要翻译: 磁感应（MI）通信由于天线尺寸小，在矿井隧道中用于应急救援车辆时非常有效。采用协作解码转发（DF）中继可以显著提升数据速率。然而，其信道增益对天线位置和姿态极其敏感，不可达空间增加了天线部署的复杂度。为找到能实现更高数据速率的中继最佳天线位置与姿态（PO），本文构建了带有隧道约束的中继MI天线PO优化问题。为更快地求解该问题，我们提出利用几何建模消除隧道约束，并开发了一种具有快速收敛和优良全局搜索能力的随机搜索算法。仿真表明，所提算法能快速收敛到一个最优解，显著提升了弱信号下车载MI系统的数据速率。

</details>


### [308] [Energy Efficiency Maximization for Hybrid RIS-Aided Communications via Deep Unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04889)
*Pouya Mobaraki,Abolfazl Zakeri,Marco Di Renzo,Markku Juntti,Nhan Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对混合RIS辅助的MU-MISO下行链路能效最大化问题，提出了一种交替优化框架，其中BS波束赋形采用迫零加闭式功率分配，RIS子问题通过模型驱动的深度展开方法求解，相比基准方案实现了约30%的能效提升，且仅激活少量RIS元件即可捕获绝大部分能效增益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RIS辅助通信系统研究中，大多数工作假设RIS元件要么全为无源、要么全为有源，缺乏对混合RIS（元件可在有源/无源模式间动态切换）的能效优化研究；同时，实际硬件效应（如功率预算、放大增益限制、放大噪声、二进制相位控制）常被忽略，导致理论方案与实际部署存在差距。

Method: 提出交替优化框架：1）BS波束赋形子问题采用迫零预编码结合闭式功率分配求解；2）RIS子问题采用模型驱动的深度展开（deep unfolding）方法处理，该方法将迭代优化过程展开为可学习的神经网络结构，兼顾模型可解释性与学习能力。

Result: 1）所提方法收敛速度更快，能效比无深度展开的基线方法高约30%；2）相比全有源RIS配置，能效提升至少10%；3）相比全无源RIS设计，能效提升可达三倍；4）仅激活少量RIS元件并分配少量动态功率预算，即可获得绝大部分能效增益。

Conclusion: 混合RIS通过动态配置有源/无源模式，能够在能效方面显著优于全有源或全无源RIS；模型驱动的深度展开方法能有效求解混合RIS的复杂优化问题；实际部署中无需激活大量有源元件即可实现接近最优的能效性能。

Original Abstract: We address energy-efficiency (EE) maximization in a multiuser (MU) multiple-input single-output (MISO) downlink system assisted by a hybrid reconfigurable intelligent surface(RIS), where each element can be dynamically configured to operate in either active or passive mode depending on whether its power amplifier is engaged. Practical hardware effects are explicitly incorporated, including base station (BS) and RIS power budgets, active-element amplifier gain limits, amplification noise, and binary phase control. To solve the problem, we develop an alternating-optimization framework in which the BS beamforming subproblem is handled via zero-forcing with closed-form power allocation, while the RIS subproblem is addressed using a model-driven deep unfolding approach. Numerical results show that the proposed method achieves faster convergence and higher EE than the considered benchmark schemes. In particular, it attains about 30% higher EE than the procedure without deep unfolding. Furthermore, our simulations demonstrate at least 10% EE improvement over the fully active RIS configuration and up to threefold EE gains compared with the fully passive RIS design. The results also show that most of the achievable EE gain can be captured by activating only a small fraction of RIS elements and allocating only a small portion of the dynamic power budget to the RIS.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了混合可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的多用户多输入单输出（MU-MISO）下行链路系统中的能效（EE）最大化问题，其中每个元件可根据其功率放大器是否启用，动态配置为有源或无源模式。我们明确纳入了实际硬件效应，包括基站（BS）和RIS的功率预算、有源元件的放大器增益限制、放大噪声以及二进制相位控制。为求解该问题，我们开发了一个交替优化框架：其中BS波束赋形子问题通过迫零结合闭式功率分配处理，而RIS子问题则采用模型驱动的深度展开方法求解。数值结果表明，所提方法相比所考虑的基准方案实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的能效。具体而言，其能效比不使用深度展开的方案高出约30%。此外，我们的仿真表明，与全有源RIS配置相比能效提升至少10%，与全无源RIS设计相比能效增益可达三倍。结果还显示，仅激活一小部分RIS元件并仅分配少量动态功率预算给RIS，即可捕获绝大部分可实现的能效增益。

</details>


### [309] [A Body-of-Revolution Human Model for RF Sensing with Measurement-Driven Calibration for Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05053)
*Haoqing Wen,Michele D'Amico,Matteo Oldoni,Federica Fieramosca,Vittorio Rampa,Stefano Savazzi,Qi Wu,Gian Guido Gentili*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于旋转体（BoR）人体模型的快速2.5维有限元法（2.5-D FEM），用于室内无设备定位（DFL）和射频（RF）感知的散射场预测，结合测量驱动的背景场建模，实现了约85%的预测精度，显著降低了计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无设备定位和RF感知系统依赖大规模数据集，但实际测量成本高、耗时长，需要一种轻量级但物理一致的传播模型来高效生成真实的RF感知数据。

Method: 提出基于旋转体（BoR）人体模型的2.5维有限元法（2.5-D FEM）计算垂直极化偶极子激励下人体散射场；引入测量驱动的背景场建模方法，隐式考虑室内复杂传播效应。

Result: BoR模型保持了接近3D人体的散射特性，计算成本低于简单圆柱模型；在典型室内DFL场景中，预测精度约达85%，能够复现实际观测到的空间RSSI变化。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于RF感知预测和大规模数据库生成，计算成本仅为全波仿真的很小一部分，具有良好的实用潜力。

Original Abstract: Model training for Device-Free Localization (DFL) and Radio-Frequency (RF) sensing systems heavily relies on large-scale datasets, which are costly and time-consuming to obtain through measurements across different environments and sensing configurations. Lightweight yet physically consistent propagation models are therefore critical for efficient generation of realistic RF sensing data. This paper presents an RF sensing prediction approach for indoor environments based on a Body of Revolution (BoR) human model. A fast 2.5-Dimensional Finite Element Method (2.5-D FEM) is proposed for computing the scattering fields of a human-like BoR model under the excitation of a vertical polarized dipole. Through comparisons, the proposed BoR model is shown to preserve scattering characteristics close to 3-D human bodies while yielding a smaller computational cost compared to a simple cylindrical model. A measurement-driven background-field modeling approach is further introduced for practical indoor applications, accounting for the complex propagation effects of indoor environments implicitly. Comparing with measurements of a typical indoor DFL scenario, the proposed approach achieves approximately 85% prediction accuracy and reproduces the spatial Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) variations observed in practice, proving its potential for RF sensing prediction and large-scale database generation at a fraction of the computational cost required for full-wave simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 无设备定位（DFL）和射频（RF）感知系统的模型训练严重依赖于大规模数据集，而通过在环境和感知配置各异的条件下进行测量来获取这些数据集，既昂贵又耗时。因此，轻量级且物理一致的传播模型对于高效生成真实的RF感知数据至关重要。本文提出一种基于旋转体（BoR）人体模型的室内RF感知预测方法。该方法采用一种快速的2.5维有限元法（2.5-D FEM），用于计算在垂直极化偶极子激励下类人体BoR模型的散射场。通过对比，所提出的BoR模型在保持接近3D人体散射特性的同时，相比简单圆柱模型具有更小的计算成本。此外，本文还引入了一种测量驱动的背景场建模方法，用于实际室内应用，隐式地考虑了室内环境的复杂传播效应。与典型室内DFL场景的实测数据对比表明，所提方法达到了约85%的预测精度，并能够复现实际观测到的空间接收信号强度指示（RSSI）变化，证明了该方法在RF感知预测和大规模数据库生成方面的潜力，而其计算成本仅为全波仿真的很小一部分。

</details>


### [310] [An Ambiguity-Function-Assisted Newtonized Channel Estimation Method for Pulse-Shaped AFDM Under Fractional Delay and Doppler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05082)
*Yuanfang Ma,Zulin Wang,Yuanhan Ni,Tao Luo,Peng Yuan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对脉冲成形AFDM系统在分数倍归一化时延-多普勒双选信道下的信道估计难题，提出了一种低复杂度模糊函数辅助牛顿化信道估计方法，通过闭式输入输出关系与离线预计算模糊函数实现高效牛顿细化估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在脉冲成形AFDM系统中，分数倍归一化时延和多普勒下的双选信道精确信道估计仍具挑战性，现有方法复杂度高或精度不足。

Method: 首先推导了分数倍时延-多普勒下脉冲成形AFDM的闭式输入输出关系；然后通过离线预计算并存储成形脉冲的离散化模糊函数，结合定制的循环移位与堆叠操作实现低复杂度表示；在此基础上，采用牛顿细化方法进行分数倍时延-多普勒信道估计，并利用低复杂度输入输出表示高效计算所需的雅可比矩阵和海森矩阵更新。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的AFNC方法能够以低复杂度实现脉冲成形AFDM系统在分数倍时延-多普勒双选信道下的精确信道估计。

Original Abstract: Accurate channel estimation for pulse-shaped AFDM systems over doubly selective channels with fractional normalized delay and Doppler remains challenging. This paper proposes a low-complexity ambiguity-function-assisted newtonized channel (AFNC) estimation method. Specifically, we first present a closed-form input-output relation for pulse-shaped affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) under fractional normalized delay and Doppler. As a further step, we demonstrate that the input-output relation admits a low-complexity representation by offline precomputing and storing the discretized ambiguity function of the shaping pulse, followed by tailored cyclic-shift and stacking operations. Building on this representation, AFNC performs fractional delay-Doppler channel estimation through Newtonized refinement, where the required Jacobian and Hessian updates are computed efficiently using the low-complexity input-output representation. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

中文摘要翻译: 在具有分数倍归一化时延和多普勒的双选信道上，对脉冲成形AFDM系统进行精确信道估计仍然具有挑战性。本文提出了一种低复杂度的模糊函数辅助牛顿化信道估计方法。具体地，我们首先给出了分数倍归一化时延和多普勒下脉冲成形仿射频分复用系统的闭式输入输出关系。进一步，我们证明通过离线预计算并存储成形脉冲的离散化模糊函数，再结合定制的循环移位与堆叠操作，该输入输出关系可以实现低复杂度表示。基于此表示，AFNC通过牛顿细化进行分数倍时延-多普勒信道估计，其中所需的雅可比矩阵和海森矩阵更新均利用低复杂度输入输出表示高效计算。仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [311] [Multiuser MIMO-AFDM Beamforming for ISAC in Doubly Dispersive Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05119)
*Rang Liu,Ming Li,A. Lee Swindlehurst,Norman Franchi,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高移动性信道中的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，提出了一种基于仿射傅里叶变换域的单站多用户MIMO AFDM下行链路设计，通过数据辅助的延迟-多普勒检测器与加权和速率最大化框架，在感知与通信的权衡中优于传统OFDM方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高移动性信道中的延迟-多普勒色散会导致传统OFDM系统的性能严重下降，而现有ISAC波形设计在应对此类色散时鲁棒性不足，因此需要开发一种能够同时兼顾通信速率与感知质量且对多普勒效应鲁棒的波形与波束赋形设计方案。

Method: 建立了离散仿射傅里叶变换（DAFT）域模型以保留多普勒引起的跨子载波耦合；推导了数据辅助的延迟-多普勒检测器；基于检测器信噪比与发射协方差波束方向图的比例关系，引入了基于检测器信噪比的扇区照射约束；采用加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）与主最小化（MM）相结合的框架求解感知约束下的加权和速率最大化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提AFDM设计在速率-感知权衡、多普勒鲁棒性以及延迟-多普勒感知质量方面均优于传统OFDM方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的AFDM系统设计能够有效应对高移动性信道中的延迟-多普勒色散，在ISAC场景下实现了比OFDM更优的性能权衡，为未来高速移动通信与感知一体化系统提供了有前景的波形设计方案。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in high-mobility channels requires waveform and beamforming designs that are robust to delay-Doppler dispersion. With this in mind, in this paper we study a monostatic multiuser multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) downlink system. We develop a discrete affine Fourier transform (DAFT)-domain model that preserves Doppler-induced inter-bin coupling and derive a data-aided delay-Doppler detector. The expected matched-bin detector signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is shown to be proportional to a transmit-covariance beampattern, which leads to a detector-SNR-based sector-illumination constraint. The resulting sensing-constrained weighted sum-rate maximization problem is solved using a combined weighted minimum mean squared error (WMMSE) and majorization-minimization (MM) formulation. Simulations show that the proposed AFDM design outperforms its orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) counterpart in terms of the rate-sensing tradeoff, robustness to Doppler, and delay-Doppler sensing quality.

中文摘要翻译: 高移动性信道中的集成感知与通信（ISAC）要求波形和波束赋形设计对延迟-多普勒色散具有鲁棒性。基于此，本文研究了一种单站多用户多输入多输出（MIMO）仿射频率分复用（AFDM）下行链路系统。我们建立了一个保留多普勒引起的跨子载波耦合的离散仿射傅里叶变换（DAFT）域模型，并推导了一种数据辅助的延迟-多普勒检测器。理论表明，期望匹配子载波检测器的信噪比（SNR）与发射协方差波束方向图成正比，由此导出一个基于检测器信噪比的扇区照射约束。采用加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）与主最小化（MM）相结合的公式求解由此产生的感知约束加权和速率最大化问题。仿真结果表明，所提出的AFDM设计在速率-感知权衡、多普勒鲁棒性以及延迟-多普勒感知质量方面均优于其正交频分复用（OFDM）对应方案。

</details>


### [312] [Optimal Base Station Placement for Beyond 5G Networks with Non-Convex Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05210)
*Mohamed Shalma,Amr Mansour,Ahmed El-Mahdy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对U形非凸拓扑环境中的毫米波基站最优部署问题，提出基于深度强化学习的DQN和分区多空间DDPG两种方法，后者以更低复杂度实现了接近全覆盖和0.99的Jain公平指数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: U形非凸拓扑环境中的毫米波基站部署问题因非凸拓扑和非凸目标函数（和速率最大化与最大最小公平性）而具有NP-hard挑战性，传统优化方法难以求解。

Method: 将基站部署问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），提出两种深度强化学习方法：1）将部署区域离散化为网格，使用深度Q网络（DQN）优化；2）将U形区域划分为连续子空间，每个子空间分配一个深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）智能体，再从中选取最优基站位置。

Result: 最优部署实现了全覆盖，Jain公平指数达到0.99；提出的分区多空间DDPG方法相比DQN取得了更优解且计算复杂度更低。

Conclusion: 基于MDP的深度强化学习方法能有效解决非凸拓扑环境中的基站最优部署问题，其中分区多空间DDPG在性能和复杂度之间取得了更好的平衡。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal placement of a millimeter-wave (mmWave) base station (BS) within a realistic U-shaped environment with non-convex topology. The problem is challenging and NP-hard due to the non-convex topology and the non-convex objective functions which are the sum-rate maximization and max-min fairness, the latter being additionally non-smooth. To address this challenge, the BS placement is formulated as a Markov Decision Process (MDP). Then, we propose two deep reinforcement learning (DRL) techniques: First, the deployment area is discretized into a grid and optimized using a Deep Q-Network (DQN). Second, the U-shaped region is partitioned into continuous subspaces, where a Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) agent is dedicated to each subspace then the best BS placement is selected among partitions. Results demonstrate that optimal placement achieves full coverage and yields a Jain index of 0.99. Furthermore, the proposed partitioned multi-space DDPG achieves better solution than DQN with lower complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在具有非凸拓扑的真实U形环境中毫米波基站的最优部署问题。由于非凸拓扑和非凸目标函数（和速率最大化与最大最小公平性，后者还额外具有非光滑性），该问题具有挑战性且为NP-hard。为应对这一挑战，将基站部署问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。继而，我们提出了两种深度强化学习技术：首先，将部署区域离散化为网格，并使用深度Q网络（DQN）进行优化；其次，将U形区域划分为连续子空间，每个子空间分配一个专用的深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）智能体，然后在各分区中选择最优的基站位置。结果表明，最优部署实现了全覆盖，Jain公平指数达到0.99。此外，所提出的分区多空间DDPG方法相比DQN取得了更优解且复杂度更低。

</details>


### [313] [Wavelet Scattering Transform for Interpretable Schizophrenia Biomarker Discovery and Classification from Resting-State EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05282)
*Md. Taksimul Ahsan Tawhid,Nasif Ahmed Rafe,Alif Tahmid Priyom,K. M. Mustafizur Rahman*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合多阶小波散射变换、留一被试出交叉验证和SHAP可解释性的脑电图诊断框架，在严格无数据泄露的条件下对精神分裂症进行分类和生物标志物发现，随机森林达到90.48%准确率（AUC=0.9339），并发现跨频率耦合的伽马波段特征是最关键的神经电生理标志物。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于EEG的精神分裂症自动分类方法存在两个核心问题：一是依赖静态功率谱密度特征，无法捕捉振幅调制动力学和跨频率耦合等对精神分裂症病理生理至关重要的动态信息；二是在交叉验证中采用epoch级别划分导致时间数据泄漏，人为虚高报告的性能指标。因此，亟需一个数学上严谨、无数据泄漏且可解释的诊断框架。

Method: 首先，从静息态多通道EEG中提取多阶小波散射变换系数，捕获多尺度振幅调制结构。然后，通过被试级别的ANOVA结合Benjamini-Hochberg错误发现率校正筛选显著生物标志物。最后，分别使用随机森林和支持向量机分类器，在严格的留一被试出交叉验证和被试级别多数投票策略下进行评估，并结合SHAP进行模型解释。

Result: 二阶散射系数（编码跨频率耦合信息）在判别性生物标志物集中占主导地位，其中伽马波段特征最为显著。电极P3被确定为最具判别力的单一位点。在严格的被试独立评估下，随机森林达到90.48%准确率（AUC=0.9339，灵敏度=95.56%）。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的WST框架建立了一个严谨、可解释的EEG驱动精神疾病生物标志物发现标准，证明时间振幅调制是精神分裂症的主要电生理特征，且该框架未来可推广应用于精神分裂症亚型的检测。

Original Abstract: Schizophrenia is a debilitating neuropsychiatric disorder characterized by profound cortical network dysregulation, for which objective, clinically translatable EEG based biomarkers remain underdeveloped. Existing automated classification pipelines rely predominantly on static power spectral density features inherently blind to amplitude modulation dynamics and cross-frequency coupling, phenomena central to schizophrenia pathophysiology, while adopting epoch level cross validation strategies that introduce temporal data leakage, artificially inflate reported performance. This study introduces a mathematically principled diagnostic framework integrating the multi-order Wavelet Scattering Transform(WST), strict Leave One Subject Out (LOSO) cross-validation, and SHAP explainability for simultaneous EEG classification and biomarker discovery. Hierarchical WST coefficients capturing multi-scale amplitude modulation structure were extracted from resting state multichannel EEG. Subject-level ANOVA with Benjamini Hochberg false discovery rate correction identified significant biomarkers, with Random Forest and SVM classifiers evaluated under strict LOSO cross validation and subject-level majority voting. Second-order scattering coefficients encoding cross frequency coupling dominated the discriminative biomarker set, with gamma-band features most prevalent, demonstrating that temporal amplitude modulation constitutes the primary electrophysiological signature of schizophrenia. Electrode P3 was identified as the single most discriminative site. Under rigorous subject independent evaluation, the Random Forest achieved 90.48% accuracy (AUC = 0.9339; sensitivity = 95.56%). The proposed WST framework establishes a rigorous, interpretable standard for EEG-driven psychiatric biomarker discovery that can also be applicable in the detection of schizophrenia subtypes in the future.

中文摘要翻译: 精神分裂症是一种以皮层网络严重失调为特征的神经精神疾病，目前尚缺乏客观、临床可转化的基于EEG的生物标志物。现有的自动分类流程主要依赖静态功率谱密度特征，这类特征本质上无法捕捉振幅调制动力学和跨频率耦合——这些现象正是精神分裂症病理生理学的核心；同时，现有方法采用epoch级别的交叉验证策略，导致时间数据泄漏，人为虚高了报告的性能。本研究引入了一个数学严谨的诊断框架，将多阶小波散射变换、严格的留一被试出交叉验证和SHAP可解释性相结合，用于同步进行EEG分类和生物标志物发现。从静息态多通道EEG中提取了能够捕获多尺度振幅调制结构的分层WST系数。通过被试级别的ANOVA结合Benjamini-Hochberg错误发现率校正识别显著生物标志物，并在严格的LOSO交叉验证和被试级别多数投票策略下评估随机森林和支持向量机分类器。编码跨频率耦合的二阶散射系数在判别性生物标志物集中占主导地位，其中伽马波段特征最为普遍，这表明时间振幅调制是精神分裂症的主要电生理学特征。电极P3被确定为最具判别力的单一位点。在严格的被试独立评估下，随机森林达到了90.48%的准确率（AUC=0.9339；灵敏度=95.56%）。所提出的WST框架建立了一个严谨、可解释的EEG驱动精神疾病生物标志物发现标准，未来也可应用于精神分裂症亚型的检测。

</details>
